  <script>
  /* TeachMate speed: detect low-end devices BEFORE first paint and switch to "lite" visuals.
     Force on:  localStorage.setItem('tm-lite','1')   Force off: localStorage.setItem('tm-lite','0') */
  (function(){
    try {
      var f = localStorage.getItem('tm-lite');
      var c = navigator.connection || {};
      var low = (navigator.deviceMemory && navigator.deviceMemory <= 4) ||
                (navigator.hardwareConcurrency && navigator.hardwareConcurrency <= 4) ||
                c.saveData === true;
      var lite = f === '1' || (f !== '0' && low);
      if (lite) document.documentElement.classList.add('tm-lite');
      window.__tmLite = !!lite;
    } catch (e) {}
  })();
  </script>
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.googleapis.com">
  <link rel="preconnect" href="https://fonts.gstatic.com" crossorigin>
  <!-- Fonts load without blocking first paint -->
  <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Poppins:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet" media="print" onload="this.media='all'">
  <noscript><link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Poppins:wght@400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet"></noscript>
  <script>
  /* Heavy libraries (jsPDF ~360KB, D3 ~280KB) are no longer loaded before the page appears.
     They are fetched in the background once the app is idle. */
  (function(){
    var loaded = {};
    window.__tmLoadScript = function(url){
      if (loaded[url]) return loaded[url];
      loaded[url] = new Promise(function(res, rej){
        var s = document.createElement('script');
        s.src = url; s.async = true;
        s.onload = function(){ res(); };
        s.onerror = function(){ delete loaded[url]; rej(new Error('load failed: ' + url)); };
        document.head.appendChild(s);
      });
      return loaded[url];
    };
    window.__tmLoadJsPDF = function(){ return window.jspdf ? Promise.resolve() : window.__tmLoadScript('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/jspdf/2.5.1/jspdf.umd.min.js'); };
    window.__tmLoadD3 = function(){ return window.d3 ? Promise.resolve() : window.__tmLoadScript('https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js'); };
    function warm(){ window.__tmLoadD3().catch(function(){}); window.__tmLoadJsPDF().catch(function(){}); }
    window.addEventListener('load', function(){
      if (window.requestIdleCallback) requestIdleCallback(warm, { timeout: 4000 });
      else setTimeout(warm, 2500);
    });
  })();
  </script>

  <script>
  // ── Loading overlay helpers ──────────────────────────────────
  function showLoading(message) {
    var modal = document.getElementById('loading-modal');
    if (!modal) return;
    var msgEl = document.getElementById('loading-msg');
    if (msgEl) msgEl.textContent = message || 'Loading…';
    modal.style.display = 'flex';
  }
  
  function hideLoading() {
    var modal = document.getElementById('loading-modal');
    if (modal) modal.style.display = 'none';
  }
  
  // Aliases to cover any casing (ShowLoading, showLoading, etc.)
  window.showLoading = showLoading;
  window.ShowLoading = showLoading; // 👈 capital ‘S’ alias
  window.hideLoading = hideLoading;
</script>

<!-- USAGE GUIDE MODAL -->
<div id="tm-help-modal" style="display:none; position:fixed; inset:0; z-index:2000; background:rgba(0,0,0,.55); align-items:flex-end; justify-content:center;">
  <div style="background:#fff; width:100%; max-width:640px; max-height:88vh; overflow-y:auto; border-radius:20px 20px 0 0; padding:1.4rem;">
    <div style="display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; margin-bottom:1rem;">
      <h2 style="margin:0; font-size:1.3rem; background:var(--gradient); -webkit-background-clip:text; -webkit-text-fill-color:transparent;">📖 How to Use TeachMate</h2>
      <button id="tm-help-close" type="button" style="width:auto; background:none; color:#333; font-size:1.4rem; padding:2px 10px; margin:0;">✕</button>
    </div>

    <div style="font-size:.92rem; line-height:1.55; color:#333;">
      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:14px 0 6px;">🧭 Getting Around</h3>
      <p>Everything you need is on the buttons under your name on the home screen. Tap any one of them to open that feature:</p>
      <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 1.1rem; padding:0;">
        <li><b>📋 Attendance Register</b> — mark and view daily attendance per class. Fully yours to edit.</li>
        <li><b>🏫 Class Management</b> — add students, edit class rosters. Fully yours to edit.</li>
        <li><b>📊 Results Tracking</b> — enter, save, and review test/exam results, then generate report cards from them. Fully yours to edit.</li>
        <li><b>💰 Fees Tracking (ZMW)</b> — <b>view-only.</b> You can see exactly what each student owes, what they've paid, the due date, and which months are covered — but the total fee amount, due date, and payments themselves are entered by your school's administrator, not here. This is intentional: it keeps one single source of truth for money records.</li>
        <li><b>📚 Lesson Planner</b> — plan lessons (appears once your account has access).</li>
        <li><b>💬 Messages</b> — communicate with parents/guardians.</li>
      </ul>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">📄 Generating Report Cards</h3>
      <p>Inside Results Tracking you have two ways to generate a report card, both producing the exact same professional PDF layout:</p>
      <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 1.1rem; padding:0;">
        <li><b>Export Student PDF</b> — one student at a time. You review and can edit their subjects, scores, and both comments before generating.</li>
        <li><b>📚 Bulk Generate Report Cards</b> — generates one for every student in the class automatically, one after another. You enter the shared details once (school logo, teacher name/phone, category, level), and it pulls each student's own scores and comments from what's already saved — using any comment you've personally edited before, or a fresh auto-generated one if you haven't. Students with no saved results yet are skipped, not force-generated with blank data. Your browser may ask permission to allow multiple downloads during bulk generation — approve it once so every file can save.</li>
      </ul>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">🔒 Premium Features</h3>
      <p>Inside Results, you'll see <b>🤖 Auto Mark</b>, <b>📲 Send Results via SMS</b>, <b>📄 Export Student PDF</b>, and <b>📚 Bulk Generate Report Cards</b>. If any of these show a small lock icon, it means your school administrator hasn't switched that feature on for your school yet — tap it to see the message, and ask your administrator directly if you need it enabled. This is a school-level setting, not something tied to you personally.</p>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">🏢 How TeachMate Fits Together</h3>
      <p>TeachMate is actually a small family of apps, each with a clear job, all reading from the same live school data:</p>
      <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 1.1rem; padding:0;">
        <li><b>This app (TeachMate 3.0)</b> — for teachers. Attendance, classes, results, report cards, lesson planning, messaging, and viewing (not editing) fees.</li>
        <li><b>The Admin Dashboard</b> — used by your school's administrator. Sets fee amounts and due dates, records parent payments, and handles deeper school administration. This is why fees appear here as view-only.</li>
        <li><b>The Headteacher Dashboard</b> — used by your headteacher. Approves new teacher accounts, and oversees school-wide data across every teacher and class.</li>
      </ul>
      <p>If something feels like it's "missing" here — like editing a fee amount, or approving a new colleague's account — it's very likely simply living in one of those other apps by design, not lost.</p>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">🏫 How Class Access Actually Works</h3>
      <p>This trips people up more than anything else, so here's exactly how it works:</p>
      <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 1.1rem; padding:0;">
        <li><b>Who assigns YOU to a class:</b> Your <b>headteacher</b> does this, from the Headteacher Dashboard — not the school administrator, and not something you can set yourself. They maintain the official list of which classes each teacher is responsible for. If a class you teach isn't showing up here, ask your headteacher to check your assignment — it's almost always a quick fix on their end.</li>
        <li><b>Who gets YOUR assignment to actually show up:</b> Once your headteacher assigns you, it should appear automatically next time you open the app or refresh. If it doesn't appear right away, it's usually just syncing — give it a moment with an internet connection, or log out and back in.</li>
        <li><b>How students land in a class:</b> Students get a <b>classId</b> on their record either by a teacher enrolling them directly (inside <b>🏫 Class Management</b> here in this app), or through a bulk import done by the school administrator (used when onboarding a whole school's worth of student data at once). Either way, once a student has a classId, every teacher assigned to that same class sees them immediately — there's no separate "adding a student to your view" step.</li>
        <li><b>Sharing a class with other teachers:</b> More than one teacher can be assigned to the exact same class at the same time — completely normal for subject-based teaching, e.g. a Math teacher and an English teacher both assigned to "Form 1 Blue." Everyone assigned sees the same student roster, and each of you enters data for your own subject independently — nobody overwrites anybody else's work. When a colleague sharing your class syncs new attendance, results, or fees data, you'll get a real-time notification naming exactly who did what, so you always know what's changed even if you weren't the one who entered it.</li>
      </ul>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">✋ Gestures & Shortcuts</h3>
      <ul style="margin:6px 0 0 1.1rem; padding:0;">
        <li><b>Double-tap a student's photo</b> to open it full-size.</li>
        <li><b>Pinch with two fingers</b> on an opened photo to zoom in/out, and <b>drag with one finger</b> to move around while zoomed in.</li>
        <li><b>Press and drag the 🌗 light/dark mode button</b> to move it anywhere on your screen — it stays there next time you open the app.</li>
        <li><b>Tap the "Tap to view your login email" pill</b> on the home screen any time you forget which email you registered with — tap again to hide it.</li>
      </ul>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">🔄 Staying in Sync</h3>
      <p>TeachMate saves your work on your device first, then syncs it automatically when you have internet — you can keep working even with no signal, and it'll catch up once you're back online. Look for "All Data Synced ✓" at the bottom of the screen to confirm everything's saved.</p>

      <h3 style="color:#00875f; margin:16px 0 6px;">🆘 Still Stuck?</h3>
      <p>Contact your school administrator or headteacher — they manage account approvals, fee records, and feature access for your school.</p>
    </div>
  </div>
</div>

<!-- ============================================================
     TEACHMATE 3.0 — LESSON PLANNER HTML
     
     HOW IT WORKS:
     • Same pattern as your attendance card — class="card", display:none
     • #lessonPlannerPanel  → JS injects all tabs + UI content here
     • #openPlannerBtn      → hidden until auth + profile are done
     • #panelOverlay        → dark backdrop, JS toggles class "hidden"
     • #closePlannerBtn     → inside panel header, JS injects it automatically
     
     TO SHOW THE OPEN BUTTON after auth + profile complete, call:
         showLessonPlanner();
     from your existing auth success / profile complete handler.
     
     PASTE THIS into your TeachMate HTML file alongside your
     other cards (attendance, results, etc.)
     ============================================================ -->


<!-- ============================================================
     1. OPEN BUTTON
     Hidden by default. Call showLessonPlanner() to reveal it
     after auth + profile setup are both complete.
     Same style as your other feature buttons.
     =====================================


<!-- ============================================================
     2. THE PANEL
     The JS (lessonPlanner_v3.js) finds this by id="lessonPlannerPanel"
     and builds the entire lesson planner UI inside it.
     It starts off-screen (translate-x-full) and slides in
     when openPlannerBtn is clicked.
     DO NOT add any content inside — the JS handles everything.
     ============================================================ -->
<div id="lessonPlannerPanel" class="card translate-x-full" style="
  display:none;
  position:fixed;
  top:0; right:0; bottom:0;
  width:100%;
  max-width:780px;
  z-index:9999;
  overflow:auto;
  padding:0;
  transition:transform 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1);
">
  <!-- JS injects header, tabs, and all content here -->
</div>


<!-- ============================================================
     3. OVERLAY
     Dark backdrop shown behind the panel when it opens.
     JS toggles class "hidden" to show/hide it.
     Clicking it closes the panel.
     ============================================================ -->
<div id="panelOverlay" class="auto" style="
  display:none;
  position:fixed;
  inset:0;
  background:rgba(0,0,0,0.55);
  z-index:9998;
  transition:opacity 0.3s;
"></div>


<!-- ============================================================
     4. SCRIPT
     Paste AFTER your lessonPlanner_v3.js <script> tag.
     
     This does 3 things:
     a) Fixes the overlay show/hide (JS uses class "hidden" but
        your overlay also needs display toggled)
     b) Exposes showLessonPlanner() for your auth/profile handlers
     c) Hides the panel + overlay properly on close
     ============================================================ -->
<script>
  (function() {
    
    // ── Wait for DOM ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function ready(fn) {
      if (document.readyState !== 'loading') fn();
      else document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', fn);
    }
    
    ready(function() {
      
      var panel = document.getElementById('lessonPlannerPanel');
      var overlay = document.getElementById('panelOverlay');
      var openBtn = document.getElementById('openPlannerBtn');
      
      if (!panel || !overlay) return;
      
      // ── Fix: JS toggles class "hidden" on overlay but
      //    it also needs display:block/none ──────────────────────────
      var overlayObserver = new MutationObserver(function() {
        if (overlay.classList.contains('hidden')) {
          overlay.style.display = 'none';
        } else {
          overlay.style.display = 'block';
        }
      });
      overlayObserver.observe(overlay, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
      
      // ── Fix: JS toggles "translate-x-full" on panel but
      //    it also needs display:block/none ──────────────────────────
      var panelObserver = new MutationObserver(function() {
        if (panel.classList.contains('translate-x-full')) {
          // Give CSS transition time to finish before hiding
          setTimeout(function() {
            if (panel.classList.contains('translate-x-full')) {
              panel.style.display = 'none';
            }
          }, 360);
        } else {
          panel.style.display = 'block';
        }
      });
      panelObserver.observe(panel, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['class'] });
      
      // ── Open function (also wired to openPlannerBtn by JS) ────────
      function openPlanner() {
        panel.style.display = 'block';
        // Small delay so display:block renders before the transition
        requestAnimationFrame(function() {
          requestAnimationFrame(function() {
            panel.classList.remove('translate-x-full');
            overlay.classList.remove('hidden');
            overlay.style.display = 'block';
          });
        });
      }
      
      // ── Close function ────────────────────────────────────────────
      function closePlanner() {
        panel.classList.add('translate-x-full');
        overlay.classList.add('hidden');
        overlay.style.display = 'none';
      }
      
      // Wire open button
      if (openBtn) {
        openBtn.addEventListener('click', openPlanner);
      }
      
      // Wire overlay click to close
      overlay.addEventListener('click', closePlanner);
      
      // ── showLessonPlanner()
      //    Call this from your auth success + profile complete handlers.
      //    Both must succeed before the button appears.
      //    Example usage in your existing code:
      //
      //      // After login succeeds:
      //      window._lpAuthOk = true;
      //      showLessonPlanner();
      //
      //      // After profile setup completes:
      //      window._lpProfileOk = true;
      //      showLessonPlanner();
      // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
      window._lpAuthOk = false;
      window._lpProfileOk = false;
      
      window.showLessonPlanner = function() {
        if (window._lpAuthOk && window._lpProfileOk) {
          if (openBtn) openBtn.style.display = 'inline-block';
        }
      };
      
      // ── Also expose open/close globally so any part of your
      //    app can trigger them ─────────────────────────────────────
      window.openLessonPlanner = openPlanner;
      window.closeLessonPlanner = closePlanner;
      
      // 👇 THIS IS WHERE YOU PASTE
      window._lpAuthOk = true;
      window._lpProfileOk = true;
      showLessonPlanner();
      
      
      
    });
    
  })();
</script>

      <h2 id="modal-title" class="modal-title">Student Photo</h2>

<div id="profile-modal" role="dialog" aria-label="Teacher Onboarding">
  <div class="tm-card">

    <!-- ── Header ── -->
    <div class="tm-header">
      <h2>🎓 Teacher Onboarding</h2>
      <p>Complete your profile to join your school</p>
    </div>

    <!-- ── Body ── -->
    <div class="tm-body">

      <!-- PERSONAL INFO -->
      <div class="tm-section">Personal Information</div>

      <div class="tm-field">
        <label for="pf-name">👤 Full Name <span class="req">*</span></label>
        <input class="tm-input" type="text" id="pf-name"
          placeholder="e.g. Chanda Mwanza" autocomplete="off">
      </div>

      <div class="tm-field">
        <label for="pf-ts-number">
          🪪 TS Number <span class="req">*</span>
          <span class="hint">(Teacher Service number)</span>
        </label>
        <input class="tm-input" type="text" id="pf-ts-number"
          placeholder="e.g. TS/2024/001234" autocomplete="off">
      </div>

      <div class="tm-field">
        <label for="pf-phone">
          📱 Phone Number <span class="req">*</span>
          <span class="hint">(+260xxxxxxxxx)</span>
        </label>
        <input class="tm-input" type="tel" id="pf-phone"
          placeholder="+260970123456" autocomplete="off">
      </div>

      <!-- SCHOOL DETAILS -->

<script>
  // Stub functions so the page doesn't error — replace with your real implementations
  function lookupSchoolCode() {
    const code = document.getElementById('pf-school-code').value.trim();
    const info = document.getElementById('pf-school-info');
    if (!code) { alert('Please enter a school code first.'); return; }
    // Simulate a lookup response — replace with your real Firebase/API call
    info.style.display = 'block';
    info.textContent = '✅ Munali Secondary School — Lusaka (demo result)';
  }
  function addSubject() {
    const input = document.getElementById('pf-subject-input');
    const chips = document.getElementById('pf-subject-chips');
    const val = input.value.trim();
    if (!val) return;
    const chip = document.createElement('span');
    chip.className = 'tm-chip';
    chip.innerHTML = `${val}<button type="button" onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" title="Remove">✕</button>`;
    chips.appendChild(chip);
    input.value = '';
    input.focus();
  }
  function previewProfilePhoto(input) {
    if (input.files && input.files[0]) {
      const reader = new FileReader();
      reader.onload = e => document.getElementById('pf-photo-preview').src = e.target.result;
      reader.readAsDataURL(input.files[0]);
    }
  }
  function saveProfile() {
    alert('saveProfile() called — connect your real implementation here.');
  }
</script>
</body>
</html>

<!-- ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     TEACHMATE LESSON PLANNER v3.1 — COMPATIBLE HTML BOOTSTRAP
     
     HOW TO USE:
     1. Copy the three blocks below into your app's HTML
     2. Load lessonPlanner_v3.js BEFORE closing </body>
     3. The JS handles everything — no extra script needed
     
     Required IDs (do NOT rename these):
       #lessonPlannerPanel  — the slide-in drawer
       #panelOverlay        — the dark backdrop
       #openPlannerBtn      — your trigger button (wherever you place it)
     
     Optional ID (only if you have a dedicated close button elsewhere):
       #closePlannerBtn
     ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->


<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     STEP 1 — TRIGGER BUTTON
     Place this wherever you want in your app's nav/toolbar.
     The JS wires the click automatically via #openPlannerBtn.
     ═════════════════════════════════════
<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     STEP 2 — SLIDE-IN PANEL
     The JS (lessonPlanner_v3.js) injects the full UI into this div.
     Do NOT put anything inside it — the JS owns this element.
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div id="lessonPlannerPanel" style="
  display: none;
  position: fixed;
  top: 0;
  right: 0;
  bottom: 0;
  width: 100%;
  max-width: 860px;
  z-index: 9999;
  overflow: hidden;
  padding: 0;
  background: #f6fbf9;
  box-shadow: -8px 0 48px rgba(0,0,0,0.28);
  transform: translateX(100%);
  transition: transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1);
">
  <!-- lessonPlanner_v3.js injects everything here -->
</div>


<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     STEP 3 — OVERLAY BACKDROP
     Clicking this closes the panel (wired by the JS automatically).
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div id="panelOverlay" style="
  display: none;
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  background: rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.52);
  z-index: 9998;
  backdrop-filter: blur(2px);
  -webkit-backdrop-filter: blur(2px);
">
</div>


<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     STEP 4 — LOAD THE JS
     Place this just before </body>. lessonPlanner_v3.js handles
     all open/close/UI logic — no extra bootstrap script required.
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->


<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     OPTIONAL — PANEL OPEN/CLOSE ANIMATION FIX
     
     The JS toggles the CSS class "translate-x-full" on the panel
     and "hidden" on the overlay. This small style block makes
     those class changes animate smoothly without Tailwind.
     
     If your app already uses Tailwind CSS, remove this block —
     Tailwind handles it automatically.
     ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════ -->
<style>
  /* Panel slide animation — synced with JS class toggling */
  #lessonPlannerPanel {
    transition: transform 0.32s cubic-bezier(0.22, 1, 0.36, 1),
                display 0s 0.32s; /* delay display:none until after slide-out */
  }
  #lessonPlannerPanel.translate-x-full {
    transform: translateX(100%);
  }

  /* Overlay fade */
  #panelOverlay {
    transition: opacity 0.28s ease;
    opacity: 0;
  }
  #panelOverlay:not(.hidden) {
    opacity: 1;
  }
  #panelOverlay.hidden {
    display: none !important;
    opacity: 0;
  }

  /* Prevent body scroll when panel is open */
  body.lp3-open {
    overflow: hidden;
  }

  /* Open Planner button hover */
  #openPlannerBtn:hover {
    filter: brightness(1.08);
    box-shadow: 0 5px 20px rgba(0,138,84,.38);
  }
  #openPlannerBtn:active {
    transform: scale(0.97);
  }
</style>

<!-- Bootstrap script -->
<script>
(function() {

  // Footer button visibility per step
  function _dmeFooterHook(n) {
    const ids = {
      'udme-back-btn':    { type:'visibility', show: n > 0 },
      'udme-btn-ws':      { type:'display',    show: n === 1 },
      'udme-btn-map':     { type:'display',    show: n === 2 },
      'udme-btn-studio':  { type:'display',    show: n === 3 },
      'udme-import-btn':  { type:'display',    show: n === 4 },
    };
    Object.entries(ids).forEach(([id, cfg]) => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (!el) return;
      if (cfg.type === 'visibility') {
        el.style.visibility = cfg.show ? 'visible' : 'hidden';
      } else {
        el.style.display = cfg.show ? 'inline-flex' : 'none';
      }
    });
  }

  window._dmeFooterHook = _dmeFooterHook;
  _dmeFooterHook(0);

  // Drop zone init after load
  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    if (typeof initDMEDropZone === 'function') initDMEDropZone();
  });

  // Escape key closes
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function(e) {
    if (e.key === 'Escape') {
      const m = document.getElementById('modal-udme');
      if (m && m.classList.contains('open')) {
        if (typeof closeDMEModal === 'function') closeDMEModal();
      }
    }
  });

  // Legacy alias
  window.addEventListener('load', function() {
    if (typeof window.openDataMigrationEngine === 'function') {
      window.openExcelImportModal = window.openDataMigrationEngine;
    }
  });

})();
</script>








<script type="module">



import { initializeApp } from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.0/firebase-app.js";


import {
getAuth,
createUserWithEmailAndPassword,
signInWithEmailAndPassword,
sendPasswordResetEmail,
onAuthStateChanged,
signOut
} from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.0/firebase-auth.js";

import {
getFirestore,
initializeFirestore,
persistentLocalCache,
persistentMultipleTabManager,
doc,
setDoc,
getDoc,
getDocs,
collection,
addDoc,
updateDoc,
deleteDoc,
query,
where,
onSnapshot,
serverTimestamp
} from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.0/firebase-firestore.js";

import {
getStorage,
ref,
uploadBytes,
getDownloadURL,
deleteObject
} from "https://www.gstatic.com/firebasejs/11.0.0/firebase-storage.js";




    const firebaseConfig = {
      apiKey: "AIzaSyC3Uco6S1xyoxdgjbNtpAe_ofUtG0nI0XI",
      authDomain: "grok-7568a.firebaseapp.com",
      projectId: "grok-7568a",
      storageBucket: "grok-7568a.appspot.com",
      messagingSenderId: "802272835730",
      appId: "1:802272835730:web:f80f3c986cebbfcd51ce4b"
    };

    const app = initializeApp(firebaseConfig);
    const auth = getAuth(app);

    // 🛠️ CRITICAL FIX: enable persistent offline cache.
    // Without this, getFirestore(app) only has an in-memory cache that is
    // wiped on every page reload. That meant onSnapshot listeners
    // (including the school subscription listener) had NOTHING to read
    // while offline — they'd just hang waiting for a network that isn't
    // there, and the subscription listener's own safe-default (read-only)
    // stayed in effect the whole time. This is why an active-subscription
    // school still got blocked from writes as soon as the device went
    // offline for more than a few minutes.
    // persistentLocalCache stores Firestore data in IndexedDB so
    // onSnapshot/getDoc can serve the LAST SYNCED version of any
    // document (including schools/{schoolId}) immediately, even with
    // zero connectivity — as long as it was fetched at least once while
    // online (which happens on every normal login).
    let _fdbInstance;
    try {
      _fdbInstance = initializeFirestore(app, {
        localCache: persistentLocalCache({ tabManager: persistentMultipleTabManager() })
      });
      console.log('[Firestore] ✅ Offline persistence enabled (multi-tab).');
    } catch (persistErr) {
      console.warn('[Firestore] Persistent cache unavailable, falling back to in-memory cache:', persistErr.message);
      _fdbInstance = getFirestore(app);
    }
    window.fdb = _fdbInstance;
    const storage = getStorage(app);
    
// -------------------------
// 🔹 Retry wrapper for Firebase calls
// -------------------------
async function retryFirebaseCall(fn, options = {}) {
  const { retries = 3, delay = 500, factor = 2, onRetry = null } = options;
  let attempt = 0;
  let currentDelay = delay;

  while (attempt <= retries) {
    try {
      return await fn();
    } catch (err) {
      attempt++;
      if (attempt > retries) throw err;
      if (typeof onRetry === 'function') onRetry(err, attempt);
      console.warn(`Retry ${attempt}/${retries} after ${currentDelay}ms due to error:`, err.message || err);
      await new Promise(res => setTimeout(res, currentDelay));
      currentDelay *= factor;
    }
  }
}

let schoolId = null;

const ALL_COLLECTIONS = [
      'classes', 'students', 'attendance', 'results', 'fees', 'feesConfig', 'messages', 'teachers'
    ];



    // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //  UTILITIES
    // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function sanitizeKey(key) {
      if (typeof key !== 'string') return '';
      return key.trim().replace(/[\/.#$\[\]\s]/g, '_');
    }

    function removeUndefined(obj) {
      if (obj === null || obj === undefined) return undefined;
      if (Array.isArray(obj)) {
        return obj.map(removeUndefined).filter(v => v !== undefined);
      }
      if (typeof obj === 'object') {
        const clean = {};
        for (const k in obj) {
          const val = removeUndefined(obj[k]);
          if (val !== undefined) clean[k] = val;
        }
        return clean;
      }
      return obj;
    }

    function escapeHTML(str) {
      if (str == null) return '';
      return String(str)
        .replace(/&/g, '&amp;')
        .replace(/</g, '&lt;')
        .replace(/>/g, '&gt;')
        .replace(/"/g, '&quot;')
        .replace(/'/g, '&#39;');
    }

    window.db = db;
    window.auth = auth;
    window.doc = doc;
    window.onSnapshot = onSnapshot;
    window.getDoc = getDoc;


              // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  TEACHMATE 3.0 — ENTERPRISE SYNC & INDEXEDDB ENGINE (Production v7)
//
//  CHANGES IN v7:
//
//  ★ ARCHITECTURE CHANGE — Shared class data routing
//    attendanceHistory, attendanceSummary, results, fees moved from
//    teacher-private path to school-level shared path for ALL teachers.
//    Previously only headteacher/admin could write to school-level
//    collections. Now ANY teacher writes their class data to the shared
//    path, protected by Firestore rules (canWriteSharedDoc checks that
//    the doc's classId is in the teacher's assignedClasses array).
//
//    OLD routing for a teacher writing attendance:
//      schools/{schoolId}/teachers/{teacherId}/attendanceHistory/{docId}
//
//    NEW routing for a teacher writing attendance:
//      schools/{schoolId}/attendanceHistory/{docId}
//
//    This means Teacher A and Teacher B both assigned to grade6_orange
//    write to and read from the same Firestore collection, filtered by
//    classId. Both see all class data instantly via live listeners.
//
//  ★ Routing table split into three tiers:
//    1. ADMIN_ONLY_COLLECTIONS — classes, students: only headteacher/admin
//       may write (unchanged from v6)
//    2. SHARED_CLASS_COLLECTIONS — attendanceHistory, attendanceSummary,
//       results, fees: ALL teachers write to school-level path; Firestore
//       rules enforce classId-based access control per document
//    3. PRIVATE_COLLECTIONS — everything else routes to teacher subcollection
//       (lesson plans, drafts, etc added in future)
//
//  All v6 fixes preserved: batch processing, jitter backoff, teacherId
//  stamping, concurrency lock, toxic payload eviction, auto-recovery.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

let isSyncing = false; // 🛡️ CONCURRENCY LOCK

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  PREMIUM BANNER FAILSAFE
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _syncBanner(message, type = 'info', duration = 4000) {
  if (typeof showPremiumBanner === 'function') {
    showPremiumBanner(message, type, duration);
  } else {
    console.log(`[SyncEngine][Banner:${type}]`, message);
  }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  INDEXEDDB HELPERS
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function getIndexedDB(storeName, key) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    if (!db) return reject(new Error('IndexedDB not initialized yet'));

    let transaction;
    try {
      transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readonly');
    } catch (e) {
      console.error(`Failed to create transaction for GET [${storeName}]`, e.name, e.message, e.code);
      return reject(e);
    }
    const store   = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
    const request = key !== undefined ? store.get(key) : store.getAll();

    request.onsuccess = () => resolve(request.result ?? (key !== undefined ? null : []));
    request.onerror   = () => {
      console.error(
        `IndexedDB GET error [${storeName}]`,
        request.error?.name, request.error?.message, request.error?.code
      );
      reject(request.error);
    };
  });
}

function getAllIndexedDB(storeName) {
  return getIndexedDB(storeName);
}

async function setIndexedDB(storeName, key, value) {
  if (!db) throw new Error('IndexedDB not initialized');

  const transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
  const store       = transaction.objectStore(storeName);

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    let request;
    if (store.keyPath) {
      let data = { ...value };
      if (!(store.keyPath in data)) {
        if (key !== undefined) data[store.keyPath] = key;
        else data[store.keyPath] = String(Date.now());
      }
      request = store.put(data);
    } else {
      if (store.autoIncrement) {
        request = store.put(value);
      } else {
        if (key === undefined) {
          reject(new Error('Key must be provided for out-of-line store without auto-increment'));
          return;
        }
        request = store.put(value, key);
      }
    }
    request.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    request.onerror   = () => reject(request.error);
  });
}

function deleteIndexedDB(storeName, key) {
  if (!db) throw new Error('IndexedDB not initialized');

  let transaction;
  try {
    transaction = db.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error(`Failed to create transaction for DELETE [${storeName}]`, e.name, e.message, e.code);
    throw e;
  }
  const store = transaction.objectStore(storeName);
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const req = store.delete(key);
    req.onsuccess = () => resolve();
    req.onerror   = () => {
      console.error(
        `IndexedDB DELETE error [${storeName}]`,
        req.error?.name, req.error?.message, req.error?.code
      );
      reject(req.error);
    };
  });
}


// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  INITIALIZE INDEXEDDB
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function initIndexedDB() {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const request = indexedDB.open('TeachMateDB', 134);

    request.onupgradeneeded = (event) => {
      db = event.target.result;



const defaultStores = [
  'classes', 'students', 'attendance', 'results', 'fees',
  'feesConfig', 'messages', 'teachers', 'userProfile', 'syncQueue',
  'attendanceDrafts', 'lessonplans',
  'teacherComments', 'lp_v3_plans',
  'smsSettings', 'smsLog', 'smsCredits','guardian phone','headteacherComments'
];
      

      defaultStores.forEach(storeName => {
        if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains(storeName)) {
          db.createObjectStore(storeName, { keyPath: 'id' });
        }
      });

      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('attendanceHistory')) {
        db.createObjectStore('attendanceHistory', { keyPath: 'recordId' });
      }
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('attendanceSummary')) {
        db.createObjectStore('attendanceSummary', { keyPath: 'studentId' });
      }
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('feesHistory')) {
        db.createObjectStore('feesHistory', { keyPath: 'recordId' });
      }
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains('pendingProfiles')) {
        db.createObjectStore('pendingProfiles', { keyPath: 'uid' });
      }

      console.log('Database upgraded/created with all stores.');
    };

    request.onsuccess = (event) => {
      db = event.target.result;
      console.log('IndexedDB opened – version', db.version);

      const requiredStores = [
        'classes', 'students', 'attendance', 'results', 'fees',
        'feesConfig', 'messages', 'teachers', 'userProfile', 'syncQueue',
        'attendanceHistory', 'attendanceSummary', 'feesHistory',
        'pendingProfiles', 'attendanceDrafts'
      ];

      const missing = requiredStores.filter(name => !db.objectStoreNames.contains(name));

      if (missing.length > 0) {
        console.warn('Missing stores:', missing, '— Deleting DB and retrying...');
        db.close();

        const deleteReq = indexedDB.deleteDatabase('TeachMateDB');
        deleteReq.onsuccess = () => {
          console.log('Old DB deleted successfully.');
          initIndexedDB().then(resolve).catch(reject);
        };
        deleteReq.onerror   = err => reject(err);
        deleteReq.onblocked = () => {
          alert('Database upgrade blocked — close other TeachMate tabs.');
          reject(new Error('DB blocked'));
        };
      } else {
        console.log('All stores present — IndexedDB READY ✔️');
        resolve();
      }
    };

    request.onerror   = event => reject(event.target.error);
    request.onblocked = () => reject(new Error('DB upgrade blocked'));
  });
}

window.dbReady = initIndexedDB();
window.dbReady.then(() => { console.log('DB READY'); });


// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  CLEAN EXISTING SYNC QUEUE
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function cleanSyncQueue() {
  let queue = await getIndexedDB('syncQueue').catch(() => []);
  if (!Array.isArray(queue)) queue = [];

  for (const entry of queue) {
    if (!entry || !entry.data) continue;
    const payload = typeof sanitizeForFirestore === 'function'
      ? sanitizeForFirestore(entry.data)
      : entry.data;
    if (!payload || Object.keys(payload).length === 0) {
      console.warn('[SyncEngine] Deleting empty syncQueue entry:', entry.id);
      await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id).catch(() => {});
    }
  }
}
cleanSyncQueue();


// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYNC QUEUE HELPERS
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function addToSyncQueue(path, action, data) {
  if (!db) return console.warn('[SyncEngine] DB not ready — cannot queue');
  try {
    const store     = db.transaction(['syncQueue'], 'readwrite').objectStore('syncQueue');
    const id        = Date.now() + Math.random();
    const safeData  = typeof removeUndefined === 'function' ? removeUndefined(data) : data;
    const teacherId = auth?.currentUser?.uid || null;

    store.add({
      id,
      path:      String(path).trim(),
      action,
      data:      safeData,
      teacherId,
      timestamp: Date.now(),
      retries:   0
    });
    updateSyncStatus('Pending sync', '#ff4e50');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('[SyncEngine] addToSyncQueue failed', e.name, e.message);
  }
}

async function hasPendingSync() {
  const q = await getIndexedDB('syncQueue').catch(() => []);
  return Array.isArray(q) && q.length > 0;
}

function updateSyncStatus(msg, color = '') {
  const el = document.getElementById('sync-status');
  if (el) {
    el.textContent           = msg;
    el.style.backgroundColor = color;
  }
}


// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  CORE SYNC LOGIC
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function syncData() {
  if (!navigator.onLine) return;
  if (isSyncing) {
    console.log('[SyncEngine] Sync already in progress, skipping.');
    return;
  }

  isSyncing = true;
  updateSyncStatus('Syncing…', '#f9d423');

  let syncedCount  = 0;
  let failedCount  = 0;
  let skippedCount = 0;

  try {
    const user = auth.currentUser;
    if (!user) {
      updateSyncStatus('Not signed in', '#ffd166');
      return;
    }

    const profile = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', user.uid).catch(() => null);
    if (!profile?.schoolId) {
      updateSyncStatus('No school ID', '#ffd166');
      return;
    }

    const safeSanitize = val => typeof sanitizeKey === 'function' ? sanitizeKey(val) : val;
    const schoolId     = safeSanitize(String(profile.schoolId));
    const teacherId    = user.uid;
    const role         = String(profile.role || '').toLowerCase();
    const isPrivileged = role === 'headteacher' || role === 'admin';

    let queue = await getIndexedDB('syncQueue').catch(() => []);
    if (!Array.isArray(queue) || queue.length === 0) {
      updateSyncStatus('All Data Synced ✓', '#00cc88');
      return;
    }

    queue.sort((a, b) => a.timestamp - b.timestamp);

    const totalItems = queue.length;
    console.log(`[SyncEngine] Processing ${totalItems} queued item(s) for teacher=${teacherId}`);

    _syncBanner(
      `🔄 Syncing ${totalItems} pending item${totalItems > 1 ? 's' : ''}…`,
      'info', 3000
    );

    // ── v7 ROUTING TABLE ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    //
    // TIER 1 — Admin-only school-level collections.
    // Only headteacher/admin writes here. Teachers never write these.
    //
    // 🛠️ ARCHITECTURE CHANGE: "fees" and "feesConfig" moved back here.
    // Fees entry (payments + total amount) is now handled exclusively by
    // the Admin Dashboard / Headteacher Portal. This teacher app is
    // READ-ONLY for fees — it still restores/reads both collections fine
    // (isInSchool() covers reads in the rules), it just never writes them.
    // The UI's save/edit/delete fee controls have all been removed;
    // this classification is a defense-in-depth backstop so even a direct
    // call would be dropped client-side before ever reaching Firestore
    // (which would reject it anyway per the updated security rules).
    const ADMIN_ONLY_COLLECTIONS = new Set(['classes', 'students', 'fees', 'feesConfig']);

    // TIER 2 — Shared class data collections.
    // ALL teachers write to the school-level path.
    // Firestore rules enforce classId-based access (canWriteSharedDoc).
    // This is the v7 change — previously these were teacher-private.
    const SHARED_CLASS_COLLECTIONS = new Set([
      'attendanceHistory',
      'attendanceSummary',
      'results',
    ]);

    // TIER 3 — Everything else → teacher's own subcollection.
    // Lesson plans, drafts, comments, etc.
    // (No explicit set needed — it's the default fallback below.)

    const BATCH_SIZE  = 10;
    const BATCH_YIELD = 50;

    for (let batchStart = 0; batchStart < queue.length; batchStart += BATCH_SIZE) {
      const batch = queue.slice(batchStart, batchStart + BATCH_SIZE);

      for (const entry of batch) {
        if (!entry || !entry.path || !entry.action || !entry.id) {
          await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry?.id).catch(() => {});
          skippedCount++;
          continue;
        }

        // Skip entries queued by a different teacher on this device
        if (entry.teacherId && entry.teacherId !== teacherId) {
          skippedCount++;
          continue;
        }

        const rawParts  = entry.path.split('/').map(p => p.trim()).filter(p => p !== '');
        const pathParts = rawParts.map(p => safeSanitize(p));

        // Prevent structural injection
        if (pathParts.includes('schools') || pathParts.includes('teachers')) {
          await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
          skippedCount++;
          continue;
        }

        const collection = pathParts[0];
        let fullParts;

        if (ADMIN_ONLY_COLLECTIONS.has(collection)) {
          // TIER 1: Only privileged users write here
          if (isPrivileged) {
            fullParts = ['schools', schoolId, ...pathParts];
          } else {
            // Teacher trying to write admin-only data — skip it
            _warn(`[SyncEngine] Teacher attempted write to admin-only collection "${collection}". Dropping.`);
            await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
            skippedCount++;
            continue;
          }

        } else if (SHARED_CLASS_COLLECTIONS.has(collection)) {
          // TIER 2: ALL teachers write to school-level shared path.
          // Firestore rules enforce classId-based access per document.
          fullParts = ['schools', schoolId, ...pathParts];

        } else {
          // TIER 3: Truly private data → teacher's own subcollection
          fullParts = ['schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId, ...pathParts];
        }

        if (fullParts.length % 2 !== 0) {
          console.warn('[SyncEngine] Invalid path length. Dropping.', fullParts);
          await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
          skippedCount++;
          continue;
        }

        let docRef;
        try {
          docRef = doc(fdb, ...fullParts);
        } catch (e) {
          console.error('[SyncEngine] Invalid reference build.', e);
          await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
          skippedCount++;
          continue;
        }

        // Strip base64 student photos to save bandwidth
        if (pathParts[0] === 'students' && entry.data?.photo?.startsWith('data:')) {
          entry.data.photo = '';
        }

        try {
          if (entry.action === 'set') {
            const payloadData = {
              ...(entry.data || {}),
              schoolId,
              teacherId,
              owner:    teacherId,
              syncedAt: Date.now()
            };
            const finalPayload = typeof removeUndefined === 'function'
              ? removeUndefined(payloadData) : payloadData;
            await setDoc(docRef, finalPayload, { merge: true });

          } else if (entry.action === 'delete') {
            await deleteDoc(docRef);
          }

          await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
          syncedCount++;

        } catch (err) {
          console.error('[SyncEngine] Sync failed for item:', entry.id, err.code, err.message);

          entry.retries = (entry.retries || 0) + 1;

          if (
            entry.retries >= 5 ||
            err.code === 'invalid-argument' ||
            err.code === 'permission-denied'
          ) {
            console.warn('[SyncEngine] Evicting toxic payload:', entry.id);
            await deleteIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id);
            failedCount++;
          } else {
            await setIndexedDB('syncQueue', entry.id, entry);
          }
        }
      }

      // Yield between batches — browser breathing room + Firestore rate relief
      if (batchStart + BATCH_SIZE < queue.length) {
        await new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, BATCH_YIELD));
      }
    }

    console.log(
      `[SyncEngine] Done — synced:${syncedCount} failed:${failedCount} ` +
      `skipped:${skippedCount} of ${totalItems} total`
    );

    // Pull latest truth from server via Restore Engine.
    // Wrapped in 30s timeout so a slow restore never blocks the finally block.
    if (typeof safeRestore_VB === 'function') {
      await Promise.race([
        safeRestore_VB(schoolId),
        new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, 30000))
      ]);
    }

    updateSyncStatus('All Data Synced ✓', '#00cc88');

    if (failedCount > 0) {
      _syncBanner(
        `⚠️ ${failedCount} item${failedCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} rejected by the server and removed. ` +
        `Please re-enter that data.`,
        'warning', 6000
      );
    } else if (syncedCount > 0) {
      _syncBanner(
        `✅ ${syncedCount} item${syncedCount > 1 ? 's' : ''} synced successfully.`,
        'success', 3000
      );
    }

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('[SyncEngine] Critical Queue Error:', error);
    updateSyncStatus('Partial Sync — check console', '#ffcc00');
    _syncBanner('⚠️ Sync encountered an error. Some data may not be up to date.', 'warning', 5000);
  } finally {
    isSyncing = false;
  }
}


// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  SYNC TRIGGERS & AUTO-RECOVERY
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function triggerSync() {
  if (navigator.onLine) {
    await syncData();
  }
}

window.addEventListener('online', () => {
  console.log('[SyncEngine] Network restored. Triggering auto-recovery sync...');
  _syncBanner('🌐 Back online — syncing your data…', 'info', 2500);
  triggerSync();
});

window.addEventListener('offline', () => {
  updateSyncStatus('Offline — changes saved locally', '#94a3b8');
  _syncBanner(
    '📴 You\'re offline. Changes are saved locally and will sync automatically.',
    'warning', 3500
  );
});

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PREMIUM BANNER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function showPremiumBanner(message, type = 'info', duration = 5000) {
  document.getElementById('tm-premium-banner')?.remove();

  const styles = {
    success:     { bg:'linear-gradient(135deg,#10b981,#059669)', icon:'✅', border:'#34d399' },
    error:       { bg:'linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#dc2626)', icon:'❌', border:'#f87171' },
    warning:     { bg:'linear-gradient(135deg,#f59e0b,#d97706)', icon:'⚠️', border:'#fbbf24' },
    info:        { bg:'linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#2563eb)', icon:'ℹ️', border:'#60a5fa' },
    celebration: { bg:'linear-gradient(135deg,#8b5cf6,#7c3aed)', icon:'🎉', border:'#a78bfa' },
  };

  const actualType = (type === 'success' &&
    (message.includes('Welcome') || message.includes('back') || message.includes('ready')))
    ? 'celebration' : type;

  const s = styles[actualType] || styles.info;

  const banner = document.createElement('div');
  banner.id = 'tm-premium-banner';
  banner.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;top:20px;right:20px;z-index:99999;
    padding:14px 20px;background:${s.bg};color:white;
    font-size:14px;font-weight:600;border-radius:12px;
    border-left:4px solid ${s.border};
    box-shadow:0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,0.2);
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;
    animation:tmSlideInRight 0.3s ease;
    font-family:system-ui,-apple-system,sans-serif;
    max-width:400px;cursor:pointer;`;

  banner.innerHTML = `
    <span style="font-size:20px;">${s.icon}</span>
    <span style="flex:1;">${message}</span>
    <button onclick="this.parentElement.remove()"
      style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);border:none;border-radius:20px;
      width:24px;height:24px;cursor:pointer;color:white;font-size:14px;
      display:inline-flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">✕</button>`;

  if (!document.getElementById('tm-banner-anim')) {
    const st = document.createElement('style');
    st.id = 'tm-banner-anim';
    st.textContent = `
      @keyframes tmSlideInRight  { from{transform:translateX(120%);opacity:0} to{transform:translateX(0);opacity:1} }
      @keyframes tmSlideOutRight { from{transform:translateX(0);opacity:1}    to{transform:translateX(120%);opacity:0} }`;
    document.head.appendChild(st);
  }

  document.body.appendChild(banner);

  if (type !== 'error') {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (banner?.parentElement) {
        banner.style.animation = 'tmSlideOutRight 0.3s ease';
        setTimeout(() => banner.remove(), 300);
      }
    }, duration);
  }
}
window.showPremiumBanner = showPremiumBanner;


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  LOGGING / TIMING / LISTENER HELPERS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _log(...args)  { console.log('[RestoreEngine]', ...args); }
function _warn(...args) { console.warn('[RestoreEngine]', ...args); }
function _err(...args)  { console.error('[RestoreEngine]', ...args); }

function _sleep(ms) {
  return new Promise(resolve => setTimeout(resolve, ms));
}

function _backoffMs(attempt) {
  const base   = Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, attempt), 10000);
  const jitter = Math.random() * 1000;
  return Math.round(base + jitter);
}

function _withTimeout(promise, ms = 8000, label = '') {
  return Promise.race([
    promise,
    new Promise((_, reject) =>
      setTimeout(
        () => reject(new Error(`Timeout after ${ms}ms${label ? ': ' + label : ''}`)),
        ms
      )
    )
  ]);
}

const MAX_COLLECTION_RETRIES = 3;

let _activeListeners       = [];
let _restoreGuardTimer     = null;
let _listenerDebounceTimer = null;
let _postRestoreCooldown   = false;

function _stopListeners() {
  _activeListeners.forEach(unsub => { try { unsub(); } catch (_) {} });
  _activeListeners = [];
  _log('All live listeners stopped.');
}
