// ── Safe HTML escape ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _escCT(s) {
  return String(s || '').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ── Resolve teacher REAL display name ────────────────────────────────────
//  Priority: Firestore teachers subcollection → docData fields → allTeachers
//  array → short-ID fallback. Results are cached in-memory.
async function _resolveTeacherName(teacherId, docData, schoolId) {
  if (!teacherId) return 'A colleague';

  // 1. In-memory cache hit
  if (_teacherNameCache[teacherId]) return _teacherNameCache[teacherId];

  // 2. Check fields already on the doc
  const inDoc = docData?.teacherName || docData?.ownerName || docData?.displayName || docData?.adminName || docData?.enteredByName;
  if (inDoc && !inDoc.includes('uid') && inDoc.length > 2) {
    _teacherNameCache[teacherId] = inDoc;
    return inDoc;
  }

  // 3. Fetch from Firestore teachers subcollection (real source of truth
  //    for teacher-authored writes)
  if (schoolId) {
    try {
      const snap = await _withTimeout(
        getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId)),
        5000, `teacherName/${teacherId}`
      );
      if (snap.exists()) {
        const d    = snap.data();
        const name = d.name || d.displayName || d.fullName || d.firstName;
        if (name) {
          _teacherNameCache[teacherId] = name;
          return name;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _warn('Teacher name lookup failed (non-fatal):', e.message);
    }

    // 🛠️ FIX: fees entry moved to the Admin Dashboard, so a writer may
    // now be an admin, not a teacher — they won't have a doc in the
    // teachers subcollection at all. Check the admins subcollection too.
    try {
      const adminSnap = await _withTimeout(
        getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'admins', teacherId)),
        5000, `adminName/${teacherId}`
      );
      if (adminSnap.exists()) {
        const d    = adminSnap.data();
        const name = d.name || d.displayName || d.fullName || d.firstName;
        if (name) {
          _teacherNameCache[teacherId] = name;
          return name;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _warn('Admin name lookup failed (non-fatal):', e.message);
    }

    // 🛠️ FIX: also check the top-level users collection, in case profile
    // records live there instead of/in addition to per-role subcollections.
    try {
      const userSnap = await _withTimeout(
        getDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', teacherId)),
        5000, `userName/${teacherId}`
      );
      if (userSnap.exists()) {
        const d    = userSnap.data();
        const name = d.name || d.displayName || d.fullName || d.firstName;
        if (name) {
          _teacherNameCache[teacherId] = name;
          return name;
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _warn('User name lookup failed (non-fatal):', e.message);
    }
  }

  // 4. Short-ID fallback. "Staff" rather than "Teacher" since the writer
  //    could now be an admin (fees) as well as a teacher.
  const fallback = 'Staff #' + String(teacherId).slice(-4).toUpperCase();
  _teacherNameCache[teacherId] = fallback;
  return fallback;
}

// ── Build context hint for the story sentence ─────────────────────────────
function _buildContextHint(collName, docData) {
  if (collName === 'attendanceHistory' || collName === 'attendanceSummary') {
    const dateStr = docData?.date || '';
    return dateStr ? `attendance for <strong>${_escCT(dateStr)}</strong>` : null;
  }
  if (collName === 'results') {
    const subject = docData?.subject || '';
    const test    = docData?.testName || '';
    return subject
      ? `<strong>${_escCT(subject)}</strong>${test ? ' — ' + _escCT(test) : ''}`
      : null;
  }
  if (collName === 'students') {
    const studentName = docData?.name || '';
    return studentName ? `student <strong>${_escCT(studentName)}</strong>` : null;
  }
  return null;
}

// ── Render the cinematic banner ───────────────────────────────────────────
function _showCrossTeacherBanner({ collName, classId, className, writerName, docData, timestamp }) {
  const meta = _collectionMeta[collName] || {
    icon: '🔄', label: collName, color: '#6366f1', accent: '#818cf8',
    glow: 'rgba(99,102,241,0.4)',
    story: (name) => `<strong>${name}</strong> synced data`,
    pill: '🔄 Data synced',
  };

  // Avatar initials
  const initials = writerName
    .replace(/(Teacher|Staff) #\w+/, '?')
    .split(' ').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2)
    .map(w => w[0]?.toUpperCase() || '').join('') || '?';

  const timeStr  = new Date(timestamp).toLocaleTimeString([], { hour: '2-digit', minute: '2-digit' });
  const ctx      = _buildContextHint(collName, docData);
  const storyHtml = meta.story(_escCT(writerName), ctx);

  const bannerId = `tm-ct-banner-${collName}-${classId}`;
  document.getElementById(bannerId)?.remove();

  const banner = document.createElement('div');
  banner.className = 'tm-ct-banner';
  banner.id        = bannerId;

  // Conic gradient ring for avatar
  const ringGradient = `conic-gradient(${meta.accent}, ${meta.color}, transparent, ${meta.accent})`;
  // Avatar background
  const avatarBg = `linear-gradient(135deg, ${meta.color}cc, ${meta.accent}88)`;

  banner.innerHTML = `
    <div class="tm-ct-inner">
      <!-- Glow orb -->
      <div class="tm-ct-glow-orb" style="background:${meta.glow};"></div>
      <!-- Shimmer -->
      <div class="tm-ct-shimmer"></div>
      <!-- Accent bar -->
      <div class="tm-ct-accent-bar"
           style="background:linear-gradient(180deg,${meta.accent},${meta.color});"></div>

      <!-- Header -->
      <div class="tm-ct-header">
        <div class="tm-ct-header-icon">${meta.icon}</div>
        <div class="tm-ct-header-text">
          <div class="tm-ct-header-title">${_escCT(meta.label)} Updated</div>
          <div class="tm-ct-header-sub">
            Class: <strong>${_escCT(className)}</strong>
          </div>
        </div>
        <button class="tm-ct-close"
                onclick="_dismissCrossTeacherBanner('${_escCT(bannerId)}')"
                title="Dismiss">✕</button>
      </div>

      <div class="tm-ct-divider"></div>

      <!-- Body -->
      <div class="tm-ct-body">
        <div class="tm-ct-story-row">
          <!-- Avatar with spinning ring -->
          <div class="tm-ct-avatar-wrap">
            <div class="tm-ct-avatar-ring"
                 style="background:${ringGradient};"></div>
            <div class="tm-ct-avatar"
                 style="background:${avatarBg};
                        box-shadow:0 0 16px ${meta.glow};">
              ${_escCT(initials)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <!-- Story text -->
          <div class="tm-ct-story-text">${storyHtml}</div>
        </div>

        <!-- Meta: pill + live indicator -->
        <div class="tm-ct-meta">
          <div class="tm-ct-pill">${meta.pill}</div>
          <div class="tm-ct-live-row">
            <div class="tm-ct-live-dot"></div>
            <span>Live · ${_escCT(timeStr)}</span>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>`;

  // Drop-shadow uses accent colour for a glow effect
  banner.style.boxShadow = `0 8px 40px ${meta.glow}, 0 2px 8px rgba(0,0,0,0.4)`;

  document.body.appendChild(banner);
  // No auto-dismiss — teacher stays aware until they close it
}

window._dismissCrossTeacherBanner = function (bannerId) {
  const el = document.getElementById(bannerId);
  if (!el) return;
  el.style.animation = 'tmCTSlideDown 0.35s cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 1, 1) forwards';
  setTimeout(() => el.remove(), 360);
};

// ── Main entry point — called per document change inside live listener ─────
//  ★ v9.1: schoolId is now passed in so we can fetch the real teacher name.
//    Called INSIDE Promise.allSettled loop so 'd' is always in scope.
async function _notifyCrossTeacherActivity(collName, docData, schoolId, assignedClasses, docId) {
  try {
    const currentUid = auth?.currentUser?.uid;
    if (!currentUid) return;

    // Only fire for OTHER users' writes — never our own
    // 🛠️ FIX: broadened to also recognize admin-authored writes. Fees
    // entry moved to the Admin Dashboard app, which likely stamps a
    // different field than teacherId (e.g. adminId/enteredBy). Without
    // this, admin-written fees never had a writerId at all, so the
    // fees alert silently never fired.
    const writerId =
      docData?.teacherId  || docData?.owner      || docData?.syncedBy ||
      docData?.adminId    || docData?.enteredBy  || docData?.updatedBy ||
      docData?.modifiedBy || docData?.staffId    || docData?.uid;
    if (!writerId || writerId === currentUid) return;

    const classId = docData?.classId;
    if (!classId) return;

    // Only fire for classes this teacher is also assigned to
    if (!assignedClasses.includes(classId)) return;

    // 🛠️ FIX: de-dupe key now includes the SPECIFIC document id, not just
    // collection+class. The old key (`${collName}:${classId}`) treated
    // every update to a class as interchangeable, so saving Biology then
    // Math results for the same class within 10 seconds meant the Math
    // update got silently dropped and the banner stayed stuck on
    // Biology. Each distinct document now gets its own dedupe slot -
    // this still blocks true duplicate re-fires of the SAME doc (e.g.
    // Firestore metadata-only reconnect events) without ever suppressing
    // a genuinely new record.
    const effectiveDocId = docId || docData?.id || `${classId}-${JSON.stringify(docData).length}`;
    const dedupeKey = `${collName}:${classId}:${effectiveDocId}`;
    const now       = Date.now();
    if (_crossTeacherNotifTracker[dedupeKey] &&
        now - _crossTeacherNotifTracker[dedupeKey] < 10000) return;
    _crossTeacherNotifTracker[dedupeKey] = now;

    // ★ Resolve REAL name from Firestore (with cache)
    const writerName = await _resolveTeacherName(writerId, docData, schoolId);

    // Resolve class name from IndexedDB
    let className = null;
    try {
      const classDoc = await getIndexedDB('classes', classId).catch(() => null);
      className = classDoc?.name || classDoc?.className || null;
    } catch (_) {}

    _showCrossTeacherBanner({
      collName,
      classId,
      className: className || classId,
      writerName,
      docData,
      timestamp: now,
    });

  } catch (e) {
    // Non-fatal — never break the main listener
    _warn('_notifyCrossTeacherActivity error (non-fatal):', e.message);
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DATA MIGRATION ENGINE PLACEHOLDER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.openDataMigrationEngine = function() {
  console.log('[TeachMate] Data Migration Engine triggered.');
  if (typeof showPremiumBanner === 'function') {
    showPremiumBanner('Data Migration Engine opening...', 'info', 3000);
  } else {
    alert('Data Migration Engine is currently offline or under construction.');
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MODULE STATE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
let isRestoring            = false;
let restoreRetryCount      = 0;
let restoreCollectionIndex = 0;
const MAX_RESTORE_RETRIES  = 5;

let _classesReadyResolve   = null;
window.__classesReady      = new Promise(res => { _classesReadyResolve = res; });

const RESTORE_COLLECTIONS = [
  'classes',
  'students',
  'results',
  'attendanceHistory',
  'attendanceSummary',
  'fees',
  'feesConfig',
];


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PUBLIC API — safeRestore_VB(schoolId)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.safeRestore_VB = async function safeRestore_VB(schoolId, _unused, _teacherIdOverride) {

  restoreCollectionIndex = 0;

  if (isRestoring) {
    _log('Already running — skipping duplicate trigger.');
    return;
  }

  if (!schoolId) {
    console.warn('[RestoreEngine] safeRestore_VB called without schoolId — aborted.');
    return;
  }

  isRestoring = true;

  if (typeof updateSyncStatus === 'function') {
    updateSyncStatus('Restoring data…', '#3b82f6');
  }

  try {
    await _runRestore(schoolId, _teacherIdOverride);

    restoreRetryCount      = 0;
    restoreCollectionIndex = 0;
    isRestoring            = false;

    _postRestoreCooldown = true;
    setTimeout(() => { _postRestoreCooldown = false; }, 6000);

    if (typeof updateSyncStatus === 'function') {
      updateSyncStatus('Data Synced ✓', '#10b981');
    }

    if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') {
      await loadClasses().catch(() => {});
    }

    if (typeof _classesReadyResolve === 'function') {
      _classesReadyResolve();
    }

    if (typeof window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange === 'function') {
      await window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange().catch(() => {});
    }

    try {
      if (window.ClassActivityListener) {
        window.ClassActivityListener.stop();
        const user      = auth.currentUser;
        const teacherId = _teacherIdOverride || user?.uid;
        if (teacherId) {
          const tSnap = await getDoc(
            doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId)
          ).catch(() => null);
          const tData        = tSnap?.exists() ? tSnap.data() : {};
          const freshClasses = [
            ...new Set([
              ...(tData.assignedClasses || []),
              ...(tData.classes || [])
            ])
          ].map(c => String(c).trim()).filter(Boolean);

          if (freshClasses.length > 0) {
            window.ClassActivityListener.start({
              db:              fdb,
              schoolId,
              currentUser:     { uid: teacherId, name: tData.name || '' },
              assignedClasses: freshClasses,
            });
          }
        }
      }
    } catch (e) {
      _warn('ClassActivityListener setup failed (non-fatal):', e.message);
    }

    showPremiumBanner('✅ Workspace fully synced and ready.', 'success', 3000);

  } catch (err) {
    console.error('[RestoreEngine] Restore failed:', err.message);
    isRestoring            = false;
    restoreCollectionIndex = 0;

    showPremiumBanner('⚠️ Partial sync. App will use offline data.', 'warning', 4000);

    if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') {
      await loadClasses().catch(() => {});
    }
    if (typeof _classesReadyResolve === 'function') {
      _classesReadyResolve();
    }
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  INTERNAL RESTORE RUNNER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _runRestore(schoolId, teacherIdOverride) {

  const user = auth.currentUser;
  if (!user) throw new Error('No authenticated user.');

  const teacherId = teacherIdOverride || user.uid;

  _log(`Starting restore — school="${schoolId}", teacher="${teacherId}"`);

  let assignedClasses = [];

  try {
    const cached    = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId).catch(() => null);
    const cachedRaw = cached?.classes || cached?.assignedClasses || [];
    if (Array.isArray(cachedRaw) && cachedRaw.length > 0) {
      assignedClasses = cachedRaw.map(c => String(c).trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_'));
      _log('Class list from IndexedDB cache:', assignedClasses);
    }

    const teacherSnap = await _withTimeout(
      getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId)),
      8000, 'teacher doc fetch'
    );

    if (teacherSnap.exists()) {
      const tData = teacherSnap.data();
      const raw   = (Array.isArray(tData.assignedClasses) && tData.assignedClasses.length > 0)
        ? tData.assignedClasses
        : (Array.isArray(tData.classes) && tData.classes.length > 0)
          ? tData.classes : [];

      if (raw.length > 0) {
        assignedClasses = raw.map(c => String(c).trim().replace(/\s+/g, '_'));
        _log('Class list from Firestore teacher doc:', assignedClasses);

        if (cached) {
          await setIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId, {
            ...cached,
            classes:         assignedClasses,
            assignedClasses: assignedClasses,
          }).catch(() => {});
        }
      } else {
        if (cached) {
          await setIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId, {
            ...cached,
            classes:         [],
            assignedClasses: [],
          }).catch(() => {});
        }
        assignedClasses = [];
      }
    } else {
      _warn('Teacher doc not found at schools/' + schoolId + '/teachers/' + teacherId);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    _warn('Could not fetch teacher doc for class list:', e.message);
  }

  // ── No classes assigned ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (assignedClasses.length === 0) {
    _log('No assigned classes — wiping stale local data and clearing UI.');

    showPremiumBanner(
      '📭 You have no assigned classes.<br>' +
      '<span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:400;opacity:0.9">' +
      'Contact your headteacher to be assigned a class.</span>',
      'warning', 0
    );

    const collectionsToWipe = [
      'classes', 'students', 'results',
      'attendanceHistory', 'attendanceSummary', 'fees', 'feesConfig'
    ];

    await Promise.allSettled(
      collectionsToWipe.map(async coll => {
        try {
          const allDocs = await getIndexedDB(coll).catch(() => []);
          await Promise.allSettled(
            (allDocs || []).filter(d => d?.id).map(d =>
              deleteIndexedDB(coll, d.id).catch(() => {})
            )
          );
          _log(`Wiped local "${coll}".`);
        } catch (e) {
          _warn(`Could not wipe "${coll}":`, e.message);
        }
      })
    );

    if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') await loadClasses().catch(() => {});
    if (typeof _classesReadyResolve === 'function') _classesReadyResolve();
    if (typeof window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange === 'function') {
      await window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange().catch(() => {});
    }

    _stopListeners();
    _setupTeacherDocListenerOnly(schoolId, teacherId);

    if (typeof updateSyncStatus === 'function') {
      updateSyncStatus('No classes assigned', '#f59e0b');
    }
    return;
  }

  // ── Teacher HAS assigned classes — full restore ───────────────────────────
  showPremiumBanner(
    `📚 Loading ${assignedClasses.length} assigned class${assignedClasses.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''}…`,
    'info', 3000
  );

  const failedCollections = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < RESTORE_COLLECTIONS.length; i++) {
    restoreCollectionIndex = i;
    const coll = RESTORE_COLLECTIONS[i];

    try {
      await _restoreCollectionWithRetry(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses);

      if (coll === 'classes') {
        if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') await loadClasses().catch(() => {});
        if (typeof _classesReadyResolve === 'function') _classesReadyResolve();
      }

    } catch (err) {
      _warn(`Collection "${coll}" failed after all retries:`, err.message);
      failedCollections.push(coll);
    }
  }

  restoreCollectionIndex = 0;

  if (typeof refreshUI === 'function') refreshUI();

  if (failedCollections.length > 0) {
    showPremiumBanner(
      `⚠️ ${failedCollections.length} of ${RESTORE_COLLECTIONS.length} sections ` +
      `failed to sync (${failedCollections.join(', ')}). Using offline data for these.`,
      'warning', 5000
    );
  }

  _stopListeners();
  try {
    _setupLiveListeners(schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses);
  } catch (e) {
    _warn('Could not set up live listeners:', e.message);
  }

  _log('✅ Restore complete.');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  TEACHER DOC LISTENER ONLY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _setupTeacherDocListenerOnly(schoolId, teacherId) {
  _log('No classes assigned — watching teacher doc for assignment changes…');

  const unsub = onSnapshot(
    doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId),
    async (snap) => {
      if (!snap.exists()) return;
      if (isRestoring) return;
      if (_postRestoreCooldown) return;
      if (_listenerDebounceTimer) return;

      const tData  = snap.data();
      const newIds = (
        Array.isArray(tData.assignedClasses) ? tData.assignedClasses
        : Array.isArray(tData.classes)       ? tData.classes
        : []
      ).map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);

      if (newIds.length === 0) return;

      _log('Classes assigned by headteacher:', newIds, '— triggering full restore…');
      _listenerDebounceTimer = setTimeout(() => { _listenerDebounceTimer = null; }, 5000);

      const added = newIds.length;
      showPremiumBanner(
        `🎉 ${added} class${added > 1 ? 'es' : ''} assigned to you! Loading your workspace…`,
        'celebration', 4000
      );

      _stopListeners();
      await _sleep(1000);
      await window.safeRestore_VB(schoolId, null, teacherId);
    },
    (err) => _warn('Teacher doc (no-class) listener error:', err.message)
  );

  _activeListeners.push(unsub);
  _log('Teacher doc listener active — waiting for class assignment.');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  ASSIGNMENT SELF-HEAL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _resolveAndHealAssignedClasses(schoolId, teacherId) {

  let cachedIds = [];
  try {
    const cached = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId);
    const raw    = cached?.assignedClasses || cached?.classes || [];
    if (Array.isArray(raw) && raw.length > 0) {
      cachedIds = raw.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    _warn('IndexedDB userProfile read failed:', e.message);
  }

  try {
    const teacherSnap = await _withTimeout(
      getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId)),
      8000, 'self-heal teacher doc'
    );

    let freshIds = [];

    if (teacherSnap.exists()) {
      const tData = teacherSnap.data();
      const raw   = (Array.isArray(tData.assignedClasses) && tData.assignedClasses.length > 0)
        ? tData.assignedClasses
        : (Array.isArray(tData.classes) && tData.classes.length > 0)
          ? tData.classes : [];
      freshIds = raw.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);
      _log('Fresh class IDs from teacher doc:', freshIds);
    } else {
      _warn(`Teacher doc not found — trying users/${teacherId} fallback.`);
      try {
        const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', teacherId));
        if (userSnap.exists()) {
          const raw = Array.isArray(userSnap.data().assignedClasses)
            ? userSnap.data().assignedClasses : [];
          freshIds = raw.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);
        }
      } catch (fbErr) {
        _warn('users/{uid} fallback read failed:', fbErr.message);
      }
    }

    if (freshIds.length > 0) {
      let usersDocIds = [];
      try {
        const usersSnap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', teacherId));
        if (usersSnap.exists()) {
          const ua = usersSnap.data().assignedClasses;
          usersDocIds = Array.isArray(ua)
            ? ua.map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean) : [];
        }
      } catch (e) {
        _warn('Could not read users/{uid} for comparison:', e.message);
      }

      const needsHeal =
        usersDocIds.length === 0 ||
        JSON.stringify([...freshIds].sort()) !== JSON.stringify([...usersDocIds].sort());

      if (needsHeal) {
        _log('Self-heal needed — writing assignedClasses to users/{uid}…');
        try {
          await setDoc(
            doc(fdb, 'users', teacherId),
            { assignedClasses: freshIds, _assignedUpdatedAt: new Date().toISOString() },
            { merge: true }
          );
          _log('✅ Self-heal write succeeded.');

          try {
            await auth.currentUser.getIdToken(true);
            _log('✅ Auth token refreshed.');
          } catch (tokenErr) {
            _warn('Token refresh failed (non-fatal):', tokenErr.message);
          }

          _log('Waiting 1.5s for write propagation…');
          await _sleep(1500);

        } catch (healErr) {
          _err('Self-heal write FAILED:', healErr.message);
        }
      } else {
        _log('users/{uid}.assignedClasses already up to date.');
      }

      try {
        const existing = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId).catch(() => ({}));
        await setIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId, {
          ...(existing || {}),
          assignedClasses: freshIds,
          classes:         freshIds,
          _cachedAt:       Date.now(),
        });
      } catch (e) {
        _warn('Could not update userProfile cache:', e.message);
      }

      return freshIds;
    }

    if (cachedIds.length > 0) {
      _warn('Firestore returned no class IDs — using IndexedDB cache.');
      return cachedIds;
    }
    return [];

  } catch (e) {
    _err('_resolveAndHealAssignedClasses failed:', e.message);
    return cachedIds.length > 0 ? cachedIds : [];
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  RESTORE WITH RETRY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _restoreCollectionWithRetry(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses) {
  let lastErr;
  for (let attempt = 0; attempt < MAX_COLLECTION_RETRIES; attempt++) {
    try {
      await _restoreCollection(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses);
      return;
    } catch (err) {
      lastErr    = err;
      const wait = _backoffMs(attempt);
      _warn(
        `"${coll}" attempt ${attempt + 1}/${MAX_COLLECTION_RETRIES} failed: ` +
        `${err.message}. Retrying in ${wait}ms…`
      );
      await _sleep(wait);
    }
  }
  throw lastErr;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  RESTORE A SINGLE COLLECTION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _restoreCollection(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses) {

  const hasAssignments = assignedClasses.length > 0;

  // ── classes ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (coll === 'classes') {
    if (!hasAssignments) { _log('"classes" — no assigned classes, skipping.'); return; }

    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      assignedClasses.map(classId =>
        _withTimeout(
          getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'classes', classId)),
          8000, `classes/${classId}`
        )
      )
    );

    const serverIds = [];
    await Promise.allSettled(
      results.map(async (result, idx) => {
        if (result.status === 'rejected') {
          _warn(`Failed to fetch class "${assignedClasses[idx]}":`, result.reason?.message);
          return;
        }
        const snap = result.value;
        if (!snap.exists()) { _warn(`Class doc not found: classes/${assignedClasses[idx]}`); return; }
        const docId = snap.id;
        serverIds.push(docId);
        await setIndexedDB('classes', docId, {
          ...snap.data(), id: docId, _restoredAt: Date.now(),
        }).catch(e => _err(`setIndexedDB classes/${docId}:`, e.message));
      })
    );

    if (serverIds.length > 0) await _pruneLocal('classes', serverIds);
    _log(`✔ "classes" — ${serverIds.length} doc(s) restored.`);
    return;
  }

  // ── feesConfig dual-path ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (coll === 'feesConfig') {
    if (!hasAssignments) { _log('"feesConfig" — no assigned classes, skipping.'); return; }

    const results = await Promise.allSettled(
      assignedClasses.map(async classId => {
        const schoolPath  = doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'feesConfig', classId);
        const teacherPath = doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId, 'feesConfig', classId);

        const [schoolSnap, teacherSnap] = await Promise.allSettled([
          _withTimeout(getDoc(schoolPath),  8000, `feesConfig(school)/${classId}`),
          _withTimeout(getDoc(teacherPath), 8000, `feesConfig(teacher)/${classId}`),
        ]);

        const schoolResult  = schoolSnap.status  === 'fulfilled' ? schoolSnap.value  : null;
        const teacherResult = teacherSnap.status === 'fulfilled' ? teacherSnap.value : null;

        if (schoolResult?.exists())  { _log(`feesConfig/${classId} — school-level.`);  return { classId, data: schoolResult.data(),  docId: schoolResult.id  }; }
        if (teacherResult?.exists()) { _log(`feesConfig/${classId} — teacher-level.`); return { classId, data: teacherResult.data(), docId: teacherResult.id }; }
        _log(`feesConfig/${classId} — not found.`);
        return null;
      })
    );

    const serverIds = [];
    await Promise.allSettled(
      results.map(async result => {
        if (result.status === 'rejected' || !result.value) return;
        const { docId, data } = result.value;
        serverIds.push(docId);
        await setIndexedDB('feesConfig', docId, {
          ...data, id: docId, _restoredAt: Date.now(),
        }).catch(e => _err(`setIndexedDB feesConfig/${docId}:`, e.message));
      })
    );

    if (serverIds.length > 0) await _pruneLocal('feesConfig', serverIds);
    _log(`✔ "feesConfig" — ${serverIds.length} doc(s) restored.`);
    return;
  }

  // ── School-level shared collections ──────────────────────────────────────
  let firestoreQuery;

  if (SCHOOL_LEVEL_QUERY_COLLECTIONS.has(coll) && hasAssignments) {
    if (assignedClasses.length <= 30) {
      firestoreQuery = query(
        collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, coll),
        where('classId', 'in', assignedClasses)
      );
    } else {
      await _restoreCollectionInChunks(coll, schoolId, assignedClasses);
      return;
    }
  } else if (TEACHER_PRIVATE_COLLECTIONS.has(coll)) {
    const base = collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId, coll);
    if (hasAssignments && assignedClasses.length <= 30) {
      firestoreQuery = query(base, where('classId', 'in', assignedClasses));
    } else if (hasAssignments) {
      await _restoreTeacherCollectionInChunks(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses);
      return;
    } else {
      firestoreQuery = base;
    }
  } else {
    firestoreQuery = collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, coll);
  }

  let snapshot;
  try {
    snapshot = await _withTimeout(getDocs(firestoreQuery), 12000, `getDocs(${coll})`);
  } catch (e) {
    throw new Error(`getDocs failed for "${coll}": ${e.message}`);
  }

  if (snapshot.empty) {
    _log(`"${coll}" — server returned 0 docs. Skipping prune to protect offline cache.`);
    return;
  }

  const serverIds = [];
  await Promise.allSettled(
    snapshot.docs.map(async d => {
      const docId      = d.id;
      const serverData = d.data() || {};
      serverIds.push(docId);

      let localData = null;
      try { localData = await getIndexedDB(coll, docId); } catch (e) {
        _warn(`getIndexedDB failed for ${coll}/${docId}:`, e.message);
      }

      const dataToSave = { ...serverData, id: docId, _restoredAt: Date.now() };
      const photoField = PHOTO_FIELDS[coll];
      if (photoField && localData?.[photoField]) dataToSave[photoField] = localData[photoField];

      await setIndexedDB(coll, docId, dataToSave)
        .catch(e => _err(`setIndexedDB failed for ${coll}/${docId}:`, e.message));
    })
  );

  if (serverIds.length > 0) await _pruneLocal(coll, serverIds);
  _log(`✔ "${coll}" — ${serverIds.length} doc(s) restored.`);
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CHUNK HANDLERS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _restoreCollectionInChunks(coll, schoolId, assignedClasses) {
  const CHUNK = 30;
  const allServerIds = [];

  for (let i = 0; i < assignedClasses.length; i += CHUNK) {
    const chunk = assignedClasses.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
    let snapshot;
    try {
      snapshot = await _withTimeout(
        getDocs(query(collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, coll), where('classId', 'in', chunk))),
        12000, `getDocs chunk(${coll})`
      );
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error(`getDocs chunk failed for "${coll}": ${e.message}`);
    }

    await Promise.allSettled(
      snapshot.docs.map(async d => {
        const docId = d.id;
        allServerIds.push(docId);
        let localData = null;
        try { localData = await getIndexedDB(coll, docId); } catch (_) {}
        const dataToSave = { ...d.data(), id: docId, _restoredAt: Date.now() };
        const photoField = PHOTO_FIELDS[coll];
        if (photoField && localData?.[photoField]) dataToSave[photoField] = localData[photoField];
        await setIndexedDB(coll, docId, dataToSave)
          .catch(e => _err(`setIndexedDB chunk failed for ${coll}/${docId}:`, e.message));
      })
    );
  }

  if (allServerIds.length > 0) await _pruneLocal(coll, allServerIds);
  _log(`✔ "${coll}" (chunked) — ${allServerIds.length} doc(s) restored.`);
}

async function _restoreTeacherCollectionInChunks(coll, schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses) {
  const CHUNK = 30;
  const allServerIds = [];
  const base  = collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId, coll);

  for (let i = 0; i < assignedClasses.length; i += CHUNK) {
    const chunk = assignedClasses.slice(i, i + CHUNK);
    let snapshot;
    try {
      snapshot = await _withTimeout(
        getDocs(query(base, where('classId', 'in', chunk))),
        12000, `getDocs teacher chunk(${coll})`
      );
    } catch (e) {
      throw new Error(`getDocs chunk failed for "${coll}": ${e.message}`);
    }

    await Promise.allSettled(
      snapshot.docs.map(async d => {
        const docId      = d.id;
        allServerIds.push(docId);
        const dataToSave = { ...d.data(), id: docId, _restoredAt: Date.now() };
        await setIndexedDB(coll, docId, dataToSave)
          .catch(e => _err(`setIndexedDB teacher chunk failed for ${coll}/${docId}:`, e.message));
      })
    );
  }

  if (allServerIds.length > 0) await _pruneLocal(coll, allServerIds);
  _log(`✔ "${coll}" (teacher, chunked) — ${allServerIds.length} doc(s) restored.`);
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  REAL-TIME LISTENERS
//  ★ _notifyCrossTeacherActivity is now async and called with await inside
//    Promise.allSettled — 'd' is always in scope, schoolId always passed.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _setupLiveListeners(schoolId, teacherId, assignedClasses) {
  _log('Setting up real-time listeners…');

  // ── Listener 1: Teacher doc — detect assignment changes ──────────────────
  const teacherUnsub = onSnapshot(
    doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', teacherId),
    async (snap) => {
      if (!snap.exists()) return;
      if (isRestoring) return;
      if (_postRestoreCooldown) return;
      if (_listenerDebounceTimer) return;

      const tData  = snap.data();
      const newIds = (
        Array.isArray(tData.assignedClasses) ? tData.assignedClasses
        : Array.isArray(tData.classes)       ? tData.classes
        : []
      ).map(id => String(id).trim()).filter(Boolean);

      const cached = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId).catch(() => ({}));
      const oldIds = (
        Array.isArray(cached?.assignedClasses) ? cached.assignedClasses
        : Array.isArray(cached?.classes)       ? cached.classes
        : []
      ).map(String).filter(Boolean);

      const changed =
        JSON.stringify([...newIds].sort()) !== JSON.stringify([...oldIds].sort());

      if (!changed) return;

      _log('Assignment changed — old:', oldIds, '→ new:', newIds);
      _listenerDebounceTimer = setTimeout(() => { _listenerDebounceTimer = null; }, 5000);
      _stopListeners();

      try {
        const existing = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId).catch(() => ({}));
        await setIndexedDB('userProfile', teacherId, {
          ...(existing || {}),
          assignedClasses: newIds,
          classes:         newIds,
          _cachedAt:       Date.now(),
        });
      } catch (e) {
        _warn('Could not update userProfile cache after assignment change:', e.message);
      }

      if (newIds.length === 0) {
        showPremiumBanner(
          '📭 Your class access has been removed.<br>' +
          '<span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:400;opacity:0.9">' +
          'Contact your headteacher to be assigned a class.</span>',
          'warning', 0
        );

        if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') await loadClasses().catch(() => {});
        if (typeof window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange === 'function') {
          await window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange().catch(() => {});
        }

        const collectionsToWipe = [
          'classes', 'students', 'results',
          'attendanceHistory', 'attendanceSummary', 'fees', 'feesConfig'
        ];
        await Promise.allSettled(
          collectionsToWipe.map(async coll => {
            const allDocs = await getIndexedDB(coll).catch(() => []);
            await Promise.allSettled(
              (allDocs || []).filter(d => d?.id).map(d =>
                deleteIndexedDB(coll, d.id).catch(() => {})
              )
            );
          })
        );

        _setupTeacherDocListenerOnly(schoolId, teacherId);
        return;

      } else {
        const added   = newIds.filter(id => !oldIds.includes(id));
        const removed = oldIds.filter(id => !newIds.includes(id));
        let msg = '';
        if (added.length > 0)   msg += `${added.length} class${added.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''} added. `;
        if (removed.length > 0) msg += `${removed.length} class${removed.length > 1 ? 'es' : ''} removed. `;
        showPremiumBanner(`🔄 Classes updated. ${msg}Reloading your workspace…`, 'info', 4000);
      }

      await _sleep(1000);
      await window.safeRestore_VB(schoolId, null, teacherId);
    },
    (err) => _warn('Teacher doc listener error:', err.message)
  );
  _activeListeners.push(teacherUnsub);

  // ── Listener 2: Each assigned class doc individually ─────────────────────
  for (const classId of assignedClasses) {
    const classUnsub = onSnapshot(
      doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'classes', classId),
      async (snap) => {
        if (!snap.exists()) return;
        await setIndexedDB('classes', snap.id, {
          ...snap.data(), id: snap.id, _restoredAt: Date.now()
        }).catch(() => {});
        if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') loadClasses().catch(() => {});
        if (typeof window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange === 'function') {
          window.refreshCM2AfterAssignmentChange().catch(() => {});
        }
      },
      (err) => _warn(`Class listener error (${classId}):`, err.message)
    );
    _activeListeners.push(classUnsub);
  }

  // ── Listeners 3+: Shared school-level data collections ───────────────────
  //  ★ v9.1: _notifyCrossTeacherActivity is now async; called with await
  //    inside Promise.allSettled so 'd' is always in scope and
  //    schoolId is always passed through for real name resolution.
  if (assignedClasses.length > 0 && assignedClasses.length <= 30) {

    const _makeSharedDataListener = (collName) => onSnapshot(
      query(
        collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, collName),
        where('classId', 'in', assignedClasses)
      ),
      async (snap) => {
        await Promise.allSettled(
          snap.docChanges().map(async change => {
            const d = change.doc;                          // 'd' is in scope

            if (change.type === 'removed') {
              await deleteIndexedDB(collName, d.id).catch(() => {});
            } else {
              const localData = collName === 'students'
                ? await getIndexedDB(collName, d.id).catch(() => ({}))
                : null;

              await setIndexedDB(collName, d.id, {
                ...d.data(),
                id: d.id,
                _restoredAt: Date.now(),
                ...(localData?.photo ? { photo: localData.photo } : {}),
              }).catch(() => {});

              // ★ await here — async name resolution happens before banner shows
              await _notifyCrossTeacherActivity(collName, d.data(), schoolId, assignedClasses, d.id);
            }
          })
        );

        if (snap.docChanges().length > 0 && typeof refreshUI === 'function') refreshUI();
      },
      (err) => _warn(`${collName} (shared) listener error:`, err.message)
    );

    _activeListeners.push(_makeSharedDataListener('students'));
    _activeListeners.push(_makeSharedDataListener('attendanceHistory'));
    _activeListeners.push(_makeSharedDataListener('attendanceSummary'));
    _activeListeners.push(_makeSharedDataListener('results'));
    _activeListeners.push(_makeSharedDataListener('fees'));
  }

  _log(`${_activeListeners.length} live listener(s) active.`);
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  PRUNE LOCAL DOCS NOT ON SERVER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _pruneLocal(coll, serverIds) {
  try {
    const localAll = await (
      typeof getAllIndexedDB === 'function' ? getAllIndexedDB(coll) : getIndexedDB(coll)
    ).catch(() => []);

    if (!Array.isArray(localAll)) return;

    await Promise.allSettled(
      localAll
        .filter(local => local && local.id != null && !serverIds.includes(local.id))
        .map(async local => {
          await deleteIndexedDB(coll, local.id).catch(() => {});
          _log(`Pruned stale local doc: ${coll}/${local.id}`);
        })
    );
  } catch (e) {
    _warn(`Prune failed for "${coll}":`, e.message);
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  MANUAL TRIGGER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.forceRestore_VB = async function (schoolId) {
  const sid = schoolId
    || (typeof currentSchoolId !== 'undefined' ? currentSchoolId : null)
    || auth.currentUser?.uid;

  if (!sid) {
    _warn('forceRestore_VB: no schoolId available.');
    showPremiumBanner('⚠️ Cannot sync — no school ID found.', 'warning', 3000);
    return;
  }

  isRestoring          = false;
  _postRestoreCooldown = false;
  clearTimeout(_restoreGuardTimer);
  _stopListeners();
  _log('Force restore triggered.');
  await window.safeRestore_VB(sid);
};

// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
// LOAD CLASSES (Premium Fixed Version with Fallback)
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────
window.loadClasses = async function() {
  try {
    let classes = await getIndexedDB('classes');
    
    if (!Array.isArray(classes)) {
      classes = [];
    }
    
    // 🛠️ FALLBACK: If classes table is empty, check the userProfile for the string array
    if (classes.length === 0 && typeof auth !== 'undefined' && auth.currentUser) {
      const profile = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', auth.currentUser.uid).catch(() => null);
      if (profile && Array.isArray(profile.classes)) {
        classes = profile.classes; // e.g., ["8_aform_1"]
      }
    }
    
    // 🛠️ NORMALIZE: Ensure everything is an object, converting raw strings if needed
    classes = classes.map(cls => {
      if (typeof cls === 'string') {
        return { id: cls, name: cls }; // Convert "8_aform_1" to an object
      }
      return cls;
    });
    
    // SORT CLASSES NICELY
    classes.sort((a, b) => {
      const nameA = (a.displayName || a.className || a.name || a.id || '').toLowerCase();
      const nameB = (b.displayName || b.className || b.name || b.id || '').toLowerCase();
      return nameA.localeCompare(nameB);
    });
    
    const selects = [
      'select-class',
      'attendance-class',
      'results-class',
      'fees-class',
      'fees-history-class'
    ];
    
    selects.forEach(selId => {
      const select = document.getElementById(selId);
      if (!select) return;
      
      const currentVal = select.value;
      
      select.innerHTML = `
        <option value="" disabled selected>
          -- Choose your class --
        </option>
      `;
      
      classes.forEach(cls => {
        const classId = cls.id || cls.classId || '';
        let displayName = cls.displayName || cls.className || cls.name || '';
        
        // FALLBACK CLEANUP IF ONLY ID EXISTS
        if (!displayName && classId) {
          displayName = classId
            .replace(/_/g, ' ')
            .replace(/\bgrade\b/gi, 'Grade ')
            .replace(/\s+/g, ' ')
            .trim();
        }
        
        const opt = document.createElement('option');
        opt.value = escapeHTML(classId);
        opt.textContent = escapeHTML(displayName);
        select.appendChild(opt);
      });
      
      if (currentVal) {
        select.value = currentVal;
        if (!select.value) {
          select.selectedIndex = 0;
        }
      }
    });
    
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('loadClasses failed', e.name, e.message, e.code);
  }
};

// ── Resolve teacher display name + school name for the home screen ───────
async function _resolveHomeScreenInfo() {
  let teacherName = 'Teacher';
  let schoolName = 'TeachMate';
  try {
    const uid = (typeof auth !== 'undefined' && auth.currentUser) ? auth.currentUser.uid : null;
    if (uid) {
      const profile = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', uid).catch(() => null);
      teacherName =
        (profile && (profile.name || profile.displayName || profile.fullName || profile.firstName)) ||
        auth.currentUser.displayName ||
        currentTeacher || 'Teacher';

      if (profile && profile.schoolName) {
        schoolName = profile.schoolName;
      } else if (profile && profile.schoolId) {
        try {
          const snap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', profile.schoolId));
          if (snap.exists()) {
            schoolName = snap.data().name || schoolName;
            await setIndexedDB('userProfile', uid, { ...profile, schoolName });
          }
        } catch (_) { /* offline or blocked — keep default, non-fatal */ }
      }
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[_resolveHomeScreenInfo] falling back to defaults:', e.message);
  }
  return { teacherName, schoolName };
}

    : 'Tap to view your login email';
});

// ── showDashboard (unchanged) ─────────────────────────────────────────────
window.showDashboard = function() {
  try {
    document.getElementById('auth-section').style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('welcome').textContent =
      `Welcome, ${escapeHTML(currentTeacher || '')}`;

    _tmEmailRevealed = false;
    const emailSpan = document.getElementById('tm-email-text');
    if (emailSpan) emailSpan.textContent = 'Tap to view your login email';

    _resolveHomeScreenInfo().then(({ teacherName, schoolName }) => {
      document.getElementById('welcome').textContent = `Welcome Teacher ${escapeHTML(teacherName)} to TeachMate`;
      const schoolEl = document.getElementById('tm-school-name');
      if (schoolEl) schoolEl.textContent = schoolName;
    }).catch(e => console.warn('Home screen info resolve failed:', e.message));

    startClock();
    loadClasses();
    history.pushState({ view: 'dashboard' }, '', '#dashboard');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('showDashboard error', e.name, e.message, e.code);
  }
};

// ── Full-screen feature pages ───────────────────────────────────────────────
// Each feature opens as a real full-screen page (phone AND laptop) with a Back
// button, a ✕ button, the Esc key and the phone/browser Back button as exits.
// Built to survive messy real-world use: double-taps, rapid open/close, logout
// or session expiry while a page is open, feature code that throws, dialogs
// that open on top, rotating the phone, and the browser Back/Forward buttons.
(function () {
  var SECTIONS = {
    'attendance':       '📋 Attendance Register',
    'class-management': '🏫 Class Management',
    'results':          '📊 Results Tracking',
    'fees':             '💰 Fees Tracking (ZMW)',
    'messages':         '💬 Messages'
  };
  var openId = null;      // which page is open right now
  var closing = false;    // true while a history.back() we started is still in flight
  var closeTimer = null;
  var watchdog = null;

  function wrapOf(id) { return document.getElementById('tm-fp-' + id); }

  // Create the full-screen shell once per feature and move the feature's own
  // element inside it. The shell lives directly under <body>, so no parent
  // (blur, transform, sticky...) can ever break position:fixed.
  function ensureWrap(id, el) {
    var wrap = wrapOf(id);
    if (wrap && wrap.contains(el)) return wrap;
    if (!wrap) {
      wrap = document.createElement('div');
      wrap.id = 'tm-fp-' + id;
      wrap.className = 'tm-fp';
      wrap.style.display = 'none';
      wrap.setAttribute('role', 'dialog');
      wrap.setAttribute('aria-modal', 'true');
      wrap.setAttribute('aria-label', SECTIONS[id]);

      var bar = document.createElement('div');
      bar.className = 'tm-fp-bar';
      var back = document.createElement('button');
      back.type = 'button'; back.className = 'tm-fp-back';
      back.textContent = '← Back'; back.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Back to home');
      var title = document.createElement('div');
      title.className = 'tm-fp-title'; title.textContent = SECTIONS[id];
      var close = document.createElement('button');
      close.type = 'button'; close.className = 'tm-fp-close';
      close.textContent = '✕'; close.setAttribute('aria-label', 'Close');
      back.addEventListener('click', function () { window.closeFullPage(); });
      close.addEventListener('click', function () { window.closeFullPage(); });
      bar.appendChild(back); bar.appendChild(title); bar.appendChild(close);

      var body = document.createElement('div');
      body.className = 'tm-fp-body';
      wrap.appendChild(bar); wrap.appendChild(body);
      document.body.appendChild(wrap);
    }
    var holder = wrap.querySelector('.tm-fp-body');
    el.classList.add('tm-fp-page');
    holder.appendChild(el);
    return wrap;
  }

  function stopWatch() { if (watchdog) { clearInterval(watchdog); watchdog = null; } }
  function startWatch() {
    if (watchdog) return;
    // While a page is open, make sure it can never be left stranded after a
    // logout / session expiry (cheap: only runs while a page is open).
    watchdog = setInterval(function () {
      try {
        if (!openId) { stopWatch(); return; }
        var auth = document.getElementById('auth-section');
        if (!currentTeacher || (auth && auth.style.display === 'block')) closeAll();
      } catch (e) {}
    }, 1500);
  }

  function endClosing() { closing = false; if (closeTimer) { clearTimeout(closeTimer); closeTimer = null; } }

  // Hide every feature page (no history changes). Safe to call any time.
  function closeAll() {
    var wasOpen = openId;
    Object.keys(SECTIONS).forEach(function (id) {
      var wrap = wrapOf(id);
      if (wrap) { wrap.style.display = 'none'; wrap.classList.remove('tm-fp-settling'); }
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
    });
    openId = null;
    stopWatch();
    document.documentElement.classList.remove('tm-fp-open');
    if (wasOpen) {
      var btn = document.querySelector('.tm-nav-group .nav-button[data-tm-sec="' + wasOpen + '"]');
      try { if (btn) btn.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}
    }
  }

  function run(fn) {
    try { var r = fn(); if (r && typeof r.catch === 'function') r.catch(function (e) { console.error('[feature load]', e); }); }
    catch (e) { console.error('[feature load]', e); }
  }

  window.showSection = function (section, opts) {
    try {
      var force = !!(opts && opts.force);

      if (!currentTeacher) {
        closeAll();
        document.getElementById('auth-section').style.display = 'block';
        document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'none';
        history.pushState({ view: 'auth' }, '', '#auth');
        alert('Please log in to access this section.');
        return;
      }

      // Going home (e.g. the browser Back button arrived at #dashboard)
      if (section === 'dashboard') {
        if (force) endClosing();
        closeAll();
        document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'block';
        return;
      }

      var el = document.getElementById(section);
      if (!SECTIONS[section] || !el) {
        console.error('[showSection] Element not found:', section);
        return;
      }

      // Ignore taps while a close is still finishing, and repeat taps on the page that is already open
      if (!force && closing) return;
      if (openId === section && el.style.display === 'block') return;

      var wasOpen = openId;
      closeAll();
      var wrap = ensureWrap(section, el);

      wrap.style.display = 'flex';
      el.style.display = 'block';
      document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'block';
      document.documentElement.classList.add('tm-fp-open');
      openId = section;
      startWatch();

      // Ghost-tap protection: a fast double tap on the button must not land on a button inside the new page
      wrap.classList.add('tm-fp-settling');
      setTimeout(function () { wrap.classList.remove('tm-fp-settling'); }, 350);

      var holder = wrap.querySelector('.tm-fp-body');
      if (holder) holder.scrollTop = 0;

      // Feature loaders — an error in one must never trap the user on this page
      if (section === 'attendance') run(function () { return loadAttendanceStudents(); });
      if (section === 'results') run(function () { return loadResultsStudents(); });
      if (section === 'fees') run(function () { return loadFeesStudents(); });
      if (section === 'messages') { run(function () { return loadRecipients(); }); run(function () { return loadMessages(); }); }
      if (section === 'class-management') run(function () { return _cm2InitSection(); });

      try { var back = wrap.querySelector('.tm-fp-back'); if (back) back.focus({ preventScroll: true }); } catch (e) {}

      // History: one entry per open page, so the phone's Back button closes it
      if (!force) {
        var st = history.state;
        if (wasOpen || (st && SECTIONS[st.view])) history.replaceState({ view: section }, '', '#' + section);
        else history.pushState({ view: section }, '', '#' + section);
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.error('showSection error', e.name, e.message, e.code);
      try { closeAll(); } catch (e2) {}
    }
  };

  // Close button / Back button / Esc
  window.closeFullPage = function () {
    if (!openId) return;
    var st = history.state;
    closeAll();   // hide immediately — never wait on the browser
    if (st && SECTIONS[st.view]) {
      closing = true;
      closeTimer = setTimeout(endClosing, 900);
      try { history.back(); } catch (e) { endClosing(); }
    }
  };

  // Used by logout and any other code that wants everything closed
  window.hideAllSections = function () {
    closeAll();
    Object.keys(SECTIONS).forEach(function (id) {
      var el = document.getElementById(id);
      if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
    });
  };

  // Esc closes the page — unless a dialog is open on top of it (then Esc belongs to that dialog)
  document.addEventListener('keydown', function (e) {
    if (e.key !== 'Escape' || !openId) return;
    var dialogs = document.querySelectorAll('.modal, [role="dialog"], [id$="-modal"], #lessonPlannerPanel');
    for (var i = 0; i < dialogs.length; i++) {
      var d = dialogs[i];
      if (d.classList.contains('tm-fp')) continue;
      var cs = window.getComputedStyle(d);
      if (cs.display !== 'none' && cs.visibility !== 'hidden' && d.getClientRects().length) return;
    }
    e.preventDefault();
    window.closeFullPage();
  });
})();

// ── Class management helpers (Fixed with Fallback) ────────────────────────

async function _cm2InitSection() {
  // 🛠️ FIX 6 (properly applied this time): await the dropdown fill
  // before trying to select a value on it. Setting .value on a <select>
  // before its <option> elements exist fails silently, which meant the
  // dropdown UI could look unselected/blank on first opening the section.
  await _cm2PopulateClassDropdown();
  const classId = window._selectedClassId ||
    localStorage.getItem('tm_selected_class');
  if (classId) {
    const sel = document.getElementById('cm2-class-select');
    if (sel) sel.value = classId;
    window.initPremiumClassDashboard(classId);
  }
}

async function _cm2PopulateClassDropdown() {
  const sel = document.getElementById('cm2-class-select');
  if (!sel) return;
  try {
    let classes = await getIndexedDB('classes');
    if (!Array.isArray(classes)) classes = [];
    
    // 🛠️ FALLBACK: Check userProfile if classes array is empty
    if (classes.length === 0 && typeof auth !== 'undefined' && auth.currentUser) {
      const profile = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', auth.currentUser.uid).catch(() => null);
      if (profile && Array.isArray(profile.classes)) {
        classes = profile.classes;
      }
    }
    
    if (!classes.length) return;
    
    while (sel.options.length > 1) sel.remove(1);
    
    classes.forEach(cls => {
      // 🛠️ Normalize raw strings to objects
      if (typeof cls === 'string') {
        cls = { id: cls, name: cls.replace(/_/g, ' ') };
      }
      
      const opt = document.createElement('option');
      opt.value = cls.id || cls.classId;
      opt.textContent = cls.name || cls.className || cls.id;
      sel.appendChild(opt);
    });
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[CM2] Could not populate class dropdown:', e.message);
  }
}

window._cm2OnClassChange = function(classId) {
  if (!classId) return;
  window._selectedClassId = classId;
  localStorage.setItem('tm_selected_class', classId);
  window.initPremiumClassDashboard(classId);
};

window.loadStudents = function() {
  const legacy = document.getElementById('select-class');
  const classId = legacy ? legacy.value : null;
  if (classId) window._cm2OnClassChange(classId);
};

//  ✅ FIX 5 — Realtime sync DISABLED by default (was wiping IndexedDB)
//  ✅ FIX 6 — _cm2InitSection async timing fixed (awaits dropdown fill)
//  ✅ FIX 7 — feesConfig lookup uses raw classId, not sanitized

// ─── Module state ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const CM2 = {
  classId:          null,
  rawClassId:       null,   // FIX 3: keep original, unsanitized classId
  loadToken:        0,      // 🛠️ FIX 9: bumped on every class switch so a
                            // slow/stale in-flight load for a PREVIOUS
                            // class can detect it's no longer current and
                            // bail out instead of overwriting the newer
                            // class's dashboard once it finally resolves.

// ─── Helpers ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function normId(id)  { return String(id ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
function esc(s)      { return String(s ?? '').replace(/[&<>"']/g, c =>
  ({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c])); }

function getZambianGrade(score) {
  if (score >= 80) return { label:'1', desc:'Distinction', color:'#059669' };
  if (score >= 70) return { label:'2', desc:'Distinction', color:'#059669' };
  if (score >= 60) return { label:'3', desc:'Merit',       color:'#2563eb' };
  if (score >= 50) return { label:'4', desc:'Merit',       color:'#2563eb' };
  if (score >= 40) return { label:'5', desc:'Credit',      color:'#d97706' };
  if (score >= 30) return { label:'6', desc:'Credit',      color:'#d97706' };
  if (score >= 20) return { label:'7', desc:'Pass',        color:'#7c3aed' };
  if (score >= 10) return { label:'8', desc:'Pass',        color:'#7c3aed' };
  return              { label:'9', desc:'Fail',        color:'#dc2626' };
}

function fmtDate(val) {
  if (!val) return '—';
  const d = new Date(val);
  return isNaN(d) ? String(val) : d.toLocaleDateString('en-ZM',
    { day:'numeric', month:'short', year:'numeric' });
}

// ── FIX 1: Extract fee amount from EITHER format ──────────────────
function _extractFeeAmount(fee) {
  if (Array.isArray(fee.payments))
    return fee.payments.reduce((s, p) => s + (Number(p.amount) || 0), 0);
  return Number(fee.amount || fee.amountPaid || 0);
}

// ── FIX 1: Normalise any IndexedDB return into a flat array ───────
function _toArray(data) {
  if (!data) return [];
  if (Array.isArray(data)) return data;
  if (typeof data === 'object') return Object.values(data);
  return [];
}

// ── FIX 2: Try multiple store names for attendance ─────────────────
async function _getAttendance() {
  let att = _toArray(await getIndexedDB('attendanceHistory').catch(() => null));
  if (!att.length) {
    att = _toArray(await getIndexedDB('attendance').catch(() => null));
  }
  // FIX 8: diagnostic
  console.log(`[CM2] attendance records found: ${att.length}`,
    att.length ? att[0] : '(empty)');
  return att;
}

// ── FIX 1: Get fees as a guaranteed flat array ─────────────────────
async function _getFees() {
  const raw = await getIndexedDB('fees').catch(() => null);
  const arr = _toArray(raw);
  console.log(`[CM2] fees records found: ${arr.length}`,
    arr.length ? arr[0] : '(empty)');
  return arr;
}

// ── FIX 4: Find a score in a scores object with case-insensitive key
function _findScore(scores, studentId, fallbackId) {
  if (!scores) return undefined;
  // Try exact key first
  if (scores[studentId] !== undefined) return Number(scores[studentId]);
  if (scores[fallbackId] !== undefined) return Number(scores[fallbackId]);
  // Try normId version of every key
  const normTarget = normId(studentId);
  for (const k of Object.keys(scores)) {
    if (normId(k) === normTarget) return Number(scores[k]);
  }
  return undefined;
}

// ── Initials avatar ───────────────────────────────────────────────
function _initialsAvatar(name, size = 36) {
  const initials = (name||'?').split(' ').slice(0,2).map(w=>w[0]||'').join('').toUpperCase();
  const colors   = ['#059669','#2563eb','#d97706','#7c3aed','#dc2626','#0891b2'];
  const color    = colors[Math.abs((name||'').split('').reduce((a,c)=>a+c.charCodeAt(0),0)) % colors.length];
  const svg = `<svg xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg" width="${size}" height="${size}" viewBox="0 0 ${size} ${size}"><circle cx="${size/2}" cy="${size/2}" r="${size/2}" fill="${color}"/><text x="${size/2}" y="${size/2+size*0.14}" text-anchor="middle" fill="white" font-size="${size*0.38}" font-weight="700" font-family="system-ui">${initials}</text></svg>`;
  return `data:image/svg+xml;base64,${btoa(svg)}`;
}
