//  INIT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.initPremiumClassDashboard = async function(classId) {
  if (!classId) return;
  _injectCM2Styles();

  // 🛠️ FIX 9: claim this load with a fresh token. If the user picks
  // another class before this one's IndexedDB reads finish, that newer
  // call will bump CM2.loadToken again — and when THIS call's reads
  // eventually resolve, it will see its token is stale and stop before
  // ever touching the DOM. This is what was causing the dashboard to
  // occasionally "stick" on the previously selected class: without this
  // guard, whichever class's reads happened to finish last simply won,
  // even if the user had already moved on to a different class.
  const myToken = ++CM2.loadToken;

  // FIX 3: store RAW classId, no sanitizeKey
  CM2.rawClassId  = classId;
  CM2.classId     = classId;
  CM2.currentPage = 1;
  CM2.searchQuery = '';

  // Animate out
  const root = document.getElementById('cm2-root');
  if (root) { root.style.opacity='0'; root.style.transition='opacity .18s'; }

  await _loadAndEnrichStudents();

  // Bail out silently if a newer class selection has come in while we
  // were awaiting IndexedDB reads above.
  if (myToken !== CM2.loadToken) {
    console.log('[CM2] Discarding stale load for classId:', classId);
    return;
  }

  _buildDashboard();

  if (root) { root.style.opacity='1'; }
  // FIX 5: realtime sync disabled — it was wiping IndexedDB with empty docs
  // Re-enable only after confirming your Firestore paths match the sync engine.
  // await _initRealtimeSync(classId);
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  DATA LOADING + ENRICHMENT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function _loadAndEnrichStudents() {
  // FIX 3: use rawClassId for ALL comparisons
  const cid = CM2.rawClassId;

  // FIX 8: log what classId we're searching for
  console.log('[CM2] Loading data for classId:', JSON.stringify(cid));

  const [allStudentsRaw, attendance, fees, results, feesConfig] = await Promise.all([
    getIndexedDB('students').catch(() => []),
    _getAttendance(),
    _getFees(),
    getIndexedDB('results').catch(() => []).then(_toArray),
    // FIX 7: use raw classId for feesConfig lookup
    getIndexedDB('feesConfig', cid).catch(() => null),
  ]);

  // FIX 3: compare classId as-is (no sanitizeKey, no normId)
  const rawStudents = _toArray(allStudentsRaw).filter(s => s.classId === cid);

  // FIX 8: diagnostic
  console.log(`[CM2] students in class: ${rawStudents.length}`,
    rawStudents.slice(0,2));
  console.log(`[CM2] attendance records for class: ${attendance.filter(a=>a.classId===cid).length}`);
  console.log(`[CM2] fees records for class: ${fees.filter(f=>f.classId===cid).length}`);
  console.log(`[CM2] results records for class: ${results.filter(r=>r.classId===cid).length}`);
  console.log('[CM2] feesConfig:', feesConfig);

  const classAtt     = attendance.filter(a => a.classId === cid);
  const classFees    = fees.filter(f => f.classId === cid);
  const classResults = results.filter(r => r.classId === cid);
  const totalFee     = feesConfig?.totalFee || feesConfig?.totalAmount || 0;

  CM2.allStudents = rawStudents.map(student => {
    const origId = student.studentId || student.id || '';
    const sid    = normId(origId);

    // Attendance — FIX 1: a.present === true
    const sAtt     = classAtt.filter(a => normId(a.studentId) === sid);
    const sPresent = sAtt.filter(a => a.present === true).length;
    const sAttPct  = sAtt.length ? Math.round((sPresent / sAtt.length) * 100) : null;

    // Results — FIX 4: case-insensitive key lookup
    let scoreSum = 0, scoreCount = 0;
    classResults.forEach(r => {
      const score = _findScore(r.scores, origId, sid);
      if (score !== undefined && !isNaN(score)) { scoreSum += score; scoreCount++; }
    });
    const avgScore = scoreCount ? Math.round(scoreSum / scoreCount) : null;

    // Fees — FIX 1 & FIX 2: handle both formats
    const sFeeRecs = classFees.filter(f =>
      normId(f.studentId || f.id) === sid);
    const sPaid = sFeeRecs.reduce((sum, f) => sum + _extractFeeAmount(f), 0);
    const feeStatus = totalFee === 0 ? 'na'
      : sPaid >= totalFee ? 'paid'
      : sPaid > 0 ? 'partial' : 'unpaid';

    return {
      ...student,
      _sid: sid, _origId: origId,
      _attPct: sAttPct, _avgScore: avgScore,
      _feeStatus: feeStatus, _paid: sPaid, _totalFee: totalFee,
    };
  });

  // Store class-level data for modal tabs
  CM2._classAtt     = classAtt;
  CM2._classFees    = classFees;
  CM2._classResults = classResults;
  CM2._feesConfig   = feesConfig;
  CM2._totalFee     = totalFee;

  // Performance ranking
  const ranked = [...CM2.allStudents]
    .filter(s => s._avgScore !== null)
    .sort((a, b) => b._avgScore - a._avgScore);
  CM2.allStudents = CM2.allStudents.map(s => ({
    ...s,
    _rank: ranked.findIndex(r => r._sid === s._sid) + 1 || null,
  }));

  CM2.filteredStudents = [...CM2.allStudents];
  CM2.lastUpdated      = new Date();
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BUILD FULL DASHBOARD
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _buildDashboard() {
  const root = document.getElementById('cm2-root');
  if (!root) return;

  // Show diagnostic bar if all stats are null (helps you spot the bug)
  const hasAny = CM2.allStudents.some(s =>
    s._attPct !== null || s._avgScore !== null || s._feeStatus !== 'na');
  const diagMsg = !CM2.allStudents.length
    ? `⚠️ No students found for classId="${CM2.rawClassId}". Check that students are stored with this exact classId.`
    : !hasAny
    ? `⚠️ Students found (${CM2.allStudents.length}) but attendance/results/fees all empty. Check store names in console.`
    : '';

  root.innerHTML = `
    ${diagMsg ? `<div class="cm2-diag-bar show">${esc(diagMsg)}</div>` : ''}
    ${_buildStatCards()}
    <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;
      margin-bottom:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
      <div style="font-size:.75rem;font-weight:700;color:var(--cm2-muted);">
        ${CM2.allStudents.length} student${CM2.allStudents.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} enrolled
      </div>
      <div class="cm2-pulse" id="cm2-update-pulse">
        <div class="cm2-pulse-dot"></div> Updated just now
      </div>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-toolbar">
      <div class="cm2-search-wrap">
        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" fill="none" stroke="currentColor" stroke-width="2">
          <circle cx="11" cy="11" r="8"/><path d="m21 21-4.35-4.35"/>
        </svg>
        <input class="cm2-search" id="cm2-search"
          placeholder="Search by name or ID…"
          oninput="window._cm2Search(this.value)" autocomplete="off">
      </div>
      <select class="cm2-sort-select" id="cm2-sort"
        onchange="window._cm2Sort(this.value)">
        <option value="name-asc">Name A→Z</option>
        <option value="name-desc">Name Z→A</option>
        <option value="att-desc">Attendance ↓</option>
        <option value="att-asc">Attendance ↑</option>
        <option value="grade-desc">Grade Best→Worst</option>
        <option value="fee-asc">Fees: Unpaid first</option>
      </select>
      <button class="cm2-export-btn" onclick="window._cm2ExportCSV()">⬇ CSV</button>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-table-wrap">
      <table class="cm2-table" id="cm2-table">
        <thead>
          <tr>
            <th onclick="window._cm2SortCol('name')">Student ↕</th>
            <th onclick="window._cm2SortCol('att')">Attendance</th>
            <th onclick="window._cm2SortCol('grade')">Avg Grade</th>
            <th onclick="window._cm2SortCol('fee')">Fees</th>
            <th>Rank</th>
          </tr>
        </thead>
        <tbody id="cm2-tbody"></tbody>
      </table>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-pagination" id="cm2-pagination"></div>

    <!-- STUDENT MODAL -->
    <div class="cm2-modal-overlay" id="cm2-modal-overlay"
      onclick="if(event.target===this)window.closeCM2Modal()">
      <div class="cm2-modal" id="cm2-modal">
        <div class="cm2-modal-header">
          <div class="cm2-modal-hero">
            <img class="cm2-modal-avatar" id="cm2-modal-avatar" src="" alt="">
            <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
              <div class="cm2-modal-name" id="cm2-modal-name"></div>
              <div class="cm2-modal-meta" id="cm2-modal-meta"></div>
            </div>
            <button class="cm2-modal-close" onclick="window.closeCM2Modal()">✕</button>
          </div>
          <div class="cm2-modal-tabs">
            <button class="cm2-modal-tab active" data-tab="profile"
              onclick="window._cm2SwitchTab('profile',this)">👤 Profile</button>
            <button class="cm2-modal-tab" data-tab="results"
              onclick="window._cm2SwitchTab('results',this)">📊 Results</button>
            <button class="cm2-modal-tab" data-tab="attendance"
              onclick="window._cm2SwitchTab('attendance',this)">📅 Attendance</button>
            <button class="cm2-modal-tab" data-tab="fees"
              onclick="window._cm2SwitchTab('fees',this)">💰 Fees</button>
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="cm2-modal-body">
          <div class="cm2-tab-panel active" id="cm2-panel-profile"></div>
          <div class="cm2-tab-panel" id="cm2-panel-results"></div>
          <div class="cm2-tab-panel" id="cm2-panel-attendance"></div>
          <div class="cm2-tab-panel" id="cm2-panel-fees"></div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  _renderStudentTable();
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STAT CARDS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _buildStatCards() {
  const students = CM2.allStudents;
  const att      = CM2._classAtt    || [];
  const fees     = CM2._classFees   || [];
  const results  = CM2._classResults || [];
  const totalFee = CM2._totalFee    || 0;

  const totalStudents = students.length;

  // Attendance
  const attPct = att.length
    ? Math.round((att.filter(a => a.present === true).length / att.length) * 100)
    : null;

  // Avg score — FIX 4 already applied in enrichment
  let scoreSum = 0, scoreCount = 0;
  students.forEach(s => {
    if (s._avgScore !== null) { scoreSum += s._avgScore; scoreCount++; }
  });
  const avgScore = scoreCount ? Math.round(scoreSum / scoreCount) : null;
  const grade    = avgScore !== null ? getZambianGrade(avgScore) : null;

  // Fees
  const totalCollected = fees.reduce((s, f) => s + _extractFeeAmount(f), 0);
  const totalExpected  = totalFee * totalStudents;
  const feesPct        = totalExpected ? Math.round((totalCollected / totalExpected) * 100) : null;
  const outstanding    = Math.max(0, totalExpected - totalCollected);

  // Health score
  const hc = [];
  if (attPct  !== null) hc.push(attPct);
  if (avgScore !== null) hc.push(avgScore);
  if (feesPct  !== null) hc.push(feesPct);
  const healthScore = hc.length ? Math.round(hc.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/hc.length) : null;
  const healthColor = healthScore === null ? '#94a3b8'
    : healthScore >= 75 ? '#059669'
    : healthScore >= 50 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';

  const _attColor  = attPct  === null ? '#94a3b8' : attPct  >= 80 ? '#059669' : attPct  >= 60 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';
  const _feeColor  = feesPct === null ? '#94a3b8' : feesPct >= 80 ? '#059669' : feesPct >= 50 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';

  const ring = healthScore !== null ? `
    <div class="cm2-health-ring" style="width:52px;height:52px;">
      <svg width="52" height="52" viewBox="0 0 52 52">
        <circle cx="26" cy="26" r="20" fill="none" stroke="#e5e7eb" stroke-width="5"/>
        <circle cx="26" cy="26" r="20" fill="none" stroke="${healthColor}" stroke-width="5"
          stroke-dasharray="${Math.round(2*Math.PI*20*healthScore/100)} 200"
          stroke-linecap="round"/>
      </svg>
      <div class="cm2-health-label" style="color:${healthColor};">
        <div style="font-size:.62rem;font-weight:900;">${healthScore}</div>
      </div>
    </div>` : '';

  return `
  <div class="cm2-stat-grid">
    <div class="cm2-stat-card" style="--cm2-bar-color:#00cc88;">
      <div class="cm2-stat-icon">👥</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-label">Students</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-value">${totalStudents}</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-sub">enrolled in class</div>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-stat-card" style="--cm2-bar-color:${_attColor};">
      <div class="cm2-stat-icon">📅</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-label">Attendance</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-value" style="color:${_attColor}">
        ${attPct !== null ? attPct + '%' : '—'}
      </div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-sub">${attPct===null?'No records':attPct>=80?'Excellent':attPct>=60?'Average':'Needs attention'}</div>
      ${attPct !== null ? `<div class="cm2-progress" style="margin-top:6px;">
        <div class="cm2-progress-fill" style="width:${attPct}%;background:${_attColor};"></div>
      </div>` : ''}
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-stat-card" style="--cm2-bar-color:${grade?grade.color:'#94a3b8'};">
      <div class="cm2-stat-icon">📊</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-label">Class Average</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-value" style="color:${grade?grade.color:'#94a3b8'}">
        ${avgScore !== null ? 'G' + grade.label : '—'}
      </div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-sub">${avgScore !== null ? grade.desc + ' · ' + avgScore + '%' : 'No results yet'}</div>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-stat-card" style="--cm2-bar-color:${_feeColor};">
      <div class="cm2-stat-icon">💰</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-label">Fees Collected</div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-value" style="font-size:1.4rem;color:${_feeColor}">
        ${feesPct !== null ? feesPct + '%' : '—'}
      </div>
      <div class="cm2-stat-sub">K${outstanding.toLocaleString()} outstanding</div>
      ${feesPct !== null ? `<div class="cm2-progress" style="margin-top:6px;">
        <div class="cm2-progress-fill" style="width:${Math.min(100,feesPct)}%;background:${_feeColor};"></div>
      </div>` : ''}
    </div>
    ${healthScore !== null ? `
    <div class="cm2-stat-card" style="--cm2-bar-color:${healthColor};">
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;position:relative;z-index:1;">
        ${ring}
        <div>
          <div class="cm2-stat-label">Class Health</div>
          <div class="cm2-stat-value" style="font-size:1.5rem;color:${healthColor};">${healthScore}</div>
          <div class="cm2-stat-sub">${healthScore>=75?'Thriving':healthScore>=50?'Moderate':'At risk'}</div>
        </div>
      </div>
    </div>` : ''}
  </div>`;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STUDENT TABLE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _renderStudentTable() {
  const tbody = document.getElementById('cm2-tbody');
  if (!tbody) return;
  const data  = CM2.filteredStudents;
  const pages = Math.max(1, Math.ceil(data.length / CM2.pageSize));
  if (CM2.currentPage > pages) CM2.currentPage = pages;
  const start = (CM2.currentPage - 1) * CM2.pageSize;
  const paged = data.slice(start, start + CM2.pageSize);

  if (!paged.length) {
    tbody.innerHTML = `<tr><td colspan="5"><div class="cm2-empty">
      <div class="cm2-empty-icon">${CM2.searchQuery ? '🔍' : '👥'}</div>
      <h3>${CM2.searchQuery ? 'No matches' : 'No students yet'}</h3>
      <p>${CM2.searchQuery
        ? `No students match "${esc(CM2.searchQuery)}".`
        : 'Students will appear here once they are admitted to this class.'}</p>
    </div></td></tr>`;
    _renderPagination(0, 1);
    return;
  }

  tbody.innerHTML = paged.map(s => {
    const avatarSrc = s.photo || _initialsAvatar(s.name, 36);
    const attColor  = s._attPct === null ? '#94a3b8' : s._attPct >= 80 ? '#059669' : s._attPct >= 60 ? '#d97706' : '#dc2626';

    const attHtml = s._attPct !== null
      ? `<span class="cm2-pill ${s._attPct>=80?'cm2-pill-green':s._attPct>=60?'cm2-pill-amber':'cm2-pill-red'}">${s._attPct}%</span>
         <div class="cm2-mini-bar"><div class="cm2-mini-fill" style="width:${s._attPct}%;background:${attColor};"></div></div>`
      : `<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:.8rem;">—</span>`;

    let gradeHtml = `<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:.8rem;">—</span>`;
    if (s._avgScore !== null) {
      const g = getZambianGrade(s._avgScore);
      gradeHtml = `<span class="cm2-pill" style="background:${g.color}18;color:${g.color};">G${g.label} · ${s._avgScore}%</span>`;
    }

    const feePills = {
      paid:    '<span class="cm2-pill cm2-pill-green">✓ Paid</span>',
      partial: '<span class="cm2-pill cm2-pill-amber">⚡ Partial</span>',
      unpaid:  '<span class="cm2-pill cm2-pill-red">✗ Unpaid</span>',
      na:      '<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:.8rem;">—</span>',
    };

    const medals = ['🥇','🥈','🥉'];
    const rankHtml = s._rank && s._rank <= 3
      ? medals[s._rank-1]
      : s._rank
      ? `<span style="font-size:.8rem;color:var(--cm2-muted);">#${s._rank}</span>`
      : `<span style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:.8rem;">—</span>`;

    return `
      <tr onclick="window.openCM2StudentModal('${esc(s.id)}')">
        <td>
          <div class="cm2-name-cell">
            <img class="cm2-avatar" src="${esc(avatarSrc)}" alt="${esc(s.name)}"
              onerror="this.src='${_initialsAvatar(s.name,36)}'">
            <div>
              <div class="cm2-student-name">${esc(s.name)}</div>
              <div class="cm2-student-id">${esc(s.studentId || s.id)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td>${attHtml}</td>
        <td>${gradeHtml}</td>
        <td>${feePills[s._feeStatus] || feePills.na}</td>
        <td>${rankHtml}</td>
      </tr>`;
  }).join('');

  _renderPagination(data.length, pages);
}

function _renderPagination(total, pages) {
  const wrap = document.getElementById('cm2-pagination');
  if (!wrap) return;
  if (!total || pages <= 1) { wrap.style.display='none'; return; }
  wrap.style.display = 'flex';
  const start = (CM2.currentPage-1)*CM2.pageSize+1;
  const end   = Math.min(CM2.currentPage*CM2.pageSize, total);
  wrap.innerHTML = `
    <span>Showing ${start}–${end} of ${total} students</span>
    <div class="cm2-pages">
      <button class="cm2-pg-btn" ${CM2.currentPage===1?'disabled':''}
        onclick="window._cm2GoPage(${CM2.currentPage-1})">‹</button>
      ${Array.from({length:pages},(_,i)=>i+1)
        .filter(p=>p===1||p===pages||Math.abs(p-CM2.currentPage)<=1)
        .reduce((acc,p,i,arr)=>{if(i>0&&p-arr[i-1]>1)acc.push('…');acc.push(p);return acc;},[])
        .map(p=>p==='…'
          ?`<span style="padding:0 4px;color:var(--cm2-muted);">…</span>`
          :`<button class="cm2-pg-btn${p===CM2.currentPage?' active':''}"
              onclick="window._cm2GoPage(${p})">${p}</button>`
        ).join('')}
      <button class="cm2-pg-btn" ${CM2.currentPage===pages?'disabled':''}
        onclick="window._cm2GoPage(${CM2.currentPage+1})">›</button>
    </div>`;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SEARCH / SORT / PAGINATE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window._cm2Search = function(q) {
  CM2.searchQuery=q.toLowerCase().trim(); CM2.currentPage=1; _applyFilters();
};
window._cm2Sort = function(val) {
  const [f,d]=val.split('-'); CM2.sortField=f; CM2.sortDir=d; CM2.currentPage=1; _applyFilters();
};
window._cm2SortCol = function(field) {
  CM2.sortDir = CM2.sortField===field && CM2.sortDir==='desc' ? 'asc' : 'desc';
  CM2.sortField=field; CM2.currentPage=1; _applyFilters();
};
window._cm2GoPage = function(p) {
  CM2.currentPage=p; _renderStudentTable();
  document.getElementById('cm2-table')?.scrollIntoView({behavior:'smooth',block:'nearest'});
};

function _applyFilters() {
  let data = [...CM2.allStudents];
  if (CM2.searchQuery) {
    data = data.filter(s =>
      (s.name||'').toLowerCase().includes(CM2.searchQuery) ||
      normId(s.studentId||s.id).includes(CM2.searchQuery));
  }
  data.sort((a,b) => {
    let va,vb;
    switch(CM2.sortField) {
      case 'name':  va=(a.name||'').toLowerCase(); vb=(b.name||'').toLowerCase(); break;
      case 'att':   va=a._attPct??-1;   vb=b._attPct??-1;   break;
      case 'grade': va=a._avgScore??-1; vb=b._avgScore??-1; break;
      case 'fee':   va=a._paid??0;      vb=b._paid??0;      break;
      default:      va=a.name||'';      vb=b.name||'';
    }
    if (typeof va==='string') return CM2.sortDir==='asc'?va.localeCompare(vb):vb.localeCompare(va);
    return CM2.sortDir==='asc'?va-vb:vb-va;
  });
  CM2.filteredStudents=data;
  _renderStudentTable();
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CSV EXPORT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window._cm2ExportCSV = function() {
  const data = CM2.filteredStudents;
  if (!data.length) return;
  const headers = ['Student ID','Name','Class','Phone','Attendance %','Avg Grade','Fee Status','Rank'];
  const rows = data.map(s => {
    const g = s._avgScore!==null ? `G${getZambianGrade(s._avgScore).label} (${s._avgScore}%)` : '';
    return [s.studentId||s.id, s.name, s.classId, s.phone||'',
      s._attPct!==null?s._attPct+'%':'', g, s._feeStatus||'', s._rank||'']
      .map(v=>`"${String(v).replace(/"/g,'""')}"`);
  });
  const csv  = [headers.join(','),...rows.map(r=>r.join(','))].join('\n');
  const blob = new Blob([csv],{type:'text/csv;charset=utf-8;'});
  const url  = URL.createObjectURL(blob);
  const a    = document.createElement('a');
  a.href=url; a.download=`class_${CM2.rawClassId}_${new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]}.csv`;
  a.click(); URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STUDENT MODAL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.openCM2StudentModal = function(studentId) {
  const student = CM2.allStudents.find(s => s.id === studentId);
  if (!student) return;
  CM2.activeStudentId = studentId;

  const avatarSrc = student.photo || _initialsAvatar(student.name, 60);
  document.getElementById('cm2-modal-avatar').src = avatarSrc;
  document.getElementById('cm2-modal-name').textContent = student.name || '—';

  const grade     = student._avgScore !== null ? getZambianGrade(student._avgScore) : null;
  const feeLabel  = {paid:'💚 Paid',partial:'🟡 Partial',unpaid:'🔴 Due',na:''}[student._feeStatus]||'';
  document.getElementById('cm2-modal-meta').innerHTML = `
    <span>${esc(student.studentId||student.id)}</span>
    ${student._attPct!==null?`<span>📅 ${student._attPct}%</span>`:''}
    ${grade?`<span>G${grade.label} · ${grade.desc}</span>`:''}
    ${feeLabel?`<span>${feeLabel}</span>`:''}`;

  // Reset tabs
  document.querySelectorAll('.cm2-modal-tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.cm2-tab-panel').forEach(p=>{p.classList.remove('active');p.innerHTML='';delete p.dataset.loaded;});
  document.querySelector('[data-tab="profile"]')?.classList.add('active');
  const profilePanel = document.getElementById('cm2-panel-profile');
  if (profilePanel) { profilePanel.classList.add('active'); _loadProfileTab(student); }

  const overlay = document.getElementById('cm2-modal-overlay');
  if (overlay) { overlay.style.display='flex'; requestAnimationFrame(()=>overlay.classList.add('open')); }
};

window.closeCM2Modal = function() {
  const overlay = document.getElementById('cm2-modal-overlay');
  if (!overlay) return;
  overlay.classList.remove('open');
  setTimeout(()=>{ overlay.style.display='none'; }, 240);
};

window._cm2SwitchTab = function(tab, btn) {
  document.querySelectorAll('.cm2-modal-tab').forEach(t=>t.classList.remove('active'));
  document.querySelectorAll('.cm2-tab-panel').forEach(p=>p.classList.remove('active'));
  btn.classList.add('active');
  const panel = document.getElementById(`cm2-panel-${tab}`);
  if (panel) {
    panel.classList.add('active');
    if (!panel.dataset.loaded) {
      panel.dataset.loaded = '1';
      const student = CM2.allStudents.find(s=>s.id===CM2.activeStudentId);
      if (student) {
        if (tab==='results')    _loadResultsTab(student, panel);
        if (tab==='attendance') _loadAttendanceTab(student, panel);
        if (tab==='fees')       _loadFeesTab(student, panel);
      }
    }
  }
};

// ── Profile ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _loadProfileTab(student) {
  const panel = document.getElementById('cm2-panel-profile');
  if (!panel) return;
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div class="cm2-field-grid">
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Full Name</label>
        <div class="val">${esc(student.name||'—')}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Student ID</label>
        <div class="val" style="font-family:'DM Mono',monospace;font-size:.8rem;">
          ${esc(student.studentId||student.id||'—')}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Class</label>
        <div class="val">${esc(student.classId||'—')}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Phone / Guardian</label>
        <div class="val">${esc(student.phone||'—')}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field" style="grid-column:span 2;"><label>Address</label>
        <div class="val">${esc(student.address||'—')}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Date Registered</label>
        <div class="val">${fmtDate(student.createdAt)}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Last Updated</label>
        <div class="val">${fmtDate(student.updatedAt)}</div></div>
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-divider"></div>
    <div class="cm2-field-grid">
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Avg Grade</label>
        <div class="val">${student._avgScore!==null
          ?`G${getZambianGrade(student._avgScore).label} · ${getZambianGrade(student._avgScore).desc} · ${student._avgScore}%`
          :'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Attendance</label>
        <div class="val">${student._attPct!==null?student._attPct+'%':'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Fee Status</label>
        <div class="val">${{paid:'✅ Fully Paid',partial:`⚡ Partial (K${(student._paid||0).toLocaleString()})`,unpaid:'❌ Unpaid',na:'—'}[student._feeStatus]||'—'}</div></div>
      <div class="cm2-field"><label>Class Rank</label>
        <div class="val">${student._rank?'#'+student._rank+' in class':'—'}</div></div>
    </div>`;
}

// ── Results ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _loadResultsTab(student, panel) {
  const sid = student._sid; const origId = student._origId;
  const sResults = (CM2._classResults||[])
    .map(r => {
      const score = _findScore(r.scores, origId, sid);  // FIX 4
      return score !== undefined ? { ...r, _score: score } : null;
    })
    .filter(Boolean)
    .sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.date||b.createdAt||0)-new Date(a.date||a.createdAt||0));

  if (!sResults.length) {
    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="cm2-empty"><div class="cm2-empty-icon">📊</div>
      <h3>No results yet</h3><p>Results appear here once test scores are recorded.</p></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const avg   = Math.round(sResults.reduce((s,r)=>s+r._score,0)/sResults.length);
  const grade = getZambianGrade(avg);
  const max   = Math.max(...sResults.map(r=>r._score));
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${_miniStatCard('#2563eb','Tests',sResults.length,'')}
      ${_miniStatCard(grade.color,'Average',avg+'%','')}
      ${_miniStatCard('#059669','Best',max+'%','')}
    </div>
    <div style="margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${sResults.slice(0,12).map(r=>{const g=getZambianGrade(r._score);return `
        <div class="cm2-bar-row">
          <div class="cm2-bar-label" title="${esc(r.subject||'—')}">${esc((r.subject||'').slice(0,13))}</div>
          <div class="cm2-bar-track"><div class="cm2-bar-fill" style="width:${r._score}%;background:${g.color};"></div></div>
          <div class="cm2-bar-val" style="color:${g.color};">${r._score}%</div>
        </div>`;}).join('')}
    </div>
    ${sResults.map(r=>{const g=getZambianGrade(r._score);return `
      <div class="cm2-hist-row">
        <div class="cm2-hist-icon" style="background:${g.color}18;">📝</div>
        <div class="cm2-hist-main">
          <div class="cm2-hist-title">${esc(r.subject||'—')} · ${esc(r.testName||r.name||'—')}</div>
          <div class="cm2-hist-sub">${fmtDate(r.date||r.createdAt)}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="cm2-hist-right">
          <span style="background:${g.color}18;color:${g.color};padding:2px 8px;
            border-radius:20px;font-size:.72rem;font-weight:700;">G${g.label} · ${r._score}%</span>
        </div>
      </div>`;}).join('')}`;
}

// ── Attendance ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _loadAttendanceTab(student, panel) {
  const sid  = student._sid;
  const sAtt = (CM2._classAtt||[])
    .filter(a=>normId(a.studentId)===sid)
    .sort((a,b)=>(b.date||'').localeCompare(a.date||''));

  if (!sAtt.length) {
    panel.innerHTML = `<div class="cm2-empty"><div class="cm2-empty-icon">📅</div>
      <h3>No attendance records</h3><p>Records appear once the teacher starts taking registers.</p></div>`;
    return;
  }
  const present = sAtt.filter(a=>a.present===true).length;
  const pct     = Math.round((present/sAtt.length)*100);
  const color   = pct>=80?'#059669':pct>=60?'#d97706':'#dc2626';
  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${_miniStatCard('#2563eb','Sessions',sAtt.length,'')}
      ${_miniStatCard('#059669','Present',present,'')}
      ${_miniStatCard(color,'Rate',pct+'%','')}
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-progress" style="margin-bottom:14px;">
      <div class="cm2-progress-fill" style="width:${pct}%;background:${color};"></div>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--cm2-muted);margin-bottom:7px;font-weight:700;">
      Timeline · <span style="color:#059669;">●</span> present
      <span style="color:#dc2626;margin-left:5px;">●</span> absent
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-att-dots">
      ${sAtt.slice(0,60).map(a=>`<div class="cm2-att-dot ${a.present===true?'present':'absent'}"
        title="${fmtDate(a.date)} — ${a.present===true?'Present':'Absent'}"></div>`).join('')}
      ${sAtt.length>60?`<span style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--cm2-muted);margin-left:4px;">+${sAtt.length-60} more</span>`:''}
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-divider"></div>
    <div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.9px;color:var(--cm2-muted);margin-bottom:9px;">Recent Sessions</div>
    ${sAtt.slice(0,20).map(a=>`
      <div class="cm2-hist-row">
        <div class="cm2-hist-icon" style="background:${a.present===true?'#d1fae5':'#fee2e2'};">
          ${a.present===true?'✅':'❌'}
        </div>
        <div class="cm2-hist-main">
          <div class="cm2-hist-title">${fmtDate(a.date)}</div>
          <div class="cm2-hist-sub">Term ${a.term||'—'} · Week ${a.week||'—'}</div>
        </div>
        <div class="cm2-hist-right">
          <div class="cm2-hist-val" style="color:${a.present===true?'#059669':'#dc2626'};">
            ${a.present===true?'Present':'Absent'}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`).join('')}`;
}

// ── Fees ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
function _loadFeesTab(student, panel) {
  const sid      = student._sid;
  const origId   = student._origId;
  const sFees    = (CM2._classFees||[])
    .filter(f=>normId(f.studentId||f.id)===sid);
  const totalFee = CM2._totalFee || 0;
  const paid     = sFees.reduce((s,f)=>s+_extractFeeAmount(f),0);
  const balance  = Math.max(0,totalFee-paid);
  const pct      = totalFee>0 ? Math.round((paid/totalFee)*100) : 0;
  const color    = pct>=100?'#059669':pct>=50?'#d97706':'#dc2626';

  const payments = [];
  sFees.forEach(f=>{
    if (Array.isArray(f.payments)) f.payments.forEach(p=>payments.push({...p}));
    else if (f.amount) payments.push({date:f.date,amount:f.amount,status:f.status||'Paid'});
  });
  payments.sort((a,b)=>(b.date||'').localeCompare(a.date||''));

  panel.innerHTML = `
    <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;flex-wrap:wrap;margin-bottom:14px;">
      ${_miniStatCard('#2563eb','Target','K'+totalFee.toLocaleString(),'')}
      ${_miniStatCard('#059669','Paid','K'+paid.toLocaleString(),'')}
      ${_miniStatCard(color,'Balance','K'+balance.toLocaleString(),'')}
    </div>
    <div class="cm2-progress" style="margin-bottom:6px;">
      <div class="cm2-progress-fill" style="width:${Math.min(100,pct)}%;background:${color};"></div>
    </div>
    <div style="font-size:.7rem;color:var(--cm2-muted);margin-bottom:14px;">${pct}% of target paid</div>
    ${!payments.length
      ?`<div class="cm2-empty"><div class="cm2-empty-icon">💳</div>
          <h3>No payments yet</h3><p>Payment records appear here once fees are recorded.</p></div>`
      :`<div style="font-size:.7rem;font-weight:800;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.9px;
           color:var(--cm2-muted);margin-bottom:9px;">Payment History</div>
        ${payments.map(p=>`
          <div class="cm2-hist-row">
            <div class="cm2-hist-icon" style="background:#d1fae5;">💳</div>
            <div class="cm2-hist-main">
              <div class="cm2-hist-title">${fmtDate(p.date)}</div>
              <div class="cm2-hist-sub">${esc(p.status||'Paid')}${p.method?` · ${esc(p.method)}`:''}</div>
            </div>
            <div class="cm2-hist-right">
              <div class="cm2-hist-val" style="color:#059669;">K${Number(p.amount||0).toLocaleString()}</div>
            </div>
          </div>`).join('')}`}`;
}

// ── Shared mini stat card ─────────────────────────────────────────
function _miniStatCard(color, label, value, sub) {
  return `<div style="flex:1;min-width:80px;background:#f8fffe;border:1.5px solid #d0f5e8;
    border-radius:12px;padding:11px 14px;">
    <div style="font-size:.68rem;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.9px;
      color:#64748b;margin-bottom:3px;">${esc(label)}</div>
    <div style="font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:900;color:${esc(color)};">${esc(String(value))}</div>
    ${sub?`<div style="font-size:.7rem;color:#64748b;">${esc(sub)}</div>`:''}
  </div>`;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SILENT REFRESH (called after realtime updates or manual refresh)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

async function _silentRefresh() {
  // Guard: don't refresh if no class is loaded
  if (!CM2.rawClassId) return;

  await _loadAndEnrichStudents();
  _applyFilters();

  // Replace stat cards in-place without rebuilding the whole dashboard
  const root = document.getElementById('cm2-root');
  if (root) {
    const oldGrid = root.querySelector('.cm2-stat-grid');
    if (oldGrid) {
      const tmp = document.createElement('div');
      tmp.innerHTML = _buildStatCards();
      const newGrid = tmp.querySelector('.cm2-stat-grid');
      if (newGrid) oldGrid.replaceWith(newGrid);
    }
  }

  // Flash the update pulse indicator
  const pulse = document.getElementById('cm2-update-pulse');
  if (pulse) {
    pulse.classList.add('show');
    clearTimeout(CM2.updatePulse);
    CM2.updatePulse = setTimeout(() => pulse.classList.remove('show'), 4000);
  }
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.destroyPremiumClassDashboard = function() {
  CM2.unsubListeners.forEach(u=>{ try{u();}catch(_){} });
  CM2.unsubListeners = [];
  CM2.classId = null; CM2.rawClassId = null;
  CM2.allStudents = []; CM2.filteredStudents = [];
  const root = document.getElementById('cm2-root');
  if (root) root.innerHTML = '';
};

// ── Legacy aliases ────────────────────────────────────────────────
window.loadStudents            = window.initPremiumClassDashboard;
window.loadClassSummaries      = _silentRefresh;
window.showPremiumStudentModal = window.openCM2StudentModal;
window.closeStudentModal       = window.closeCM2Modal;

// ── Expose internal helpers for showSection wiring ────────────────
window._cm2InitSection = _cm2InitSection;

console.log('[TeachMate] ✅ Premium Class Dashboard v3 loaded — 8 bugs fixed');









// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// TEACHMATE — ATTENDANCE MODULE v3
// ── Original logic:          100% preserved
// ── v2 additions:            100% preserved
// ── v3 NEW enhancements:
//      • Subject modal shows actual student list with Present/Absent toggle
//      • Subject attendance history — separate view, date-grouped tables
//      • General attendance history — date-grouped tables
//      • Per-student subject attendance filter (name + ID + term + week)
//      • AI subject-attendance comments (subject-context-aware)
//      • Subject attendance PDF generation + share (WhatsApp / email / download)
//      • Auto term/week attach from general fields (with real-date validation)
//      • Startup AI alert carousel — bad-attendance alerts with 3 options
//        (Never show again | Cancel for 2 days) for every affected student
// ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
(function () {

  // ── stores ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const STORE            = 'attendanceHistory';
  const DRAFT_STORE      = 'attendanceDrafts';
  const ALERT_PREFS_KEY  = 'tm_att_alert_prefs'; // localStorage key for alert suppression

  // ── today ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const today = new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0]; // YYYY-MM-DD

  // ── tiny helpers ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function normId(id)       { return sanitizeKey(String(id ?? '').trim()); }
  function normText(t)      { return String(t ?? '').trim().toLowerCase(); }
  function esc(s)           { return escapeHTML(String(s ?? '')); }
  function draftKey(cls, d) { return `draft:${normId(cls)}:${d}`; }

  function fmtDate(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '-';
    const [y, m, d2] = iso.split('-');
    const months = ['Jan','Feb','Mar','Apr','May','Jun','Jul','Aug','Sep','Oct','Nov','Dec'];
    return `${parseInt(d2, 10)} ${months[parseInt(m,10)-1]} ${y}`;
  }

  // ── alert-pref helpers (localStorage — tiny, no IndexedDB overhead) ────────
  function loadAlertPrefs() {
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(ALERT_PREFS_KEY) || '{}'); } catch { return {}; }
  }
  function saveAlertPrefs(p) {
    try { localStorage.setItem(ALERT_PREFS_KEY, JSON.stringify(p)); } catch {}
  }
  /** Returns true if this studentId's alert should be suppressed right now */
  function isAlertSuppressed(studentId) {
    const prefs = loadAlertPrefs();
    const p = prefs[normId(studentId)];
    if (!p) return false;
    if (p.mode === 'never') return true;
    if (p.mode === 'snooze') {
      const showAfter = new Date(p.showAfter);
      return new Date() < showAfter;
    }
    return false;
  }
  function suppressForever(studentId) {
    const p = loadAlertPrefs();
    p[normId(studentId)] = { mode: 'never' };
    saveAlertPrefs(p);
  }
  function snoozeFor2Days(studentId) {
    const p = loadAlertPrefs();
    const d = new Date(); d.setDate(d.getDate() + 2);
    p[normId(studentId)] = { mode: 'snooze', showAfter: d.toISOString() };
    saveAlertPrefs(p);
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  DRAFT PERSISTENCE  ██████████████████████████████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  async function loadDraft(className) {
    try { return (await getIndexedDB(DRAFT_STORE, draftKey(className, today))) || null; }
    catch { return null; }
  }
  async function saveDraft(className, subjectRows) {
    try {
      const key  = draftKey(className, today);
      await setIndexedDB(DRAFT_STORE, key,
        { key, classId: className, date: today, subjectRows, savedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    } catch (e) { console.warn('Draft save failed', e); }
  }
  async function clearDraft(className) {
    try { await deleteIndexedDB(DRAFT_STORE, draftKey(className, today)); } catch {}
  }
  async function hasPendingDraft(className) {
    const d = await loadDraft(className);
    return !!(d && d.subjectRows && d.subjectRows.length > 0);
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  GENERAL AI INSIGHT ENGINE (v2 — unchanged)  █████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  function computeAttendanceInsight(presents, total, studentName) {
    if (total === 0) return null;
    const rate    = (presents / total) * 100;
    const absents = total - presents;
    const name    = String(studentName || 'This student').split(' ')[0];

    if (rate >= 95) return {
      level: 'excellent', badge: '🌟 Excellent Attendance',
      headline: `${name} has outstanding attendance at ${rate.toFixed(1)}% — only ${absents} absence${absents===1?'':'s'} recorded.`,
      effects: ['Consistent presence gives full exposure to all taught content.','High attendance strongly correlates with better academic performance.','The student is building strong habits that will benefit them beyond school.'],
      actions: ['Acknowledge and commend this student publicly — positive reinforcement matters.','Consider nominating them for an attendance award at term end.','Use this student as a peer mentor for those with lower attendance.']
    };
    if (rate >= 80) return {
      level: 'good', badge: '✅ Good Attendance',
      headline: `${name} has good attendance at ${rate.toFixed(1)}% with ${absents} absence${absents===1?'':'s'}.`,
      effects: ['Minor gaps in content coverage — mostly manageable with self-study.','Academic performance is unlikely to be significantly affected at this rate.','Continued good attendance will ensure consistent understanding.'],
      actions: ['Encourage the student to maintain or improve this rate next term.','Briefly follow up on missed lessons to ensure no gaps were left unresolved.','Positive reinforcement will help sustain this pattern.']
    };
    if (rate >= 65) return {
      level: 'watch', badge: '⚠️ Needs Monitoring',
      headline: `${name}'s attendance has dropped to ${rate.toFixed(1)}% — ${absents} absences are becoming a concern.`,
      effects: ['Missing roughly 1 in every 5 lessons leads to noticeable content gaps.','The student may struggle to follow lessons that build on missed topics.','Test and exam performance could begin to decline if this pattern continues.','Peers in the class are advancing while this student falls further behind.'],
      actions: ['Have a private, supportive conversation with the student to understand why absences are occurring.','Contact parents or guardians to discuss the pattern and seek their support.','Provide the student with notes or catch-up materials for missed lessons.','Set a target rate of at least 85% for the remainder of the term.','Monitor weekly — if absences continue, escalate to the head teacher.']
    };
    if (rate >= 50) return {
      level: 'warning', badge: '🔴 Poor Attendance',
      headline: `${name} is attending only ${rate.toFixed(1)}% of lessons — this is a serious concern requiring immediate action.`,
      effects: ['The student is missing more than one-third of all taught content.','Subject teachers cannot effectively assess a student who is rarely present.','There is a high risk of failing end-of-term assessments and examinations.','Social and academic integration with classmates will be impaired.','Without intervention, the student may face grade retention or inability to sit exams.','Long-term: poor attendance habits at school age strongly predict lower educational outcomes.'],
      actions: ['Arrange an urgent parent-teacher meeting — do not wait for the next report day.','Investigate the root cause: health issues, home responsibilities, financial barriers, or bullying.','Create a documented attendance improvement plan with clear weekly targets.','Assign a peer buddy to help the student catch up on missed content.','Notify the head teacher and school welfare officer if the cause is safeguarding-related.','Record all interventions taken for accountability purposes.','Schedule fortnightly check-in meetings with the student directly.']
    };
    return {
      level: 'critical', badge: '🚨 CRITICAL — Urgent Action Required',
      headline: `${name} has attended only ${rate.toFixed(1)}% of lessons (${absents} absences). This is a critical situation demanding immediate escalation.`,
      effects: ['The student has missed more than half of all taught lessons — content knowledge is severely incomplete.','Passing any end-of-term or final examination is highly unlikely without drastic intervention.','Grade retention or school dropout is a real risk if this continues unaddressed.','Peer and social development is severely impacted by chronic absence.','The psychological effect of falling this far behind can discourage further attendance — a negative cycle.','Long-term consequences include severely limited career and life opportunities.','If a minor is involved, chronic absenteeism may have child welfare/safeguarding implications.'],
      actions: ['⚡ IMMEDIATE: Notify the head teacher and school welfare officer today.','⚡ IMMEDIATE: Contact parents/guardians by phone — do not rely on written notes alone.','Arrange a formal multi-party meeting: student, parents, class teacher, and head teacher.','Investigate all possible causes: health, finance, family, distance from school, safety.','Create a formal written attendance improvement contract signed by student and parent.','Explore support mechanisms: bursary assistance, transport support, counselling referral.','If safeguarding concerns exist, follow the school\'s child protection procedures.','Document every action taken with dates and outcomes for the school record.','Set a weekly attendance review — do not wait a full term to reassess.','Consider whether alternative learning arrangements are needed as a short-term bridge.']
    };
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  SUBJECT-SPECIFIC AI COMMENT ENGINE (NEW v3)  ████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Generates a subject-specific AI comment.
   * subjectRecords = array of { date, subject, subjectTeacher, present (bool), studentName }
   */
  function computeSubjectInsight(subjectRecords, studentName, subjectName) {
    if (!subjectRecords || subjectRecords.length === 0) return null;
    const name     = String(studentName || 'This student').split(' ')[0];
    const total    = subjectRecords.length;
    const presents = subjectRecords.filter(r => r.present).length;
    const absents  = total - presents;
    const rate     = (presents / total) * 100;
    const subj     = subjectName || 'this subject';

    if (rate >= 95) return {
      level: 'excellent', badge: '🌟 Excellent Subject Attendance',
      headline: `${name} has exceptional attendance for ${subj} at ${rate.toFixed(1)}% — only ${absents} lesson${absents===1?'':'s'} missed.`,
      comment: `${name}'s attendance in ${subj} is outstanding. This level of commitment shows a strong work ethic and a genuine interest in the subject. Keep encouraging and challenging this student — they are making the most of every lesson.`,
      actions: ['Acknowledge this achievement in class or in writing.','Consider giving extended or enrichment work to keep the student engaged.','This student could serve as a subject-specific peer tutor for classmates who are struggling.']
    };
    if (rate >= 80) return {
      level: 'good', badge: '✅ Good Subject Attendance',
      headline: `${name} has good attendance for ${subj} at ${rate.toFixed(1)}% with ${absents} absence${absents===1?'':'s'}.`,
      comment: `${name} is attending ${subj} lessons consistently. The few absences are unlikely to cause major gaps, but it is worth following up to ensure no key topics were missed. Encourage the student to keep up this good pattern.`,
      actions: ['Briefly check in with the student to ensure missed lessons are covered.','Encourage the student to maintain or improve attendance for the rest of term.']
    };
    if (rate >= 65) return {
      level: 'watch', badge: '⚠️ Subject Attendance Concern',
      headline: `${name}'s attendance in ${subj} has slipped to ${rate.toFixed(1)}% — ${absents} lesson${absents===1?'':'s'} missed and growing.`,
      comment: `${name} is beginning to miss a noticeable number of ${subj} lessons. At this rate, gaps in subject knowledge will start to affect performance in tests and assessments. It is advisable to have a quiet word with ${name} and, if the pattern continues, inform the class teacher so they can follow up with the student or their parents.`,
      actions: [`Speak privately with ${name} — find out if there is a specific reason for missing ${subj} lessons.`,'Share lesson notes or materials for the missed sessions.','Alert the class teacher so they are aware of the pattern in this specific subject.','Monitor attendance weekly and escalate if it continues to drop.']
    };
    if (rate >= 50) return {
      level: 'warning', badge: '🔴 Poor Subject Attendance',
      headline: `${name}'s attendance in ${subj} is poor at ${rate.toFixed(1)}% — ${absents} lessons missed out of ${total}.`,
      comment: `${name}'s subject attendance record in ${subj} is poor and is a serious concern. Missing this many lessons means the student is falling significantly behind the rest of the class in this subject. You should inform the class teacher about this immediately so they can follow up. Alternatively, you may choose to confront ${name} directly in a professional and supportive manner, or inform their parents or guardians so they are aware and can take action at home.`,
      actions: [`⚠️ Inform ${name}'s class teacher about this subject-specific attendance issue.`,`Consider speaking directly with ${name} in a calm, supportive way — ask what is preventing them from attending ${subj}.`,'Share the attendance data with parents or guardians so they can reinforce the importance of attending.','Provide catch-up materials for all missed lessons immediately.','Set a written attendance improvement target for this subject with clear consequences if not met.','Record all follow-up actions taken for accountability.']
    };
    return {
      level: 'critical', badge: '🚨 CRITICAL Subject Absence',
      headline: `${name} has attended only ${rate.toFixed(1)}% of ${subj} lessons — ${absents} absences. This is a critical subject attendance failure.`,
      comment: `${name}'s subject attendance record in ${subj} is critically low — this student has missed more than half of all ${subj} lessons. At this point, meaningful learning in ${subj} has been severely disrupted and passing any related assessment is unlikely without immediate and intensive intervention. This situation must be escalated without delay. Inform ${name}'s class teacher and head teacher today. Parents or guardians must also be contacted directly — a phone call, not just a note. If ${name} is consistently absent from a specific subject only, this may also indicate a deeper issue such as a conflict with the learning environment, bullying, or subject anxiety that needs professional attention.`,
      actions: ['⚡ IMMEDIATE: Report to class teacher and head teacher today — do not wait.','⚡ IMMEDIATE: Contact parents/guardians by phone directly.','Schedule an urgent meeting with the student, parents, class teacher, and subject teacher.','Investigate whether the absence pattern is specific to this subject or crosses multiple subjects.','Provide a complete set of all missed lesson materials and arrange extra catch-up sessions.','Create a written subject attendance improvement contract with clear weekly targets.','Consider referral to school counsellor if anxiety or a personal issue is suspected.','Document all actions taken with dates for the school record.','Review after 2 weeks — if no improvement, involve the school welfare officer.']
    };
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  INSIGHT PANEL RENDERER (shared — v2 style preserved)  ██████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  function renderInsightPanel(containerEl, insight) {
    if (!insight || !containerEl) return;
    const colorMap = {
      excellent: { bg:'#f0fdf4', border:'#86efac', title:'#166534', badge:'#dcfce7', badgeText:'#15803d' },
      good:      { bg:'#f0fdf4', border:'#6ee7b7', title:'#065f46', badge:'#d1fae5', badgeText:'#047857' },
      watch:     { bg:'#fffbeb', border:'#fcd34d', title:'#92400e', badge:'#fef3c7', badgeText:'#b45309' },
      warning:   { bg:'#fff7ed', border:'#fdba74', title:'#7c2d12', badge:'#ffedd5', badgeText:'#c2410c' },
      critical:  { bg:'#fef2f2', border:'#fca5a5', title:'#7f1d1d', badge:'#fee2e2', badgeText:'#b91c1c' }
    };
    const c = colorMap[insight.level] || colorMap.watch;
    const commentBlock = insight.comment
      ? `<div style="background:${c.badge};border-radius:8px;padding:10px 12px;margin-bottom:10px;">
           <p style="margin:0 0 4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:${c.title};text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;">🤖 AI Comment</p>
           <p style="margin:0;font-size:12px;color:${c.title};line-height:1.6;font-style:italic;">"${insight.comment}"</p>
         </div>` : '';
    containerEl.innerHTML = `
      <div style="background:${c.bg};border:2px solid ${c.border};border-radius:12px;padding:16px;margin-top:14px;
        font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:flex-start;gap:10px;margin-bottom:10px;">
          <span style="background:${c.badge};color:${c.badgeText};font-size:11px;font-weight:800;
            padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;white-space:nowrap;flex-shrink:0;
            letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;">${insight.badge}</span>
          <p style="margin:0;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:${c.title};line-height:1.5;">${insight.headline}</p>
        </div>
        ${commentBlock}
        <div style="margin-bottom:10px;">
          <p style="margin:0 0 5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:${c.title};text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;">📊 Likely Effects</p>
          <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:18px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
            ${insight.effects.map(e=>`<li style="font-size:12px;color:#374151;line-height:1.5;">${e}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <div>
          <p style="margin:0 0 5px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:${c.title};text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;">✅ Suggested Actions</p>
          <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:18px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
            ${insight.actions.map(a=>`<li style="font-size:12px;color:#374151;line-height:1.5;">${a}</li>`).join('')}
          </ul>
        </div>
        <p style="margin:10px 0 0;font-size:10px;color:#6b7280;font-style:italic;text-align:right;">🤖 TeachMate AI Attendance Insight</p>
      </div>`;
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  DATE-GROUPED TABLE BUILDER (shared utility)  ████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Renders records into date-grouped sections.
   * Each date gets a bold heading + its own sub-table below it.
   * @param {HTMLElement} container  - where to append
   * @param {Array}       records    - array of attendance record objects
   * @param {Function}    rowBuilder - (rec) => <tr> element
   * @param {Array}       headers    - column header strings
   */
  function renderDateGroupedTables(container, records, rowBuilder, headers) {
    // Group by date
    const groups = {};
    records.forEach(rec => {
      const d = rec.date || 'Unknown Date';
      if (!groups[d]) groups[d] = [];
      groups[d].push(rec);
    });
    // Sort dates descending (newest first)
    const sortedDates = Object.keys(groups).sort((a,b) => b.localeCompare(a));
    sortedDates.forEach(dateKey => {
      // Date heading
      const heading = document.createElement('div');
      heading.style.cssText = `
        margin:18px 0 6px; padding:8px 14px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg,#00cc88,#00cc88);
        border-radius:8px; display:inline-block;
      `;
      heading.innerHTML = `<span style="color:#fff;font-size:15px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:.02em;">
        📅 ${fmtDate(dateKey)}</span>`;
      container.appendChild(heading);

      // Sub-table
      const tbl   = document.createElement('table');
      tbl.className = 'attendance-history-table';
      tbl.style.cssText = 'width:100%;margin-bottom:4px;border-collapse:collapse;';
      const thead = document.createElement('thead');
      thead.innerHTML = `<tr>${headers.map(h=>`<th style="background:#81D4FA;color:#fff;padding:7px 10px;font-size:11px;text-align:left;">${h}</th>`).join('')}</tr>`;
      tbl.appendChild(thead);
      const tbody = document.createElement('tbody');
      groups[dateKey].forEach((rec, i) => {
        const tr = rowBuilder(rec, i);
        if (tr) tbody.appendChild(tr);
      });
      tbl.appendChild(tbody);
      container.appendChild(tbl);
    });
  }

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  STARTUP AI ALERT CAROUSEL  ██████████████████████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Scans all attendance records for the currently selected class.
   * For every student whose rate falls below 65%, shows a dismissible
   * alert modal one at a time (carousel). Each alert has:
   *   [Never show again]  [Cancel — remind in 2 days]
   */
  async function runStartupAlerts(className) {
    try {
      let attendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
      if (!Array.isArray(attendance)) attendance = [];
      let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
      if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];

      const classStudents = students.filter(s => s.classId === className);
      if (!classStudents.length) return;

      const studentMap = new Map(classStudents.map(s => [normId(s.id), s]));

      // Build per-student stats
      const alerts = [];
      studentMap.forEach((stu, sid) => {
        if (isAlertSuppressed(sid)) return;
        const recs    = attendance.filter(a => a.classId === className && normId(a.studentId) === sid && (a.type !== 'subject-summary'));
        if (recs.length < 3) return; // not enough data yet
        const presents = recs.filter(r => r.present).length;
        const rate     = (presents / recs.length) * 100;
        if (rate < 65) {
          alerts.push({ stu, sid, presents, total: recs.length, rate });
        }
      });

      if (!alerts.length) return;

      // Sort: lowest rate first (worst first)
      alerts.sort((a, b) => a.rate - b.rate);

      let idx = 0;

      function showAlert(alertObj) {
        if (idx >= alerts.length) return;
        const { stu, sid, presents, total, rate } = alertObj;
        const absents = total - presents;
        const insight = computeAttendanceInsight(presents, total, stu.name);
        const c = {
          critical: { bg:'#fef2f2', border:'#fca5a5', title:'#7f1d1d', accent:'#b91c1c' },
          warning:  { bg:'#fff7ed', border:'#fdba74', title:'#7c2d12', accent:'#c2410c' },
          watch:    { bg:'#fffbeb', border:'#fcd34d', title:'#92400e', accent:'#b45309' }
        }[insight.level] || { bg:'#fff7ed', border:'#fdba74', title:'#7c2d12', accent:'#c2410c' };

        document.getElementById('tm-startup-alert')?.remove();

        const overlay = document.createElement('div');
        overlay.id = 'tm-startup-alert';
        overlay.style.cssText = `
          position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:999999;
          background:rgba(15,23,42,0.82);
          display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
          padding:16px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
          backdrop-filter:blur(4px);animation:tmFadeIn .25s ease;
        `;
        if (!document.getElementById('tm-anim-style')) {
          const st = document.createElement('style');
          st.id = 'tm-anim-style';
          st.textContent = '@keyframes tmFadeIn{from{opacity:0;transform:scale(.96)}to{opacity:1;transform:scale(1)}}';
          document.head.appendChild(st);
        }

        overlay.innerHTML = `
          <div style="background:${c.bg};border:2.5px solid ${c.border};border-radius:16px;
            width:100%;max-width:520px;max-height:88vh;overflow-y:auto;
            box-shadow:0 30px 70px rgba(0,0,0,.4);">

            <!-- header -->
            <div style="background:${c.accent};border-radius:13px 13px 0 0;padding:16px 20px;
              display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;">
              <div style="font-size:28px;">⚠️</div>
              <div>
                <div style="color:#fff;font-size:15px;font-weight:800;">Attendance Alert — ${esc(stu.name)}</div>
                <div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,.8);font-size:11px;margin-top:2px;">
                  Alert ${idx+1} of ${alerts.length} &nbsp;|&nbsp; ID: ${esc(stu.id)} &nbsp;|&nbsp; Class: ${esc(className)}
                </div>
              </div>
            </div>

            <!-- body -->
            <div style="padding:18px 20px;">
              <!-- stats strip -->
              <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:8px;margin-bottom:14px;">
                ${[['Present',presents,'#166534'],['Absent',absents,'#b91c1c'],['Rate',rate.toFixed(1)+'%',c.accent]]
                  .map(([l,v,col])=>`
                  <div style="background:#fff;border-radius:8px;padding:10px;text-align:center;
                    box-shadow:0 1px 4px rgba(0,0,0,.08);">
                    <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:${col};">${v}</div>
                    <div style="font-size:10px;color:#6b7280;font-weight:600;text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:2px;">${l}</div>
                  </div>`).join('')}
              </div>

              <!-- AI comment -->
              <div style="background:#fff;border-radius:10px;padding:12px 14px;margin-bottom:14px;
                border-left:4px solid ${c.accent};">
                <p style="margin:0 0 4px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:${c.title};
                  text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;">🤖 AI Assessment</p>
                <p style="margin:0;font-size:13px;color:${c.title};line-height:1.6;">${insight.headline}</p>
              </div>

              <!-- suggested actions (top 3) -->
              <div style="margin-bottom:16px;">
                <p style="margin:0 0 6px;font-size:11px;font-weight:800;color:${c.title};
                  text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;">📋 Recommended Actions</p>
                <ul style="margin:0;padding-left:16px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;">
                  ${insight.actions.slice(0,3).map(a=>
                    `<li style="font-size:12px;color:#374151;line-height:1.5;">${a}</li>`).join('')}
                </ul>
              </div>

              <!-- 3 action buttons -->
              <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:8px;">
                <button id="tm-alert-never" style="
                  padding:11px;border:none;background:#6b7280;color:#fff;
                  border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
                  transition:background .15s;">
                  🚫 Never show this alert for ${esc(stu.name.split(' ')[0])} again
                </button>
                <button id="tm-alert-snooze" style="
                  padding:11px;border:2px solid ${c.border};background:#fff;color:${c.title};
                  border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
                  transition:background .15s;">
                  ⏰ Cancel — remind me again in 2 days
                </button>
                ${idx + 1 < alerts.length
                  ? `<button id="tm-alert-next" style="
                      padding:11px;border:none;background:${c.accent};color:#fff;
                      border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">
                      ➜ Next Alert (${idx+2} of ${alerts.length})
                    </button>`
                  : ''}
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>`;

        document.body.appendChild(overlay);

        overlay.querySelector('#tm-alert-never').addEventListener('click', () => {
          suppressForever(sid);
          overlay.remove();
          idx++;
          if (idx < alerts.length) showAlert(alerts[idx]);
        });
        overlay.querySelector('#tm-alert-snooze').addEventListener('click', () => {
          snoozeFor2Days(sid);
          overlay.remove();
          idx++;
          if (idx < alerts.length) showAlert(alerts[idx]);
        });
        overlay.querySelector('#tm-alert-next')?.addEventListener('click', () => {
          overlay.remove();
          idx++;
          if (idx < alerts.length) showAlert(alerts[idx]);
        });
      }

      // Small delay so the page settles before the alert fires
      setTimeout(() => showAlert(alerts[0]), 1200);

    } catch (e) { console.warn('runStartupAlerts error', e); }
  }



  async function ensurePdfMake() {
  if (window.pdfMake) return window.pdfMake;

  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const script = document.createElement('script');
    script.src = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdfmake/0.2.7/pdfmake.min.js";
    script.onload = () => {
      const vfs = document.createElement('script');
      vfs.src = "https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdfmake/0.2.7/vfs_fonts.js";
      vfs.onload = () => resolve(window.pdfMake);
      vfs.onerror = reject;
      document.head.appendChild(vfs);
    };
    script.onerror = reject;
    document.head.appendChild(script);
  });
}



  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  PDF GENERATOR FOR SUBJECT ATTENDANCE  ███████████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  /**
   * Generates and shares/downloads a PDF for a subject attendance report.
   * @param {object} opts
   *   className, subject, subjectTeacher, term, week, date,
   *   presentStudents  [{id, name}]
   *   absentStudents   [{id, name}]
   *   totalStudents    number
   *   className, schoolName
   */
  async function generateSubjectAttendancePDF(opts) {
    const {
      className = '', subject = '', subjectTeacher = '',
      term = '', week = '', date = today,
      presentStudents = [], absentStudents = [],
      totalStudents = 0, schoolName = 'TEACHMATE SCHOOL'
    } = opts;

    const presentCount = presentStudents.length;
    const absentCount  = absentStudents.length;
    const rate         = totalStudents > 0 ? ((presentCount / totalStudents) * 100).toFixed(1) : '0.0';

    const insight = computeSubjectInsight(
      presentStudents.map(() => ({ present: true })),
      `Class ${className}`, subject
    );

    const content = [];

    // School header
    content.push({ text: schoolName.toUpperCase(), style: 'school', margin:[0,0,0,2] });
    content.push({ text: 'Subject Attendance Report', style: 'title', margin:[0,0,0,4] });
    content.push({ canvas:[
      {type:'line',x1:0,y1:0,x2:515,y2:0,lineWidth:2,lineColor:'#1e3a8a'},
      {type:'line',x1:0,y1:3,x2:515,y2:3,lineWidth:1,lineColor:'#059669'}
    ], margin:[0,0,0,10] });

    // Meta info
    content.push({
      table:{
        widths:['*','*'],
        body:[
          [{text:[{text:'Subject: ',bold:true},subject]},{text:[{text:'Teacher: ',bold:true},subjectTeacher]}],
          [{text:[{text:'Class: ',bold:true},className]},{text:[{text:'Date: ',bold:true},fmtDate(date)]}],
          [{text:[{text:'Term: ',bold:true},term]},{text:[{text:'Week: ',bold:true},week]}]
        ]
      }, layout:'noBorders', margin:[0,0,0,10], fontSize:9
    });

    // Stats band
    content.push({
      table:{
        widths:['*','*','*','*'],
        body:[
          [{text:'Total',style:'statL'},{text:'Present',style:'statL'},{text:'Absent',style:'statL'},{text:'Rate',style:'statL'}],
          [
            {text:String(totalStudents),style:'statV'},
            {text:String(presentCount),style:'statV',color:'#15803d'},
            {text:String(absentCount),style:'statV',color:'#b91c1c'},
            {text:`${rate}%`,style:'statV',color: parseFloat(rate)>=80?'#15803d':parseFloat(rate)>=65?'#b45309':'#b91c1c'}
          ]
        ]
      }, layout:'noBorders', margin:[0,0,0,10]
    });

    // Present students table
    if (presentStudents.length) {
      content.push({text:'Students Present',style:'secHeader',margin:[0,0,0,4]});
      content.push({
        table:{
          headerRows:1,
          widths:['auto','*'],
          body:[
            [{text:'ID',style:'tH'},{text:'Name',style:'tH'}],
            ...presentStudents.map(s=>[
              {text:String(s.id),fontSize:8},
              {text:String(s.name),fontSize:8}
            ])
          ]
        },
        layout:{
          fillColor:(r)=>r===0?'#1e3a8a':(r%2===0?'#f0fdf4':'#ffffff'),
          hLineColor:()=>'#94a3b8', vLineColor:()=>'#94a3b8',
          hLineWidth:()=>.5, vLineWidth:()=>.5,
          paddingTop:()=>3, paddingBottom:()=>3
        }, margin:[0,0,0,10]
      });
    }

    // Absent students table
    if (absentStudents.length) {
      content.push({text:'Students Absent',style:'secHeader',color:'#b91c1c',margin:[0,0,0,4]});
      content.push({
        table:{
          headerRows:1,
          widths:['auto','*'],
          body:[
            [{text:'ID',style:'tH'},{text:'Name',style:'tH'}],
            ...absentStudents.map(s=>[
              {text:String(s.id),fontSize:8},
              {text:String(s.name),fontSize:8}
            ])
          ]
        },
        layout:{
          fillColor:(r)=>r===0?'#991b1b':(r%2===0?'#fff7f7':'#ffffff'),
          hLineColor:()=>'#94a3b8', vLineColor:()=>'#94a3b8',
          hLineWidth:()=>.5, vLineWidth:()=>.5,
          paddingTop:()=>3, paddingBottom:()=>3
        }, margin:[0,0,0,10]
      });
    }

    // AI comment
    if (insight) {
      content.push({canvas:[{type:'line',x1:0,y1:0,x2:515,y2:0,lineWidth:.75,lineColor:'#cbd5e1'}],margin:[0,0,0,6]});
      content.push({text:'AI Attendance Assessment',style:'secHeader',margin:[0,0,0,4]});
      content.push({text:insight.comment || insight.headline, fontSize:8, italics:true, color:'#374151', margin:[0,0,0,4]});
      content.push({text:'Suggested Actions:', fontSize:8, bold:true, margin:[0,0,0,3]});
      insight.actions.slice(0,4).forEach(a=>{
        content.push({text:`• ${a}`, fontSize:7.5, color:'#4b5563', margin:[6,0,0,2]});
      });
    }

    // Signature
    content.push({
      columns:[
        {text:'Subject Teacher Signature: ___________________', width:'50%', fontSize:8, margin:[0,10,0,0]},
        {text:'Class Teacher Signature: ___________________', width:'50%', alignment:'right', fontSize:8, margin:[0,10,0,0]}
      ]
    });

    const dd = {
      pageSize:'A4', pageMargins:[25,25,25,25],
      watermark:{text:schoolName,color:'#f1f5f9',opacity:.45,bold:true},
      content,
      footer:{text:`TeachMate 3.0 Pro — Subject Attendance Report — Generated ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`,
        alignment:'center', fontSize:7, color:'#94a3b8'},
      styles:{
        school:    {fontSize:16,bold:true,alignment:'center',color:'#0f172a'},
        title:     {fontSize:11,alignment:'center',color:'#475569'},
        tH:        {bold:true,fontSize:8,color:'#ffffff',alignment:'center'},
        secHeader: {fontSize:10,bold:true,color:'#1e3a8a'},
        statL:     {fontSize:8,color:'#64748b',alignment:'center',bold:true},
        statV:     {fontSize:13,bold:true,alignment:'center',color:'#0f172a'}
      },
      defaultStyle:{fontSize:9}
    };

    try {
     window. showLoading('Generating Subject Attendance PDF...');
      const pdfMake = await ensurePdfMake();
      const gen     = pdfMake.createPdf(dd);
      const fileName= `SubjectAttendance_${normId(subject)}_${normId(className)}_${date}.pdf`;

      gen.getBlob(async (blob) => {
        window.hideLoading();
        const file = new File([blob], fileName, {type:'application/pdf'});
        if (navigator.canShare && navigator.canShare({files:[file]})) {
          try {
            await navigator.share({ files:[file], title:`Subject Attendance — ${subject}`,
              text:`Subject attendance report for ${subject}, ${className} on ${fmtDate(date)} via TeachMate.` });
            showToast('✅ Report shared successfully','success');
          } catch { gen.download(fileName); }
        } else {
          gen.download(fileName);
          showToast('✅ PDF downloaded','success');
        }
      });
    } catch (e) {
      window.hideLoading();
      console.error('PDF gen failed', e);
      showToast('⚠️ PDF generation failed: ' + (e.message||'Unknown'),'error');
    }
  }





// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
// ██  SUBJECT ATTENDANCE HISTORY MODAL — ENHANCED v4
//     Original logic: 100% preserved
//     New:
//       • General view expands each record to show EXACTLY which students
//         were Present ✅ and Absent ❌ with photos, names, IDs
//       • Collapsible student roster per date/subject record
//       • Stats bar per record (rate %, present count, absent count)
//       • AI subject insight rendered per record in general view
//       • Student-specific view shows lesson-by-lesson breakdown table
//         (was present/absent on each exact date for that subject)
//       • "View Roster" toggle button on every record row
//       • All filters (name, ID, subject, term, week) still work exactly
//       • PDF and live-search unchanged
// ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

window.openSubjectAttendanceHistory = async function () {
  const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
  if (!classEl || !classEl.value) return showToast('⚠️ Please select a class first', 'error');
  const className = classEl.value.trim();

  let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
  if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];
  const studentMap    = new Map(students.map(s => [normId(s.id), s]));
  const classStudents = students.filter(s => s.classId === className);

  let attendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
  if (!Array.isArray(attendance)) attendance = [];

  // Only subject-level records for this class
  const subjectRecs = attendance.filter(a =>
    a && a.classId === className && a.type === 'subject-attendance'
  );

  document.getElementById('tm-subhist-modal')?.remove();

  // ── colour helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function rateColor(rate) {
    if (rate >= 80) return { bg:'#f0fdf4', text:'#166534', bar:'#22c55e' };
    if (rate >= 60) return { bg:'#fefce8', text:'#854d0e', bar:'#eab308' };
    return              { bg:'#fef2f2', text:'#991b1b', bar:'#ef4444' };
  }

  function fmtDateLong(iso) {
    if (!iso) return '-';
    const [y, m, d] = iso.split('-');
    const months = ['January','February','March','April','May','June',
                    'July','August','September','October','November','December'];
    return `${parseInt(d,10)} ${months[parseInt(m,10)-1]} ${y}`;
  }

  // ── modal shell ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const modal = document.createElement('div');
  modal.id = 'tm-subhist-modal';
  modal.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99997;
    background:rgba(15,23,42,0.82);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    padding:10px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
    backdrop-filter:blur(3px);
  `;

  modal.innerHTML = `
    <div style="background:#fff;border-radius:16px;width:100%;max-width:940px;
      max-height:96vh;overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
      box-shadow:0 32px 80px rgba(0,0,0,.38);">

      <!-- ── header ── -->
      <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#009960 0%,#00cc88 100%);
        padding:16px 20px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;
        flex-shrink:0;position:relative;overflow:hidden;">
        <!-- shimmer line -->
        <div style="position:absolute;top:0;left:0;right:0;height:3px;
          background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,rgba(255,255,255,0.7),transparent);
          background-size:200% 100%;animation:tmShimmer 2.5s linear infinite;"></div>
        <div>
          <div style="color:#fff;font-size:16px;font-weight:800;letter-spacing:-0.01em;">
            📚 Subject Attendance History
          </div>
          <div style="color:rgba(255,255,255,0.82);font-size:12px;margin-top:3px;">
            Class: <strong style="color:#fff;">${esc(className)}</strong>
            &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${subjectRecs.length} session record${subjectRecs.length!==1?'s':''} found
          </div>
        </div>
        <button id="tm-subhist-close" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.18);border:1.5px solid
          rgba(255,255,255,0.3);color:#fff;font-size:18px;width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:50%;
          cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-weight:700;
          transition:background 0.15s;flex-shrink:0;">✕</button>
      </div>

      <!-- ── filter bar ── -->
      <div style="background:#f8fafc;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;padding:10px 18px;
        display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;align-items:flex-end;flex-shrink:0;">

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
          <label style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Student Name</label>
          <input id="tmsh-name" placeholder="e.g. Michael Phiri"
            style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:7px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;
            width:155px;outline:none;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s;">
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
          <label style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Student ID</label>
          <input id="tmsh-id" placeholder="Exact student ID"
            style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:7px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;
            width:130px;outline:none;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s;">
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
          <label style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Subject</label>
          <input id="tmsh-subject" placeholder="e.g. Mathematics"
            style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:7px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;
            width:140px;outline:none;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s;">
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
          <label style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Term</label>
          <input id="tmsh-term" placeholder="e.g. 1"
