            style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:7px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;
            width:66px;outline:none;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s;">
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:3px;">
          <label style="font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Week</label>
          <input id="tmsh-week" placeholder="e.g. 3"
            style="border:1.5px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:7px;padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;
            width:66px;outline:none;font-family:inherit;transition:border-color .15s;">
        </div>

        <div style="display:flex;gap:6px;align-items:flex-end;">
          <button id="tmsh-search" style="padding:7px 16px;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#009960,#00cc88);
            color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
            height:34px;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,204,136,0.25);font-family:inherit;">
            🔍 Search
          </button>
          <button id="tmsh-clear" style="padding:7px 12px;background:#f1f5f9;color:#475569;border:1.5px solid
            #e2e8f0;border-radius:7px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;height:34px;font-family:inherit;">
            ✕ Clear
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- ── view-mode toggle ── -->
      <div style="background:#fff;border-bottom:1px solid #e8f5f0;padding:8px 18px;
        display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0;">
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">View:</span>
        <button id="tmsh-mode-sessions" style="padding:5px 14px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;
          font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;border:2px solid #00cc88;background:#00cc88;color:#fff;
          font-family:inherit;transition:all .15s;">By Session</button>
        <button id="tmsh-mode-student" style="padding:5px 14px;border-radius:20px;font-size:11px;
          font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;background:#fff;color:#64748b;
          font-family:inherit;transition:all .15s;">By Student</button>
        <span id="tmsh-record-count" style="margin-left:auto;font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-style:italic;"></span>
      </div>

      <!-- ── results ── -->
      <div id="tm-subhist-results" style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:14px 18px;background:#fafcff;"></div>
    </div>

    <style>
      @keyframes tmShimmer { 0%{background-position:-100% 0} 100%{background-position:200% 0} }
      @keyframes tmFadeIn  { from{opacity:0;transform:translateY(8px)} to{opacity:1;transform:translateY(0)} }
      #tmsh-name:focus,#tmsh-id:focus,#tmsh-subject:focus,#tmsh-term:focus,#tmsh-week:focus {
        border-color:#00cc88 !important;
        box-shadow:0 0 0 3px rgba(0,204,136,0.14);
      }
      .tmsh-roster-row:hover { background:rgba(0,204,136,0.05) !important; }
    </style>`;

  document.body.appendChild(modal);

  // ── close ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const closeModal = () => modal.remove();
  document.getElementById('tm-subhist-close').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
  modal.addEventListener('click', e => { if (e.target === modal) closeModal(); });

  // ── view mode state ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
  let viewMode = 'sessions'; // 'sessions' | 'student'
  const modeSessions = document.getElementById('tmsh-mode-sessions');
  const modeStudent  = document.getElementById('tmsh-mode-student');

  function setMode(m) {
    viewMode = m;
    if (m === 'sessions') {
      modeSessions.style.background='#00cc88'; modeSessions.style.color='#fff'; modeSessions.style.borderColor='#00cc88';
      modeStudent.style.background='#fff';    modeStudent.style.color='#64748b'; modeStudent.style.borderColor='#e2e8f0';
    } else {
      modeStudent.style.background='#00cc88'; modeStudent.style.color='#fff'; modeStudent.style.borderColor='#00cc88';
      modeSessions.style.background='#fff';   modeSessions.style.color='#64748b'; modeSessions.style.borderColor='#e2e8f0';
    }
    doSearch();
  }
  modeSessions.addEventListener('click', () => setMode('sessions'));
  modeStudent.addEventListener('click',  () => setMode('student'));

  // ── photo helper ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function photoHTML(stu) {
    if (stu && stu.photo && stu.photo.startsWith('data:image/')) {
      return `<img src="${esc(stu.photo)}"
        style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;object-fit:cover;
        border:2px solid #e2e8f0;flex-shrink:0;">`;
    }
    return `<div style="width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;background:linear-gradient(135deg,#d1fae5,#a7f3d0);
      display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;flex-shrink:0;font-size:13px;">👤</div>`;
  }

  // ── render a student roster (present + absent list) ───────────────────
  function buildRosterEl(rec) {
    const presentIds = rec.presentStudentIds || [];
    const absentIds  = rec.absentStudentIds  || [];
    const total      = rec.totalStudents || (presentIds.length + absentIds.length);
    const rate       = total > 0 ? Math.round((presentIds.length / total) * 100) : 0;
    const col        = rateColor(rate);

    const el = document.createElement('div');
    el.style.cssText = `
      margin-top:10px;border-radius:10px;overflow:hidden;
      border:1px solid #e8f5f0;animation:tmFadeIn .22s ease;`;

    // ── stats strip ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div style="background:${col.bg};padding:9px 14px;display:flex;align-items:center;
        gap:14px;flex-wrap:wrap;border-bottom:1px solid #e8f5f0;">
        <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;">
          <div style="width:80px;height:7px;background:#e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;overflow:hidden;">
            <div style="width:${rate}%;height:100%;background:${col.bar};border-radius:6px;"></div>
          </div>
          <span style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:${col.text};">${rate}%</span>
        </div>
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#059669;">
          ✅ Present: <strong>${presentIds.length}</strong>
        </span>
        <span style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#dc2626;">
          ❌ Absent: <strong>${absentIds.length}</strong>
        </span>
        <span style="font-size:11px;color:#64748b;">
          Total: <strong>${total}</strong>
        </span>
      </div>

      <!-- two columns: present | absent -->
      <div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;background:#fff;">

        <!-- PRESENT column -->
        <div style="border-right:1px solid #f0fdf4;padding:10px 12px;">
          <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#059669;text-transform:uppercase;
            letter-spacing:.07em;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;">
            <span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#22c55e;display:inline-block;"></span>
            Present (${presentIds.length})
          </div>
          <div id="roster-present-${rec.recordId}" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;">
            ${presentIds.length === 0
              ? `<span style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-style:italic;">No students present</span>`
              : presentIds.map(id => {
                  const stu = studentMap.get(normId(id));
                  return `
                    <div class="tmsh-roster-row" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;
                      padding:5px 7px;border-radius:7px;transition:background .12s;">
                      ${photoHTML(stu)}
                      <div style="min-width:0;">
                        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;
                          white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">
                          ${esc(stu ? stu.name : id)}
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;">ID: ${esc(id)}</div>
                      </div>
                      <span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:14px;flex-shrink:0;">✅</span>
                    </div>`;
                }).join('')
            }
          </div>
        </div>

        <!-- ABSENT column -->
        <div style="padding:10px 12px;">
          <div style="font-size:10px;font-weight:800;color:#dc2626;text-transform:uppercase;
            letter-spacing:.07em;margin-bottom:8px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:5px;">
            <span style="width:8px;height:8px;border-radius:50%;background:#ef4444;display:inline-block;"></span>
            Absent (${absentIds.length})
          </div>
          <div id="roster-absent-${rec.recordId}" style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:5px;">
            ${absentIds.length === 0
              ? `<span style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;font-style:italic;">No students absent</span>`
              : absentIds.map(id => {
                  const stu = studentMap.get(normId(id));
                  return `
                    <div class="tmsh-roster-row" style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;
                      padding:5px 7px;border-radius:7px;transition:background .12s;">
                      ${photoHTML(stu)}
                      <div style="min-width:0;">
                        <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;
                          white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">
                          ${esc(stu ? stu.name : id)}
                        </div>
                        <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;">ID: ${esc(id)}</div>
                      </div>
                      <span style="margin-left:auto;font-size:14px;flex-shrink:0;">❌</span>
                    </div>`;
                }).join('')
            }
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    return el;
  }

  // ── render a single session card ──────────────────────────────────────
  function buildSessionCard(rec) {
    const presentIds = rec.presentStudentIds || [];
    const absentIds  = rec.absentStudentIds  || [];
    const total      = rec.totalStudents || (presentIds.length + absentIds.length);
    const rate       = total > 0 ? Math.round((presentIds.length / total) * 100) : 0;
    const col        = rateColor(rate);

    const card = document.createElement('div');
    card.style.cssText = `
      background:#fff;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid #e8f5f0;
      margin-bottom:12px;overflow:hidden;
      box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,204,136,0.08);
      animation:tmFadeIn .2s ease;`;

    // Card header
    const header = document.createElement('div');
    header.style.cssText = `
      padding:11px 14px;display:flex;align-items:center;
      gap:12px;flex-wrap:wrap;cursor:pointer;
      background:linear-gradient(90deg,#f0fdf8,#fff);
      border-bottom:1px solid #e8f5f0;
      transition:background .15s;`;
    header.innerHTML = `
      <!-- Subject + teacher -->
      <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
        <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a;
          white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">
          📖 ${esc(rec.subject || 'Unknown Subject')}
        </div>
        <div style="font-size:11px;color:#64748b;margin-top:1px;">
          👨‍🏫 ${esc(rec.subjectTeacher || 'Unknown Teacher')}
          &nbsp;·&nbsp; 📅 ${esc(fmtDateLong(rec.date))}
          &nbsp;·&nbsp; Term ${esc(rec.term||'-')}, Wk ${esc(rec.week||'-')}
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Rate badge -->
      <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0;">
        <div style="text-align:center;background:${col.bg};border-radius:8px;
          padding:5px 10px;border:1px solid ${col.bar}22;">
          <div style="font-size:16px;font-weight:900;color:${col.text};">${rate}%</div>
          <div style="font-size:9px;font-weight:700;color:${col.text};text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Rate</div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:center;">
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#059669;">✅ ${presentIds.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:9px;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;">Present</div>
        </div>
        <div style="text-align:center;">
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:800;color:#dc2626;">❌ ${absentIds.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:9px;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.04em;">Absent</div>
        </div>

        <!-- Toggle + PDF -->
        <div style="display:flex;flex-direction:column;gap:4px;flex-shrink:0;">
          <button class="tm-roster-toggle" style="
            padding:4px 10px;background:#00cc88;color:#fff;border:none;
            border-radius:6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;
            font-family:inherit;letter-spacing:.03em;white-space:nowrap;
            transition:background .15s;">
            👁 View Roster
          </button>
          <button class="tm-pdf-btn" style="
            padding:4px 10px;background:#1e3a8a;color:#fff;border:none;
            border-radius:6px;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;cursor:pointer;
            font-family:inherit;letter-spacing:.03em;white-space:nowrap;
            transition:background .15s;">
            📄 PDF
          </button>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    card.appendChild(header);

    // Roster panel (collapsed by default)
    const rosterWrapper = document.createElement('div');
    rosterWrapper.style.display = 'none';
    const rosterEl = buildRosterEl(rec);
    rosterWrapper.appendChild(rosterEl);
    card.appendChild(rosterWrapper);

    // Optional AI insight panel (collapsed)
    const aiWrapper = document.createElement('div');
    aiWrapper.style.cssText = 'padding:0 12px 12px;display:none;';
    if (typeof computeSubjectInsight === 'function' && total >= 2) {
      const subRecs = [
        ...presentIds.map(() => ({ present: true  })),
        ...absentIds.map(()  => ({ present: false }))
      ];
      const insight = computeSubjectInsight(subRecs, `Class ${className}`, rec.subject);
      if (insight && typeof renderInsightPanel === 'function') {
        renderInsightPanel(aiWrapper, insight);
        aiWrapper.style.display = 'block';
      }
    }
    card.appendChild(aiWrapper);

    // ── toggle roster open/close ───────────────────────────────────────
    let rosterOpen = false;
    const toggleBtn = header.querySelector('.tm-roster-toggle');
    toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      rosterOpen = !rosterOpen;
      rosterWrapper.style.display = rosterOpen ? 'block' : 'none';
      toggleBtn.textContent = rosterOpen ? '🔼 Hide Roster' : '👁 View Roster';
      toggleBtn.style.background = rosterOpen ? '#059669' : '#00cc88';
    });

    // Click card header to also toggle
    header.addEventListener('click', e => {
      if (e.target.classList.contains('tm-pdf-btn') ||
          e.target.classList.contains('tm-roster-toggle')) return;
      rosterOpen = !rosterOpen;
      rosterWrapper.style.display = rosterOpen ? 'block' : 'none';
      toggleBtn.textContent = rosterOpen ? '🔼 Hide Roster' : '👁 View Roster';
      toggleBtn.style.background = rosterOpen ? '#059669' : '#00cc88';
    });

    // ── PDF button ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const pdfBtn = header.querySelector('.tm-pdf-btn');
    pdfBtn.addEventListener('click', e => {
      e.stopPropagation();
      if (typeof generateSubjectAttendancePDF !== 'function') return;
      const presents = (rec.presentStudentIds||[]).map(id => {
        const s = studentMap.get(normId(id));
        return s ? { id: s.id, name: s.name } : { id, name: id };
      });
      const absents = (rec.absentStudentIds||[]).map(id => {
        const s = studentMap.get(normId(id));
        return s ? { id: s.id, name: s.name } : { id, name: id };
      });
      generateSubjectAttendancePDF({
        className,
        subject:         rec.subject,
        subjectTeacher:  rec.subjectTeacher || '',
        term:            rec.term,
        week:            rec.week,
        date:            rec.date,
        presentStudents: presents,
        absentStudents:  absents,
        totalStudents:   rec.totalStudents || classStudents.length,
        schoolName:      rec.schoolName    || 'TEACHMATE SCHOOL'
      });
    });

    return card;
  }

  // ── render BY-STUDENT view ────────────────────────────────────────────
  // Groups all filtered records by student, shows each student's
  // lesson-by-lesson attendance table per subject.
  // nameQ / idQ are passed in so we only render the searched student(s).
  function renderStudentView(filtered, resultsEl, nameQ, idQ) {
    // Build: studentId → { name, photo, subjects: { subjectName → [{ date, present, teacher, term, week }] } }
    const studentData = {};

    filtered.forEach(rec => {
      const allIds = [
        ...(rec.presentStudentIds||[]).map(id => ({ id, present: true  })),
        ...(rec.absentStudentIds ||[]).map(id => ({ id, present: false }))
      ];
      allIds.forEach(({ id, present }) => {
        const sid = normId(id);

        // ── KEY FIX: if a name or ID filter is active, skip students who
        //    don't match — prevents showing all classmates in the same session
        if (idQ && sid !== idQ) return;
        if (nameQ) {
          const stu = studentMap.get(sid);
          if (!stu || !normText(stu.name).includes(nameQ)) return;
        }

        const stu = studentMap.get(sid);
        if (!studentData[sid]) {
          studentData[sid] = {
            id, name: stu ? stu.name : id, photo: stu ? stu.photo : null,
            subjects: {}
          };
        }
        const subj = rec.subject || 'Unknown';
        if (!studentData[sid].subjects[subj]) studentData[sid].subjects[subj] = [];
        studentData[sid].subjects[subj].push({
          date:    rec.date,
          present,
          teacher: rec.subjectTeacher || '-',
          term:    rec.term || '-',
          week:    rec.week || '-'
        });
      });
    });

    const entries = Object.values(studentData).sort((a,b) =>
      a.name.localeCompare(b.name)
    );

    if (!entries.length) {
      resultsEl.innerHTML = `<div style="text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;padding:40px;font-size:13px;">
        No records found.</div>`;
      return;
    }

    document.getElementById('tmsh-record-count').textContent =
      `${entries.length} student${entries.length!==1?'s':''} found`;

    entries.forEach(stu => {
      const card = document.createElement('div');
      card.style.cssText = `
        background:#fff;border-radius:12px;border:1px solid #e8f5f0;
        margin-bottom:14px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,204,136,0.08);
        animation:tmFadeIn .2s ease;`;

      // Student header
      const stuHeader = document.createElement('div');
      stuHeader.style.cssText = `
        padding:12px 14px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;
        background:linear-gradient(90deg,#f0fdf8,#fff);
        border-bottom:1px solid #e8f5f0;`;
      stuHeader.innerHTML = `
        ${photoHTML({ photo: stu.photo })}
        <div style="flex:1;min-width:0;">
          <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a;">${esc(stu.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#64748b;">
            ID: ${esc(stu.id)}
            &nbsp;·&nbsp; ${Object.keys(stu.subjects).length} subject${Object.keys(stu.subjects).length!==1?'s':''}
          </div>
        </div>`;
      card.appendChild(stuHeader);

      // Per-subject lesson tables
      Object.entries(stu.subjects).forEach(([subj, lessons]) => {
        const presents = lessons.filter(l => l.present).length;
        const total    = lessons.length;
        const rate     = Math.round((presents / total) * 100);
        const col      = rateColor(rate);

        const subjBlock = document.createElement('div');
        subjBlock.style.cssText = 'padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;';

        // Subject sub-header
        subjBlock.innerHTML = `
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;
            margin-bottom:8px;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px;">
            <div style="font-size:12px;font-weight:800;color:#059669;
              display:flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;">
              📖 ${esc(subj)}
            </div>
            <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;">
              <div style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:${col.text};
                background:${col.bg};padding:3px 10px;border-radius:20px;
                border:1px solid ${col.bar}33;">
                ${rate}% attendance
              </div>
              <span style="font-size:11px;color:#059669;font-weight:700;">✅ ${presents}/${total}</span>
              <span style="font-size:11px;color:#dc2626;font-weight:700;">❌ ${total-presents}/${total}</span>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- Progress bar -->
          <div style="height:5px;background:#e2e8f0;border-radius:4px;overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:10px;">
            <div style="width:${rate}%;height:100%;background:${col.bar};border-radius:4px;"></div>
          </div>

          <!-- Lesson-by-lesson table -->
          <div style="overflow-x:auto;border-radius:8px;border:1px solid #e8f5f0;">
            <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11px;">
              <thead>
                <tr style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#009960,#00cc88);">
                  <th style="padding:7px 10px;color:#fff;font-weight:700;text-align:left;
                    font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;white-space:nowrap;">Date</th>
                  <th style="padding:7px 10px;color:#fff;font-weight:700;text-align:left;
                    font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;white-space:nowrap;">Term</th>
                  <th style="padding:7px 10px;color:#fff;font-weight:700;text-align:left;
                    font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;white-space:nowrap;">Week</th>
                  <th style="padding:7px 10px;color:#fff;font-weight:700;text-align:left;
                    font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;white-space:nowrap;">Teacher</th>
                  <th style="padding:7px 10px;color:#fff;font-weight:700;text-align:center;
                    font-size:10px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>
                ${lessons.sort((a,b)=>a.date<b.date?-1:1).map((l,i)=>`
                  <tr style="background:${i%2===0?'#fff':'#f9fffe'};">
                    <td style="padding:6px 10px;color:#0f172a;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;
                      border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;">${esc(fmtDateLong(l.date))}</td>
                    <td style="padding:6px 10px;color:#475569;border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;">${esc(l.term)}</td>
                    <td style="padding:6px 10px;color:#475569;border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;">${esc(l.week)}</td>
                    <td style="padding:6px 10px;color:#475569;border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;
                      white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;max-width:120px;">
                      ${esc(l.teacher)}
                    </td>
                    <td style="padding:6px 10px;text-align:center;border-bottom:1px solid #f0fdf8;">
                      ${l.present
                        ? `<span style="background:#dcfce7;color:#166534;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;
                            padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px;white-space:nowrap;">✅ Present</span>`
                        : `<span style="background:#fee2e2;color:#991b1b;font-size:10px;font-weight:800;
                            padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px;white-space:nowrap;">❌ Absent</span>`
                      }
                    </td>
                  </tr>`).join('')}
              </tbody>
            </table>
          </div>`;

        card.appendChild(subjBlock);
      });

      resultsEl.appendChild(card);
    });
  }

  // ── render BY-SESSION view ────────────────────────────────────────────
  function renderSessionView(filtered, resultsEl) {
    // Group by date descending
    const groups = {};
    filtered.forEach(rec => {
      const d = rec.date || 'Unknown';
      if (!groups[d]) groups[d] = [];
      groups[d].push(rec);
    });
    const sortedDates = Object.keys(groups).sort((a,b) => b.localeCompare(a));

    let totalSessions = filtered.length;
    document.getElementById('tmsh-record-count').textContent =
      `${totalSessions} session${totalSessions!==1?'s':''} found`;

    sortedDates.forEach(dateKey => {
      // Date group heading
      const heading = document.createElement('div');
      heading.style.cssText = `
        display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;margin:14px 0 8px;`;
      heading.innerHTML = `
        <div style="flex:1;height:1px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#00cc88,transparent);"></div>
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#009960,#00cc88);color:#fff;
          font-size:12px;font-weight:800;padding:5px 14px;border-radius:20px;
          white-space:nowrap;box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(0,204,136,0.25);">
          📅 ${esc(fmtDateLong(dateKey))}
          <span style="opacity:.75;font-weight:600;"> · ${groups[dateKey].length} session${groups[dateKey].length!==1?'s':''}</span>
        </div>
        <div style="flex:1;height:1px;background:linear-gradient(90deg,transparent,#00cc88);"></div>`;
      resultsEl.appendChild(heading);

      // Session cards for this date
      groups[dateKey].forEach(rec => {
        resultsEl.appendChild(buildSessionCard(rec));
      });
    });
  }

  // ── main search/filter + render ───────────────────────────────────────
  function doSearch() {
    const nameQ    = normText(document.getElementById('tmsh-name').value);
    const idQ      = normId  (document.getElementById('tmsh-id').value);
    const subjectQ = normText(document.getElementById('tmsh-subject').value);
    const termQ    = normText(document.getElementById('tmsh-term').value);
    const weekQ    = normText(document.getElementById('tmsh-week').value);

    const resultsEl = document.getElementById('tm-subhist-results');
    resultsEl.innerHTML = '';

    // ── filter records ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let filtered = subjectRecs.filter(rec => {
      if (subjectQ && !normText(rec.subject||'').includes(subjectQ)) return false;
      if (termQ    && normText(rec.term||'')    !== termQ)           return false;
      if (weekQ    && normText(rec.week||'')    !== weekQ)           return false;

      if (idQ || nameQ) {
        const allIds = [
          ...(rec.presentStudentIds||[]),
          ...(rec.absentStudentIds ||[])
        ];
        if (idQ && !allIds.some(id => normId(id) === idQ)) return false;
        if (nameQ) {
          const matched = allIds.some(id => {
            const stu = studentMap.get(normId(id));
            return stu && normText(stu.name).includes(nameQ);
          });
          if (!matched) return false;
        }
      }
      return true;
    });

    // ── empty state ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if (!filtered.length) {
      resultsEl.innerHTML = `
        <div style="text-align:center;padding:50px 20px;">
          <div style="font-size:36px;margin-bottom:12px;">📭</div>
          <div style="font-size:14px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;">No subject attendance records found</div>
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:4px;">
            Try adjusting your search filters above
          </div>
        </div>`;
      document.getElementById('tmsh-record-count').textContent = '0 records';
      return;
    }

    // ── student-specific view (original preserved + enhanced) ───────────
    if ((idQ || nameQ) && viewMode !== 'student') {
      // Auto-switch to student view when searching by name/ID
      renderStudentView(filtered, resultsEl, nameQ, idQ);
      return;
    }

    if (viewMode === 'student') {
      renderStudentView(filtered, resultsEl, nameQ, idQ);
    } else {
      renderSessionView(filtered, resultsEl);
    }
  }

  // ── wire events ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  document.getElementById('tmsh-search').addEventListener('click', doSearch);
  document.getElementById('tmsh-clear').addEventListener('click', () => {
    ['tmsh-name','tmsh-id','tmsh-subject','tmsh-term','tmsh-week'].forEach(id => {
      const el = document.getElementById(id); if (el) el.value = '';
    });
    doSearch();
  });

  // Live search with debounce (ORIGINAL — preserved)
  ['tmsh-name','tmsh-id','tmsh-subject','tmsh-term','tmsh-week'].forEach(id => {
    document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('input', () => {
      clearTimeout(window._tmshDebounce);
      window._tmshDebounce = setTimeout(doSearch, 400);
    });
  });

  // Initial render
  doSearch();
};







  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  SUBJECT ATTENDANCE MODAL v3  ████████████████████████████████████████
  //     Full student list with Present/Absent toggles
  //     Auto term/week attach with real-date validation
  //     Draft restore, PDF export button per committed record
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  window.openSubjectAttendanceModal = async function () {
    const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
    if (!classEl || !classEl.value) return showToast('⚠️ Please select a class first','error');
    const className = classEl.value.trim();

    // ── Auto term/week attach with real-date validation ───────────────────
    const termElGen = document.getElementById('attendance-term');
    const weekElGen = document.getElementById('attendance-week');
    let term = '', week = '', termWeekSource = 'manual';

    const genTerm = termElGen ? termElGen.value.trim() : '';
    const genWeek = weekElGen ? weekElGen.value.trim() : '';

    // Check if the general form's term/week was entered today
    // We store a metadata key each time saveAttendance runs or the user sets them
    let termWeekMeta = null;
    try { termWeekMeta = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tm_termweek_meta')||'null'); } catch {}

    const enteredToday = termWeekMeta &&
      termWeekMeta.date === today &&
      termWeekMeta.classId === className &&
      genTerm && genWeek;

    if (enteredToday) {
      term = genTerm; week = genWeek; termWeekSource = 'auto';
    }

    // Load students
    let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
    if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];
    const classStudents = students.filter(s => s.classId === className);
    const totalStudents = classStudents.length;

    // Check already committed today
    let allAttendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
    if (!Array.isArray(allAttendance)) allAttendance = [];

    // Load draft
    const existingDraft = await loadDraft(className);
    const draftData = (existingDraft && existingDraft.subjectRows) ? existingDraft : null;

    document.getElementById('tm-subject-att-modal')?.remove();

    const modal = document.createElement('div');
    modal.id = 'tm-subject-att-modal';
    modal.style.cssText = `
      position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99998;
      background:rgba(15,23,42,0.82);
      display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
      padding:12px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
      backdrop-filter:blur(3px);
    `;

    const autoAttachBadge = termWeekSource === 'auto'
      ? `<span style="background:#d1fae5;color:#065f46;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;
          padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;margin-left:8px;">✅ Auto-attached from today's entry</span>`
      : `<span style="background:#fef3c7;color:#92400e;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;
          padding:2px 8px;border-radius:10px;margin-left:8px;">⚠️ Enter term &amp; week manually</span>`;

    modal.innerHTML = `
      <div style="background:#fff;border-radius:14px;width:100%;max-width:780px;
        max-height:94vh;overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
        box-shadow:0 30px 70px rgba(0,0,0,.35);">


        <!-- header -->
        <div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#00cc88,#00cc88);padding:18px 22px;
          display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;">
          <div>


            <div style="color:#fff;font-size:16px;font-weight:800;">📋 Subject Attendance Entry</div>
            <div style="color:#00cc88;font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;">
              Class: <strong style="color:#fff">${esc(className)}</strong> &nbsp;|&nbsp; ${fmtDate(today)}
            </div>
          </div>
          <button id="tm-subatt-close" style="background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);border:none;
            color:#0f601;font-size:20px;width:45px;height:45px;border-radius:50%;cursor:pointer;">✕</button>
        </div>

        <!-- meta bar: term/week + school name -->
        <div style="background:#eff6ff;border-bottom:1px solid #bfdbfe;padding:10px 22px;
          display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:12px;align-items:center;flex-shrink:0;">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
            <label style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#00cc88;">Term</label>
            <input id="tm-subatt-term" value="${esc(term)}" placeholder="e.g. 1"
              ${termWeekSource==='auto'?'readonly style="background:#d1fae5;':'style="background:#fff;'}
              border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:12px;width:60px;outline:none;">
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
            <label style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#00cc88;">Week</label>
            <input id="tm-subatt-week" value="${esc(week)}" placeholder="e.g. 3"
              ${termWeekSource==='auto'?'readonly style="background:#d1fae5;':'style="background:#fff;'}
              border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:12px;width:60px;outline:none;">
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
            <label style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#00cc88;">Subject</label>
            <input id="tm-subatt-subject" placeholder="e.g. Mathematics"
              style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:12px;width:140px;outline:none;">
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
            <label style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#00cc88;">Teacher</label>
            <input id="tm-subatt-teacher" placeholder="Subject teacher name"
              style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:12px;width:150px;outline:none;">
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
            <label style="font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#00cc88;">School</label>
            <input id="tm-subatt-school" placeholder="School name"
              style="background:#fff;border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 8px;font-size:12px;width:140px;outline:none;">
          </div>
          ${autoAttachBadge}
        </div>

        <!-- info + search strip -->
        <div style="background:#f8fafc;border-bottom:1px solid #e2e8f0;padding:8px 22px;
          display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:8px;flex-shrink:0;">
          <div style="font-size:12px;color:#1e40af;font-weight:600;">
            👥 <strong>${totalStudents}</strong> student${totalStudents!==1?'s':''} in ${esc(className)}
            &nbsp;|&nbsp; <span id="tm-subatt-count" style="color:#059669;font-weight:700;">0 marked present</span>
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center;">
            <input id="tm-subatt-search" placeholder="🔍 Search student..." style="
              border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:6px;padding:5px 10px;font-size:12px;
              width:180px;outline:none;">
            <button id="tm-subatt-markall-present" style="
              background:#15803d;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:6px;
              padding:5px 12px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">✅ All Present</button>
            <button id="tm-subatt-markall-absent" style="
              background:#b91c1c;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:6px;
              padding:5px 12px;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">❌ All Absent</button>
          </div>
          <span id="tm-subatt-draft-status" style="font-size:11px;color:#059669;font-style:italic;">
            ${draftData ? `✅ Draft restored (${draftData.subjectRows?.length||0} previous entries)` : ''}
          </span>
        </div>

        <!-- student list -->
        <div id="tm-subatt-student-list" style="flex:1;overflow-y:auto;padding:12px 22px;"></div>

        <!-- footer -->
        <div style="padding:12px 22px;border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0;display:flex;
          justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;background:#f8fafc;
          border-radius:0 0 14px 14px;flex-shrink:0;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:8px;">
          <div style="font-size:11px;color:#dc2626;font-weight:700;">
            💾 Attendance is auto-saved as you mark — data will NOT be lost
          </div>
          <div style="display:flex;gap:10px;">
            <button id="tm-subatt-cancel" style="padding:9px 18px;border:2px solid #cbd5e1;
              background:#fff;color:#475569;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">
              Cancel
            </button>
            <button id="tm-subatt-pdf-only" style="padding:9px 18px;border:none;
              background:#7c3aed;color:#fff;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;">
              📄 Save &amp; Export PDF
            </button>
            <button id="tm-subatt-commit" style="padding:9px 22px;border:none;background:#15803d;
              color:#fff;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
              box-shadow:0 2px 8px rgba(21,128,61,.35);">
              ✅ Commit &amp; Save Day
            </button>
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    document.body.appendChild(modal);

    // ── student attendance state map  id → 'present'|'absent'|null ────────
    const attState = {};
    classStudents.forEach(s => { attState[normId(s.id)] = null; });

    // Restore from draft if available
    if (draftData && draftData.subjectRows) {
      draftData.subjectRows.forEach(row => {
        if (row.studentId) attState[normId(row.studentId)] = row.present ? 'present' : 'absent';
      });
      // Also restore subject/teacher/term/week
      if (draftData.subject)  document.getElementById('tm-subatt-subject').value = draftData.subject;
      if (draftData.teacher)  document.getElementById('tm-subatt-teacher').value = draftData.teacher;
      if (draftData.school)   document.getElementById('tm-subatt-school').value  = draftData.school;
      if (draftData.term && termWeekSource !== 'auto') {
        document.getElementById('tm-subatt-term').value = draftData.term;
        document.getElementById('tm-subatt-week').value = draftData.week || '';
      }
    }

    const listEl   = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-student-list');
    const countEl  = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-count');

    function updateCount() {
      const n = Object.values(attState).filter(v=>v==='present').length;
      countEl.textContent = `${n} marked present`;
    }

    function buildDraftRowsForSave() {
      const t = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-term').value.trim();
      const w = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-week').value.trim();
      const s = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-subject').value.trim();
      const tc = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-teacher').value.trim();
      const sc = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-school').value.trim();
      return { rows: classStudents.map(stu => ({
        studentId: stu.id, present: attState[normId(stu.id)] === 'present'
      })), term:t, week:w, subject:s, teacher:tc, school:sc };
    }

    async function autosave() {
      const { rows, term:t, week:w, subject:s, teacher:tc, school:sc } = buildDraftRowsForSave();
      const key = draftKey(className, today);
      await setIndexedDB(DRAFT_STORE, key, {
        key, classId:className, date:today,
        subjectRows:rows, term:t, week:w, subject:s, teacher:tc, school:sc,
        savedAt:new Date().toISOString()
      });
      const ds = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-draft-status');
      if(ds) ds.textContent = `💾 Auto-saved ${new Date().toLocaleTimeString()}`;
    }

    // ── render student rows ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function renderStudentList(filter='') {
      listEl.innerHTML = '';
      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      const filtered = classStudents.filter(s =>
        !filter || normText(s.name).includes(normText(filter)) || normText(s.id).includes(normText(filter))
      );
      if (!filtered.length) {
        listEl.innerHTML = `<p style="text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;padding:20px;">No students match your search.</p>`;
        return;
      }
      filtered.forEach(stu => {
        const sid   = normId(stu.id);
        const state = attState[sid];

        const row = document.createElement('div');
        row.style.cssText = `
          display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 10px;
          border-radius:8px;margin-bottom:5px;transition:background .15s;
          background:${state==='present'?'#f0fdf4':state==='absent'?'#fef2f2':'#ffffff'};
          border:1px solid ${state==='present'?'#86efac':state==='absent'?'#fca5a5':'#e2e8f0'};
        `;

        // Photo
        const photoEl = document.createElement('div');
        photoEl.style.cssText = 'width:34px;height:34px;border-radius:50%;overflow:hidden;flex-shrink:0;background:#e2e8f0;';
        if (stu.photo && stu.photo.startsWith('data:image/')) {
          photoEl.innerHTML = `<img src="${esc(stu.photo)}" style="width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:cover;">`;
        } else {
          photoEl.innerHTML = `<span style="display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;height:100%;font-size:14px;">👤</span>`;
        }

        // Name + ID
        const info = document.createElement('div');
        info.style.cssText = 'flex:1;min-width:0;';
        info.innerHTML = `
          <div style="font-size:13px;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">${esc(stu.name)}</div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;">ID: ${esc(stu.id)}</div>`;

        // Buttons
        const btnWrap = document.createElement('div');
        btnWrap.style.cssText = 'display:flex;gap:6px;flex-shrink:0;';

        const pBtn = document.createElement('button');
        pBtn.textContent = '✅ Present';
        pBtn.style.cssText = `
          padding:5px 12px;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
          border:2px solid ${state==='present'?'#15803d':'#d1d5db'};
          background:${state==='present'?'#15803d':'#fff'};
          color:${state==='present'?'#fff':'#374151'};`;

        const aBtn = document.createElement('button');
        aBtn.textContent = '❌ Absent';
        aBtn.style.cssText = `
          padding:5px 12px;border-radius:6px;font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
          border:2px solid ${state==='absent'?'#b91c1c':'#d1d5db'};
          background:${state==='absent'?'#b91c1c':'#fff'};
          color:${state==='absent'?'#fff':'#374151'};`;

        pBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          attState[sid] = 'present';
          renderStudentList(document.getElementById('tm-subatt-search').value);
          updateCount(); autosave();
        });
        aBtn.addEventListener('click', () => {
          attState[sid] = 'absent';
          renderStudentList(document.getElementById('tm-subatt-search').value);
          updateCount(); autosave();
        });

        btnWrap.appendChild(pBtn);
        btnWrap.appendChild(aBtn);
        row.appendChild(photoEl);
        row.appendChild(info);
        row.appendChild(btnWrap);
        frag.appendChild(row);
      });
      listEl.appendChild(frag);
    }

    renderStudentList();
    updateCount();

    // Search
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-search').addEventListener('input', e => {
      renderStudentList(e.target.value);
    });

    // Mark all present/absent
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-markall-present').addEventListener('click', () => {
      classStudents.forEach(s => { attState[normId(s.id)] = 'present'; });
      renderStudentList(document.getElementById('tm-subatt-search').value);
      updateCount(); autosave();
    });
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-markall-absent').addEventListener('click', () => {
      classStudents.forEach(s => { attState[normId(s.id)] = 'absent'; });
      renderStudentList(document.getElementById('tm-subatt-search').value);
      updateCount(); autosave();
    });

    // Auto-save on meta input changes
    ['tm-subatt-subject','tm-subatt-teacher','tm-subatt-school','tm-subatt-term','tm-subatt-week'].forEach(id=>{
      document.getElementById(id)?.addEventListener('input', autosave);
    });

    // ── Collect commit data ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function getCommitData() {
      const t   = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-term').value.trim();
      const w   = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-week').value.trim();
      const s   = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-subject').value.trim();
      const tc  = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-teacher').value.trim();
      const sc  = document.getElementById('tm-subatt-school').value.trim() || 'TEACHMATE SCHOOL';
      if (!t || !w) { showToast('⚠️ Please enter Term and Week','error'); return null; }
      if (!s)       { showToast('⚠️ Please enter the subject name','error'); return null; }
      if (!tc)      { showToast('⚠️ Please enter the subject teacher name','error'); return null; }
      const unmarked = classStudents.filter(stu => attState[normId(stu.id)] === null);
      if (unmarked.length > 0) {
        const go = confirm(`${unmarked.length} student${unmarked.length>1?'s are':' is'} not yet marked.\n\nUnmarked students will be saved as Absent. Continue?`);
        if (!go) return null;
        unmarked.forEach(stu => { attState[normId(stu.id)] = 'absent'; });
      }
      const presentStudents = classStudents.filter(stu => attState[normId(stu.id)] === 'present');
      const absentStudents  = classStudents.filter(stu => attState[normId(stu.id)] === 'absent');
      return { t, w, s, tc, sc, presentStudents, absentStudents };
    }

    // ── COMMIT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-commit').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const d = getCommitData(); if (!d) return;
      const { t, w, s, tc, sc, presentStudents, absentStudents } = d;

      const confirmed = confirm(
        `Save subject attendance for ${s} in ${className}?\n\n` +
        `Present: ${presentStudents.length}  |  Absent: ${absentStudents.length}  |  Total: ${classStudents.length}\n\n` +
        `Term ${t}, Week ${w}  —  ${fmtDate(today)}\n\nContinue?`
      );
      if (!confirmed) return;

      try {
     window.showLoading('Committing subject attendance...');
        const now  = new Date().toISOString();
        const recId = `${today}-${normId(className)}-subj-${normId(s)}-${now.slice(0,19).replace(/:/g,'')}`;
        const rec = {
          recordId:           recId,
          date:               today,
          classId:            className,
          subject:            s,
          subjectTeacher:     tc,
          schoolName:         sc,
          presentCount:       presentStudents.length,
          absentCount:        absentStudents.length,
          totalStudents:      classStudents.length,
          presentStudentIds:  presentStudents.map(stu=>stu.id),
          absentStudentIds:   absentStudents.map(stu=>stu.id),
          term:               t,
          week:               w,
          type:               'subject-attendance',
          _committedAt:       now,
          updatedAt:          now
        };
        await setIndexedDB(STORE, recId, rec);
        await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${recId}`, 'set', rec);
        await clearDraft(className);
        triggerSync();
        modal.remove();
        showToast(`✅ Subject attendance committed — ${s}, ${className}`, 'success');
        await loadAttendanceStudents();
      } catch (e) {
        console.error('Commit error', e);
        showToast('⚠️ Failed to commit. Data is safe in draft.', 'error');
      } finally {window. hideLoading(); }
    });

    // ── SAVE & PDF ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-pdf-only').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const d = getCommitData(); if (!d) return;
      const { t, w, s, tc, sc, presentStudents, absentStudents } = d;

      // First commit to store
      try {
     window.Loading('Saving and generating PDF...');
        const now   = new Date().toISOString();
        const recId = `${today}-${normId(className)}-subj-${normId(s)}-pdf-${now.slice(0,19).replace(/:/g,'')}`;
        const rec = {
          recordId: recId, date: today, classId: className,
          subject: s, subjectTeacher: tc, schoolName: sc,
          presentCount: presentStudents.length, absentCount: absentStudents.length,
          totalStudents: classStudents.length,
          presentStudentIds: presentStudents.map(stu=>stu.id),
          absentStudentIds:  absentStudents.map(stu=>stu.id),
          term: t, week: w, type: 'subject-attendance',
          _committedAt: now, updatedAt: now
        };
        await setIndexedDB(STORE, recId, rec);
        await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${recId}`, 'set', rec);
        await clearDraft(className);
        triggerSync();
        window.hideLoading();
        modal.remove();

        // Now generate PDF
        await generateSubjectAttendancePDF({
          className, subject:s, subjectTeacher:tc, term:t, week:w, date:today,
          presentStudents: presentStudents.map(stu=>({id:stu.id,name:stu.name})),
          absentStudents:  absentStudents.map(stu=>({id:stu.id,name:stu.name})),
          totalStudents: classStudents.length, schoolName: sc
        });
        await loadAttendanceStudents();
      } catch (e) {
        hideLoading();
        console.error('Save+PDF error', e);
        showToast('⚠️ Error: ' + (e.message||'Unknown'), 'error');
      }
    });

    // Close / Cancel — draft preserved
    const closeModal = () => { autosave(); modal.remove(); };
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-close').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    document.getElementById('tm-subatt-cancel').addEventListener('click', closeModal);
    modal.addEventListener('click', e => { if(e.target===modal) closeModal(); });
  };

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  ENHANCED: loadAttendanceStudents (v2 preserved + startup alerts)  ██
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  window.loadAttendanceStudents = async function() {
    const tbody   = document.querySelector('#attendance-table tbody');
    const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
    if (!tbody || !classEl) return;
    tbody.innerHTML = '';

    const className = classEl.value;
    if (!className) return;

    try {
     window.showLoading('Loading students...');
      let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
      if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];
      let attendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
      if (!Array.isArray(attendance)) attendance = [];

      const now = new Date().getTime();
      const todayAttendance = attendance.filter(a => a.date === today && a.classId === className);
      const todayMap = new Map();
      todayAttendance.forEach(rec => {
        const recTime = new Date(rec.updatedAt).getTime();
        if (now - recTime < 3600000) todayMap.set(normId(rec.studentId), !!rec.present);
      });

      const frag = document.createDocumentFragment();
      const classStudents = students.filter(s => s.classId === className);

      // Pending draft banner
      const pending = await hasPendingDraft(className);
      if (pending) {
        const banner = document.createElement('tr');
        banner.innerHTML = `
          <td colspan="4" style="background:#fffbeb;border:2px solid #fcd34d;border-radius:8px;
            padding:10px 14px;text-align:center;font-size:12px;color:#92400e;font-weight:700;">
            ⚠️ You have an <strong>unsaved subject attendance draft</strong> for today.
            <button onclick="window.openSubjectAttendanceModal()"
              style="margin-left:10px;background:#b45309;color:#fff;border:none;border-radius:5px;
              padding:3px 10px;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;font-weight:700;">Resume &amp; Commit →</button>
          </td>`;
        frag.appendChild(banner);
      }

      classStudents.forEach(s => {
        const sanitized = normId(s.id);
        const att       = todayMap.get(sanitized);
        const tr        = document.createElement('tr');

        const tdId    = document.createElement('td'); tdId.textContent = esc(s.id);
        const tdPhoto = document.createElement('td');
        if (s.photo) {
          const img = document.createElement('img');
          img.src = s.photo; img.alt = `${s.name}'s photo`;
          img.className = 'student-photo zoomable';
          img.dataset.src = s.photo; img.dataset.zoomable = true;
          tdPhoto.appendChild(img);
        } else { tdPhoto.innerHTML = '<span class="no-data">No photo</span>'; }

        const tdName = document.createElement('td'); tdName.textContent = esc(s.name);
        const tdStatus = document.createElement('td');

        const presentBtn = document.createElement('button');
        presentBtn.className = `status-btn present-btn ${att===true?'active':''}`;
        presentBtn.dataset.id = s.id; presentBtn.dataset.status = 'present';
        presentBtn.textContent = 'Present ✅';

        const absentBtn = document.createElement('button');
        absentBtn.className = `status-btn absent-btn ${att===false?'active':''}`;
        absentBtn.dataset.id = s.id; absentBtn.dataset.status = 'absent';
        absentBtn.textContent = 'Absent ❌';

        tdStatus.appendChild(presentBtn); tdStatus.appendChild(absentBtn);
        if (att === undefined) tr.style.backgroundColor = '#fffbe6';
        tr.appendChild(tdId); tr.appendChild(tdPhoto);
        tr.appendChild(tdName); tr.appendChild(tdStatus);
        frag.appendChild(tr);
      });

      tbody.appendChild(frag);

      document.querySelectorAll('#attendance-table .status-btn').forEach(btn => {
        btn.addEventListener('click', function () {
          const id = this.dataset.id;
          document.querySelectorAll(`#attendance-table [data-id="${CSS.escape(id)}"]`)
            .forEach(b => b.classList.remove('active'));
          this.classList.add('active');
          const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveAttendanceBtn') || document.getElementById('save-attendance-btn');
          if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = document.querySelectorAll('#attendance-table .status-btn.active').length === 0;
        });
      });

      const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveAttendanceBtn') || document.getElementById('save-attendance-btn');
      if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = document.querySelectorAll('#attendance-table .status-btn.active').length === 0;

      // ── Startup AI alerts ────────────────────────────────────────────────
      runStartupAlerts(className);

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('loadAttendanceStudents failed', err);
      alert('⚠️ Failed to load students for attendance. See console.');
    } finally { window.hideLoading(); }
  };



  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  ENHANCED: saveAttendance — stores term/week metadata for auto-attach
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  window.saveAttendance = async function () {
    if (typeof _tmBlockWrite === 'function' && _tmBlockWrite('Saving attendance')) return;

    const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
    if (!classEl) return alert('Select a class');
    const className = classEl.value;
    if (!className) return alert('Select a class');

    const termEl = document.getElementById('attendance-term');
    const weekEl = document.getElementById('attendance-week');
    const term   = termEl ? termEl.value.trim() : '';
    const week   = weekEl ? weekEl.value.trim() : '';
    if (!term || !week) return alert('Please enter the term and week before saving attendance.');

    // Pending draft guard
    const pending = await hasPendingDraft(className);
    if (pending) {
      const go = confirm(`⚠️ You have an unsaved subject attendance draft for today.\n\nYou must commit it before saving overall attendance.\n\nOpen the Subject Attendance form now?`);
      if (go) window.openSubjectAttendanceModal();
      return;
    }

    const activeBtns = document.querySelectorAll('#attendance-table .status-btn.active');
    if (!activeBtns.length) return alert('No attendance marked');

    try {
      // 🐛 FIX: Changed "showloading" to "showLoading" (CamelCase)
      if (typeof window.showLoading === 'function') {
        window.showLoading('Saving attendance...');
      }

      const now = new Date().toISOString();
      // Ensure 'today' is defined. If it's a global variable, this acts as a safe fallback.
      const currentDate = typeof today !== 'undefined' ? today : new Date().toISOString().split('T')[0];

      for (const btn of activeBtns) {
        const rawStudentId = btn.dataset.id;
        const studentId    = String(rawStudentId ?? '').trim();
        const present      = btn.dataset.status === 'present';
        
        // Ensure normId exists to prevent another TypeError
        const safeClassId = typeof normId === 'function' ? normId(className) : className;
        const safeStudentId = typeof normId === 'function' ? normId(studentId) : studentId;
        
        const attId        = `${currentDate}-${safeClassId}-${safeStudentId}`;
        const rec = { recordId:attId, date:currentDate, classId:className, studentId, present:!!present, term, week, updatedAt:now };
        
        await setIndexedDB(STORE, attId, rec);
        await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${attId}`, 'set', rec);
      }

      // ── Store term/week metadata so subject modal can auto-attach it ───
      try {
        localStorage.setItem('tm_termweek_meta', JSON.stringify({ date: currentDate, classId:className, term, week }));
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Could not save metadata to localStorage', e);
      }

      // Trigger your Sync Engine
      if (typeof triggerSync === 'function') triggerSync();
      
      alert('✅ Attendance saved successfully');
      
      if (typeof loadAttendanceStudents === 'function') {
        await loadAttendanceStudents();
      }
      
    } catch (err) {
      // Log the exact message so you can see if it's something else!
      console.error('saveAttendance failed. Exact error message:', err.message);
      alert(`⚠️ Failed to save attendance: ${err.message}`);
    } finally {
      if (typeof window.hideLoading === 'function') {
        window.hideLoading(); 
      }
    }
  };



  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  ENHANCED: viewAttendanceHistory — date-grouped, separated  ██████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  window.viewAttendanceHistory = async function () {
    const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
    if (!classEl) return alert('Select a class');
    const className = classEl.value;
    if (!className) return alert('Select a class');

    const studentIdInput = (document.getElementById('history-student-id')?.value || '').trim();
    const termInput      = (document.getElementById('history-term')?.value || '').trim();
    const weekInput      = (document.getElementById('history-week')?.value || '').trim();

    const historyDiv = document.getElementById('attendance-history');
    if (!historyDiv) return alert('Attendance history container not found');
    historyDiv.innerHTML = ''; historyDiv.style.display = 'block';

    try {
    window. showLoading('Loading attendance history...');
      let attendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
      if (!Array.isArray(attendance)) attendance = [];
      let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
      if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];
      const studentMap = new Map(students.map(s=>[normId(s.id),s]));

      // Filter to per-student records only (no subject-summary/subject-attendance)
      const generalRecs = attendance.filter(rec => {
        if (!rec) return false;
        if (rec.classId !== className) return false;
        if (rec.type === 'subject-summary' || rec.type === 'subject-attendance') return false;
        if (studentIdInput && normId(rec.studentId) !== normId(studentIdInput)) return false;
        if (termInput  && normText(rec.term)  !== normText(termInput))  return false;
        if (weekInput  && normText(rec.week)  !== normText(weekInput))  return false;
        return true;
      });

      if (!generalRecs.length) {
        historyDiv.innerHTML = '<p>No general attendance records found.</p>';
        return;
      }

      // Performance summary + AI (single student view)
      if (studentIdInput && termInput && weekInput) {
        const presents   = generalRecs.filter(r=>r.present).length;
        const absents    = generalRecs.length - presents;
        const total      = generalRecs.length;
        const percentage = total>0 ? (presents/total*100).toFixed(2) : 0;
        const stu  = studentMap.get(normId(studentIdInput));
        const name = stu ? stu.name : 'Unknown';

        const summaryP = document.createElement('p');
        summaryP.innerHTML = `Attendance performance for ${esc(name)} (ID: ${esc(studentIdInput)}) in Term ${esc(termInput)}, Week ${esc(weekInput)}: <br>Present: ${presents}, Absent: ${absents}, Total: ${total}, Percentage: ${percentage}%`;
        historyDiv.appendChild(summaryP);

        if (total > 0) {
          const insightPanel = document.createElement('div');
          insightPanel.id = 'tm-ai-insight-panel';
          renderInsightPanel(insightPanel, computeAttendanceInsight(presents, total, name));
          historyDiv.appendChild(insightPanel);
        }

        // D3 chart (original — preserved)
        const chartDiv = document.createElement('div');
        chartDiv.id = 'attendance-chart';
        chartDiv.style.cssText = 'width:100%;max-width:400px;margin:20px auto;';
        historyDiv.appendChild(chartDiv);

        function drawChart() {
          const data = [{label:'Present',value:presents},{label:'Absent',value:absents}];
          const margin={top:20,right:20,bottom:30,left:40};
          const width=300-margin.left-margin.right, height=200-margin.top-margin.bottom;
          const svg = d3.select('#attendance-chart').append('svg')
            .attr('width',width+margin.left+margin.right).attr('height',height+margin.top+margin.bottom)
            .attr('viewBox',`0 0 ${width+margin.left+margin.right} ${height+margin.top+margin.bottom}`)
            .attr('preserveAspectRatio','xMidYMid meet').style('width','100%').style('height','auto')
            .append('g').attr('transform',`translate(${margin.left},${margin.top})`);
          const x = d3.scaleBand().range([0,width]).domain(data.map(d=>d.label)).padding(0.1);
          const y = d3.scaleLinear().domain([0,d3.max(data,d=>d.value)]).range([height,0]);
          svg.selectAll('.bar').data(data).enter().append('rect')
            .attr('class','bar').attr('x',d=>x(d.label)).attr('width',x.bandwidth())
            .attr('y',d=>y(d.value)).attr('height',d=>height-y(d.value))
            .attr('fill',d=>d.label==='Present'?'green':'red');
          svg.append('g').attr('transform',`translate(0,${height})`).call(d3.axisBottom(x));
          svg.append('g').call(d3.axisLeft(y));
        }
        if (typeof d3 === 'undefined') {
          const sc = document.createElement('script');
          sc.src = 'https://d3js.org/d3.v7.min.js'; sc.onload = drawChart;
          document.head.appendChild(sc);
        } else { drawChart(); }
      }

      // Section heading
      const genHdr = document.createElement('div');
      genHdr.style.cssText = 'margin:16px 0 10px;font-size:15px;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a;';
      genHdr.textContent = '📋 General Attendance Records';
      historyDiv.appendChild(genHdr);

      // Date-grouped general records
      renderDateGroupedTables(
        historyDiv, generalRecs,
        (rec) => {
          const stu = studentMap.get(normId(rec.studentId));
          const tr  = document.createElement('tr');
          [rec.term||'-', rec.week||'-', rec.studentId||'-',
           stu?stu.name:'Unknown', rec.present?'Present ✅':'Absent ❌']
            .forEach(v => {
              const td = document.createElement('td');
              td.style.cssText = 'padding:6px 10px;font-size:12px;border-bottom:1px solid #f1f5f9;';
              td.textContent = v; tr.appendChild(td);
            });
          const tdAction = document.createElement('td');
          tdAction.style.cssText = 'padding:6px 10px;';
          const editBtn = document.createElement('button');
          editBtn.className='edit-attendance-btn'; editBtn.textContent='Edit';
          editBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>editAttendanceRecord(rec.recordId));
          const delBtn = document.createElement('button');
          delBtn.className='delete-btn'; delBtn.textContent='Delete';
          delBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>deleteAttendanceRecord(rec.recordId));
          tdAction.appendChild(editBtn); tdAction.appendChild(delBtn);
          tr.appendChild(tdAction);
          return tr;
        },
        ['Term','Week','ID','Name','Status','Action']
      );

    } catch (err) {
      console.error('viewAttendanceHistory failed', err);
      alert('⚠️ Failed to load attendance history. See console.');
    } finally {window. hideLoading(); }
  };

  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  // ██  ALL ORIGINAL FUNCTIONS — 100% PRESERVED  ████████████████████████████
  // ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────

  window.clearAttendance = function () {
    document.querySelectorAll('#attendance-table .status-btn').forEach(btn=>btn.classList.remove('active'));
    const saveBtn = document.getElementById('saveAttendanceBtn')||document.getElementById('save-attendance-btn');
    if (saveBtn) saveBtn.disabled = true;
    alert('✅ Attendance selection cleared');
  };

  window.hideAttendanceHistory = function () {
    const h = document.getElementById('attendance-history');
    if (!h) return;
    h.style.display = 'none';
    alert('✅ Attendance history hidden');
  };

  window.editAttendanceRecord = async function (recordId) {
    if (!recordId) return alert('Invalid record id');
    try {
      window.showLoading('Editing attendance...');
      const record = await getIndexedDB(STORE, recordId);
      if (!record) return alert('Record not found');
      const current = record.present ? 'Present' : 'Absent';
      const ans = prompt(`New status for ${record.studentId} on ${record.date} (current: ${current}):`, current);
      if (ans === null) return;
      const cleaned = ans.trim().toLowerCase();
      const parsed = ['present','p'].includes(cleaned)?true:['absent','a'].includes(cleaned)?false:null;
      if (parsed === null) return alert('Enter Present/P or Absent/A');
      record.present = parsed; record.updatedAt = new Date().toISOString();
      await setIndexedDB(STORE, recordId, record);
      await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${recordId}`,'set',record);
      triggerSync(); alert(`✅ Updated to ${parsed?'Present':'Absent'}`);
      await viewAttendanceHistory(); await loadAttendanceStudents();
    } catch (err) { console.error('editAttendanceRecord failed',err); alert('⚠️ Failed to edit record.'); }
    finally { hideLoading(); }
  };

  window.deleteAttendanceRecord = async function (recordId) {
    if (!recordId) return alert('Invalid record id');
    try {
      if (!confirm('Delete this attendance record?')) return;
      window.showLoading('Deleting record...');
      await deleteIndexedDB(STORE, recordId);
      await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${recordId}`,'delete',null);
      triggerSync(); alert('✅ Record deleted');
      await viewAttendanceHistory(); await loadAttendanceStudents();
    } catch (err) { console.error('deleteAttendanceRecord failed',err); alert('⚠️ Failed to delete record.'); }
    finally {window. hideLoading(); }
  };

  window.deleteAllAttendance = async function () {
    const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
    if (!classEl) return alert('Select a class');
    const className = classEl.value;
    if (!className) return alert('Select a class');
    const studentInput = (document.getElementById('history-student-id')?.value||'').trim();
    const termInput    = (document.getElementById('history-term')?.value||'').trim();
    const weekInput    = (document.getElementById('history-week')?.value||'').trim();
    let message = 'Delete all records in '+className+'?';
    if (studentInput) message = `Delete all records for ${studentInput} in ${className}?`;
    if (termInput) message += ` (Term: ${termInput})`;
    if (weekInput) message += ` (Week: ${weekInput})`;
    if (!confirm(message)) return;
    try {
     window. showLoading('Deleting attendance records...');
      let attendance = await getIndexedDB(STORE);
      if (!Array.isArray(attendance)) attendance = [];
      for (const att of attendance) {
        if (!att) continue;
        if (att.classId !== className) continue;
        if (studentInput && normId(att.studentId) !== normId(studentInput)) continue;
        if (termInput && normText(att.term) !== normText(termInput)) continue;
        if (weekInput && normText(att.week) !== normText(weekInput)) continue;
        await deleteIndexedDB(STORE, att.recordId);
        await addToSyncQueue(`attendanceHistory/${att.recordId}`,'delete',null);
      }
      triggerSync(); alert('✅ Deleted records');
      await viewAttendanceHistory(); await loadAttendanceStudents();
    } catch (err) { console.error('deleteAllAttendance failed',err); alert('⚠️ Failed to delete records.'); }
    finally {window. hideLoading(); }
  };

  // ── Auto-listener (original — preserved) ──────────────────────────────────
  (function initAttendanceAutoListener() {
    if (window.__ATTENDANCE_AUTO_LISTENER__) return;
    window.__ATTENDANCE_AUTO_LISTENER__ = true;
    const debounce = (fn,delay=300)=>{ let t; return (...a)=>{ clearTimeout(t); t=setTimeout(()=>fn(...a),delay); }; };
    const triggerUpdate = debounce(async () => {
      const classEl = document.getElementById('attendance-class');
      const sid = (document.getElementById('history-student-id')?.value||'').trim();
      const ti  = (document.getElementById('history-term')?.value||'').trim();
      const wi  = (document.getElementById('history-week')?.value||'').trim();
      if (!classEl?.value || !sid || !ti || !wi) return;
      const h = document.getElementById('attendance-history');
      if (h) { h.innerHTML='<p>Loading attendance history...</p>'; h.style.display='block'; }
      if (typeof window.viewAttendanceHistory === 'function') {
        await window.viewAttendanceHistory();
        if (h && h.innerHTML.includes('Loading attendance history')) h.innerHTML='';
      }
    }, 600);
    document.addEventListener('input', e => {
      if (['history-student-id','history-term','history-week','attendance-class'].includes(e.target.id)) triggerUpdate();
    });
  })();

  // ── Wire all buttons ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  document.getElementById('viewAttendanceHistoryBtn')?.addEventListener('click', viewAttendanceHistory);
  document.getElementById('hideAttendanceHistoryBtn')?.addEventListener('click', hideAttendanceHistory);
  document.getElementById('saveAttendanceBtn')?.addEventListener('click', saveAttendance);
  document.getElementById('clearAttendanceBtn')?.addEventListener('click', clearAttendance);
  document.getElementById('deleteAllAttendanceBtn')?.addEventListener('click', deleteAllAttendance);
  document.getElementById('openSubjectAttendanceBtn')?.addEventListener('click', openSubjectAttendanceModal);
  document.getElementById('viewSubjectAttendanceHistoryBtn')?.addEventListener('click', openSubjectAttendanceHistory);

})();



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  TEACHMATE 3.0 — SCHOOL SUBSCRIPTION LISTENER  (PRODUCTION v2)
//
//  BUGS FIXED vs original:
//  ① window.doc / window.onSnapshot — Firebase v9 modular imports
//    are NEVER on window. Listener always crashed silently.
//    Fix: accepts firestoreSDK object {doc, onSnapshot, getDoc}
//    passed in from the module that has those imports.
//
//  ② _processSubscriptionState celebration check was wrong:
//    _currentStatus was set BEFORE the previousStatus comparison,
//    so the "reactivated" banner never fired.
//    Fix: save previousStatus before mutating _currentStatus.
//
//  ③ localStorage cache keyed by LISTENER_CACHE_KEY (constant):
//    Logging out and back in as a different teacher from a different
//    school would load the previous school's cached subscription.
//    Fix: cache keyed by schoolId.
//
//  ④ No guard against double-attach:
//    If _routeUser ran twice (network retry), two onSnapshot
//    listeners attached to the same document — duplicate banners,
//    duplicate state changes.
//    Fix: early-return if already listening to same schoolId.
//
//  ⑤ Banner fires "Full access granted" on EVERY snapshot even when
//    status hasn't changed (e.g. offline reconnect).
//    Fix: banners only shown on actual status changes, not on
//    same-status snapshots after first load.
//
//  ⑥ stopSchoolListener() was never wired into logout.
//    Fix: called in the auth engine's logout + no-user paths.
//
//  INTEGRATION:
//  This file is loaded in the same <script type="module"> as your
//  Firebase imports. Call window.startSchoolListener() ONLY after
//  a teacher is confirmed active in a school (paths 2, 4, approval).
//  Call window.stopSchoolListener() on logout.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ─── Constants ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────
const _SL_CACHE_PREFIX       = 'tm_sub_';   // + schoolId = cache key
const _SL_MAX_CACHE_AGE_MS   = 7 * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000; // 7 days — was 5 min.
                                             // This is only the INSTANT
                                             // first-paint fallback shown
                                             // before Firestore's own
                                             // persistent cache answers
                                             // (see initializeFirestore
                                             // fix above). 5 minutes made
                                             // it useless for teachers who
                                             // are genuinely offline for
                                             // hours/days at a time.
const _SL_MAX_RECONNECT      = 5;

// ─── Module-level state ──────────────────────────────────────────────
let _slUnsubscribe        = null;
let _slReconnectAttempts  = 0;
let _slIsReconnecting     = false;
let _slCurrentSchoolId    = null;
let _slCurrentStatus      = null;   // last applied status
let _slCurrentExpiryDate  = null;
let _slIsFirstSnapshot    = true;   // suppresses banner on cold load
let _slFirestoreSDK       = null;   // { doc, onSnapshot } from module scope


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  UI STATE CLASSES
//  CSS on body controls what's visible/clickable globally.
//  Add these to your stylesheet:
//    .tm-locked   .write-action { display: none !important; }
//    .tm-readonly .write-action { pointer-events: none; opacity: .45; }
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _slApplyFullAccess() {
  document.body.classList.add('tm-active');
  document.body.classList.remove('tm-locked', 'tm-readonly');
  console.log('[SubListener] ✅ Full access');
}
function _slApplyReadOnly() {
  document.body.classList.add('tm-readonly');
  document.body.classList.remove('tm-locked', 'tm-active');
  console.log('[SubListener] 📖 Read-only');
}
function _slApplyFullLock() {
  document.body.classList.add('tm-locked');
  document.body.classList.remove('tm-readonly', 'tm-active');
  console.log('[SubListener] 🔒 Locked');
}
function _slRemoveAllStates() {
  document.body.classList.remove('tm-locked', 'tm-readonly', 'tm-active');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  WRITE-ACTION DISABLER (BULLETPROOF UPGRADE)
//  Disables buttons that mutate data when subscription is inactive.
//  Uses CSS Injection + Event Interception to catch dynamic DOM elements.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

const _slRestrictedIds = [
  'saveAttendanceBtn', 'clearAttendanceBtn', 'addStudentButton',
  'saveResultBtn', 'saveFeeBtn', 'openSubjectAttendanceBtn',
  'saveSubjectAttendanceBtn', 'saveLessonPlanBtn',
  'ExportStudentPdfBtn', 'exportResultsPdfBtn'
];

// 1. Inject Bulletproof CSS
// This guarantees that even if a button is rendered 5 minutes later 
// inside a modal, it will instantly be disabled if the body is locked.
if (!document.getElementById('_tm-disable-css')) {
  const style = document.createElement('style');
  style.id = '_tm-disable-css';
  
  // Create a CSS selector for all restricted IDs
  const selectors = _slRestrictedIds.map(id => 
    `body.tm-readonly #${id}, body.tm-locked #${id}`
  ).join(',\n');

  style.textContent = `
    ${selectors} {
      pointer-events: none !important;
      opacity: 0.45 !important;
      cursor: not-allowed !important;
      filter: grayscale(100%) !important;
    }
  `;
  document.head.appendChild(style);
}

// 2. Global Event Interceptor
// Stops keyboard activation (Enter/Space) on restricted buttons 
// when the system is not active.
window.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
  const isLocked = document.body.classList.contains('tm-locked') || 
                   document.body.classList.contains('tm-readonly');
                   
  if (!isLocked) return;

  // Check if clicked element or its parent is one of the restricted IDs
  const clickedRestrictedElement = e.target.closest(_slRestrictedIds.map(id => `#${id}`).join(','));
  
  if (clickedRestrictedElement) {
    e.preventDefault();
    e.stopPropagation();
    e.stopImmediatePropagation();
    _slShowBanner('⚠️ Action disabled: Subscription is inactive.', 'warning', 3000);
  }
