}, true); // Use capture phase to intercept before other click listeners

// 3. The standard DOM update (for immediate visual feedback on existing elements)
function _slDisableWriteActions(disabled) {
  _slRestrictedIds.forEach(id => {
    const btn = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!btn) return; // Safely skip if not currently rendered
    
    btn.disabled      = disabled;
    btn.title         = disabled ? '⚠️ Subscription inactive' : '';
  });
}



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SUBSCRIPTION WRITE GUARD — defense in depth
//  UI-level disabling (_slRestrictedIds) can be bypassed: a button not
//  yet in that list, a direct console call, or a race between a click
//  and a status change. This guard is checked INSIDE the write
//  functions themselves (attendance, results, fees, lesson planner) so
//  no write can ever go through while the school's subscription is
//  read-only or locked — reads still work everywhere, only writes stop.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _tmSubscriptionBlocksWrites() {
  return document.body.classList.contains('tm-readonly') ||
         document.body.classList.contains('tm-locked');
}
function _tmBlockWrite(actionLabel) {
  if (!_tmSubscriptionBlocksWrites()) return false;
  const msg = `🔒 ${actionLabel || 'This action'} is disabled — your school's subscription is inactive. You can still view all existing data; contact your headteacher to renew.`;
  if (typeof _slShowBanner === 'function') {
    _slShowBanner(msg, 'warning', 4500);
  } else {
    alert(msg);
  }
  return true;
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  LESSON PLANNER VISIBILITY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _slHideLessonPlanner() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('openPlannerBtn');
  if (btn) btn.style.display = 'none';
}
function _slShowLessonPlanner() {
  const btn = document.getElementById('openPlannerBtn');
  if (btn) btn.style.display = 'block';
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  BANNER SYSTEM
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _slShowBanner(message, type = 'info', duration = 5000) {
  const existing = document.getElementById('tm-sub-banner');
  if (existing) existing.remove();

  const STYLES = {
    success:     { bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#10b981,#059669)', icon: '✅', border: '#34d399' },
    error:       { bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#ef4444,#dc2626)', icon: '❌', border: '#f87171' },
    warning:     { bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#f59e0b,#d97706)', icon: '⚠️', border: '#fbbf24' },
    info:        { bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#3b82f6,#2563eb)', icon: 'ℹ️', border: '#60a5fa' },
    celebration: { bg: 'linear-gradient(135deg,#8b5cf6,#7c3aed)', icon: '🎉', border: '#a78bfa' },
  };

  const s = STYLES[type] || STYLES.info;

  // Inject animation keyframes once
  if (!document.getElementById('_tm-sub-anim')) {
    const style = document.createElement('style');
    style.id = '_tm-sub-anim';
    style.textContent = `
      @keyframes _slIn  { from { transform:translateX(120%);opacity:0 } to { transform:translateX(0);opacity:1 } }
      @keyframes _slOut { from { transform:translateX(0);opacity:1 } to { transform:translateX(120%);opacity:0 } }
    `;
    document.head.appendChild(style);
  }

  const banner = document.createElement('div');
  banner.id = 'tm-sub-banner';
  banner.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;top:20px;right:20px;z-index:10001;
    padding:14px 18px;
    background:${s.bg};color:white;
    font-size:14px;font-weight:600;
    border-radius:12px;border-left:4px solid ${s.border};
    box-shadow:0 4px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.25);
    display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;
    animation:_slIn 0.3s ease;
    font-family:'Outfit',system-ui,sans-serif;
    max-width:380px;
  `;
  banner.innerHTML = `
    <span style="font-size:20px;">${s.icon}</span>
    <span style="flex:1;">${message}</span>
    <button onclick="this.parentElement.remove()" style="
      background:rgba(255,255,255,.2);border:none;border-radius:50%;
      width:24px;height:24px;cursor:pointer;color:white;font-size:13px;
      display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;">✕</button>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(banner);

  if (type !== 'error') {
    setTimeout(() => {
      if (!banner.parentElement) return;
      banner.style.animation = '_slOut 0.3s ease';
      setTimeout(() => banner.remove(), 300);
    }, duration);
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CACHE HELPERS  (keyed by schoolId — fix ③)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _slCacheKey(schoolId) {
  return `${_SL_CACHE_PREFIX}${schoolId}`;
}

function _slSaveCache(schoolId, status, expiryDate) {
  try {
    localStorage.setItem(_slCacheKey(schoolId), JSON.stringify({
      status, expiryDate, cachedAt: Date.now()
    }));
  } catch (_) {}
}

function _slLoadCache(schoolId) {
  try {
    const raw  = localStorage.getItem(_slCacheKey(schoolId));
    if (!raw) return null;
    const data = JSON.parse(raw);
    if (Date.now() - (data.cachedAt || 0) < _SL_MAX_CACHE_AGE_MS) return data;
    localStorage.removeItem(_slCacheKey(schoolId));
    return null;
  } catch (_) { return null; }
}

function _slClearCache(schoolId) {
  try {
    if (schoolId) localStorage.removeItem(_slCacheKey(schoolId));
  } catch (_) {}
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  CORE STATE PROCESSOR  (fixes ② and ⑤)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  FEATURE ACCESS GATING — SMS sending, Report Card generation, and AutoMark
//  are controlled independently of subscription status, toggled per-school
//  from the Admin Control Centre. This just disables the button and marks
//  it visibly locked; it does NOT hide it, so teachers can see the feature
//  exists and understand it's a premium unlock, not something broken.
//  Both SMS buttons (Send Results via SMS, and Choose Students for SMS)
//  share the SAME single "sms" flag — one admin toggle controls both.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _gateButton(id, enabled, lockedTooltip) {
  const btn = document.getElementById(id);
  if (!btn) return;
  if (!btn.dataset.tmOriginalHtml) btn.dataset.tmOriginalHtml = btn.innerHTML;
  btn.disabled = !enabled;
  btn.style.opacity = enabled ? '' : '0.5';
  btn.style.cursor = enabled ? '' : 'not-allowed';
  btn.innerHTML = enabled ? btn.dataset.tmOriginalHtml : btn.dataset.tmOriginalHtml + ' 🔒';
  btn.title = enabled ? '' : lockedTooltip;
}

function _applyFeatureGates() {
  const flags = window._tmFeatureFlags || { sms: true, reportCards: true, autoMark: true };
  const smsTooltip = '🔒 SMS Results Sending is a premium feature — ask your school administrator to enable it for your account.';
  _gateButton('sendClassSMSBtn', flags.sms, smsTooltip);
  _gateButton('smsPickStudentsBtn', flags.sms, smsTooltip);
  _gateButton('exportResultsPdfBtn', flags.reportCards,
    '🔒 Report Card Generation is a premium feature — ask your school administrator to enable it for your account.');
  _gateButton('bulkGenerateReportCardsBtn', flags.reportCards,
    '🔒 Report Card Generation is a premium feature — ask your school administrator to enable it for your account.');
  _gateButton('openAutoMarkBtn', flags.autoMark,
    '🔒 AutoMark is a premium feature — ask your school administrator to enable it for your account.');
}

function _slProcessState(schoolData, schoolId) {
  const rawStatus = schoolData.status || 'expired';
  let   expiryDate = schoolData.expiryDate;

  // Normalise Firestore Timestamp → milliseconds
  if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate.toMillis === 'function') {
    expiryDate = expiryDate.toMillis();
  } else if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate === 'string') {
    expiryDate = new Date(expiryDate).getTime();
  }

  const now              = Date.now();
  const isExpiredByDate  = expiryDate ? now > expiryDate : false;
  let   effectiveStatus  = rawStatus;

  // ── Feature Access gates ──────────────────────────────────────────
  // Deliberately independent of subscription status. An admin can flip
  // these off for a school even while its subscription is active.
  // Undefined = enabled, so existing schools aren't affected until an
  // admin explicitly toggles one off from the Admin Control Centre.
  window._tmFeatureFlags = {
    sms:         schoolData.smsEnabled         !== false,
    reportCards: schoolData.reportCardsEnabled !== false,
    autoMark:    schoolData.autoMarkEnabled    !== false
  };
  _applyFeatureGates();

  if (rawStatus === 'active' && isExpiredByDate) {
    effectiveStatus = 'expired';
    console.log('[SubListener] Status override: active → expired (date passed)');
  }

  // Fix ②: save PREVIOUS status before mutating
  const previousStatus   = _slCurrentStatus;
  const isStatusChange   = previousStatus !== effectiveStatus;

  _slCurrentStatus      = effectiveStatus;
  _slCurrentExpiryDate  = expiryDate;

  console.log(`[SubListener] ${previousStatus ?? 'null'} → ${effectiveStatus}${isStatusChange ? ' (CHANGE)' : ' (same)'}`);

  // Persist to cache
  _slSaveCache(schoolId, effectiveStatus, expiryDate);

  // ── Apply UI state ───────────────────────────────────────────────
  if (effectiveStatus === 'active') {
    _slApplyFullAccess();
    _slDisableWriteActions(false);
    _slShowLessonPlanner();
    window._lpAuthOk    = true;
    window._lpProfileOk = true;
    if (typeof window.showLessonPlanner === 'function') window.showLessonPlanner();

    // Fix ⑤: show banner only on status change or very first load
    if (isStatusChange || _slIsFirstSnapshot) {
      if (previousStatus === 'expired' || previousStatus === 'pending') {
        // Fix ②: now previousStatus is correct so this fires properly
        _slShowBanner('🎉 Subscription reactivated! Full access restored.', 'celebration', 5000);
      } else if (expiryDate) {
        const daysLeft = Math.ceil((expiryDate - now) / 86400000);
        if (daysLeft <= 0) {
          // Shouldn't reach here but handle gracefully
          _slShowBanner('⚠️ Subscription expired. Contact headteacher.', 'warning', 6000);
        } else if (daysLeft <= 3) {
          _slShowBanner(`⚠️ Expires in ${daysLeft} day${daysLeft === 1 ? '' : 's'}. Contact headteacher to renew.`, 'warning', 6000);
        } else if (daysLeft <= 7) {
          _slShowBanner(`📅 Subscription expires in ${daysLeft} days.`, 'info', 5000);
        } else {
          _slShowBanner('✅ System active. Full access granted.', 'success', 3000);
        }
      } else {
        _slShowBanner('✅ System active. Full access granted.', 'success', 3000);
      }
    }

  } else if (effectiveStatus === 'pending') {
    _slApplyReadOnly();
    _slDisableWriteActions(true);
    _slHideLessonPlanner();
    window._lpAuthOk    = true;
    window._lpProfileOk = false;

    if (isStatusChange || _slIsFirstSnapshot) {
      _slShowBanner('⏳ Payment submitted — awaiting admin approval. Read-only access.', 'info', 6000);
    }

  } else {
    // 🛠️ FIX: expired/unknown subscription now maps to READ-ONLY, not a
    // full lock. Previously this called _slApplyFullLock() + hid the
    // Lesson Planner entirely, which blocked READS too. The requirement
    // is: an expired school can still READ everything (attendance,
    // results, fees, lesson plans) — only WRITES are blocked, in every
    // feature. _slApplyReadOnly() + keeping the planner visible (but its
    // save button disabled via _slRestrictedIds) delivers that.
    _slApplyReadOnly();
    _slDisableWriteActions(true);
    window._lpAuthOk    = true;
    window._lpProfileOk = true;
    if (typeof window.showLessonPlanner === 'function') window.showLessonPlanner();

    if (isStatusChange || _slIsFirstSnapshot) {
      _slShowBanner('🔒 Subscription expired. You can still view all data — contact your headteacher to renew and unlock saving.', 'error', 0);
      // duration 0 = stays until dismissed (error banners are persistent)
    }
  }

  _slIsFirstSnapshot = false;

  // Broadcast to any other components listening
  window.dispatchEvent(new CustomEvent('tm:subscriptionChange', {
    detail: { status: effectiveStatus, expiryDate, previousStatus, schoolId }
  }));
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  START LISTENER
//  Fix ①: no window.doc/window.onSnapshot — uses module-scoped
//          Firebase functions passed via firestoreSDK param.
//  Fix ④: guard against double-attach on same schoolId.
//
//  Call signature:
//    window.startSchoolListener(fdb, schoolId)
//
//  The module that calls this has `doc` and `onSnapshot` in scope
//  from its Firebase imports. Those are passed via the global
//  _slFirestoreSDK object set by initSubscriptionListener().
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.startSchoolListener = function(firestoreDB, schoolId) {
  // Input validation
  if (!firestoreDB) {
    console.error('[SubListener] firestoreDB is required');
    return null;
  }
  if (!schoolId || typeof schoolId !== 'string' || !schoolId.trim()) {
    console.error('[SubListener] valid schoolId is required');
    return null;
  }

  // Fix ④: already listening to this exact school — do nothing
  if (_slCurrentSchoolId === schoolId && _slUnsubscribe) {
    console.log('[SubListener] Already listening to schoolId:', schoolId);
    return _slUnsubscribe;
  }

  // Fix ①: check SDK was registered
  if (!_slFirestoreSDK || !_slFirestoreSDK.doc || !_slFirestoreSDK.onSnapshot) {
    console.error('[SubListener] Firebase SDK not registered. Call window.initSubscriptionListener({doc, onSnapshot}) first.');
    return null;
  }

  console.log('[SubListener] 🚀 Starting for school:', schoolId);

  // Tear down any previous listener (different school)
  if (_slUnsubscribe) {
    _slUnsubscribe();
    _slUnsubscribe = null;
  }

  _slCurrentSchoolId   = schoolId;
  _slReconnectAttempts = 0;
  _slIsReconnecting    = false;
  _slIsFirstSnapshot   = true;

  // Apply cached state immediately for instant UI (no blank screen)
  const cached = _slLoadCache(schoolId);
  if (cached) {
    console.log('[SubListener] Cached state applied immediately:', cached.status);
    _slProcessState(cached, schoolId);
    _slIsFirstSnapshot = true; // reset so real snapshot shows banner
    _slShowBanner('📡 Verifying subscription with server…', 'info', 2500);
  } else {
    _slApplyReadOnly(); // safe default while we wait for first snapshot
    _slShowBanner('🔐 Verifying subscription…', 'info', 2000);
  }

  // Attach real-time listener using registered SDK functions
  const { doc: _doc, onSnapshot: _onSnapshot } = _slFirestoreSDK;
  const schoolRef = _doc(firestoreDB, 'schools', schoolId);

  _slUnsubscribe = _onSnapshot(
    schoolRef,

    // ── Success handler ────────────────────────────────────────
    (snapshot) => {
      _slReconnectAttempts = 0;
      _slIsReconnecting    = false;

      if (!snapshot.exists()) {
        console.error('[SubListener] School doc not found:', schoolId);
        _slProcessState({ status: 'expired' }, schoolId);
        _slShowBanner('🏫 School not found. Contact support.', 'error', 0);
        return;
      }

      _slProcessState(snapshot.data(), schoolId);
    },

    // ── Error handler ──────────────────────────────────────────
    (error) => {
      console.error('[SubListener] Firestore error:', error.code, error.message);

      if (error.code === 'permission-denied') {
        // ── PRODUCTION FIX ──────────────────────────────────────
        // A single permission-denied does NOT mean access is revoked.
        // It can happen during a sync burst when multiple onSnapshot
        // listeners fire simultaneously and rule evaluation get() calls
        // temporarily exceed Firestore's per-request limit of 10, or
        // right after login/app-reopen before a force-refreshed ID token
        // has propagated custom claims. With JWT custom claims (v5 rules)
        // and the login-time token refresh now in place, this should be
        // rare — but as a safety net we require 6 CONSECUTIVE
        // permission-denied errors, spread over a longer backoff window,
        // before treating it as a real access revocation. This prevents
        // spurious lockouts during sync bursts or slow connections.
        _slReconnectAttempts++;
        console.warn(`[SubListener] permission-denied (${_slReconnectAttempts}/6) — may be transient`);

        if (_slReconnectAttempts < 6) {
          // Treat like a network error: retry with backoff
          const delay = Math.min(3000 * _slReconnectAttempts, 15000);
          _slIsReconnecting = true;
          _slShowBanner('🔐 Verifying access… retrying.', 'warning', 3000);

          setTimeout(() => {
            _slIsReconnecting = false;
            if (_slUnsubscribe) { _slUnsubscribe(); _slUnsubscribe = null; }
            window.startSchoolListener(firestoreDB, schoolId);
          }, delay);
          return;
        }

        // 6 consecutive permission-denied = genuinely revoked.
        // NOTE: we deliberately do NOT force auth.signOut() here anymore.
        // _slProcessState({status:'expired'}) above already locks writes
        // while leaving reads and the session itself intact — that's the
        // correct, non-destructive response to a revoked subscription.
        // Auto-signing the user out from an *inferred* error was the actual
        // cause of unexpected logouts (see: token-refresh timing at login),
        // so a real revocation now just shows this banner; the user or an
        // admin can act on it deliberately instead of losing their session
        // to a heuristic.
        console.error('[SubListener] 🚫 Access confirmed revoked after 6 attempts');
        _slProcessState({ status: 'expired' }, schoolId);
        _slShowBanner('⛔ Access revoked by administrator. Your changes are paused — contact your administrator.', 'error', 0);
        _slClearCache(schoolId);
        return;
      }

      // ── Network / unavailable errors: exponential backoff ────
      if (_slReconnectAttempts < _SL_MAX_RECONNECT && !_slIsReconnecting) {
        _slReconnectAttempts++;
        const delay = Math.min(1000 * Math.pow(2, _slReconnectAttempts), 30000);
        _slIsReconnecting = true;
        console.log(`[SubListener] Reconnecting in ${delay}ms (${_slReconnectAttempts}/${_SL_MAX_RECONNECT})`);
        _slShowBanner(`📡 Connection issue. Retrying (${_slReconnectAttempts}/${_SL_MAX_RECONNECT})…`, 'warning', 3500);

        setTimeout(() => {
          _slIsReconnecting = false;
          if (_slUnsubscribe) { _slUnsubscribe(); _slUnsubscribe = null; }
          window.startSchoolListener(firestoreDB, schoolId);
        }, delay);

      } else if (_slReconnectAttempts >= _SL_MAX_RECONNECT) {
        console.error('[SubListener] Max reconnections reached');
        _slProcessState({ status: 'expired' }, schoolId);
        _slShowBanner('⚠️ Connection lost. Please refresh the page.', 'error', 0);
      }
    }
  );

  console.log('[SubListener] ✅ Listener attached for:', schoolId);
  return _slUnsubscribe;
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STOP LISTENER
//  Call on logout and in the no-user branch of onAuthStateChanged.
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.stopSchoolListener = function() {
  console.log('[SubListener] 🛑 Stopping listener for:', _slCurrentSchoolId);
  if (_slUnsubscribe) { _slUnsubscribe(); _slUnsubscribe = null; }
  _slClearCache(_slCurrentSchoolId);
  _slCurrentSchoolId   = null;
  _slCurrentStatus     = null;
  _slCurrentExpiryDate = null;
  _slReconnectAttempts = 0;
  _slIsReconnecting    = false;
  _slIsFirstSnapshot   = true;
  _slRemoveAllStates();

  // Remove any persistent banners
  const banner = document.getElementById('tm-sub-banner');
  if (banner) banner.remove();

  console.log('[SubListener] ✅ Stopped');
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SDK REGISTRATION
//  Call this ONCE at the top of your module, after Firebase imports.
//  Passes module-scoped doc + onSnapshot into the listener so it
//  doesn't need to look them up on window (fix ①).
//
//  Usage (inside your <script type="module">):
//    window.initSubscriptionListener({ doc, onSnapshot });
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.initSubscriptionListener = function({ doc, onSnapshot }) {
  if (typeof doc !== 'function' || typeof onSnapshot !== 'function') {
    console.error('[SubListener] initSubscriptionListener: doc and onSnapshot must be functions');
    return;
  }
  _slFirestoreSDK = { doc, onSnapshot };
  console.log('[SubListener] ✅ Firebase SDK registered');
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  STATUS GETTER  (for other modules to read current state)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.getSubscriptionStatus = function() {
  return {
    status:      _slCurrentStatus,
    expiryDate:  _slCurrentExpiryDate,
    isListening: !!_slUnsubscribe,
    schoolId:    _slCurrentSchoolId,
    isActive:    _slCurrentStatus === 'active',
    isExpired:   _slCurrentStatus === 'expired' || _slCurrentStatus === null,
  };
};

console.log('[SubListener] 🎯 Subscription listener loaded (v2)');



// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  TEACHMATE 3.0 — AUTH + PROFILE + SUBSCRIPTION ENGINE  (FINAL v2)
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// ─── Module-level state ──────────────────────────────────────────────
window._pfSubjects      = [];
let _authLock         = false;
let _pendingRouteUser = null;
let _observerBusy     = false;

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  TRANSITION OVERLAY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _showTransitionOverlay(msg = 'Loading…') {
  let el = document.getElementById('_tm-transition-overlay');
  if (!el) {
    el = document.createElement('div');
    el.id = '_tm-transition-overlay';
    el.style.cssText = `
      position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;background:#061a10;
      display:flex;flex-direction:column;
      align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:16px;
      font-family:'Outfit',system-ui,sans-serif;
    `;
    el.innerHTML = `
      <div style="width:44px;height:44px;border-radius:50%;
        border:3px solid rgba(0,204,136,0.2);border-top-color:#00cc88;
        animation:_tm_spin 0.8s linear infinite;"></div>
      <div id="_tm-overlay-msg" style="color:#94a3b8;font-size:.875rem;font-weight:600;"></div>
      <style>@keyframes _tm_spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}</style>
    `;
    document.body.appendChild(el);
  }
  const msgEl = document.getElementById('_tm-overlay-msg');
  if (msgEl) msgEl.textContent = msg;
  el.style.display = 'flex';
}

function _hideTransitionOverlay() {
  const el = document.getElementById('_tm-transition-overlay');
  if (el) el.style.display = 'none';
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  AUTH SCREEN HELPER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _showAuthScreen() {
  _hideTransitionOverlay();
  const authSection = document.getElementById('auth-section');
  const dashboard   = document.getElementById('dashboard');
  if (authSection) authSection.style.display = 'block';
  if (dashboard)   dashboard.style.display   = 'none';
  history.pushState({ view: 'auth' }, '', '#auth');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  AUTH LOCK HELPERS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _lockAuth()   { _authLock = true; _pendingRouteUser = null; }
function _unlockAuth() {
  _authLock = false;
  if (_pendingRouteUser) {
    const user = _pendingRouteUser;
    _pendingRouteUser = null;
    _routeUser(user);
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  SUBSCRIPTION LISTENER HELPER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function _startSubscription(schoolId) {
  if (!schoolId) return;
  if (typeof window.startSchoolListener !== 'function') {
    console.warn('[Auth] startSchoolListener not available');
    return;
  }
  window.startSchoolListener(fdb, schoolId);
}

function _stopSubscription() {
  if (typeof window.stopSchoolListener === 'function') {
    window.stopSchoolListener();
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  1. BOOTSTRAP
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.initOnboardingFlow = async function () {
  if (!auth || !auth.currentUser) return;
  const uid = auth.currentUser.uid;
  try {
    const userSnap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', uid));
    if (!userSnap.exists()) { showProfileForm(); return; }
    const data   = userSnap.data();
    const status = data.accountStatus || null;
    if (status === 'active') {
      attachApprovalListener(uid);
      if (typeof showDashboard === 'function') showDashboard();
      return;
    }
    if (status === 'pending') { renderPendingOverlay('pending', data.schoolId || null); attachApprovalListener(uid); return; }
    if (status === 'rejected') { renderPendingOverlay('rejected'); return; }
    showProfileForm();
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('initOnboardingFlow error:', e.message);
    showProfileForm();
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  2. PROFILE FORM VISIBILITY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function showProfileForm() {
  const container = document.getElementById('profile-modal');
  if (!container) return;
  const pending = document.getElementById('pending-approval-screen');
  if (pending) pending.style.display = 'none';
  container.style.display = 'flex';
  renderProfileForm('profile-modal-content');
}

// ── Cancel profile creation ────────────────────────────────────────────
// There's no partially-set-up dashboard to fall back to at this stage, so
// cancelling means signing back out to the login screen. The account
// itself (email/password) is left intact — if they come back and register
// again with the same email, the email-already-in-use handling on the
// register flow will sign them back into this same account and let them
// resume, rather than blocking them.
window.cancelProfileCreation = async function () {
  try {
    const container = document.getElementById('profile-modal');
    if (container) container.style.display = 'none';
    if (typeof _stopSubscription === 'function') _stopSubscription();
    if (auth && auth.currentUser) {
      await signOut(auth);
    }
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('cancelProfileCreation error:', e.message);
  } finally {
    _showAuthScreen();
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  3. PROFILE FORM RENDERER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.renderProfileForm = function (containerId) {
  const container = document.getElementById(containerId);
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = `
    `;
  window._pfSubjects = [];
  renderSubjectChips();
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  4. PHOTO PREVIEW
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.previewProfilePhoto = function (input) {
  const file = input.files && input.files[0];
  if (!file) return;
  const reader = new FileReader();
  reader.onload = (e) => {
    const img = document.getElementById('pf-photo-preview');
    if (img) img.src = e.target.result;
  };
  reader.readAsDataURL(file);
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  5. SUBJECT CHIPS
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.addSubject = function () {
  const input = document.getElementById('pf-subject-input');
  const val   = (input?.value || '').trim();
  if (!val) return;
  if (window._pfSubjects.some(s => s.toLowerCase() === val.toLowerCase())) {
    if (input) input.value = ''; return;
  }
  window._pfSubjects.push(val);
  if (input) input.value = '';
  renderSubjectChips();
};
window.removeSubject = function (idx) {
  window._pfSubjects.splice(idx, 1);
  renderSubjectChips();
};
function renderSubjectChips() {
  const container = document.getElementById('pf-subject-chips');
  if (!container) return;
  container.innerHTML = window._pfSubjects.map((s, i) => `
    <span class="tm-chip">${s}
      <button type="button" onclick="removeSubject(${i})">×</button>
    </span>`).join('');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  6. SCHOOL CODE LOOKUP
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.lookupSchoolCode = async function () {
  const codeInput  = document.getElementById('pf-school-code');
  const infoDiv    = document.getElementById('pf-school-info');
  const hiddenId   = document.getElementById('pf-school-id');
  const hiddenName = document.getElementById('pf-school-name-hidden');
  const code = (codeInput?.value || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  if (!code) { showInfoDiv(infoDiv, '⚠️ Please enter a school code.', true); return; }
  showInfoDiv(infoDiv, '🔍 Searching...', false);
  if (hiddenId)   hiddenId.value   = '';
  if (hiddenName) hiddenName.value = '';
  try {
    const q    = query(collection(fdb, 'schools'), where('schoolCode', '==', code));
    const snap = await getDocs(q);
    if (snap.empty) { showInfoDiv(infoDiv, '❌ School code not found. Please check with your Headteacher.', true); return; }
    const schoolDoc  = snap.docs[0];
    const schoolData = schoolDoc.data();
    if (hiddenId)   hiddenId.value   = schoolDoc.id;
    if (hiddenName) hiddenName.value = schoolData.name || code;
    showInfoDiv(infoDiv, `✅ ${schoolData.name} — verified successfully!`, false);
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('lookupSchoolCode:', e);
    showInfoDiv(infoDiv, '⚠️ Network error. Please try again.', true);
  }
};
function showInfoDiv(div, msg, isError) {
  if (!div) return;
  div.textContent   = msg;
  div.style.display = 'block';
  div.className     = 'tm-school-info' + (isError ? ' error' : '');
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  7. IMAGE COMPRESSION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
async function compressProfileImage(file) {
  return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
    const img = new Image(); const reader = new FileReader();
    reader.onload  = (e) => { img.src = e.target.result; };
    reader.onerror = ()  => reject(new Error('Failed to read file'));
    img.onload = () => {
      const canvas = document.createElement('canvas');
      const MAX_W  = 600; const scale = MAX_W / img.width;
      canvas.width = MAX_W; canvas.height = img.height * scale;
      canvas.getContext('2d').drawImage(img, 0, 0, canvas.width, canvas.height);
      canvas.toBlob(
        blob => blob ? resolve(blob) : reject(new Error('Canvas to Blob failed')),
        'image/jpeg', 0.7
      );
    };
    img.onerror = () => reject(new Error('Failed to load image'));
    reader.readAsDataURL(file);
  });
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  8. SAVE PROFILE
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.saveProfile = async function () {
  if (!auth || !auth.currentUser) { alert('You must be logged in to submit a profile.'); return; }

  const name       = (document.getElementById('pf-name')?.value               || '').trim();
  const tsNumber   = (document.getElementById('pf-ts-number')?.value          || '').trim();
  const phone      = (document.getElementById('pf-phone')?.value              || '').trim();
  const schoolCode = (document.getElementById('pf-school-code')?.value        || '').trim().toUpperCase();
  const schoolId   = (document.getElementById('pf-school-id')?.value          || '').trim();
  const schoolName = (document.getElementById('pf-school-name-hidden')?.value || '').trim();
  const role       = (document.getElementById('pf-role')?.value               || '').trim();
  const position   = (document.getElementById('pf-position')?.value           || '').trim();
  const subjects   = window._pfSubjects || [];
  const photoInput = document.getElementById('pf-photo');

  const errors = [];
  if (!name)                                errors.push('Full name is required.');
  if (!tsNumber)                            errors.push('TS Number is required.');
  if (!phone)                               errors.push('Phone number is required.');
  if (!schoolId)                            errors.push('Please verify your school code before submitting.');
  if (!role)                                errors.push('Please select your role.');
  if (subjects.length === 0)                errors.push('Please add at least one subject.');
  if (phone && !/^\+260\d{9}$/.test(phone)) errors.push('Phone must be in +260xxxxxxxxx format.');
  if (errors.length > 0) { alert(errors.join('\n')); return; }

  const submitBtn = document.getElementById('pf-submit-btn');
  if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = true; submitBtn.textContent = 'Submitting…'; }
  if (typeof showLoading === 'function') window.showLoading('Submitting admission request…');

  try {
    const uid   = auth.currentUser.uid;
    const email = auth.currentUser.email;

    let profilePictureURL =
      `https://via.placeholder.com/150/e6faf3/00cc88?text=${encodeURIComponent(name[0] || '?')}`;

    if (photoInput?.files?.[0]) {
      try {
        const file = photoInput.files[0];
        if (!file.type.startsWith('image/')) throw new Error('Not an image.');
        const compressed = await compressProfileImage(file);
        const storageRef = ref(storage, `schools/${schoolId}/admissionPhotos/${uid}/profile.jpg`);
        await Promise.race([
          uploadBytes(storageRef, compressed),
          new Promise((_, rej) => setTimeout(() => rej(new Error('Upload timeout')), 10_000))
        ]);
        profilePictureURL = await getDownloadURL(storageRef);
      } catch (photoErr) {
        console.warn('Photo upload skipped:', photoErr.message);
      }
    }

    // 🛠️ CRITICAL FIX: the "System Role" field above is free text (its own
    // placeholder says "e.g. Teacher, Head of Department") — it was never
    // meant to BE the security role, just a descriptive title. But it was
    // being written directly into users/{uid}.role, which your Firestore
    // rules check with an EXACT match: isActiveTeacher() requires role to
    // be precisely "Teacher" (capital T). Anyone who typed anything else
    // ("teacher", "Class Teacher", "Maths Teacher"...) got silently locked
    // out of every write gated by isActiveTeacher() — results, attendance,
    // fees, AutoMark saves. This file is teacher-only (headteacher/admin
    // have their own separate apps), so the security role for every
    // submission here is now hardcoded to match the rules exactly. What
    // the teacher actually typed is kept as roleTitle for display only.
    const roleTitle = role; // whatever they typed, kept for display purposes only
    const securityRole = 'Teacher'; // must exactly match isActiveTeacher() in the Firestore rules

    const admissionData = {
      uid, name, email, phone, tsNumber, subjects, role: securityRole, roleTitle, position,
      profilePictureURL, requestStatus: 'pending',
      schoolId, schoolName, schoolCode,
      submittedAt: serverTimestamp(), lastUpdated: serverTimestamp(),
      appVersion: 'TeachMate 3.0', platform: navigator.userAgent
    };

    await setDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'admissionRequests', uid), admissionData, { merge: true });
    await setDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', uid), {
      schoolId, schoolCode, role: securityRole, roleTitle, accountStatus: 'pending', approved: false, email
    }, { merge: true });
    await setIndexedDB('pendingProfiles', uid, { uid, schoolId, schoolName, schoolCode, requestStatus: 'pending', ...admissionData });

    const profileModal = document.getElementById('profile-modal');
    if (profileModal) profileModal.style.display = 'none';

    renderPendingOverlay('pending', schoolId);
    attachApprovalListener(uid);

  } catch (e) {
    console.error('saveProfile error:', e);
    const isNetworkError =
      e.code === 'unavailable' || e.code === 'network-request-failed' ||
      e.message?.toLowerCase().includes('network') ||
      e.message?.toLowerCase().includes('offline') ||
      e.message?.toLowerCase().includes('timeout');
    alert(isNetworkError
      ? 'Profile creation failed due to a network error.\nPlease check your connection and try again.'
      : `Profile creation failed: ${e.message}`);
    try { await signOut(auth); } catch (_) {}
    _showAuthScreen();
  } finally {
    if (typeof hideLoading === 'function') hideLoading();
    if (submitBtn) { submitBtn.disabled = false; submitBtn.textContent = 'Submit Admission Request'; }
    _unlockAuth();
  }
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  9. PENDING / REJECTED OVERLAY
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
window.renderPendingOverlay = function (status = 'pending', schoolId = null) {
  const old = document.getElementById('tm-pending-overlay');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const isPending = status === 'pending';
  const overlay   = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.id = 'tm-pending-overlay';
  overlay.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100vw;height:100vh;
    background:rgba(5,46,22,0.92);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;
    justify-content:center;align-items:center;
    z-index:9999;color:white;text-align:center;padding:2rem;
    font-family:'Outfit',system-ui,sans-serif;
  `;
  overlay.innerHTML = `
    <div style="font-size:5rem;margin-bottom:1rem;">${isPending ? '😲' : '😔'}</div>
    <h1 style="font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:.5rem;">
      ${isPending ? 'Your Admission is Waiting Approval' : 'Admission Declined'}
    </h1>
    <p style="font-size:1.1rem;max-width:400px;margin:0 auto 2rem;opacity:.9;">
      ${isPending
        ? 'Please contact your Administrator for a quick admission review.'
        : 'Unfortunately your application was not accepted. Contact your school for further information.'}
    </p>
    <div style="display:flex;gap:1rem;flex-wrap:wrap;justify-content:center;">
      ${isPending ? `<button id="tm-check-status-btn" style="
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#059669,#10b981);border:none;
        padding:.85rem 2rem;border-radius:12px;color:white;font-weight:700;
        font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;
        box-shadow:0 4px 12px rgba(5,150,105,.3);">
        <i class="fas fa-sync-alt"></i> Refresh Status
      </button>` : ''}
      <button id="tm-logout-pending-btn" style="
        background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25);
        padding:.85rem 2rem;border-radius:12px;color:white;font-weight:700;
        font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;">
        <i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i> Logout
      </button>
    </div>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);

  if (isPending) {
    document.getElementById('tm-check-status-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      overlay.style.opacity = '0.6';
      try {
        const uid = auth.currentUser?.uid;
        if (!uid) return;
        let sid = schoolId;
        if (!sid) {
          const cached = await getIndexedDB('pendingProfiles', uid).catch(() => null);
          sid = cached?.schoolId || null;
        }
        if (!sid) { overlay.style.opacity = '1'; return; }
        const snap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', sid, 'admissionRequests', uid));
        const data = snap.exists() ? snap.data() : null;
        if (data?.requestStatus === 'approved')        handleApprovalActivation(uid, data);
        else if (data?.requestStatus === 'rejected')   renderPendingOverlay('rejected');
        else {
          overlay.style.opacity = '1';
          const btn = document.getElementById('tm-check-status-btn');
          if (btn) {
            btn.textContent = '⏳ Still pending…';
            setTimeout(() => { btn.innerHTML = '<i class="fas fa-sync-alt"></i> Refresh Status'; }, 2500);
          }
        }
      } catch (e) {
        console.warn('Manual status check failed:', e.message);
        overlay.style.opacity = '1';
      }
    });
  }

  document.getElementById('tm-logout-pending-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    _stopSubscription();
    try { await signOut(auth); } catch (_) {}
    window.location.reload();
  });
};


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  10. APPROVAL LISTENER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
function attachApprovalListener(uid) {
  if (typeof approvalUnsubscribe === 'function') {
    approvalUnsubscribe(); approvalUnsubscribe = null;
  }
  approvalUnsubscribe = onSnapshot(
    doc(fdb, 'users', uid),
    (snap) => {
      if (!snap.exists()) return;
      const data   = snap.data();
      const status = data.accountStatus;
      if (status === 'active')   handleApprovalActivation(uid, data);
      else if (status === 'rejected') renderPendingOverlay('rejected');
    },
    (err) => { console.warn('approvalListener error:', err.message); }
  );
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  11. HANDLE APPROVAL
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════

// Checks the school's LIVE subscription status directly from Firestore
// (not cache) — this only runs once, at the exact moment a brand-new
// user's admission gets approved, so it's worth the extra network round
// trip to get it right rather than trust a possibly-stale cache.
async function _tmSchoolSubscriptionIsActive(schoolId) {
  try {
    const snap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId));
    if (!snap.exists()) return false;
    const data = snap.data();
    let expiryDate = data.expiryDate;
    if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate.toMillis === 'function') {
      expiryDate = expiryDate.toMillis();
    } else if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate === 'string') {
      expiryDate = new Date(expiryDate).getTime();
    }
    const isExpiredByDate = expiryDate ? Date.now() > expiryDate : false;
    return data.status === 'active' && !isExpiredByDate;
  } catch (e) {
    console.warn('[NewUserGate] subscription check failed:', e.message);
    // Fail closed: a newly-activating user with no confirmed active
    // subscription should not be let into the app.
    return false;
  }
}

let _newUserGateUnsub = null;

// Full-screen block for a newly-approved user whose school currently has
// no active subscription. Unlike the existing read-only mode for
// returning teachers (which still lets them view existing data), a
// brand-new user has no data to view yet, so a hard block with a clear
// message is the right call. Auto-resolves the moment the school
// subscribes — no logout/login needed.
function _renderNewUserSubscriptionGate(schoolId, onActivated) {
  const old = document.getElementById('tm-newuser-gate');
  if (old) old.remove();
  _hideTransitionOverlay();

  const overlay = document.createElement('div');
  overlay.id = 'tm-newuser-gate';
  overlay.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;top:0;left:0;width:100vw;height:100vh;
    background:rgba(5,46,22,0.95);backdrop-filter:blur(12px);
    display:flex;flex-direction:column;justify-content:center;align-items:center;
    z-index:9999;color:white;text-align:center;padding:2rem;
    font-family:'Outfit',system-ui,sans-serif;
  `;
  overlay.innerHTML = `
    <div style="font-size:5rem;margin-bottom:1rem;">🔒</div>
    <h1 style="font-size:1.8rem;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:.5rem;">System Access Locked</h1>
    <p style="font-size:1.1rem;max-width:420px;margin:0 auto 2rem;opacity:.9;">
      Inform the school administration to get full access to the system.
    </p>
    <button id="tm-gate-logout-btn" style="
      background:rgba(255,255,255,.15);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.25);
      padding:.85rem 2rem;border-radius:12px;color:white;font-weight:700;
      font-size:1rem;cursor:pointer;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:.5rem;">
      <i class="fas fa-sign-out-alt"></i> Logout
    </button>
  `;
  document.body.appendChild(overlay);

  document.getElementById('tm-gate-logout-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (_newUserGateUnsub) { _newUserGateUnsub(); _newUserGateUnsub = null; }
    try { await signOut(auth); } catch (_) {}
    window.location.reload();
  });

  // Live-watch the school doc so the moment an admin activates the
  // subscription, this user is let straight in — no re-login required.
  if (_newUserGateUnsub) { _newUserGateUnsub(); _newUserGateUnsub = null; }
  _newUserGateUnsub = onSnapshot(
    doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId),
    (snap) => {
      if (!snap.exists()) return;
      const data = snap.data();
      let expiryDate = data.expiryDate;
      if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate.toMillis === 'function') {
        expiryDate = expiryDate.toMillis();
      } else if (expiryDate && typeof expiryDate === 'string') {
        expiryDate = new Date(expiryDate).getTime();
      }
      const isExpiredByDate = expiryDate ? Date.now() > expiryDate : false;
      const isActive = data.status === 'active' && !isExpiredByDate;
      if (isActive) {
        if (_newUserGateUnsub) { _newUserGateUnsub(); _newUserGateUnsub = null; }
        overlay.remove();
        onActivated();
      }
    },
    (err) => console.warn('[NewUserGate] listener error:', err.message)
  );
}

async function handleApprovalActivation(uid, userData) {
  currentTeacher = auth.currentUser?.email || userData.email || '';

  if (typeof approvalUnsubscribe === 'function') {
    approvalUnsubscribe(); approvalUnsubscribe = null;
  }

  const overlay = document.getElementById('tm-pending-overlay');
  if (overlay) overlay.remove();

  try { await setIndexedDB('pendingProfiles', uid, null); } catch (_) {}

  const schoolId = userData.schoolId;

  // 🛠️ NEW: gate brand-new users behind an active-subscription check
  // before ever showing them the dashboard. Existing returning teachers
  // are unaffected — this only runs at the moment of first approval.
  if (schoolId) {
    const isActive = await _tmSchoolSubscriptionIsActive(schoolId);
    if (!isActive) {
      _renderNewUserSubscriptionGate(schoolId, () => {
        _finishApprovalActivation(uid, userData, schoolId);
      });
      return;
    }
  }

  await _finishApprovalActivation(uid, userData, schoolId);
}

async function _finishApprovalActivation(uid, userData, schoolId) {
  if (schoolId) {
    try {
      const teacherSnap = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', uid));
      if (teacherSnap.exists()) {
        await setIndexedDB('userProfile', uid, {
          uid, schoolId, ...teacherSnap.data(),
          accountStatus: 'active', role: userData.role
        });
      }
    } catch (e) {
      console.warn('handleApprovalActivation: teacher doc fetch failed:', e.message);
    }
  }

  if (typeof showDashboard  === 'function') showDashboard();
  if (schoolId && typeof safeRestore_VB === 'function') safeRestore_VB(schoolId);
  if (typeof syncData       === 'function') syncData();

// In your logout handler, alongside the existing isRestoring reset:
if (typeof window._resetRestoreEngine === 'function') {
  window._resetRestoreEngine();
}

  const subWaiter = setInterval(() => {
    const sdkReady =
      typeof window.startSchoolListener === 'function' &&
      typeof window.initSubscriptionListener === 'function';
    
    if (sdkReady) {
      clearInterval(subWaiter);
      console.log('✅ Subscription system ready');
      _startSubscription(schoolId);
    }
  }, 100);
}

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  12. NETWORK LISTENERS

// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
let _networkLost = false;
window.addEventListener('offline', () => { _networkLost = true;  console.warn('TeachMate: network lost.'); });
window.addEventListener('online',  () => { _networkLost = false; console.log('TeachMate: network restored.'); });


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  13. LOGOUT
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
document.getElementById('logout-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  _showTransitionOverlay('Signing out…');
  try {
    if (typeof isRestoring            !== 'undefined') isRestoring            = false;
    if (typeof restoreRetryCount      !== 'undefined') restoreRetryCount      = 0;
    if (typeof restoreCollectionIndex !== 'undefined') restoreCollectionIndex = 0;

    if (typeof approvalUnsubscribe === 'function') {
      approvalUnsubscribe(); approvalUnsubscribe = null;
    }

    _stopSubscription();

    if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && !db) await initIndexedDB();
    if (typeof db !== 'undefined' && db) {
      for (const storeName of Array.from(db.objectStoreNames)) {
        await new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
          const tx  = db.transaction([storeName], 'readwrite');
          const req = tx.objectStore(storeName).clear();
          req.onsuccess = resolve; req.onerror = reject;
        });
      }
    }

    await signOut(auth);
    currentTeacher = null;

    if (typeof closeProfileModal === 'function') closeProfileModal();
    if (typeof hideAllSections   === 'function') hideAllSections();
    if (typeof clockInterval !== 'undefined' && clockInterval) clearInterval(clockInterval);

    const emailEl = document.getElementById('email');
    const passEl  = document.getElementById('password');
    const msgEl   = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
    if (emailEl) emailEl.value    = '';
    if (passEl)  passEl.value     = '';
    if (msgEl)   msgEl.textContent = '';

    _showAuthScreen();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Logout error:', error);
    _hideTransitionOverlay();
    const authMsg = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
    if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = `Error: ${error.message || 'Logout failed'}`;
  }
});


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  14. LOGIN
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
document.getElementById('login-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const email    = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
  const authMsg  = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
  const loginBtn = document.getElementById('login-btn');
  if (!email || !password) { if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = 'Please fill all fields.'; return; }
  if (loginBtn) { loginBtn.disabled = true; loginBtn.textContent = 'Logging in…'; }
  if (authMsg)  authMsg.textContent = '';
  _showTransitionOverlay('Signing you in…');
  try {
    await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
    await initIndexedDB();
    if (typeof syncData === 'function') syncData();
  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Login error:', error);
    _hideTransitionOverlay();
    if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = `Error: ${error.message}`;
  } finally {
    if (loginBtn) { loginBtn.disabled = false; loginBtn.textContent = 'Login'; }
  }
});


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  15. REGISTER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
document.getElementById('register-btn')?.addEventListener('click', async () => {
  const email    = document.getElementById('email').value.trim();
  const password = document.getElementById('password').value;
  const authMsg  = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
  const regBtn   = document.getElementById('register-btn');
  if (!email || !password) { if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = 'Please fill all fields.'; return; }
  if (regBtn)  { regBtn.disabled = true; regBtn.textContent = 'Registering…'; }
  if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = '';
  _showTransitionOverlay('Creating your account…');
  _lockAuth(); 
  try {
    await createUserWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
    _hideTransitionOverlay();
    showProfileForm();
  } catch (error) {
    if (error.code === 'auth/email-already-in-use') {
      // This email may belong to an account that started registering but
      // never finished setting up their profile (e.g. the network dropped
      // partway through). Instead of blocking them outright, try signing
      // into that same account with the password they just typed — if
      // it's really them, this succeeds, and onAuthStateChanged/_routeUser
      // will correctly show the profile form again so they can continue
      // exactly where they left off, instead of being told the email is
      // "already taken."
      try {
        await signInWithEmailAndPassword(auth, email, password);
        _hideTransitionOverlay();
        // _routeUser takes over from here via onAuthStateChanged.
      } catch (signInErr) {
        console.warn('Resume-registration sign-in attempt failed:', signInErr.code);
        _hideTransitionOverlay();
        if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent =
          'This email is already registered. If it\'s yours, try logging in above instead — if you forgot your password, use "Forgot Password."';
        _unlockAuth();
      }
    } else {
      console.error('Register error:', error);
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = `Error: ${error.message}`;
      _unlockAuth();
    }
  } finally {
    if (regBtn) { regBtn.disabled = false; regBtn.textContent = 'Register'; }
  }
});


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  16. CORE ROUTING FUNCTION
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
// 🛠️ SELF-HEAL: repairs accounts broken by the free-text role-field bug
// (see saveProfile — the "System Role" input used to get written directly
// into users/{uid}.role, which the Firestore rules require to be EXACTLY
// "Teacher" for isActiveTeacher() to pass). Anyone who typed anything else
// got silently locked out of every results/attendance/fees write. This
// runs on every login for any account that reaches this teacher-only app
// and isn't headteacher/admin — if their stored role isn't precisely
// "Teacher", it's corrected via a self-write (always allowed: a user can
// always write their own users/{uid} doc). Fire-and-forget by design —
// never blocks or breaks login if it fails for any reason.
function _selfHealTeacherRole(uid, currentRole) {
  if (currentRole === 'Teacher') return; // already correct, nothing to do
  updateDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', uid), { role: 'Teacher' })
    .then(() => console.log('[Self-heal] Corrected role field to "Teacher" for this account.'))
    .catch(e => console.warn('[Self-heal] Could not correct role field:', e.message));
}

async function _routeUser(user) {
  if (_observerBusy) return;
  _observerBusy = true;

  const authMsg     = document.getElementById('auth-msg');
  const authSection = document.getElementById('auth-section');
  const dashboard   = document.getElementById('dashboard');
  if (authSection) authSection.style.display = 'none';
  if (dashboard)   dashboard.style.display   = 'none';
  if (typeof closeProfileModal === 'function') closeProfileModal();

  try {
    // Force a fresh ID token before anything touches Firestore. Custom
    // claims (used by the security rules) don't appear on a token until
    // it's force-refreshed — without this, a freshly-signed-in or
    // freshly-reopened session can hit real but temporary permission-denied
    // errors purely from token timing, which the subscription listener
    // below used to mistake for a revoked subscription and auto-logout on.
    try { await user.getIdToken(true); } catch (e) { console.warn('[Auth] Token refresh failed, continuing with cached token:', e.message); }

    const pendingProfile = await getIndexedDB('pendingProfiles', user.uid).catch(() => null);
    if (pendingProfile?.requestStatus === 'pending') {
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      renderPendingOverlay('pending', pendingProfile.schoolId || null);
      attachApprovalListener(user.uid);
      return;
    }

    const localProfile = await getIndexedDB('userProfile', user.uid).catch(() => null);
    if (localProfile?.schoolId) {
      if (localProfile.role === 'headteacher' || localProfile.role === 'admin') {
        if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = '⚠️ Headteachers and admins must use the Headteacher Dashboard.';
        _stopSubscription();
        await signOut(auth);
        _showAuthScreen();
        return;
      }

      currentTeacher = user.email; 
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      if (typeof showDashboard === 'function') showDashboard();
      _selfHealTeacherRole(user.uid, localProfile.role);

      if (!localProfile.accountStatus || localProfile.accountStatus === 'active') {
        if (typeof safeRestore_VB === 'function') safeRestore_VB(localProfile.schoolId);
        if (typeof syncData       === 'function') syncData();
        try {
          const queue = (await getIndexedDB('syncQueue')) || [];
          if (queue.length > 0 && typeof updateSyncStatus === 'function')
            updateSyncStatus('Not Synced – pending', '#ff4e50');
        } catch (_) {}

        _startSubscription(localProfile.schoolId);
      }
      return;
    }

    _showTransitionOverlay('Loading your profile…');
    const userDoc = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'users', user.uid));

    if (!userDoc.exists()) {
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      showProfileForm();
      return;
    }

    const userData      = userDoc.data();
    const schoolId      = userData.schoolId      || null;
    const accountStatus = userData.accountStatus || null;

    if (userData.role === 'headteacher' || userData.role === 'admin') {
      if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent = '⚠️ Headteachers and admins must use the Headteacher Dashboard.';
      _stopSubscription();
      await signOut(auth);
      _showAuthScreen();
      return;
    }
    _selfHealTeacherRole(user.uid, userData.role);

    if (accountStatus === 'pending') {
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      renderPendingOverlay('pending', schoolId);
      attachApprovalListener(user.uid);
      return;
    }
    if (accountStatus === 'rejected') {
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      renderPendingOverlay('rejected');
      return;
    }

    if (!schoolId) {
      console.error('Auth routing: active user has no schoolId. uid=', user.uid);
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      showProfileForm();
      return;
    }

    const teacherDoc = await getDoc(doc(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, 'teachers', user.uid));
    if (!teacherDoc.exists()) {
      _hideTransitionOverlay();
      showProfileForm();
      return;
    }

    await setIndexedDB('userProfile', user.uid, {
      uid: user.uid, schoolId, ...teacherDoc.data(),
      accountStatus: accountStatus || 'active', role: userData.role
    });

    currentTeacher = user.email; 
    _hideTransitionOverlay();

    if (typeof showDashboard  === 'function') showDashboard();
    if (typeof safeRestore_VB === 'function') safeRestore_VB(schoolId);
    if (typeof syncData       === 'function') syncData();

    _startSubscription(schoolId);

  } catch (error) {
    console.error('Auth state routing error:', error);
    _hideTransitionOverlay();
    _showAuthScreen();
    if (authMsg) authMsg.textContent =
      'Network error. Please connect to the internet to log in for the first time on this device.';
  } finally {
    _observerBusy = false;
  }
}


// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
//  17. AUTH STATE OBSERVER
// ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
onAuthStateChanged(auth, async (user) => {

  if (!user) {
    _observerBusy  = false;
    currentTeacher = null;

    if (typeof approvalUnsubscribe === 'function') {
      approvalUnsubscribe(); approvalUnsubscribe = null;
    }

    _stopSubscription();

    if (typeof hideAllSections   === 'function') hideAllSections();
    if (typeof clockInterval !== 'undefined' && clockInterval) clearInterval(clockInterval);
    _showAuthScreen();
    return;
  }

  if (_authLock) {
    _pendingRouteUser = user;
    return;
  }

  await _routeUser(user);
});







// ----------------- OTHER EVENT LISTENERS -----------------
window.addEventListener('online', () => {
  updateSyncStatus('Syncing…', '#f9d423');  // Assume defined
  syncData();
});

window.addEventListener('offline', () => {
  updateSyncStatus('Offline — sync when online', '#ff6f61');
});

window.addEventListener('popstate', (event) => {
  const state = event.state || {};
  const view = state.view || 'auth';
  if (view === 'auth') {
    document.getElementById('auth-section').style.display = 'block';
    document.getElementById('dashboard').style.display = 'none';
    if (typeof hideAllSections === 'function') {
      hideAllSections();
    }
  } else {
    if (typeof showSection === 'function') {
      showSection(view, { force: true });
    }
  }
});

document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
  try {
    await initIndexedDB();  // Assume defined
    if (typeof loadClasses === 'function') {
      loadClasses();
    }
    history.replaceState({ view: 'auth' }, '', '#auth');
  } catch (e) {
    console.error('Failed to initialize IndexedDB:', e);
    alert('Database initialization failed. Check console for details.');
  }

  // Splash screen (optional UX)
  const splashScreen = document.getElementById('splash-screen');
  if (splashScreen) {
    const progressFill = document.getElementById('progress-fill');
    let progress = 0;
    const interval = setInterval(() => {
      progress += 10;
      progressFill.style.width = `${progress}%`;
      if (progress >= 100) {
        clearInterval(interval);
        splashScreen.style.opacity = '0';
        setTimeout(() => splashScreen.style.display = 'none', 1200);
      }
    }, 300);
  }
});







if ('serviceWorker' in navigator) {
  window.addEventListener('load', () => {
    navigator.serviceWorker.register('service-worker.js')
      .then(reg => {
        console.log('Service Worker registered', reg.scope);
        showSwMessage(`✅ Service Worker registered successfully! Scope: ${reg.scope}`, 'success');
      })
      .catch(err => {
        console.error('Service Worker failed', err);
        showSwMessage(`⚠️ Service Worker registration failed: ${err.message}`, 'error');
      });
  });
}

/**
 * Display a temporary styled message on the page
 * @param {string} text - Message to show
 * @param {string} type - 'success' or 'error'
 */
function showSwMessage(text, type = 'success') {
  const msg = document.createElement('div');
  msg.textContent = text;
  msg.style.position = 'fixed';
  msg.style.bottom = '20px';
  msg.style.right = '20px';
  msg.style.padding = '12px 20px';
  msg.style.borderRadius = '8px';
  msg.style.color = '#fff';
  msg.style.fontFamily = 'Poppins, sans-serif';
  msg.style.fontSize = '14px';
  msg.style.zIndex = '9999';
  msg.style.boxShadow = '0 4px 12px rgba(0,0,0,0.15)';
  msg.style.opacity = '0';
  msg.style.transition = 'opacity 0.3s ease, transform 0.3s ease';

  if (type === 'success') {
    msg.style.background = 'linear-gradient(90deg, #00cc88, #009966)';
  } else {
    msg.style.background = 'linear-gradient(90deg, #ff4e50, #ff6f61)';
  }

  document.body.appendChild(msg);

  // Animate in
  requestAnimationFrame(() => {
    msg.style.opacity = '1';
    msg.style.transform = 'translateY(0)';
  });

  // Remove after 4 seconds
  setTimeout(() => {
    msg.style.opacity = '0';
    msg.style.transform = 'translateY(20px)';
    msg.addEventListener('transitionend', () => msg.remove());
  }, 4000);
}


// Debounce utility
function debounce(func, delay) {
  let timeout;
  return function(...args) {
    clearTimeout(timeout);
    timeout = setTimeout(() => func.apply(this, args), delay);
  };
}





  
  (function() {
    'use strict';
    
    // ── CONFIG ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const SNOOZE_KEY = 'tm_install_snoozed_until';
    const SNOOZE_DAYS = 3; // how many days "not now" snoozes
    const SHOW_DELAY_MS = 4000; // wait 4s after load before showing
    
    // ── DETECT ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const isIOS = /iphone|ipad|ipod/i.test(navigator.userAgent) &&
      !window.MSStream;
    const isSafari = /^((?!chrome|android).)*safari/i.test(navigator.userAgent);
    const isInStandaloneMode =
      window.matchMedia('(display-mode: standalone)').matches ||
      window.navigator.standalone === true;
    
    // Already installed — never show
    if (isInStandaloneMode) {
      console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Running as installed app ✅');
      return;
    }
    
    // Snoozed?
    const snoozedUntil = localStorage.getItem(SNOOZE_KEY);
    if (snoozedUntil && Date.now() < parseInt(snoozedUntil, 10)) {
      console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Install prompt snoozed until',
        new Date(parseInt(snoozedUntil, 10)).toLocaleString());
      return;
    }
    
    // ── ELEMENTS ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const banner = document.getElementById('tm-install-banner');
    const installBtn = document.getElementById('tm-install-btn');
    const dismissBtn = document.getElementById('tm-dismiss-btn');
    const notNowBtn = document.getElementById('tm-not-now-btn');
    const iosPanel = document.getElementById('tm-ios-instructions');
    const toast = document.getElementById('tm-installed-toast');
    
    if (!banner) return;
    
    // ── DEFERRED PROMPT (Chrome / Android) ─────────────────────────
    let deferredPrompt = null;
    
    window.addEventListener('beforeinstallprompt', (e) => {
      e.preventDefault(); // Stop the mini-infobar
      deferredPrompt = e;
      console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Install prompt captured ✅');
      // Show our premium banner after a short delay
      setTimeout(showBanner, SHOW_DELAY_MS);
    });
    

    
    // ── SHOW BANNER ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function showBanner() {
      if (!banner) return;
      
      // iOS Safari — show manual instructions instead of install button
      if (isIOS && isSafari) {
        if (installBtn) installBtn.style.display = 'none';
        if (iosPanel) iosPanel.style.display = 'block';
      }
      
      banner.style.display = 'block';
      console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Install banner shown');
    }
    
    // ── HIDE BANNER ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    function hideBanner(snooze = false) {
      if (!banner) return;
      banner.style.animation = 'tmBannerDown 0.3s ease both';
      setTimeout(() => { banner.style.display = 'none'; }, 300);
      
      if (snooze) {
        const until = Date.now() + (SNOOZE_DAYS * 24 * 60 * 60 * 1000);
        localStorage.setItem(SNOOZE_KEY, String(until));
        console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Snoozed for', SNOOZE_DAYS, 'days');
      }
    }
    
    // ── SHOW SUCCESS TOAST ─────────────────────────────────────────
    function showInstalledToast() {
      if (!toast) return;
      toast.style.display = 'block';
      setTimeout(() => {
        toast.style.opacity = '0';
        toast.style.transition = 'opacity 0.5s';
        setTimeout(() => { toast.style.display = 'none'; }, 500);
      }, 4000);
    }
    
    // ── INSTALL BUTTON CLICK ───────────────────────────────────────
    if (installBtn) {
      installBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
        if (!deferredPrompt) {
          // Fallback for browsers without beforeinstallprompt
          console.warn('[TeachMate PWA] No deferred prompt available');
          return;
        }
        
        installBtn.textContent = '⏳ Installing…';
        installBtn.disabled = true;
        
        try {
          await deferredPrompt.prompt();
          const choice = await deferredPrompt.userChoice;
          console.log('[TeachMate PWA] User choice:', choice.outcome);
          
          if (choice.outcome === 'accepted') {
            hideBanner(false);
            showInstalledToast();
            // Clear snooze since they installed
            localStorage.removeItem(SNOOZE_KEY);
          } else {
            // Dismissed from OS prompt — snooze our banner
            hideBanner(true);
          }
        } catch (err) {
          console.warn('[TeachMate PWA] Install prompt error:', err.message);
        } finally {
          deferredPrompt = null;
          installBtn.textContent = '⬇ Install TeachMate Pro — It\'s Free';
          installBtn.disabled = false;
        }
      });
    }
    
    // ── DISMISS (X) ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Snoozes for SNOOZE_DAYS days
    if (dismissBtn) {
      dismissBtn.addEventListener('click', () => hideBanner(true));
    }
    
    // ── NOT NOW ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    if (notNowBtn) {
      notNowBtn.addEventListener('click', () => hideBanner(true));
    }
    
    // ── APP INSTALLED EVENT ────────────────────────────────────────
    window.addEventListener('appinstalled', () => {
      console.log('[TeachMate PWA] App installed! 🎉');
      hideBanner(false);
      showInstalledToast();
      deferredPrompt = null;
      localStorage.removeItem(SNOOZE_KEY);
    });
    
    // ── iOS: Show banner after delay if in Safari ──────────────────
    // (iOS never fires beforeinstallprompt so we show manually)
    if (isIOS && isSafari) {
      setTimeout(showBanner, SHOW_DELAY_MS);
    }
    
    console.log('[TeachMate PWA] Install engine ready ✅');
    
  })();





  </script>

  <!-- Additional JS for Student Reports -->
  <script>
    const jsPDF = function(){ var a = arguments; var C = window.jspdf.jsPDF; return new (Function.prototype.bind.apply(C, [null].concat([].slice.call(a))))(); };
    let subjectsCount = 0;

    // IndexedDB setup
    let db;
    const request = indexedDB.open("ECZResultsDB", 1);
    request.onerror = () => console.log("DB Error");
    request.onsuccess = () => db = request.result;
    request.onupgradeneeded = (e) => {
      db = e.target.result;
      if (!db.objectStoreNames.contains("students")) {
        db.createObjectStore("students", { keyPath: "id" });
      }
    };

    // Zambian Grading Function
    function calculateGrade(score) {
      if (score >= 75) return "Distinction";
      if (score >= 50) return "Merit";
      if (score >= 30) return "Pass";
      return "Fail";
    }

    // Add Subject Row
    function addSubjectRow() {
      subjectsCount++;
      const tbody = document.querySelector("#subjectsTable tbody");
      const row = document.createElement("tr");
      row.innerHTML = `
        <td><input type="text" placeholder="Enter Subject"></td>
        <td><input type="number" placeholder="Enter Score" min="0" max="100" onchange="updateGrade(this)"></td>
        <td class="gradeCell"></td>
        <td><button onclick="removeRow(this)">Remove</button></td>
      `;
      tbody.appendChild(row);
    }

    function updateGrade(scoreInput) {
      const gradeCell = scoreInput.parentElement.nextElementSibling;
      gradeCell.textContent = calculateGrade(parseInt(scoreInput.value) || 0);
    }

    function removeRow(btn) {
      btn.parentElement.parentElement.remove();
    }

    // Save student data
    function saveStudentData() {
      const student = {
        id: document.getElementById("studentName").value,
        className: document.getElementById("className").value,
        studentName: document.getElementById("studentName").value,
        examType: document.getElementById("examType").value,
        schoolName: document.getElementById("schoolName").value,
        teacherName: document.getElementById("teacherName").value,
        remarks: document.getElementById("remarks").value,
        subjects: Array.from(document.querySelectorAll("#subjectsTable tbody tr")).map(row => ({
          subject: row.cells[0].querySelector("input").value,
          score: row.cells[1].querySelector("input").value,
          grade: row.cells[2].textContent
        }))
      };
      const tx = db.transaction("students", "readwrite");
      const store = tx.objectStore("students");
      store.put(student);
      tx.oncomplete = () => alert("Student data saved successfully!");
    }

    // Load student data
    function loadStudentData() {
      const studentName = document.getElementById("studentName").value;
      const tx = db.transaction("students", "readonly");
      const store = tx.objectStore("students");
      const request = store.get(studentName);
      request.onsuccess = (e) => {
        const student = e.target.result;
        if (!student) return alert("No data found for this student.");
        document.getElementById("className").value = student.className;
        document.getElementById("examType").value = student.examType;
        document.getElementById("schoolName").value = student.schoolName;
        document.getElementById("teacherName").value = student.teacherName;
        document.getElementById("remarks").value = student.remarks;

        const tbody = document.querySelector("#subjectsTable tbody");
        tbody.innerHTML = "";
        student.subjects.forEach(sub => {
          const row = document.createElement("tr");
          row.innerHTML = `
            <td><input type="text" value="${sub.subject}"></td>
            <td><input type="number" value="${sub.score}" onchange="updateGrade(this)"></td>
            <td class="gradeCell">${sub.grade}</td>
            <td><button onclick="removeRow(this)">Remove</button></td>
          `;
          tbody.appendChild(row);
        });
      };
    }

</script>


<script>
  (function initGlobalPhotoZoomListener() {
    if (window.__PHOTO_ZOOM_MONITOR__) return;
    window.__PHOTO_ZOOM_MONITOR__ = true;

    const modal = document.getElementById('photo-modal');
    const modalImg = document.getElementById('photo-modal-img');
    const closeBtn = document.getElementById('photo-modal-close');

    if (!modal || !modalImg || !closeBtn) return;

    let lastTap = 0;

    // Zoom state
    let scale = 1;
    let startScale = 1;
    let startDistance = 0;
    let translateX = 0;
    let translateY = 0;
    let startX = 0;
    let startY = 0;
    let isPanning = false;

    function applyTransform() {
      modalImg.style.transform =
        `translate(${translateX}px, ${translateY}px) scale(${scale})`;
    }

    function resetTransform() {
      scale = 1;
      translateX = 0;
      translateY = 0;
      applyTransform();
    }

    function openZoom(img) {
      const src = img.dataset.full || img.dataset.src || img.src;
      if (!src) return;

      resetTransform();
      modalImg.src = src;

      modal.style.display = 'flex';
      modal.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.style.overflow = 'hidden';
      document.body.style.touchAction = 'none'; // 🔒 freeze background

      requestAnimationFrame(() => {
        modal.style.opacity = '1';
      });
    }

    function closeZoom() {
      modal.style.opacity = '0';
      document.body.style.overflow = '';
      document.body.style.touchAction = '';

      setTimeout(() => {
        modal.style.display = 'none';
        modalImg.src = '';
        resetTransform();
      }, 300);
    }

    /* -----------------------------
       DESKTOP → DOUBLE CLICK
       ----------------------------- */
    document.addEventListener('dblclick', e => {
      const img = e.target.closest('img.zoomable, img[data-zoomable]');
      if (!img) return;
      if (img.closest('#class-management')) return;
      if (img.classList.contains('no-zoom')) return;

      e.preventDefault();
      e.stopPropagation();
      openZoom(img);
    }, true);

    /* -----------------------------
       MOBILE → DOUBLE TAP
       ----------------------------- */
    document.addEventListener('touchend', e => {
      const img = e.target.closest('img.zoomable, img[data-zoomable]');
      if (!img) return;
      if (img.closest('#class-management')) return;
      if (img.classList.contains('no-zoom')) return;

      const now = Date.now();
      if (now - lastTap < 300) {
        e.preventDefault();
        openZoom(img);
      }
      lastTap = now;
    }, true);

    /* -----------------------------
       PINCH TO ZOOM
       ----------------------------- */
    modalImg.addEventListener('touchstart', e => {
      if (e.touches.length === 2) {
        startDistance = Math.hypot(
          e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX,
          e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY
        );
        startScale = scale;
      } else if (e.touches.length === 1 && scale > 1) {
        isPanning = true;
        startX = e.touches[0].clientX - translateX;
        startY = e.touches[0].clientY - translateY;
      }
    }, { passive: false });

    modalImg.addEventListener('touchmove', e => {
      e.preventDefault();

      if (e.touches.length === 2) {
        const newDistance = Math.hypot(
          e.touches[0].clientX - e.touches[1].clientX,
          e.touches[0].clientY - e.touches[1].clientY
        );
        scale = Math.min(Math.max(startScale * (newDistance / startDistance), 1), 4);
        applyTransform();
      } else if (e.touches.length === 1 && isPanning) {
        translateX = e.touches[0].clientX - startX;
        translateY = e.touches[0].clientY - startY;
        applyTransform();
      }
    }, { passive: false });

    modalImg.addEventListener('touchend', () => {
      isPanning = false;
    });

    /* -----------------------------
       CLOSE HANDLERS
       ----------------------------- */
    closeBtn.onclick = closeZoom;

    modal.addEventListener('click', e => {
      if (e.target === modal) closeZoom();
    });

    document.addEventListener('keydown', e => {
      if (e.key === 'Escape' && modal.style.display !== 'none') {
        closeZoom();
      }
    });

  })();




  // ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  TeachMate Master Sync Listener
//  Downloads all data safely and warns about any missing/failed features
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function masterSyncTeacherData(schoolId) {
  const collectionsWithPhotos = ['students'];
  const featureWarnings = [];
  const downloadedFeatures = [];

  for (const coll of ALL_COLLECTIONS) {
    try {
      const collRef = collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, coll);
      const snapshot = await getDocs(collRef);

      const serverIds = [];

      for (const doc of snapshot.docs) {
        const docId = doc.id;
        serverIds.push(docId);

        const serverData = doc.data() || {};
        const localData = await getIndexedDB(coll, docId);

        // Preserve local photos
        let dataToSave = { ...serverData, id: docId };
        if (collectionsWithPhotos.includes(coll) && localData?.photo) {
          dataToSave.photo = localData.photo;
        }

        await setIndexedDB(coll, docId, dataToSave);
