        await addToSyncQueue(`${coll}/${docId}`, 'set', dataToSave);
      }

      // Only delete truly corrupt local entries: students with no studentId
      const localAll = await getIndexedDB(coll);
      if (Array.isArray(localAll)) {
        for (const local of localAll) {
          if (
            local &&
            typeof local.id !== 'undefined' &&
            coll === 'students' &&
            (!local.studentId || !local.studentId.trim())
          ) {
            await deleteIndexedDB(coll, local.id);
          }
        }
      }

      downloadedFeatures.push(coll);
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`⚠️ Failed to sync ${coll}: ${err.message}`);
      featureWarnings.push(coll);
    }
  }

  // Show feature-specific messages
  if (featureWarnings.length > 0) {
    showToast(
      `⚠️ Sync completed, but these features may have missing data: ${featureWarnings.join(', ')}`,
      'warning'
    );
  } else {
    showToast(
      `✅ All features synced successfully: ${downloadedFeatures.join(', ')}`,
      'success'
    );
  }

  // Refresh UI for all features
  try {
    if (typeof refreshAttendance === 'function') await refreshAttendance();
    if (typeof refreshResults === 'function') await refreshResults();
    if (typeof refreshFees === 'function') await refreshFees();
    if (typeof refreshClassManagement === 'function') await refreshClassManagement();
  } catch (uiErr) {
    console.warn('UI refresh failed:', uiErr.message);
  }
}

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Automatic Listener for Teacher Login
//  Will trigger master sync after login
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded', async () => {
  if (!window.currentTeacherSchoolId) return;

  try {
    showLoading('Syncing your data with TeachMate...');
    await masterSyncTeacherData(window.currentTeacherSchoolId);
  } catch (err) {
    console.error('Master sync failed:', err.message);
    showToast('⚠️ Could not sync all data. Check console for details.', 'error');
  } finally {
    hideLoading();
  }
});

// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
//  Feature Checker – highlights missing records after master sync
// ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function checkFeatureSync(schoolId) {
  const featureIssues = {};

  for (const coll of ALL_COLLECTIONS) {
    try {
      const collRef = collection(fdb, 'schools', schoolId, coll);
      const snapshot = await getDocs(collRef);

      const serverIds = snapshot.docs.map(d => d.id);
      const localAll = await getIndexedDB(coll);
      const localIds = Array.isArray(localAll) ? localAll.map(l => l?.id).filter(Boolean) : [];

      // Find missing locally
      const missingLocally = serverIds.filter(id => !localIds.includes(id));

      // Find extra/missing on server for records with IDs
      const missingOnServer = localIds.filter(id => !serverIds.includes(id));

      if (missingLocally.length || missingOnServer.length) {
        featureIssues[coll] = { missingLocally, missingOnServer };
      }
    } catch (err) {
      console.warn(`⚠️ Failed feature check for ${coll}:`, err.message);
      featureIssues[coll] = { error: err.message };
    }
  }

// Show results
if (Object.keys(featureIssues).length > 0) {
  console.group('%c⚠️ TeachMate Sync Issues', 'color: orange; font-weight: bold;');
  for (const [feature, info] of Object.entries(featureIssues)) {
    console.group(`Feature: ${feature}`);
    if (info.error) {
      console.log('Error during check:', info.error);
    } else {
      if (info.missingLocally.length > 0) {
        console.log('Missing locally:', info.missingLocally);
      }
      if (info.missingOnServer.length > 0) {
        console.log('Extra/missing on server:', info.missingOnServer);
      }
    }
    console.groupEnd();
  }
  console.groupEnd();

  showToast(
    '⚠️ Some features have missing records. Check console for details.',
    'warning'
  );
} else {
  showToast('✅ All features fully synced with server!', 'success');
}
}

  ];

  // Zambian context pools used by the template engine
  const ZM_TOWNS    = ['Lusaka','Ndola','Kitwe','Livingstone','Chipata','Solwezi','Kasama','Mongu','Kabwe','Mazabuka','Choma','Mansa','Petauke','Mpika','Samfya'];
  const ZM_NAMES    = ['Mulenga','Chanda','Bwalya','Mutale','Nkonde','Tembo','Phiri','Banda','Zulu','Mwanza','Kaputa','Sichone','Mwape','Chiluba','Lesa'];

  const pick = arr => arr[Math.floor(Math.random() * arr.length)];
  const picks = (arr, n) => { const s = [...arr].sort(()=>Math.random()-.5); return s.slice(0,n); };

  // ─── STORE KEY ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const LP_STORE = 'lp_v4_plans';

  // ─── UTILS ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  const esc  = s => String(s ?? '').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
  const uid  = () => 'lp_' + Date.now() + '_' + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2,6);
  const now  = () => new Date().toISOString();
  const sel  = id => document.getElementById(id);
  const val  = id => sel(id)?.value?.trim() ?? '';

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  SMART AUTO-FILL ENGINE  (identical to AI version)
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function smartAutoFill() {
    const level    = val('f-level');
    const subject  = val('f-subject');
    const learners = parseInt(val('f-learners') || '0', 10);
    const setup    = val('f-setup');
    const sen      = val('f-sen');
    const qtypes   = val('f-qtypes');
    const extra    = val('f-extra');

    const isECE     = level.startsWith('ECE');
    const isLower   = ['Grade 1','Grade 2','Grade 3','Grade 4'].includes(level);
    const isUpper   = ['Grade 5','Grade 6','Grade 7'].includes(level);
    const isJunSec  = ['Form 1','Form 2'].includes(level);
    const isSenSec  = ['Form 3','Form 4','Form 5','Form 6'].includes(level);
    const isSecondary = isJunSec || isSenSec;

    const scienceSubjects = ['Biology','Chemistry','Physics','Science','Mathematics and Science','Agricultural Science','Computer Science','Information and Communication Technology'];
    const mathSubjects    = ['Mathematics','Mathematics and Science','Principles of Accounts'];
    const practicalSubjects = ['Art and Design','Music','Physical Education','Fashion and Fabrics','Food and Nutrition','Home Economics and Hospitality','Technology Studies','Creative and Technology Studies','Design and Technology','Travel and Tourism','Expressive Arts'];
    const langSubjects = ['English Language','Literacy and Language','French','Literature in English','Literature in Zambian Languages','Pre-Literacy and Language','Sign Language','Braille'];

    const isScience   = scienceSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isMath      = mathSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isPractical = practicalSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isLang      = langSubjects.includes(subject);

    const autoFilled = [];

    if (!sen || sen === 'No identified SEN') {
      let autoSEN = 'No identified SEN';
      if (learners >= 35 || isECE || isLower) autoSEN = 'General Mixed Ability';
      if (autoSEN !== sen) { const el=sel('f-sen'); if(el) el.value=autoSEN; autoFilled.push(`SEN → "${autoSEN}"`); }
    }

    const isDefaultSetup = !setup || setup === 'Mixed Grouping';
    if (isDefaultSetup) {
      let autoSetup = 'Mixed Grouping';
      if (isECE || isLower) autoSetup = 'Whole Class';
      else if (isPractical) autoSetup = 'Group Work';
      else if (isScience && isSecondary) autoSetup = 'Group Work';
      else if (isMath && isSecondary) autoSetup = 'Individual Work';
      else if (isLang && isUpper) autoSetup = 'Pair Work';
      else if (isSenSec) autoSetup = 'Individual Work';
      if (autoSetup !== setup) { const el=sel('f-setup'); if(el) el.value=autoSetup; autoFilled.push(`Setup → "${autoSetup}"`); }
    }

    const isDefaultQtype = !qtypes || qtypes === 'Mixed Variety (Recommended)';
    if (isDefaultQtype) {
      let autoQtype = 'Mixed Variety (Recommended)';
      if (isECE || isLower) autoQtype = 'Fill-in-the-Blanks';
      else if (isUpper && isLang) autoQtype = 'Short Answer + Structured';
      else if (isMath) autoQtype = 'Problem-Solving';
      else if (isScience && isSecondary) autoQtype = 'MCQ + Short Answer';
      else if (isSenSec) autoQtype = 'Short Answer + Structured';
      else if (isPractical) autoQtype = 'Short Answer Only';
      else if (isJunSec) autoQtype = 'MCQ + Short Answer';
      if (autoQtype !== qtypes) { const el=sel('f-qtypes'); if(el) el.value=autoQtype; autoFilled.push(`Question Types → "${autoQtype}"`); }
    }

    if (!extra) {
      const instrParts = [];
      instrParts.push('Use authentic Zambian local examples, names, towns, and contexts throughout');
      if (isECE || isLower) { instrParts.push('Use very simple language suitable for young learners'); instrParts.push('Include a short rhyme, chant, or song related to the topic'); instrParts.push('Use lots of pictures, drawings, and hands-on activities'); }
      else if (isUpper) { instrParts.push('Include at least one practical hands-on activity'); instrParts.push('Use relatable examples from Zambian daily life and environment'); }
      else if (isJunSec) { instrParts.push('Balance theory with practical application'); instrParts.push('Include critical thinking questions to stretch learners'); }
      else if (isSenSec) { instrParts.push('Align questions to ECZ examination standard and format'); instrParts.push('Include higher-order thinking questions'); instrParts.push('Reference past ECZ exam question patterns where applicable'); }
      if (isScience) instrParts.push('Suggest a simple experiment or demonstration using locally available materials');
      if (isMath) instrParts.push('Use real Zambian contexts for word problems (market prices, farming, distances between towns)');
      if (isPractical) instrParts.push('Describe practical steps clearly and include safety precautions where applicable');
      if (isLang) instrParts.push('Include a short reading or listening comprehension passage set in a Zambian context');
      if (learners >= 40) instrParts.push('Suggest class management strategies suitable for large classes of 40+ learners with limited resources');
      const el=sel('f-extra'); if(el) el.value=instrParts.join('. ');
      autoFilled.push('Additional Instructions (auto-generated)');
    }

    return autoFilled;
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  TEMPLATE-BASED LESSON PLAN GENERATOR
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function generatePlan(f) {
    const level    = f.level;
    const subject  = f.subject;
    const topic    = f.topic;
    const subtopic = f.subtopic;
    const comp     = f.competences;
    const acts     = f.activities;
    const standard = f.standard;
    const sen      = f.sen;
    const setup    = f.setup;
    const qtypes   = f.qtypes;
    const duration = f.duration;
    const learners = f.learners || '35';

    const isECE    = level.startsWith('ECE');
    const isLower  = ['Grade 1','Grade 2','Grade 3','Grade 4'].includes(level);
    const isUpper  = ['Grade 5','Grade 6','Grade 7'].includes(level);
    const isJunSec = ['Form 1','Form 2'].includes(level);
    const isSenSec = ['Form 3','Form 4','Form 5','Form 6'].includes(level);
    const isPrimary   = isECE || isLower || isUpper;
    const isSecondary = isJunSec || isSenSec;

    const scienceSubjects = ['Biology','Chemistry','Physics','Science','Mathematics and Science','Agricultural Science','Computer Science','Information and Communication Technology'];
    const mathSubjects    = ['Mathematics','Mathematics and Science','Principles of Accounts'];
    const practicalSubjects = ['Art and Design','Music','Physical Education','Fashion and Fabrics','Food and Nutrition','Home Economics and Hospitality','Technology Studies','Creative and Technology Studies','Design and Technology','Travel and Tourism','Expressive Arts'];
    const langSubjects = ['English Language','Literacy and Language','French','Literature in English','Literature in Zambian Languages','Pre-Literacy and Language','Sign Language','Braille'];
    const socialSubjects = ['Civic Education','Commerce','Geography','History','Religious Education','Social Studies'];

    const isScience   = scienceSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isMath      = mathSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isPractical = practicalSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isLang      = langSubjects.includes(subject);
    const isSocial    = socialSubjects.includes(subject);

    const town1 = pick(ZM_TOWNS);
    const town2 = pick(ZM_TOWNS.filter(t=>t!==town1));
    const name1 = pick(ZM_NAMES);
    const name2 = pick(ZM_NAMES.filter(n=>n!==name1));
    const river = pick(ZM_RIVERS);
    const crop  = pick(ZM_CROPS);
    const market = pick(ZM_MARKETS);

    // ── Parse duration into minutes ──────────────────────────────────────────
    const totalMins = parseInt(duration) || 40;
    const introMins = Math.max(5, Math.round(totalMins * 0.22));
    const devMins   = Math.max(10, Math.round(totalMins * 0.60));
    const concMins  = totalMins - introMins - devMins;

    // ── Lesson Goal ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const goalTemplates = [
      `By the end of this lesson, learners should be able to demonstrate understanding of ${subtopic} within the context of ${topic} as outlined in the Zambia ${isSecondary ? 'secondary' : 'primary'} school curriculum.`,
      `This lesson aims to equip learners with the knowledge and skills related to ${subtopic}, enabling them to apply concepts of ${topic} in real-life Zambian situations.`,
      `Learners will develop a clear understanding of ${topic}, specifically ${subtopic}, and relate it to everyday experiences in Zambia.`,
    ];
    const lessonGoal = pick(goalTemplates);

    // ── Rationale ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const rationalePool = [
      `Understanding ${topic} is essential for learners at ${level} as it forms the foundation for advanced concepts in ${subject}. In Zambia, knowledge of ${topic} is directly relevant to daily life in communities like ${town1} and ${town2}. ${isScience ? `Scientific understanding of this topic enables learners to explain natural phenomena observed in the Zambian environment.` : ''} ${isMath ? `Mathematical competency in this area supports practical problem-solving in local markets, farming, and commerce.` : ''} ${isLang ? `Language skills built here improve communication, reading, and comprehension across all subjects.` : ''} ${isSocial ? `Civic awareness of this topic prepares learners for responsible citizenship in Zambian society.` : ''}`,
      `${topic} is a key component of the ${subject} syllabus for ${level} in Zambia. Learners who grasp ${subtopic} will be better prepared for ${isSenSec ? 'ECZ examinations and further education' : 'higher grade-level concepts'}. Connecting classroom learning to Zambian contexts — such as ${isScience ? `experiments using local materials` : isMath ? `word problems involving ${market} prices and ${crop} farming` : `examples from ${town1}`} — makes learning meaningful and memorable.`,
    ];
    const rationale = pick(rationalePool);

    // ── Prior Knowledge ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const priorPool = [
      `Learners are expected to have a basic understanding of ${subject} from their previous ${isECE||isLower ? 'early childhood' : isUpper ? 'lower primary' : isJunSec ? 'primary school' : 'junior secondary'} studies. They should be familiar with general terminology used in ${subject} and have encountered introductory concepts related to ${topic} in earlier lessons or grades.`,
      `Before this lesson, learners should already know: (1) Basic definitions and concepts in ${subject}, (2) General knowledge of ${topic} at an introductory level, (3) Simple vocabulary and terms relevant to ${subject} as taught in previous lessons. The teacher should briefly revise these at the start of the lesson.`,
    ];
    const priorKnowledge = pick(priorPool);

    // ── Learning Environment ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    let naturalEnv, artificialEnv, techEnv;
    if (isScience) {
      naturalEnv = `Natural specimens related to ${topic} where available (e.g. plant samples, soil from around ${town1}, local water sources). Outdoor observation near the school grounds.`;
      artificialEnv = `Classroom with blackboard/chalkboard, charts, diagrams of ${topic}, lab equipment if available (test tubes, beakers), printed worksheets.`;
      techEnv = `Scientific calculator, projector or TV screen if available, educational posters. Mobile phone torch for demonstrations if electricity is unavailable.`;
    } else if (isMath) {
      naturalEnv = `Real-world objects for counting and measurement: stones, seeds, bottle caps, maize grains for younger classes; rulers, measuring tapes for older classes.`;
      artificialEnv = `Classroom with blackboard, graph paper, rulers, protractors, printed number charts and formula sheets. ${isSenSec ? 'ECZ past paper question sets.' : ''}`;
      techEnv = `Scientific calculator (${isSecondary?'essential':'optional'}), projector if available. No advanced ICT required.`;
    } else if (isPractical) {
      naturalEnv = `Materials sourced locally where possible (clay, fabric, local wood, garden produce). School garden or sports field.`;
      artificialEnv = `Workshop or classroom space arranged for practical work. Tools, equipment and materials relevant to ${topic}. Safety equipment where required.`;
      techEnv = `Minimal ICT required. Printed reference guides and illustrated instruction sheets.`;
    } else if (isLang) {
      naturalEnv = `Real texts and reading materials found in the Zambian community: newspapers, packaging, signs, local stories.`;
      artificialEnv = `Classroom with blackboard, printed reading passages set in ${town1}, vocabulary charts, writing materials, sentence-building cards.`;
      techEnv = `Audio recordings of passages if available. Radio broadcast educational programmes.`;
    } else {
      naturalEnv = `Community resources relevant to ${topic}: maps of Zambia, photographs of ${town1} and ${town2}, local newspapers with relevant articles.`;
      artificialEnv = `Classroom with blackboard, printed diagrams and illustrations of ${topic}, cut-out charts, reference textbooks.`;
      techEnv = `Projector or TV if available. Printed visual aids as primary teaching tools.`;
    }

    // ── Teaching Materials ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const baseMaterials = ['Chalk and chalkboard', 'Notebooks and pens/pencils', 'Printed worksheets', `${subject} textbook (${level} level)`, 'Charts and diagrams related to the topic'];
    const extraMaterials = isScience ? ['Specimen samples', 'Lab equipment (basic)', 'Safety goggles (if available)'] :
                          isMath    ? ['Geometric sets', 'Graph paper', 'Calculators'] :
                          isPractical ? ['Workshop tools', 'Safety equipment', 'Raw materials'] :
                          isLang    ? ['Reading passage handouts', 'Vocabulary word cards', 'Dictionary'] :
                                      ['Map of Zambia', 'Coloured markers', 'Reference posters'];
    const teachingMaterials = [...baseMaterials, ...picks(extraMaterials, 2)];

    // ── Lesson Progression ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    // Introduction
    const introTeacher_pool = [
      `Greet learners and call the class to order. Write the topic "${topic}" and sub-topic "${subtopic}" on the board. Begin with a brief review question: ask learners what they already know about ${topic}. Take 2–3 responses and affirm correct answers. ${isECE||isLower ? `Lead learners in a short rhyme or chant related to the topic to capture attention.` : `Pose a thought-provoking question linking ${topic} to everyday life in ${town1}.`} State the lesson objectives clearly to learners.`,
      `Start the lesson by welcoming learners. Write the date, topic, and sub-topic on the board. Conduct a 3-minute warm-up activity: ${isECE||isLower ? `ask learners to name things they see around them related to the topic` : `ask 2–3 questions linking ${topic} to their daily experiences in Zambia`}. ${isSenSec ? `Briefly review a relevant ECZ-style question from a past paper to activate prior knowledge.` : ``} Clearly state what learners will be able to do by the end of the lesson.`,
    ];
    const introLearner_pool = [
      `Learners respond to the teacher's warm-up questions. They listen attentively, write the topic and sub-topic in their notebooks. ${isECE||isLower ? `Learners participate in the rhyme/chant enthusiastically.` : `Learners contribute ideas about ${topic} from their own experience.`} They write the lesson objective in their notebooks.`,
      `Learners settle quickly and participate in the warm-up activity. They share their prior knowledge about ${topic}, respond to teacher questions, and copy the topic, sub-topic, and objectives into their notes.`,
    ];
    const introAssess_pool = [
      `Observe learner participation. Check if learners can recall basic prior knowledge about ${topic}. Ask a targeted question: "Who can tell me one thing you know about ${topic}?" Use responses to gauge starting level.`,
      `Use oral questioning to assess prior knowledge. Correct misconceptions immediately. Note which learners are confident and which need additional support during the lesson.`,
    ];

    // Development
    const devTeacher_pool = [
      `Present the main content of ${subtopic} step by step on the board. Use diagrams, charts and illustrations. ${isScience ? `Conduct a brief demonstration related to ${topic} using locally available materials. Explain each step clearly.` : isMath ? `Work through 2 example problems related to ${topic} on the board, showing all working. Use Zambian contexts: e.g. "${name1} buys ${crop} at ${market} in ${town1} — calculate..."` : isPractical ? `Demonstrate the practical skill step by step. Emphasise safety. Have learners observe closely before attempting.` : isLang ? `Distribute the reading passage. Read it aloud with good expression. Point out key vocabulary words: write them on the board and explain meanings with examples from ${town1}.` : `Use the board to present key concepts of ${subtopic} with labelled diagrams. Draw comparisons to Zambian situations in ${town1} and ${town2}.`} Divide learners into ${setup === 'Group Work' ? 'groups of 4–5' : setup === 'Pair Work' ? 'pairs' : setup === 'Individual Work' ? 'individual seats' : 'mixed groups'} for a guided activity. Circulate and provide support. ${isSenSec ? `Highlight links to ECZ examination questions and marking schemes.` : ``}`,
      `Introduce ${subtopic} using a structured explanation. Write key definitions on the board and have learners copy them. ${isScience ? `Involve learners in a class experiment or demonstration. Assign roles: recorder, observer, materials handler.` : isMath ? `Model 3 worked examples on the board, increasing in difficulty. Pause after each and ask: "Does everyone follow? Who can explain the next step?"` : isPractical ? `Break the practical task into stages. Demonstrate each stage before learners attempt it. Monitor for correct technique.` : `Use questioning to build understanding step by step: "What do you notice about...?", "Why do you think...?", "How does this connect to what you already know?"`} Facilitate the structured activity, asking probing questions as you circulate.`,
    ];
    const devLearner_pool = [
      `Learners listen attentively and take notes. ${isScience ? `Learners participate in the demonstration, recording observations in their notebooks.` : isMath ? `Learners copy worked examples and attempt guided problems on mini-boards or in their books.` : isPractical ? `Learners observe the demonstration then attempt the practical activity under teacher supervision.` : isLang ? `Learners read the passage silently, then chorally. They identify key vocabulary and write definitions.` : `Learners engage with the content, answer teacher questions, and copy diagrams and notes from the board.`} Learners carry out the ${setup === 'Group Work' ? 'group' : setup === 'Pair Work' ? 'pair' : 'individual'} activity assigned by the teacher. They ask questions when unclear.`,
      `Learners engage actively with the lesson content. They write definitions, copy diagrams, and answer oral questions. In their assigned groups/pairs, learners work on the guided activity, discuss ideas, and compare answers. They use their notes to support their responses.`,
    ];
    const devAssess_pool = [
      `Circulate and observe learner work. Ask targeted questions: "Can you explain this step in your own words?" Check notebooks for correct notes and diagrams. Identify learners who are struggling and offer targeted support. Adjust pace if most learners are confused.`,
      `Use questioning throughout: ask both confident and quiet learners. Check group work for accuracy. Use mini-whiteboards or slates if available for quick whole-class checks. Redirect misconceptions on the board immediately.`,
    ];

    // Conclusion
    const concTeacher_pool = [
      `Bring the class back together. Summarise the key points of ${subtopic} on the board in 3–5 bullet points. Ask 2–3 learners to share one thing they learned today. Link today's content to the next lesson topic. Give instructions for the class exercise and homework clearly.`,
      `Consolidate learning by reviewing the lesson objectives: "Did we achieve what we set out to do?" Ask learners to give a thumbs-up/thumbs-down to self-assess their understanding. Summarise key points. Assign the class exercise and homework. Remind learners to revise for the next lesson.`,
    ];
    const concLearner_pool = [
      `Learners participate in the summary discussion. They share what they have learned. Learners write down the class exercise questions and homework in their notes. They pack up quietly and orderly.`,
      `Learners respond to the teacher's closing questions. They self-assess their understanding. Learners copy class exercise and homework assignments and confirm they understand what is required.`,
    ];
    const concAssess_pool = [
      `Use exit-ticket questioning: "Name one new thing you learned about ${topic}." Observe engagement levels. Review a sample of notebooks to check quality of notes before dismissing. Make notes of learners needing follow-up.`,
      `Ask 3 learners to summarise the lesson in one sentence. Check homework instructions are clear to all. Note any common misconceptions observed during the lesson to address at the start of the next lesson.`,
    ];

    const lessonProgression = [
      { phase: 'Introduction', duration: `${introMins} minutes`, teacherActivity: pick(introTeacher_pool), learnerActivity: pick(introLearner_pool), assessmentCriteria: pick(introAssess_pool) },
      { phase: 'Development',  duration: `${devMins} minutes`,   teacherActivity: pick(devTeacher_pool),   learnerActivity: pick(devLearner_pool),   assessmentCriteria: pick(devAssess_pool)   },
      { phase: 'Conclusion',   duration: `${concMins} minutes`,  teacherActivity: pick(concTeacher_pool),  learnerActivity: pick(concLearner_pool),  assessmentCriteria: pick(concAssess_pool)  },
    ];

    // ── Cross-Cutting Issues ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const allCCI = [
      { issue: 'Gender', description: `Ensure equal participation of boys and girls in all activities. Avoid gender-stereotyped examples. Use both male and female Zambian names (e.g. ${name1} and ${name2}) in examples and questions to promote gender equity.` },
      { issue: 'HIV/AIDS Awareness', description: `Where appropriate, link the lesson to health awareness messages. Encourage learners to support peers who may be affected and reinforce messages about healthy living relevant to Zambian communities.` },
      { issue: 'Environmental Education', description: `Connect ${topic} to environmental stewardship. Discuss the importance of protecting Zambia's natural resources such as the ${river} River and wildlife. ${isScience ? `Link experimental materials to sustainable use of resources.` : ``}` },
      { issue: 'Peace and Values', description: `Promote teamwork, respect for others, and peaceful co-existence during group and pair activities. Model positive values in classroom interactions and encourage learners to respect diverse viewpoints.` },
      { issue: 'Life Skills', description: `Link ${topic} to practical life skills. ${isMath ? `Show how mathematical concepts apply to budgeting, market trading, and farming in Zambia.` : isLang ? `Highlight how strong communication skills support success in daily Zambian life.` : `Encourage learners to think critically and apply concepts from this lesson to real problems in their communities.`}` },
      { issue: 'Inclusive Education', description: `Accommodate learners with diverse needs (${sen}). Use multi-modal teaching: verbal explanations, board diagrams, and hands-on activities. Ensure learners with learning difficulties receive simplified instructions and additional support.` },
    ];
    const crossCuttingIssues = picks(allCCI, 3);

    // ── Exercise Questions ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const questions = buildQuestions(topic, subtopic, subject, qtypes, level, { name1, name2, town1, town2, river, crop, market, isScience, isMath, isPractical, isLang, isSocial, isECE, isLower, isUpper, isJunSec, isSenSec });

    return {
      lessonGoal,
      rationale,
      priorKnowledge,
      learningEnvironment: { natural: naturalEnv, artificial: artificialEnv, technological: techEnv },
      teachingMaterials,
      lessonProgression,
      crossCuttingIssues,
      classExercise: questions.classExercise,
      homework:      questions.homework,
      answerKey:     questions.answerKey,
    };
  }

  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  //  QUESTION BUILDER
  // ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
  function buildQuestions(topic, subtopic, subject, qtypes, level, ctx) {
    const { name1, name2, town1, town2, river, crop, market, isScience, isMath, isPractical, isLang, isSocial, isECE, isLower, isUpper, isJunSec, isSenSec } = ctx;

    const classExercise = [];
    const homework      = [];
    const answerKey     = { classExercise: [], homework: [] };

    // Helper: generate question sets by type
    if (isMath) {
      classExercise.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`${name1} sells ${crop} at ${market} in ${town1}. Each bag costs K45. If ${name1} sells 8 bags, how much money does ${name1} earn in total?`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:2, question:`A farmer in ${town2} has a rectangular field that is 15 m long and 9 m wide. Calculate the area of the field.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Simplify the following expression related to ${subtopic}: (a) Write the formula for ${subtopic} in your own words. (b) Apply it to solve: find the value of the unknown in 3x + 7 = 22.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:4, question:`The distance between ${town1} and ${town2} is 240 km. A bus travels at 80 km/h. How long will the journey take? Show all working.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:5, question:`Define "${subtopic}" as used in ${subject}. Give one real-life example from Zambia where this concept is applied.`, marks:2 }
      );
      answerKey.classExercise = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`K45 × 8 = K360. ${name1} earns K360.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Area = length × width = 15 m × 9 m = 135 m².` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`(a) Learner's own words — accept any correct definition. (b) 3x = 22 − 7 = 15; x = 5.` },
        { qnum:4, answer:`Time = Distance ÷ Speed = 240 ÷ 80 = 3 hours.` },
        { qnum:5, answer:`Correct definition of ${subtopic} (accept any syllabus-accurate wording). Example: any valid Zambian real-life application.` },
      ];
      homework.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`${name2} earns K2,400 per month working in ${town1}. ${name2} spends 35% on food, 20% on rent and saves the rest. Calculate: (a) the amount spent on food, (b) the amount saved per month.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Using your knowledge of ${topic}, solve the following: A Zambian water tank holds 1,500 litres. If 350 litres are used each day, after how many complete days will the tank be empty? Show all working.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Explain the importance of ${subtopic} in everyday Zambian life. Use at least TWO specific local examples in your answer.`, marks:4 }
      );
      answerKey.homework = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`(a) Food: 35% of K2,400 = 0.35 × 2400 = K840. (b) Rent: 20% of K2,400 = K480. Savings = K2,400 − K840 − K480 = K1,080.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`1,500 ÷ 350 = 4.28... → 4 complete days (accept 4 days with working shown).` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Any two correct, specific Zambian real-life examples demonstrating application of ${subtopic}. Award 2 marks for each valid example with explanation.` },
      ];

    } else if (isScience) {
      classExercise.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Define "${subtopic}" as used in ${subject}.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:2, question:`List THREE factors that affect ${topic}. For each factor, give a brief explanation.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:3, question:`A scientist in ${town1} conducts an experiment on ${topic}. What safety precautions should they observe? State at least TWO.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:4, question:`Explain the relationship between ${topic} and daily life in Zambia. Use an example from farming, health, or the environment near the ${river} River.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:5, question:`Draw a labelled diagram to illustrate ONE key concept from ${subtopic}.`, marks:2 }
      );
      answerKey.classExercise = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Correct, syllabus-accurate definition of ${subtopic}. Accept any wording that captures the key idea.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Any three valid factors affecting ${topic}, each with a brief, accurate explanation. Award 1 mark per correct factor (max 2 marks).` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Any two valid safety precautions (e.g. wear protective clothing, handle chemicals carefully, follow teacher instructions, wash hands after experiment).` },
        { qnum:4, answer:`A clear, logical explanation linking ${topic} to Zambian life with a valid, specific example. Award full marks for specificity and accuracy.` },
        { qnum:5, answer:`A neat, labelled diagram illustrating a key concept from ${subtopic}. Labels must be correct and relevant.` },
      ];
      homework.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Using your knowledge of ${topic}, describe a simple experiment you could conduct at home using locally available materials in Zambia to demonstrate ONE principle of ${subtopic}. State the aim, materials, procedure (3 steps), and expected result.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Compare and contrast TWO aspects of ${topic}. Use a table to present your comparison. Give one example from Zambia for each aspect.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Write a short essay (at least 10 sentences) on the importance of ${topic} to Zambia's environment, health, or economy. Include the ${river} River or a town you know in your answer.`, marks:4 }
      );
      answerKey.homework = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Any valid experiment format: aim (1 mk), materials using local items (1 mk), 3 logical procedure steps (1 mk). Expected result must match the aim.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Table with at least 2 clear comparison rows. Both Zambian examples must be relevant and accurate. Award 1.5 marks per side of the comparison.` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Well-structured essay. Award marks for: content accuracy (2), Zambian context (1), language and structure (1). Minimum 10 sentences required.` },
      ];

    } else if (isLang) {
      classExercise.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Read the following sentence and identify the main idea: "${name1} walked to the market in ${town1} every Saturday morning to sell vegetables grown on the family farm near the ${river} River." What is ${name1} doing?`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Write the meaning of the following word as used in the context of ${subtopic}: ________. Use it in a sentence about life in Zambia.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Rewrite the following sentence correctly: "${name2} go to school in ${town2} every days." Identify the TWO grammatical errors and correct them.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:4, question:`Write THREE sentences describing your home town (${town1} or any Zambian town you know). Use at least ONE new vocabulary word from today's lesson.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:5, question:`What is the meaning of "${subtopic}" as used in ${subject}? Give ONE example from everyday Zambian life.`, marks:2 }
      );
      answerKey.classExercise = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`${name1} is walking/going to the market in ${town1} to sell vegetables. (Accept any accurate paraphrase.)` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Correct definition of the target word in context. Original sentence using the word correctly. Accept any grammatically correct Zambian-context sentence.` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Corrected sentence: "${name2} goes to school in ${town2} every day." Errors: (1) "go" → "goes" (subject-verb agreement), (2) "days" → "day" (incorrect plural).` },
        { qnum:4, answer:`Three grammatically correct sentences. At least one vocabulary word from the lesson used correctly. Zambian context present.` },
        { qnum:5, answer:`Correct, age-appropriate definition of ${subtopic}. Valid Zambian-context example.` },
      ];
      homework.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Write a short story (at least 8 sentences) about a child in ${town1} who goes on an adventure. Use at least FIVE vocabulary words from this week's lessons in your story.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Read a newspaper article or a notice in your community. Write FIVE sentences in your own words explaining what you read. State where you found the text.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Write a formal letter to the headteacher of your school requesting permission to start a school garden. Your letter must include: a greeting, the purpose of the letter, at least TWO reasons why the garden is a good idea, and a polite closing.`, marks:4 }
      );
      answerKey.homework = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Story with clear beginning, middle, and end. Five vocabulary words used correctly (1 mark per word, max 2 marks). Grammar and structure (1 mark). Minimum 8 sentences.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Five accurate, original sentences summarising the learner's reading. Source stated. Award marks for comprehension accuracy and language quality.` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Letter with all required elements: greeting (0.5), purpose (1), two reasons (1), polite close (0.5), correct letter format (1).` },
      ];

    } else if (isPractical) {
      classExercise.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Name FOUR tools or materials used in ${topic}. For each, state its specific purpose.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:2, question:`List the steps involved in completing a basic task related to ${subtopic}. Number each step clearly.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Why is safety important when practising ${topic}? State THREE specific safety rules you must follow.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:4, question:`Describe ONE common mistake learners make when working on ${subtopic}. How can this mistake be avoided?`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:5, question:`How does knowledge of ${topic} benefit communities in Zambia? Give a specific example from a town like ${town1} or ${town2}.`, marks:2 }
      );
      answerKey.classExercise = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Any four valid tools/materials from ${topic}, each with a correct purpose. Award 0.5 marks per correct pair.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Logical, numbered steps for the given task. Award marks for correct sequence, completeness, and clarity.` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Any three valid, specific safety rules relevant to ${topic} (e.g. wear protective equipment, keep work area clean, follow instructions, handle tools correctly).` },
        { qnum:4, answer:`Any valid common mistake with a practical, correct prevention method. Both parts must be answered.` },
        { qnum:5, answer:`Clear explanation of how ${topic} benefits Zambian communities with a specific local example.` },
      ];
      homework.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Design a simple project related to ${topic} that you could make using materials available in Zambia. Draw a labelled diagram of your design and list the materials needed.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Describe the process of creating one product related to ${subtopic}. Include: materials needed, step-by-step procedure, and finishing/quality check.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:3, question:`How can skills learned in ${subject} help young Zambians earn income? Write at least 10 sentences describing a small business idea based on ${topic} that someone in ${town1} could start.`, marks:4 }
      );
      answerKey.homework = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Labelled diagram with all key parts identified. Materials list using Zambian-available items. Design must be logical and practical.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Correct materials list (1), logical procedure with at least 4 steps (1), quality check described (1).` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Business idea clearly explained with Zambian context. Content (2), entrepreneurship relevance (1), language (1).` },
      ];

    } else {
      // General / Social / Civic / Other
      classExercise.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Define "${subtopic}" as taught in ${subject}. Use your own words.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:2, question:`State THREE key facts about ${topic} that you have learned in today's lesson.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:3, question:`How does ${topic} affect the daily lives of people in Zambia? Use examples from ${town1} or ${town2} in your answer.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:4, question:`Compare TWO aspects of ${subtopic}. Explain one similarity and one difference.`, marks:2 },
        { qnum:5, question:`"Every Zambian citizen has a role to play in ${topic}." Do you agree or disagree? Give at least TWO reasons for your position.`, marks:2 }
      );
      answerKey.classExercise = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Any accurate, age-appropriate definition of ${subtopic} in the learner's own words. Award full marks for clarity and correctness.` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Any three factually correct key facts from ${topic} as taught in the lesson. Award 1 mark per correct fact (max 2 marks for accuracy + completeness).` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`A clear, logical explanation with at least one specific example from a named Zambian location.` },
        { qnum:4, answer:`One valid similarity and one valid difference, both explained clearly. Accept any logical comparison.` },
        { qnum:5, answer:`A clear position (agree/disagree) with at least two supporting reasons. Award marks for reasoning quality and use of Zambian context.` },
      ];
      homework.push(
        { qnum:1, question:`Research and write a report (at least 15 sentences) about how ${topic} is applied in a Zambian community you know. Name the community and give specific examples.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:2, question:`Draw a detailed, labelled diagram or chart that illustrates the main concept of ${subtopic}. Write a paragraph explaining your diagram.`, marks:3 },
        { qnum:3, question:`Interview a member of your family or community about ${topic}. Write down at least FIVE things they told you, then write your own opinion: Do you agree with what they said? Why or why not?`, marks:4 }
      );
      answerKey.homework = [
        { qnum:1, answer:`Report with clear community reference, specific examples, and factual accuracy. Minimum 15 sentences. Award marks for content (2), context (0.5), language (0.5).` },
        { qnum:2, answer:`Labelled diagram/chart that is accurate and neat (1.5). Explanatory paragraph that correctly describes the diagram (1.5).` },
        { qnum:3, answer:`Five points recorded from the interview (2). Learner's opinion clearly stated with reasons (2). Award full marks for depth and critical thinking.` },
      ];
    }

    return { classExercise, homework, answerKey };
  }

  // ─── STORAGE ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  async function lpLoad() {
    try { if (typeof getIndexedDB === 'function') { const d = await getIndexedDB(LP_STORE); if (Array.isArray(d)) return d; } } catch {}
    try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(LP_STORE) || '[]'); } catch { return []; }
  }
  async function lpSave(plans) {
    try { if (typeof saveIndexedDB === 'function') { await saveIndexedDB(LP_STORE, plans); return; } } catch {}
    try { localStorage.setItem(LP_STORE, JSON.stringify(plans)); } catch {}
  }

  // ─── TOAST ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
  function lpToast(msg, type = 'success') {
    if (typeof showToast === 'function') { showToast(msg, type); return; }
    const t = document.createElement('div');
    t.textContent = msg;
    Object.assign(t.style, {
      position:'fixed', bottom:'90px', left:'50%', transform:'translateX(-50%)',
      background: type === 'error' ? '#dc2626' : type === 'info' ? '#0369a1' : '#009960',
      color:'#fff', padding:'11px 22px', borderRadius:'10px',
      fontSize:'13px', fontWeight:'700', zIndex:'9999999',
      fontFamily:'inherit', boxShadow:'0 4px 20px rgba(0,0,0,.4)',
      whiteSpace:'nowrap', opacity:'1', transition:'opacity .3s'
    });
    document.body.appendChild(t);
    setTimeout(() => { t.style.opacity='0'; setTimeout(()=>t.remove(),350); }, 3800);
  }
