                <span id="ai-badge-icon">${isStartingWithSaved?'✏️':'🤖'}</span>
                <span id="ai-badge-label">${isStartingWithSaved?'Using Your Saved Edit':'AI Generated'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <textarea id="pdf-comments" rows="4"
              class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-3 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none leading-relaxed"
              placeholder="AI comment will appear here once subjects are added..."
            >${escapeHTML(initialCommentText)}</textarea>
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mt-2 gap-2">
              <p id="ai-comment-hint" class="text-xs text-slate-400 italic">${isStartingWithSaved?'Showing your previously saved edit.':initialCommentText?'Comment auto-generated. Edit freely — changes are saved.':'Add scores above, then click Regenerate.'}</p>
              <div class="flex gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
                <button id="ai-regenerate-btn" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-emerald-600 hover:bg-emerald-700 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded shadow transition-colors">↺ Regenerate</button>
                <button id="ai-clear-btn" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold rounded shadow transition-colors">✕ Clear</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <!-- SECTION 3b: Head Teacher's Remarks (NEW) -->
          <div class="bg-amber-50 p-5 rounded-lg border border-amber-200 shadow-sm">
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mb-3 border-b border-amber-200 pb-2">
              <div>
                <h4 class="text-md font-bold text-amber-900">4. Head Teacher's Remarks</h4>
                <p class="text-xs text-amber-700 mt-0.5">Short endorsement · Auto-generated · Edit freely</p>
              </div>
              <div id="ht-comment-badge" class="mt-2 sm:mt-0 flex items-center gap-1.5 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-1.5 rounded-full border ${isHtStartingWithSaved?'bg-indigo-100 text-indigo-700 border-indigo-200':'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border-amber-200'}">
                <span id="ht-badge-icon">${isHtStartingWithSaved?'✏️':'🏫'}</span>
                <span id="ht-badge-label">${isHtStartingWithSaved?'Your Saved Edit':'Auto Generated'}</span>
              </div>
            </div>
            <textarea id="pdf-ht-comments" rows="2"
              class="w-full border border-amber-200 bg-white rounded p-3 text-sm focus:ring-2 focus:ring-amber-400 outline-none leading-relaxed"
              placeholder="Short head teacher endorsement will appear here..."
            >${escapeHTML(initialHtCommentText)}</textarea>
            <div class="flex flex-col sm:flex-row justify-between items-start sm:items-center mt-2 gap-2">
              <p id="ht-comment-hint" class="text-xs text-amber-600 italic">${isHtStartingWithSaved?'Showing your previously saved edit.':initialHtCommentText?'Short endorsement auto-generated. Edit freely.':'Add scores above, then click Regenerate.'}</p>
              <div class="flex gap-2 flex-shrink-0">
                <button id="ht-regenerate-btn" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-amber-500 hover:bg-amber-600 text-white text-xs font-bold rounded shadow transition-colors">↺ Regenerate</button>
                <button id="ht-clear-btn" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold rounded shadow transition-colors">✕ Clear</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

        </div>

        <!-- Footer Buttons -->
        <div class="bg-slate-100 p-5 rounded-b-xl flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-300">
          <button id="close-pdf-form" class="px-6 py-2.5 text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded hover:bg-slate-50 font-bold shadow-sm transition-colors">Cancel</button>
          <button id="generate-pdf-btn" class="px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-700 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-800 font-bold shadow-md transition-colors">Generate Premium PDF</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    // ── DOM refs ────────────────────────────────────────────────
    const subjectsContainer  = document.getElementById('subjects-container');
    const commentsTextarea   = document.getElementById('pdf-comments');
    const htCommentsTextarea = document.getElementById('pdf-ht-comments');
    const aiBadge            = document.getElementById('ai-comment-badge');
    const aiBadgeIcon        = document.getElementById('ai-badge-icon');
    const aiBadgeLabel       = document.getElementById('ai-badge-label');
    const aiHint             = document.getElementById('ai-comment-hint');
    const htBadge            = document.getElementById('ht-comment-badge');
    const htBadgeIcon        = document.getElementById('ht-badge-icon');
    const htBadgeLabel       = document.getElementById('ht-badge-label');
    const htHint             = document.getElementById('ht-comment-hint');
    const logoInput          = document.getElementById('logo-file-input');
    const logoPreviewImg     = document.getElementById('logo-preview-img');
    const logoPlaceholder    = document.getElementById('logo-placeholder-text');
    const logoClearBtn       = document.getElementById('logo-clear-btn');
    const schoolNameInput    = document.getElementById('pdf-school');

    // ── Logo state ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    let _currentLogoBase64 = null;

    function showLogoPreview(base64) {
      if (!base64) {
        logoPreviewImg.src = ''; logoPreviewImg.style.display = 'none';
        logoPlaceholder.style.display = ''; logoClearBtn.style.display = 'none';
        _currentLogoBase64 = null;
      } else {
        logoPreviewImg.src = base64; logoPreviewImg.style.display = 'block';
        logoPlaceholder.style.display = 'none'; logoClearBtn.style.display = '';
        _currentLogoBase64 = base64;
      }
    }

    // Auto-load logo when school name typed (debounced 600ms)
    let _logoTimer = null;
    schoolNameInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
      clearTimeout(_logoTimer);
      _logoTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
        const name = schoolNameInput.value.trim();
        if (!name || _currentLogoBase64) return; // don't clobber a freshly uploaded logo
        const saved = await loadSchoolLogo(name);
        if (saved) { showLogoPreview(saved); showToast('Logo loaded for ' + name, 'info'); }
      }, 600);
    });

    // Upload handler
    logoInput.addEventListener('change', async () => {
      const file = logoInput.files && logoInput.files[0];
      if (!file) return;
      try {
        const base64 = await readFileAsBase64(file);
        showLogoPreview(base64);
        const schoolName = schoolNameInput.value.trim();
        if (schoolName) { await saveSchoolLogo(schoolName, base64); showToast('✅ Logo saved for ' + schoolName, 'success'); }
        else showToast('Logo loaded. Enter school name to save it for reuse.', 'info');
      } catch(e) { showToast('⚠️ Could not read logo file', 'error'); }
      logoInput.value = '';
    });

    // Save logo when school name confirmed
    schoolNameInput.addEventListener('blur', async () => {
      const name = schoolNameInput.value.trim();
      if (name && _currentLogoBase64) await saveSchoolLogo(name, _currentLogoBase64);
    });

    logoClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { showLogoPreview(null); showToast('Logo removed from this report', 'info'); });

    // ── Comment state ───────────────────────────────────────────
    let _commentState   = isStartingWithSaved   ? 'manual' : (initialCommentText   ? 'auto' : 'empty');
    let _htCommentState = isHtStartingWithSaved ? 'manual' : (initialHtCommentText ? 'auto' : 'empty');

    function updateCommentBadge(state) {
      _commentState = state;
      if (state === 'auto') {
        aiBadge.className = aiBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        aiBadge.classList.add('bg-emerald-100','text-emerald-700','border-emerald-200');
        aiBadgeIcon.textContent='🤖'; aiBadgeLabel.textContent='AI Generated';
        aiHint.textContent='Comment auto-generated from scores. Edit freely — changes are saved.';
      } else if (state === 'manual') {
        aiBadge.className = aiBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        aiBadge.classList.add('bg-indigo-100','text-indigo-700','border-indigo-200');
        aiBadgeIcon.textContent='✏️'; aiBadgeLabel.textContent='Manually Edited';
        aiHint.textContent='You\'ve edited this comment. Click Regenerate for a fresh AI comment.';
      } else {
        aiBadge.className = aiBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        aiBadge.classList.add('bg-slate-100','text-slate-500','border-slate-200');
        aiBadgeIcon.textContent='💬'; aiBadgeLabel.textContent='No Comment';
        aiHint.textContent='Add subjects and scores, then click Regenerate.';
      }
    }

    function updateHtCommentBadge(state) {
      _htCommentState = state;
      if (state === 'auto') {
        htBadge.className = htBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        htBadge.classList.add('bg-amber-100','text-amber-700','border-amber-200');
        htBadgeIcon.textContent='🏫'; htBadgeLabel.textContent='Auto Generated';
        htHint.textContent='Short endorsement auto-generated. Edit freely.';
      } else if (state === 'manual') {
        htBadge.className = htBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        htBadge.classList.add('bg-indigo-100','text-indigo-700','border-indigo-200');
        htBadgeIcon.textContent='✏️'; htBadgeLabel.textContent='Manually Edited';
        htHint.textContent='You\'ve edited this remark. Click Regenerate to reset to auto.';
      } else {
        htBadge.className = htBadge.className.replace(/bg-\S+|text-\S+|border-\S+/g,'').trim();
        htBadge.classList.add('bg-slate-100','text-slate-500','border-slate-200');
        htBadgeIcon.textContent='💬'; htBadgeLabel.textContent='No Remark';
        htHint.textContent='Add scores, then click Regenerate.';
      }
    }

    commentsTextarea.addEventListener('input', () => {
      updateCommentBadge(commentsTextarea.value.trim() === '' ? 'empty' : 'manual');
    });
    htCommentsTextarea.addEventListener('input', () => {
      updateHtCommentBadge(htCommentsTextarea.value.trim() === '' ? 'empty' : 'manual');
    });

    // ── Compute both comments from current form rows ────────────
    function computeAutoCommentsFromForm() {
      const selLevel   = document.getElementById('pdf-level') ? document.getElementById('pdf-level').value : level;
      const teacherName = document.getElementById('pdf-teacher') ? document.getElementById('pdf-teacher').value.trim() : '';
      const subRows = Array.from(subjectsContainer.querySelectorAll('div.grid'));
      const currentEntries = subRows.map(row => {
        const subject  = row.querySelector('.sub-subject').value.trim();
        const score    = Number(row.querySelector('.sub-score').value);
        const cat      = row.querySelector('.sub-category').value;
        const date     = row.querySelector('.sub-date').value;
        const testName = row.querySelector('.sub-test').value.trim();
        if (!subject || isNaN(score) || score < 0 || score > 100) return null;
        const { grade, remark } = zambianGrade(score, selLevel);
        return { subject, testName, category:cat, date, score:round2(score), grade, remark };
      }).filter(e => e !== null);

      if (currentEntries.length === 0) return { teacher:'', headteacher:'' };

      const curAvg      = round2(currentEntries.reduce((a,e)=>a+e.score,0)/currentEntries.length);
      const { grade: curGrade } = zambianGrade(curAvg, selLevel);
      const curPassRate = round2((currentEntries.filter(e=>e.score>=40).length/currentEntries.length)*100);

      return {
        teacher: generateAutoTeacherComment({ studentName:student.name||studentId, avg:curAvg, overallGrade:curGrade, overallRemark:'', passRate:curPassRate, entries:currentEntries, reportLevel:selLevel, teacher:teacherName }),
        headteacher: generateAutoHeadteacherComment({ studentName:student.name||studentId, avg:curAvg, overallGrade:curGrade, passRate:curPassRate })
      };
    }

    // ── Regenerate / Clear buttons ──────────────────────────────
    document.getElementById('ai-regenerate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const { teacher } = computeAutoCommentsFromForm();
      if (!teacher) { showToast('⚠️ Add at least one valid subject and score first', 'error'); return; }
      commentsTextarea.value = teacher; updateCommentBadge('auto');
    });
    document.getElementById('ht-regenerate-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      const { headteacher } = computeAutoCommentsFromForm();
      if (!headteacher) { showToast('⚠️ Add at least one valid subject and score first', 'error'); return; }
      htCommentsTextarea.value = headteacher; updateHtCommentBadge('auto');
    });
    document.getElementById('ai-clear-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { commentsTextarea.value=''; updateCommentBadge('empty'); });
    document.getElementById('ht-clear-btn').addEventListener('click', () => { htCommentsTextarea.value=''; updateHtCommentBadge('empty'); });

    // Level change → auto-refresh both if still in AI mode
    document.getElementById('pdf-level').addEventListener('change', () => {
      const { teacher, headteacher } = computeAutoCommentsFromForm();
      if (_commentState   === 'auto' && teacher)      { commentsTextarea.value   = teacher; }
      if (_htCommentState === 'auto' && headteacher)  { htCommentsTextarea.value = headteacher; }
    });

    // ── addSubjectRow ───────────────────────────────────────────
    function addSubjectRow(subject='', testName='', category='', date='', score='') {
      const div = document.createElement('div');
      div.className = 'grid grid-cols-1 md:grid-cols-12 gap-2 bg-white p-3 border border-slate-200 rounded shadow-sm items-center hover:border-blue-300 transition-colors';
      div.innerHTML = `
        <input type="text" class="sub-subject md:col-span-3 border border-slate-300 rounded p-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="Subject (e.g. Math)" value="${escapeHTML(subject)}">
        <input type="text" class="sub-test md:col-span-3 border border-slate-300 rounded p-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" placeholder="Test Name" value="${escapeHTML(testName)}">
        <select class="sub-category md:col-span-2 border border-slate-300 rounded p-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500">
          <option value="">Category</option>
          ${cats.map(c=>`<option value="${c}" ${c===category?'selected':''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}
        </select>
        <input type="date" class="sub-date md:col-span-2 border border-slate-300 rounded p-2 text-sm outline-none focus:border-blue-500" value="${date}">
        <input type="number" class="sub-score md:col-span-1 border border-slate-300 rounded p-2 text-sm outline-none font-bold text-blue-900 focus:border-blue-500" min="0" max="100" step="0.01" placeholder="Score%" value="${score?round2(score):''}">
        <button class="remove-sub md:col-span-1 text-red-500 bg-red-50 hover:bg-red-100 hover:text-red-700 py-2 rounded font-bold transition-colors w-full text-sm border border-red-100">Delete</button>`;
      subjectsContainer.appendChild(div);

      div.querySelector('.remove-sub').addEventListener('click', () => {
        div.remove();
        const { teacher, headteacher } = computeAutoCommentsFromForm();
        if (_commentState   === 'auto') { commentsTextarea.value   = teacher   || ''; if(!teacher)   updateCommentBadge('empty'); }
        if (_htCommentState === 'auto') { htCommentsTextarea.value = headteacher || ''; if(!headteacher) updateHtCommentBadge('empty'); }
      });
      div.querySelector('.sub-score').addEventListener('change', () => {
        const { teacher, headteacher } = computeAutoCommentsFromForm();
        if (_commentState   === 'auto' && teacher)     commentsTextarea.value   = teacher;
        if (_htCommentState === 'auto' && headteacher) htCommentsTextarea.value = headteacher;
      });
    }

    // Pre-fill entries
    if (entries.length === 0 && useHistory) {
      subjectsContainer.innerHTML = `
        <div class="bg-blue-50 text-blue-800 p-4 rounded text-center border border-blue-200">
          <p class="font-semibold">No records found for category: ${requestedCategory}.</p>
          <p class="text-sm mt-1">Try changing the category above, or add a subject manually!</p>
        </div>`;
    } else {
      entries.forEach(e => addSubjectRow(e.subject, e.testName, e.category, e.date, e.score));
    }

    document.getElementById('add-subject-btn').addEventListener('click', () => {
      if (subjectsContainer.querySelector('.bg-blue-50')) subjectsContainer.innerHTML = '';
      addSubjectRow();
    });

    updateCommentBadge(_commentState);
    updateHtCommentBadge(_htCommentState);

    // ── Generate PDF ────────────────────────────────────────────
    document.getElementById('generate-pdf-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const school    = document.getElementById('pdf-school').value.trim().toUpperCase();
      const rawClass  = document.getElementById('pdf-class').value.trim();
      const pdfClass  = rawClass.replace(/_/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').toUpperCase();
      const teacher   = document.getElementById('pdf-teacher').value.trim();
      const phone     = document.getElementById('pdf-phone').value.trim();
      const pdfLevel  = document.getElementById('pdf-level').value;
      const finalComment    = commentsTextarea.value.trim();
      const finalHtComment  = htCommentsTextarea.value.trim();
      const commentIsManual   = _commentState   === 'manual';
      const htCommentIsManual = _htCommentState === 'manual';
      const finalCategory = document.getElementById('pdf-report-category').value;

      const subRows   = Array.from(subjectsContainer.querySelectorAll('div.grid'));
      const pdfEntries = subRows.map(row => {
        const subject  = row.querySelector('.sub-subject').value.trim();
        const testName = row.querySelector('.sub-test').value.trim();
        const category = row.querySelector('.sub-category').value;
        const date     = row.querySelector('.sub-date').value;
        const score    = Number(row.querySelector('.sub-score').value);
        if (!subject || isNaN(score) || score < 0 || score > 100) return null;
        const { grade, remark } = zambianGrade(score, pdfLevel);
        return { subject, testName, category, date, score:round2(score), grade, remark };
      }).filter(e => e !== null);

      if (pdfEntries.length === 0) return showToast('Please add at least one valid subject and score', 'error');

      // Persist comments
      await saveTeacherComment(className, studentId, { text:finalComment, isManual:commentIsManual, generatedAt:new Date().toISOString() });
      await saveHeadteacherComment(className, studentId, { text:finalHtComment, isManual:htCommentIsManual, generatedAt:new Date().toISOString() });

      // Persist logo
      if (school && _currentLogoBase64) await saveSchoolLogo(school, _currentLogoBase64);

      formContainer.style.display = 'none';

      await exportStudentResultsPDF(className, studentId, {
        title: finalCategory && finalCategory !== 'All' ? `${finalCategory} Results Report` : `Comprehensive Academic Report`,
        level: pdfLevel, category: finalCategory,
        comments:   finalComment,
        htComments: finalHtComment,
        skipCommentPrompt: true,
        testName: '', school, pdfClass, teacher, phone,
        logoBase64: _currentLogoBase64
      }, pdfEntries);
    });

    const closeHandler = () => formContainer.style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('close-pdf-form').addEventListener('click', closeHandler);
    document.getElementById('close-pdf-form-x').addEventListener('click', closeHandler);
  };

  // ==========================================
  // BULK REPORT CARD GENERATION
  //
  // Reuses everything the single-student flow already uses — the same
  // buildStudentPdfDefinition() builder, the same auto-comment generators,
  // the same saved-comment lookup (a teacher's manual edit for a student is
  // respected here too, not overwritten), the same school logo persistence.
  // The only genuinely new logic is: collect the SHARED settings once
  // (school/logo/teacher/phone/category/level), then loop every student in
  // the class, pulling THEIR OWN results/comments automatically.
  // ==========================================

  // Bulk-safe PDF export: the single-student version tries navigator.share()
  // first, which is right for one file — but popping a native share sheet
  // 30 times in a row for a whole class would be unusable. Bulk mode always
  // downloads directly instead.
  async function exportStudentResultsPDFBulk(className, studentId, opts = {}, manualEntries = []) {
    const { dd } = await buildStudentPdfDefinition(className, studentId, opts, manualEntries);
    const pdfMakeLib = await ensurePdfMake();
    const pdfDocGenerator = pdfMakeLib.createPdf(dd);
    const fileName = `${studentId}_Official_Report.pdf`;
    pdfDocGenerator.download(fileName);
  }

  async function runBulkReportCardGeneration(className, classStudents, sharedOpts) {
    const { school, pdfClass, teacher, phone, category, level, logoBase64 } = sharedOpts;

    let allResults = await getIndexedDB('results');
    if (!Array.isArray(allResults)) allResults = [];
    let classResults = allResults.filter(r => r.classId === className);
    if (category && category.toLowerCase() !== 'all') {
      classResults = classResults.filter(r => String(r.category).toLowerCase() === category.toLowerCase());
    }

    const skipped = [];
    const succeeded = [];

    let progressBanner = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-progress-banner');
    if (!progressBanner) {
      progressBanner = document.createElement('div');
      progressBanner.id = 'bulk-pdf-progress-banner';
      progressBanner.style.cssText = 'position:fixed;bottom:20px;left:50%;transform:translateX(-50%);background:#1e293b;color:#fff;padding:14px 22px;border-radius:12px;box-shadow:0 8px 24px rgba(0,0,0,.3);z-index:9999;font-size:14px;font-weight:600;max-width:90vw;text-align:center;';
      document.body.appendChild(progressBanner);
    }
    progressBanner.style.display = 'block';
    progressBanner.textContent = `📄 Starting bulk generation for ${classStudents.length} student(s)…`;

    for (let i = 0; i < classStudents.length; i++) {
      const student = classStudents[i];
      const studentId = student.id;
      progressBanner.textContent = `📄 Generating ${i + 1} of ${classStudents.length}: ${student.name || studentId}…`;

      const studentResults = classResults.filter(r => r.scores && r.scores[studentId] !== undefined);
      if (!studentResults.length) {
        skipped.push(student.name || studentId);
        continue;
      }

      const entries = studentResults.map(r => {
        const score = r.scores[studentId];
        const { grade, remark } = zambianGrade(score, level);
        return { subject: r.subject, testName: r.testName, category: r.category, date: r.date, score: round2(score), grade, remark };
      });

      const avg = round2(entries.reduce((a, e) => a + (Number(e.score) || 0), 0) / entries.length);
      const { grade: overallGrade } = zambianGrade(avg, level);
      const passRate = round2((entries.filter(e => Number(e.score) >= 40).length / entries.length) * 100);

      // Respect a teacher's previously saved manual comment for this student
      // if one exists — exactly like the single-student flow — otherwise
      // auto-generate, same as the single-student flow's initial state.
      const savedComment = await loadTeacherComment(className, studentId);
      const savedHt       = await loadHeadteacherComment(className, studentId);
      const comment = (savedComment && savedComment.isManual) ? savedComment.text
        : generateAutoTeacherComment({ studentName: student.name || studentId, avg, overallGrade, overallRemark: '', passRate, entries, reportLevel: level, teacher });
      const htComment = (savedHt && savedHt.isManual) ? savedHt.text
        : generateAutoHeadteacherComment({ studentName: student.name || studentId, avg, overallGrade, passRate });

      try {
        await exportStudentResultsPDFBulk(className, studentId, {
          title: category && category !== 'All' ? `${category} Results Report` : `Comprehensive Academic Report`,
          level, category, comments: comment, htComments: htComment,
          skipCommentPrompt: true, testName: '', school, pdfClass, teacher, phone, logoBase64
        }, entries);
        succeeded.push(student.name || studentId);
      } catch (e) {
        console.error(`Bulk generation failed for ${studentId}:`, e);
        skipped.push(`${student.name || studentId} (error)`);
      }

      // Small gap between downloads — browsers can flag rapid automatic
      // downloads as spam and block them, and this keeps pdfMake from
      // choking on back-to-back large PDF builds.
      await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 500));
    }

    progressBanner.remove();
    showToast(
      `✅ Bulk generation complete: ${succeeded.length} report card(s) created` +
      (skipped.length ? `, ${skipped.length} skipped (no results found)` : ''),
      succeeded.length ? 'success' : 'error'
    );
    if (skipped.length) console.warn('[Bulk Report Cards] Skipped (no saved results yet):', skipped);
  }

  window.openBulkReportCardForm = async function () {
    const classEl          = document.getElementById('results-class');
    const levelFilterEl    = document.getElementById('results-level-filter') || document.getElementById('results-level');
    const categoryFilterEl = document.getElementById('results-category-filter') || document.getElementById('results-category');
    if (!classEl || !classEl.value) return showToast('Select a class first', 'error');
    const className        = classEl.value.trim();
    const level             = levelFilterEl ? levelFilterEl.value : 'Senior Secondary';
    const requestedCategory = categoryFilterEl ? categoryFilterEl.value : 'All';

    let students = await getIndexedDB('students');
    if (!Array.isArray(students)) students = [];
    const classStudents = students.filter(s => classMatches(s, className));
    if (!classStudents.length) return showToast('No students found in this class', 'error');

    const cats       = typeof CATEGORIES !== 'undefined' ? CATEGORIES : ['Weekly Test','Assignment','Project Work','Midterm Test','End of Term Test','Final Examination'];
    const levelsList = typeof LEVELS !== 'undefined'     ? LEVELS     : ['Primary','Junior Secondary','Senior Secondary'];

    let formContainer = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-form');
    if (!formContainer) { formContainer = document.createElement('div'); document.body.appendChild(formContainer); }
    formContainer.id = 'bulk-pdf-form';
    formContainer.style.display = 'flex';
    formContainer.className = 'fixed inset-0 bg-slate-900 bg-opacity-75 flex items-center justify-center z-50 p-4 backdrop-blur-sm';

    formContainer.innerHTML = `
      <div class="bg-white rounded-xl shadow-2xl w-full max-w-lg max-h-[95vh] overflow-y-auto flex flex-col font-sans">
        <div class="bg-blue-700 text-white p-5 rounded-t-xl flex justify-between items-center shadow-md">
          <div>
            <h3 class="text-xl font-bold tracking-wide">📚 Bulk Generate Report Cards</h3>
            <p class="text-blue-100 text-sm mt-1">Class: <span class="font-bold text-white bg-blue-800 px-2 py-0.5 rounded">${escapeHTML(className)}</span> · ${classStudents.length} student(s)</p>
          </div>
          <button id="close-bulk-pdf-form-x" class="text-white hover:text-red-300 text-3xl font-bold transition-colors">&times;</button>
        </div>
        <div class="p-6 space-y-5">
          <p class="text-sm text-slate-600">These details are shared across every student's report card. Subjects, scores, and comments are pulled automatically from each student's own saved results and comments — same as generating one at a time, just repeated for the whole class.</p>

          <div class="p-4 rounded-lg border-2 border-dashed border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 flex flex-col sm:flex-row items-center gap-4">
            <div id="bulk-logo-preview-wrap" class="flex-shrink-0 w-20 h-20 rounded-lg border border-slate-200 bg-white flex items-center justify-center overflow-hidden shadow-sm">
              <span id="bulk-logo-placeholder-text" class="text-slate-400 text-xs text-center px-1 leading-tight">No<br>Logo</span>
              <img id="bulk-logo-preview-img" src="" alt="School Logo" style="display:none;width:100%;height:100%;object-fit:contain;">
            </div>
            <div class="flex-1">
              <p class="text-sm font-bold text-blue-900 mb-0.5">School Logo <span class="font-normal text-blue-600">(Optional)</span></p>
              <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mb-2">Type the school name below — a previously saved logo loads automatically.</p>
              <div class="flex flex-wrap gap-2">
                <label for="bulk-logo-file-input" class="cursor-pointer bg-blue-600 hover:bg-blue-700 text-white text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded shadow transition-colors select-none">📁 Upload Logo</label>
                <input type="file" id="bulk-logo-file-input" accept="image/*" style="display:none;">
                <button id="bulk-logo-clear-btn" style="display:none;" class="bg-slate-200 hover:bg-slate-300 text-slate-700 text-xs font-bold px-3 py-2 rounded shadow transition-colors">✕ Remove</button>
              </div>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div>
            <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">School Name</label>
            <input type="text" id="bulk-pdf-school" class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-2.5 text-sm outline-none" placeholder="e.g., HILLCREST NATIONAL TECH">
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Teacher Name</label>
              <input type="text" id="bulk-pdf-teacher" class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-2.5 text-sm outline-none" placeholder="Teacher's Name">
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Teacher Phone</label>
              <input type="text" id="bulk-pdf-phone" class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-2.5 text-sm outline-none" placeholder="+260...">
            </div>
          </div>
          <div class="grid grid-cols-2 gap-4">
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Target Category</label>
              <select id="bulk-pdf-category" class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-2.5 text-sm outline-none font-semibold text-blue-900">
                <option value="All">All Categories (Comprehensive)</option>
                ${cats.map(c => `<option value="${c}" ${String(c).toLowerCase() === String(requestedCategory).toLowerCase() ? 'selected' : ''}>${c}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
            <div>
              <label class="block text-sm font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1">Grading Level</label>
              <select id="bulk-pdf-level" class="w-full border border-slate-300 rounded p-2.5 text-sm outline-none">
                <option value="${level}">${level}</option>
                ${levelsList.filter(l => l !== level).map(l => `<option value="${l}">${l}</option>`).join('')}
              </select>
            </div>
          </div>

          <div class="bg-amber-50 border border-amber-200 rounded-lg p-3 text-xs text-amber-800">
            ⚠️ Only students with at least one saved result in this class get a report card — others are skipped and listed in the console, nothing crashes. Your browser may ask permission to allow multiple downloads — approve it once so every report card can save.
          </div>
        </div>
        <div class="bg-slate-100 p-5 rounded-b-xl flex justify-end gap-3 border-t border-slate-300">
          <button id="close-bulk-pdf-form" class="px-6 py-2.5 text-slate-700 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded hover:bg-slate-50 font-bold shadow-sm transition-colors">Cancel</button>
          <button id="start-bulk-generate-btn" class="px-6 py-2.5 bg-blue-700 text-white rounded hover:bg-blue-800 font-bold shadow-md transition-colors">🚀 Generate All (${classStudents.length})</button>
        </div>
      </div>`;

    const bulkSchoolInput     = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-school');
    const bulkLogoImg         = document.getElementById('bulk-logo-preview-img');
    const bulkLogoPlaceholder = document.getElementById('bulk-logo-placeholder-text');
    const bulkLogoClearBtn    = document.getElementById('bulk-logo-clear-btn');
    let _bulkLogoBase64 = null;

    let _bulkLogoManuallySet = false;
    function showBulkLogoPreview(base64) {
      if (!base64) {
        bulkLogoImg.style.display = 'none'; bulkLogoPlaceholder.style.display = '';
        bulkLogoClearBtn.style.display = 'none'; _bulkLogoBase64 = null;
      } else {
        bulkLogoImg.src = base64; bulkLogoImg.style.display = 'block';
        bulkLogoPlaceholder.style.display = 'none'; bulkLogoClearBtn.style.display = '';
        _bulkLogoBase64 = base64;
      }
    }

    let _bulkLogoTimer = null;
    bulkSchoolInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
      clearTimeout(_bulkLogoTimer);
      _bulkLogoTimer = setTimeout(async () => {
        // 🛠️ CRITICAL FIX: this was the actual bug. Typing a school name
        // that doesn't have a SAVED logo yet used to call
        // showBulkLogoPreview(null), which unconditionally wiped out
        // whatever logo the teacher had just manually uploaded seconds
        // earlier - even though it was sitting there correctly the whole
        // time. Auto-load must never clear a logo the user provided
        // themselves; it should only ever fill one in when they haven't.
        if (_bulkLogoManuallySet) return;
        const name = bulkSchoolInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();
        if (!name) { showBulkLogoPreview(null); return; }
        const logo = await loadSchoolLogo(name);
        if (logo) showBulkLogoPreview(logo);
      }, 600);
    });
    document.getElementById('bulk-logo-file-input').addEventListener('change', async (e) => {
      const file = e.target.files[0]; if (!file) return;
      const base64 = await readFileAsBase64(file);
      _bulkLogoManuallySet = true;
      showBulkLogoPreview(base64);
    });
    bulkLogoClearBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { _bulkLogoManuallySet = false; showBulkLogoPreview(null); });

    const closeBulk = () => formContainer.style.display = 'none';
    document.getElementById('close-bulk-pdf-form').addEventListener('click', closeBulk);
    document.getElementById('close-bulk-pdf-form-x').addEventListener('click', closeBulk);

    document.getElementById('start-bulk-generate-btn').addEventListener('click', async () => {
      const school       = bulkSchoolInput.value.trim().toUpperCase();
      const teacherName  = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-teacher').value.trim();
      const phone        = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-phone').value.trim();
      const finalCategory = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-category').value;
      const pdfLevel      = document.getElementById('bulk-pdf-level').value;
      const pdfClass      = className.replace(/_/g, ' ').replace(/\s+/g, ' ').toUpperCase();

      // 🛠️ CRITICAL FIX: the school-name field auto-loads a previously
      // saved logo, but that lookup is debounced by 600ms so it doesn't
      // fire on every keystroke. If this button was clicked before that
      // 600ms window finished (very easy to do when typing a school name
      // you've used before, then immediately hitting Generate),
      // _bulkLogoBase64 was still null at this exact moment - meaning
      // EVERY student in the whole run got no logo at all, with nothing
      // ever retrying or catching it. Cancel that pending timer and
      // resolve the logo directly, right here, before proceeding -
      // removing any dependency on background timing entirely.
      clearTimeout(_bulkLogoTimer);
      if (!_bulkLogoBase64 && school) {
        _bulkLogoBase64 = await loadSchoolLogo(school);
        if (_bulkLogoBase64) showBulkLogoPreview(_bulkLogoBase64);
      }

      if (school && _bulkLogoBase64) await saveSchoolLogo(school, _bulkLogoBase64);

      closeBulk();
      await runBulkReportCardGeneration(className, classStudents, {
        school, pdfClass, teacher: teacherName, phone,
        category: finalCategory, level: pdfLevel, logoBase64: _bulkLogoBase64
      });
    });
  };


  // ==========================================
  // buildPrintableReportHTML (class PDF - unchanged)
  // ==========================================

  async function buildPrintableReportHTML(className, { title='', includeAnalytics=true, category='', comments='', testName='', level='' }={}) {
    let html = `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${escapeHTML(title||'Report')}</title>
    <style>
      *{box-sizing:border-box;} body{font-family:Arial,sans-serif;padding:20px;color:#333;background:#fff;max-width:610px;margin:0 auto;line-height:1.4;font-size:12pt;}
      h1,h2,h3{color:#2c3e50;text-align:center;page-break-after:avoid;margin:10px 0;}
      table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:20px 0;page-break-inside:avoid;}
      table,th,td{border:1px solid #ccc;padding:8px;text-align:left;font-size:10pt;}
      th{background:#f4f4f4;font-weight:bold;text-align:center;} td{vertical-align:middle;word-break:break-word;}
      .photo{max-width:80px;max-height:80px;border-radius:50%;margin-right:10px;vertical-align:middle;}
      .comments{margin-top:20px;padding:10px;border:1px solid #ddd;background:#f9f9f9;page-break-inside:avoid;}
      .signature{margin-top:30px;text-align:right;} .header{text-align:center;margin-bottom:20px;page-break-after:avoid;}
      .footer{text-align:center;font-size:0.8em;margin-top:40px;color:#777;page-break-before:always;}
      .inline-chart{width:100%;max-width:700px;height:auto;display:block;margin:10px auto;page-break-inside:avoid;}
      @page{size:A4;margin:1in;}
      @media print{body{padding:0;font-size:10pt;} .no-print{display:none!important;} .btn,input,select{display:none!important;} table{font-size:8pt;}}
    </style></head><body>
    <div class="header"><h1>${escapeHTML(title||'Results Report')}</h1><p>Generated: ${new Date().toLocaleString()}</p></div>`;

    let students = await getIndexedDB('students'); if (!Array.isArray(students)) students=[];
    let results  = await getIndexedDB('results');  if (!Array.isArray(results))  results=[];
    results = results.filter(r=>{const c=r.classId||r.class||r.className;return c&&String(c).trim().toLowerCase()===String(className).trim().toLowerCase();});
    if (category) results=results.filter(r=>r.category===category);
    if (testName) results=results.filter(r=>r.testName===testName);
    if (level)    results=results.filter(r=>r.level===level);
    let reportLevel=level||results[0]?.level||'Senior Secondary';

    const studentBuckets={};
    results.forEach(r=>{for(const[sid,score]of Object.entries(r.scores||{})){if(!studentBuckets[sid])studentBuckets[sid]=[];studentBuckets[sid].push(Number(score));}});
    const rows=students.map(s=>{const arr=studentBuckets[s.id]||[];const mean=arr.length?(arr.reduce((a,b)=>a+(Number(b)||0),0)/arr.length):0;const{grade,remark}=zambianGrade(mean,reportLevel);return{id:s.id,name:s.name,photo:s.photo||'',mean:round2(mean),grade,remark};}).sort((a,b)=>b.mean-a.mean);

    html+=`<table><thead><tr><th>Rank</th><th>ID</th><th>Student</th><th>Avg %</th><th>Grade</th><th>Remark</th></tr></thead><tbody>`;
    rows.forEach((r,i)=>{html+=`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHTML(r.id)}</td><td>${r.photo?`<img src="${escapeHTML(r.photo)}" class="photo">`:''}${escapeHTML(r.name)}</td><td>${escapeHTML(String(r.mean))}</td><td>${escapeHTML(r.grade)}</td><td>${escapeHTML(r.remark)}</td></tr>`;});
    html+=`</tbody></table>`;

    if (includeAnalytics) {
      const{classAverage,passRate}=await computeClassStats(className,reportLevel);
      const dist={'Distinction':0,'Merit':0,'Credit':0,'Satisfactory':0,'Unsatisfactory':0};
      for(const r of rows){const{remark}=zambianGrade(r.mean,reportLevel);if(remark in dist)dist[remark]++;}
      const distKeys=Object.keys(dist).filter(k=>dist[k]>0);const maxVal=Math.max(...distKeys.map(k=>dist[k]),1);const barW=Math.max(700/distKeys.length,80);
      let svg=`<svg class="inline-chart" viewBox="0 0 700 240" xmlns="http://www.w3.org/2000/svg">`;
      distKeys.forEach((k,i)=>{const h=(dist[k]/maxVal)*160;const x=i*barW+20;const y=200-h;svg+=`<rect x="${x}" y="${y}" width="${barW-40}" height="${h}" fill="${getGradeColor(zambianGrade(50,reportLevel).grade)}"></rect><text x="${x+(barW-40)/2}" y="${200+15}" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle">${escapeHTML(k)}</text><text x="${x+(barW-40)/2}" y="${y-6}" font-size="12" text-anchor="middle">${dist[k]}</text>`;});
      svg+=`</svg>`;
      html+=`<h3>Analytics Summary</h3><p>Class average: ${round2(classAverage)}%</p><p>Pass rate: ${escapeHTML(String(passRate))}%</p>${svg}`;
    }
    if (comments) {
      html+=`<div class="comments"><h4>Teacher's Remarks</h4><p>${escapeHTML(comments)}</p><div class="signature"><p>Teacher Signature: __________________________  Date: __________</p></div></div>`;
    }
    html+=`<div class="footer">TeachMate Results Report — Generated on ${todayString()}</div></body></html>`;
    return html;
  }

  // ── Script loaders ───────────────────────────────────────────

  function loadScript(url){ return new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{ if(document.querySelector(`script[src="${url}"]`)) return resolve(); const s=document.createElement('script');s.src=url;s.onload=resolve;s.onerror=reject;document.head.appendChild(s); }); }
  async function ensurePdfMake(){ if(typeof pdfMake!=='undefined') return pdfMake; await loadScript('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdfmake/0.2.10/pdfmake.min.js'); await loadScript('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/pdfmake/0.2.10/vfs_fonts.min.js'); return pdfMake; }
  async function ensureChartJs(){ if(typeof Chart!=='undefined') return Chart; await loadScript('https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/npm/chart.js'); return Chart; }
  async function ensureHtml2pdfLib(){ if(typeof html2pdf!=='undefined') return html2pdf; await loadScript('https://cdnjs.cloudflare.com/ajax/libs/html2pdf.js/0.10.1/html2pdf.bundle.min.js'); return html2pdf; }

  async function generateChartImage(entries) {
    if(!entries||!entries.length) return null; await ensureChartJs();
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas'); canvas.width=300; canvas.height=150; canvas.style.display='none'; document.body.appendChild(canvas);
    new Chart(canvas,{type:'bar',data:{labels:entries.map(e=>e.subject.substring(0,8)),datasets:[{label:'Scores (%)',data:entries.map(e=>e.score),backgroundColor:'rgba(54,162,235,0.6)',borderColor:'rgba(54,162,235,1)',borderWidth:1}]},options:{responsive:false,plugins:{legend:{display:false}},scales:{y:{beginAtZero:true,max:100}}}});
    const dataURL=canvas.toDataURL('image/png'); document.body.removeChild(canvas); return dataURL;
  }

  // ==========================================
  // 🤖 AI COMMENT ENGINE — TEACHER (unchanged)
  // ==========================================

  function generateAutoTeacherComment({ studentName='the student', avg=0, overallGrade='F', overallRemark='', passRate=0, entries=[], reportLevel='Senior Secondary', teacher='' }={}) {
    const firstName=String(studentName).split(/\s+/)[0]||'the student';
    const subjectMap={};
    entries.forEach(e=>{const subj=(e.subject||'Unknown').trim();if(!subjectMap[subj])subjectMap[subj]=[];subjectMap[subj].push(Number(e.score)||0);});
    const subjectAverages=Object.entries(subjectMap).map(([subj,scores])=>({subject:subj,avg:scores.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/scores.length})).sort((a,b)=>b.avg-a.avg);
    const bestSubject=subjectAverages[0]||null; const worstSubject=subjectAverages[subjectAverages.length-1]||null;
    let trendPhrase='';
    if(entries.length>=3){const sorted=[...entries].sort((a,b)=>(a.date<b.date?-1:1));const fh=sorted.slice(0,Math.floor(sorted.length/2));const sh=sorted.slice(Math.floor(sorted.length/2));const fa=fh.reduce((a,e)=>a+(Number(e.score)||0),0)/fh.length;const sa=sh.reduce((a,e)=>a+(Number(e.score)||0),0)/sh.length;if(sa-fa>=5)trendPhrase='showing a commendable upward trend in recent assessments';else if(fa-sa>=5)trendPhrase='whose scores have dipped in more recent assessments';else trendPhrase='maintaining a fairly consistent performance pattern';}
    let comment='';
    if(avg>=75){const o=[`${firstName} has delivered an outstanding academic performance this term`,`It is with great pleasure that I commend ${firstName} on an exceptional term`,`${firstName} has distinguished themselves as one of the top-performing students this term`];let b=`, achieving an impressive overall average of ${avg}% and earning a ${overallGrade} grade.`;if(bestSubject&&subjectAverages.length>1)b+=` Particular strength has been demonstrated in ${bestSubject.subject}, reflecting both natural aptitude and consistent effort.`;if(trendPhrase)b+=` ${firstName} is ${trendPhrase}, which is a very positive sign.`;if(passRate>=80)b+=` With a pass rate of ${passRate}%, ${firstName} has shown remarkable reliability across all subjects.`;const c=avg>=90?` I encourage ${firstName} to continue this exemplary standard and consider taking on leadership roles within the class. The potential here is exceptional.`:` I urge ${firstName} to maintain this momentum going into the next term. Continue practising good study habits — they are clearly paying off.`;comment=o[Math.floor(avg)%o.length]+b+c;}
    else if(avg>=60){const o=[`${firstName} has performed well this term`,`This term, ${firstName} has shown a solid level of academic commitment`,`${firstName} has produced a good performance across most areas this term`];let b=`, recording an overall average of ${avg}% and attaining a ${overallGrade}.`;if(bestSubject&&worstSubject&&bestSubject.subject!==worstSubject.subject)b+=` ${firstName} shows particular confidence in ${bestSubject.subject}, while ${worstSubject.subject} presents an opportunity for further development.`;if(trendPhrase)b+=` The student is ${trendPhrase}.`;comment=o[Math.floor(avg)%o.length]+b+` With targeted revision and continued engagement in class, ${firstName} is well-positioned to reach the Distinction band. I am optimistic about what the next term holds.`;}
    else if(avg>=50){const o=[`${firstName} has demonstrated a satisfactory level of academic performance this term`,`This term, ${firstName} has shown reasonable effort and achieved a passing standard`,`${firstName} has met the minimum academic requirements this term`];let b=`, with an overall average of ${avg}% and a final grade of ${overallGrade}.`;if(bestSubject)b+=` ${firstName} demonstrates the most confidence in ${bestSubject.subject}.`;if(worstSubject&&subjectAverages.length>1)b+=` Dedicated attention to ${worstSubject.subject} is recommended.`;if(trendPhrase)b+=` The student is currently ${trendPhrase}.`;comment=o[Math.floor(avg)%o.length]+b+` I strongly encourage ${firstName} to increase study time, seek help when needed, and remain actively engaged during lessons.`;}
    else if(avg>=40){const o=[`${firstName} has found this term academically challenging`,`This term has presented some difficulties for ${firstName}`,`${firstName}'s academic performance this term has been below expectation`];let b=`, with an overall average of ${avg}%, placing them in the ${overallGrade} grade category.`;if(passRate<50)b+=` A pass rate of ${passRate}% suggests that several subjects require urgent and focused attention.`;if(bestSubject)b+=` There are clear sparks of ability in ${bestSubject.subject}.`;if(worstSubject&&subjectAverages.length>1)b+=` ${worstSubject.subject} in particular needs significant improvement before the next term.`;comment=o[Math.floor(avg)%o.length]+b+` I recommend that ${firstName}, together with parents or guardians, develops a structured revision plan.`;}
    else{const o=[`${firstName} has had a very difficult term academically`,`It is with concern that I report ${firstName}'s academic performance this term`,`This term has been a particularly challenging academic period for ${firstName}`];let b=`, with an overall average of ${avg}% and a grade of ${overallGrade}.`;if(passRate<=30)b+=` Passing only ${passRate}% of assessments is a serious concern that needs to be addressed immediately.`;if(bestSubject)b+=` While ${bestSubject.subject} showed some potential, the results across the board indicate that more support and effort are urgently needed.`;comment=o[Math.floor(avg)%o.length]+b+` I strongly urge that a parent-teacher meeting be arranged to discuss a clear and actionable improvement plan.`;}
    if(teacher&&String(teacher).trim()) comment+=`\n— ${String(teacher).trim()}`;
    return comment;
  }

  // ==========================================
  // 🗂️ COMMENT STORE — Teacher & Headteacher
  // ==========================================

  const COMMENTS_STORE    = 'teacherComments';
  const HT_COMMENTS_STORE = 'headteacherComments';

  function buildCommentKey(className,studentId){ return `${COMMENTS_STORE}:${String(className).trim().toLowerCase()}:${String(studentId).trim().toLowerCase()}`; }
  function buildHtCommentKey(className,studentId){ return `ht:${String(className).trim().toLowerCase()}:${String(studentId).trim().toLowerCase()}`; }

  async function saveTeacherComment(className,studentId,rec){ try{ const k=buildCommentKey(className,studentId);let all=await getIndexedDB(COMMENTS_STORE)||{};all[k]=rec;await setIndexedDB(COMMENTS_STORE,all); }catch(err){console.warn('⚠️ Could not save teacher comment:',err);} }
  async function loadTeacherComment(className,studentId){ try{ const k=buildCommentKey(className,studentId);const all=await getIndexedDB(COMMENTS_STORE)||{};return all[k]||null; }catch(err){console.warn('⚠️ Could not load teacher comment:',err);return null;} }
  async function saveHeadteacherComment(className,studentId,rec){ try{ const k=buildHtCommentKey(className,studentId);let all=await getIndexedDB(HT_COMMENTS_STORE)||{};all[k]=rec;await setIndexedDB(HT_COMMENTS_STORE,all); }catch(err){console.warn('⚠️ Could not save headteacher comment:',err);} }
  async function loadHeadteacherComment(className,studentId){ try{ const k=buildHtCommentKey(className,studentId);const all=await getIndexedDB(HT_COMMENTS_STORE)||{};return all[k]||null; }catch(err){console.warn('⚠️ Could not load headteacher comment:',err);return null;} }

  // ==========================================
  // 💬 COMMENT EDITOR MODAL (unchanged)
  // ==========================================

  function openCommentEditorModal(autoComment,savedComment=null){
    return new Promise(resolve=>{
      const existing=document.getElementById('tm-comment-modal');if(existing)existing.remove();
      const initialText=savedComment||autoComment;const isEditedPreviously=!!savedComment;
      const modal=document.createElement('div');modal.id='tm-comment-modal';
      modal.style.cssText='position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;background:rgba(15,23,42,0.75);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;padding:16px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,"Segoe UI",sans-serif;backdrop-filter:blur(4px);';
      modal.innerHTML=`<div style="background:#ffffff;border-radius:14px;width:100%;max-width:600px;box-shadow:0 25px 60px rgba(0,0,0,0.3);overflow:hidden;display:flex;flex-direction:column;"><div style="background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1e3a8a 0%,#1d4ed8 100%);padding:18px 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;"><div style="background:rgba(255,255,255,0.2);border-radius:8px;width:36px;height:36px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;font-size:18px;flex-shrink:0;">💬</div><div><div style="color:#ffffff;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;">Teacher's Remarks</div><div style="color:#bfdbfe;font-size:12px;margin-top:1px;">Auto-generated · Edit freely before printing</div></div></div><div style="background:#f0fdf4;border-bottom:1px solid #dcfce7;padding:10px 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;"><span style="background:#059669;color:#fff;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;padding:2px 8px;border-radius:20px;text-transform:uppercase;">AI Generated</span><span style="color:#166534;font-size:12px;">${isEditedPreviously?'✏️ You have a previously saved edit — shown below.':'This comment was automatically created from the student\'s results.'}</span></div><div style="padding:18px 22px;"><label style="display:block;font-size:11px;font-weight:700;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:0.07em;margin-bottom:8px;">Comment (editable)</label><textarea id="tm-comment-textarea" style="width:100%;min-height:150px;padding:12px 14px;border:2px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:10px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.7;color:#0f172a;resize:vertical;outline:none;font-family:inherit;box-sizing:border-box;">${initialText.replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;')}</textarea><div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;margin-top:8px;"><span id="tm-char-count" style="font-size:11px;color:#94a3b8;"></span><button id="tm-reset-btn" style="background:none;border:none;color:#6366f1;font-size:12px;cursor:pointer;padding:0;font-family:inherit;text-decoration:underline;">↺ Reset to AI comment</button></div></div><div style="padding:14px 22px 18px;display:flex;gap:10px;justify-content:flex-end;border-top:1px solid #f1f5f9;"><button id="tm-use-auto-btn" style="padding:9px 18px;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:2px solid #059669;background:#f0fdf4;color:#166534;font-family:inherit;">✅ Use AI Comment</button><button id="tm-save-manual-btn" style="padding:9px 18px;border-radius:8px;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:none;background:#1e3a8a;color:#ffffff;font-family:inherit;">💾 Save & Use My Edit</button></div></div>`;
      document.body.appendChild(modal);
      const textarea=modal.querySelector('#tm-comment-textarea');const charCount=modal.querySelector('#tm-char-count');const resetBtn=modal.querySelector('#tm-reset-btn');
      textarea.addEventListener('focus',()=>{textarea.style.borderColor='#1d4ed8';});textarea.addEventListener('blur',()=>{textarea.style.borderColor='#e2e8f0';});
      const updateCount=()=>{charCount.textContent=`${textarea.value.length} characters`;};textarea.addEventListener('input',updateCount);updateCount();
      resetBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{textarea.value=autoComment;updateCount();});
      modal.querySelector('#tm-use-auto-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{modal.remove();resolve({text:autoComment,isManual:false});});
      modal.querySelector('#tm-save-manual-btn').addEventListener('click',()=>{const t=textarea.value.trim()||autoComment;modal.remove();resolve({text:t,isManual:t!==autoComment});});
      modal.addEventListener('click',e=>{if(e.target===modal){modal.remove();resolve({text:initialText,isManual:isEditedPreviously});}});
    });
  }

  // ── detectGradeLevel (unchanged) ─────────────────────────────
  function detectGradeLevel(className){ if(!className)return'Senior Secondary';const cls=String(className).toLowerCase();if(cls.match(/(10|11|12)/))return'Senior Secondary';if(cls.match(/(8|9)/))return'Junior Secondary';if(cls.match(/(1|2|3|4|5|6|7)/))return'Primary';return'Senior Secondary'; }


async function buildStudentPdfDefinition(className, studentId, opts={}, manualEntries=[]) {
    try {
      let students = await getIndexedDB('students'); if(!Array.isArray(students)) students=[];
      const normalize = v => String(v??'').trim().toLowerCase();
      const student = students.find(s => normalize(s.id) === normalize(studentId));
      if (!student) throw new Error(`Student not found for ID: ${studentId}`);

      let reportLevel = opts.level || detectGradeLevel(className);
      let entries = Array.isArray(manualEntries) ? manualEntries.slice() : [];

      if (entries.length === 0) {
        let results = await getIndexedDB('results') || [];
        results = results.filter(r => {
          const c=r.classId||r.class||r.className;
          if(!c||String(c).trim().toLowerCase()!==String(className).trim().toLowerCase()) return false;
          return r.scores && Object.keys(r.scores).some(k=>normalize(k)===normalize(studentId));
        });
        if (opts.category && opts.category.toLowerCase()!=='all') results=results.filter(r=>String(r.category).toLowerCase()===String(opts.category).toLowerCase());
        if (opts.testName) results=results.filter(r=>r.testName===opts.testName);
        entries = results.map(r=>{
          let sv=0; for(const[k,v]of Object.entries(r.scores)){if(normalize(k)===normalize(studentId)){sv=Number(v);break;}}
          const{grade,remark}=zambianGrade(sv,reportLevel);
          return{date:r.date,subject:r.subject,testName:r.testName,category:r.category,score:round2(sv),grade,remark};
        }).sort((a,b)=>(a.date<b.date?1:-1));
      }

      const totalRecords = entries.length;
      const avg = totalRecords ? round2(entries.reduce((a,e)=>a+(Number(e.score)||0),0)/totalRecords) : 0;
      const{grade:overallGrade,remark:overallRemark}=zambianGrade(avg,reportLevel);
      let passedSubjects=0; entries.forEach(e=>{if(Number(e.score)>=40)passedSubjects++;});
      const currentPassRate = totalRecords ? round2((passedSubjects/totalRecords)*100) : 0;
      const studentStatus = currentPassRate>=50?'Proceed':'Needs Improvement';
      const statusColor   = currentPassRate>=50?'#15803d':'#b91c1c';

      // ── Teacher comment ───────────────────────────────────────
      let finalCommentText = ''; let commentIsAuto = true;
      if (opts.comments && opts.comments.trim()!=='' && opts.comments!=='No comments provided.') {
        finalCommentText=opts.comments; commentIsAuto=false;
      } else {
        const autoComment=generateAutoTeacherComment({studentName:student.name||studentId,avg,overallGrade,overallRemark,passRate:currentPassRate,entries,reportLevel,teacher:opts.teacher||''});
        const savedRecord=await loadTeacherComment(className,studentId);
        const savedManual=(savedRecord&&savedRecord.isManual)?savedRecord.text:null;
        if(opts.skipCommentPrompt){finalCommentText=savedManual||autoComment;commentIsAuto=!savedManual;}
        else{const result=await openCommentEditorModal(autoComment,savedManual);finalCommentText=result.text;commentIsAuto=!result.isManual;await saveTeacherComment(className,studentId,{text:finalCommentText,isManual:result.isManual,generatedAt:new Date().toISOString()});}
      }

      // ── Headteacher comment ───────────────────────────────────
      let finalHtCommentText = '';
      if (opts.htComments && opts.htComments.trim()!=='') {
        finalHtCommentText = opts.htComments;
      } else {
        const autoHt = generateAutoHeadteacherComment({studentName:student.name||studentId,avg,overallGrade,passRate:currentPassRate});
        const savedHt = await loadHeadteacherComment(className,studentId);
        finalHtCommentText = (savedHt&&savedHt.isManual) ? savedHt.text : autoHt;
      }

      // ── Strip emoji for pdfMake ───────────────────────────────
      const stripEmoji = t => t.replace(/[\u{1F600}-\u{1F64F}\u{1F300}-\u{1F5FF}\u{1F680}-\u{1F6FF}\u{1F700}-\u{1F77F}\u{1F780}-\u{1F7FF}\u{1F800}-\u{1F8FF}\u{1F900}-\u{1F9FF}\u{1FA00}-\u{1FA6F}\u{1FA70}-\u{1FAFF}\u{2600}-\u{26FF}\u{2700}-\u{27BF}]/gu,'');
      const safeComments   = stripEmoji(finalCommentText);
      const safeHtComments = stripEmoji(finalHtCommentText);

      // ── Load school logo ──────────────────────────────────────
      let logoBase64 = opts.logoBase64 || null;
      if (!logoBase64 && opts.school) logoBase64 = await loadSchoolLogo(opts.school);

      const content = [];


// ── 1. Header: Logo stacked above School Name ──
if (logoBase64 && logoBase64.startsWith('data:image/')) {
content.push({ image: logoBase64, fit: [40, 40], alignment: 'center', margin: [0, 0, 0, 2] });
}
content.push({ text: opts.school || 'OFFICIAL ACADEMIC REPORT', style: 'schoolName' });
content.push({
text: opts.category && opts.category.toLowerCase() !== 'all'
? `${opts.category} Results Report`
: 'Comprehensive Academic Report',
style: 'header'
});
content.push({ canvas: [
{ type: 'line', x1: 0, y1: 0, x2: 555, y2: 0, lineWidth: 1.5, lineColor: '#1e3a8a' },
{ type: 'line', x1: 0, y1: 2.5, x2: 555, y2: 2.5, lineWidth: 0.75, lineColor: '#059669' }
], margin: [0, 0, 0, 3] });



      // ── 2. Student info row ───────────────────────────────────
      const studentInfoStack = [
        { text: [{ text: 'Student: ', bold: true }, `${student.name || 'Unknown'}`] },
        { text: [{ text: 'ID: ', bold: true }, `${String(studentId)}`] },
        { text: [{ text: 'Class: ', bold: true }, `${opts.pdfClass || className}`] }
      ];
      let photoBlock = { width: 'auto', text: '' };
      if (student.photo && student.photo.startsWith('data:image/'))
        photoBlock = { image: student.photo, fit: [38, 38], margin: [0, 0, 6, 0] };

      content.push({
        columns: [
          photoBlock,
          { width: '*', stack: studentInfoStack },
          { width: '40%', stack: [
              { text: [{ text: 'Level: ', bold: true }, `${reportLevel}`] },
              { text: [{ text: 'Teacher: ', bold: true }, `${opts.teacher || 'Not Assigned'}`] },
              { text: [{ text: 'Date: ', bold: true }, `${new Date().toLocaleDateString()}`] }
            ], alignment: 'right'
          }
        ],
        margin: [0, 0, 0, 3]
      });

      // ── 3. Results table ──────────────────────────────────────
      if (!entries.length) {
        content.push({ text: `No ${opts.category && opts.category !== 'All' ? opts.category : ''} academic records found.`, italics: true, alignment: 'center', margin: [0, 10, 0, 0] });
      } else {
        const tableBody = [[
          { text: 'Date',     style: 'tableHeader' },
          { text: 'Subject',  style: 'tableHeader' },
          { text: 'Category', style: 'tableHeader' },
          { text: 'Score(%)', style: 'tableHeader' },
          { text: 'Grade',    style: 'tableHeader' },
          { text: 'Remark',   style: 'tableHeader' }
        ]];
        entries.forEach(e => tableBody.push([
          e.date || '', e.subject || '', e.category || '-',
          { text: String(e.score || '0'), bold: true },
          e.grade || '', e.remark || ''
        ]));

        content.push({
          table: { headerRows: 1, widths: ['auto', '*', 'auto', 'auto', 'auto', '*'], body: tableBody },
          layout: {
            fillColor:    ri => ri === 0 ? '#1e3a8a' : (ri % 2 === 0 ? '#f8fafc' : '#ffffff'),
            hLineWidth:   () => 0.5,
            vLineWidth:   () => 0.5,
            hLineColor:   (i, node) => (i === 0 || i === node.table.body.length) ? '#1e3a8a' : '#cbd5e1',
            vLineColor:   () => '#cbd5e1',
            paddingTop:   () => 1,    // ← was 2, now 1
            paddingBottom:() => 1,    // ← was 2, now 1
            paddingLeft:  () => 3,
            paddingRight: () => 3
          },
          margin: [0, 0, 0, 3]        // ← was 5, now 3
        });

        // ── 4. Analytics block ────────────────────────────────
        content.push({ text: 'Performance Analytics Summary', style: 'summaryHeader', margin: [0, 0, 0, 1] });
        content.push({
          table: { widths: ['*', '*', '*', '*'], body: [
            [
              { text: 'Overall Average', style: 'statLabel' },
              { text: 'Final Grade',     style: 'statLabel' },
              { text: 'Pass Rate',       style: 'statLabel' },
              { text: 'Status',          style: 'statLabel' }
            ],
            [
              { text: `${avg}%`,           style: 'statValue' },
              { text: `${overallGrade}`,   style: 'statValue' },
              { text: `${currentPassRate}%`, style: 'statValue', color: statusColor },
              { text: `${studentStatus}`,  style: 'statValue', color: statusColor }
            ]
          ]},
          layout: 'noBorders',
          margin: [0, 0, 0, 3]        // ← was 5, now 3
        });
      }

      // ── 5. Remarks ────────────────────────────────────────────
      content.push({ canvas: [{ type: 'line', x1: 0, y1: 0, x2: 555, y2: 0, lineWidth: 0.75, lineColor: '#cbd5e1' }], margin: [0, 1, 0, 3] });

      content.push({ text: "Class Teacher's Remarks:", style: 'remarksHeader', margin: [0, 0, 0, 1] });
      if (commentIsAuto) {
        content.push({ columns: [
          { width: '*',    text: safeComments, fontSize: 6, margin: [0, 0, 0, 2] },
          { width: 'auto', text: '[Auto]', fontSize: 5, color: '#059669', italics: true, margin: [4, 0, 0, 2], alignment: 'right' }
        ]});
      } else {
        content.push({ text: safeComments, fontSize: 6, margin: [0, 0, 0, 2] });
      }

      content.push({ text: "Head Teacher's Remarks:", style: 'remarksHeader', margin: [0, 0, 0, 1] });
      content.push({ text: safeHtComments || 'No remarks provided.', fontSize: 6, margin: [0, 0, 0, 3] });

      // ── 6. Signatures ─────────────────────────────────────────
      content.push({ columns: [
        { text: 'Class Teacher Signature: ___________________', width: '50%', fontSize: 6 },
        { text: 'Head Teacher Signature: ___________________',  width: '50%', alignment: 'right', fontSize: 6 }
      ]});

      // ── Document definition ───────────────────────────────────
      const dd = {
        pageSize: 'A4',
        pageMargins: [18, 18, 18, 22],   // ← tighter than [20,20,20,20]
        watermark: { text: opts.school || 'TEACHMATE', color: '#f1f5f9', opacity: 0.4, bold: true, italics: false },
        content,
        footer: { text: `TeachMate 3.0 Pro — Generated ${new Date().toLocaleString()}`, alignment: 'center', style: 'footer' },
        styles: {
          schoolName:    { fontSize: 12, bold: true, alignment: 'center', color: '#0f172a' },   // ← was 14
          header:        { fontSize: 8,  alignment: 'center', color: '#475569' },               // ← was 9
          tableHeader:   { bold: true, fontSize: 7, color: '#ffffff', alignment: 'center' },    // ← was 8
          summaryHeader: { fontSize: 8,  bold: true, color: '#059669' },                        // ← was 9
          statLabel:     { fontSize: 6,  color: '#64748b', alignment: 'center' },              // ← was 7
          statValue:     { fontSize: 9,  bold: true, color: '#0f172a', alignment: 'center' },   // ← was 11 ⚠️ this was a big offender
          remarksHeader: { fontSize: 6,  bold: true },                                          // same
          footer:        { fontSize: 5,  color: '#94a3b8', margin: [0, 2, 0, 0] },
        },
        defaultStyle: { fontSize: 7 }    // ← was 7.5
      };

      return { dd, entries };
    } catch(err) { console.error('❌ buildStudentPdfDefinition error:', err); throw err; }
  }


  // ==========================================
  // exportStudentResultsPDF (unchanged)
  // ==========================================

  async function exportStudentResultsPDF(className,studentId,opts={},manualEntries=[]) {
    try{
      if(!className) return showToast('⚠️ Please select a class','error');
      if(!studentId) return showToast('⚠️ Enter a valid student ID','error');
      showLoading(`Generating Professional ${opts.category&&opts.category!=='All'?opts.category:''} Report...`);
      const{dd}=await buildStudentPdfDefinition(className,studentId,opts,manualEntries);
      const pdfMakeLib=await ensurePdfMake();
      const pdfDocGenerator=pdfMakeLib.createPdf(dd);
      pdfDocGenerator.getBlob(async blob=>{
        const fileName=`${studentId}_Official_Report.pdf`;
        const file=new File([blob],fileName,{type:'application/pdf'});
        hideLoading();
        if(navigator.canShare&&navigator.canShare({files:[file]})){
          try{await navigator.share({files:[file],title:'Student Academic Report',text:`Official academic report for ${studentId} generated via TeachMate.`});showToast('✅ Report shared successfully','success');}
          catch(e){console.log('Share cancelled, downloading instead.');pdfDocGenerator.download(fileName);}
        }else{pdfDocGenerator.download(fileName);showToast('✅ Report downloaded successfully','success');}
      });
    }catch(err){console.error('❌ exportStudentResultsPDF error:',err);hideLoading();showToast('⚠️ Error: '+(err.message||'Unknown'),'error');}
  }

  // ==========================================
  // exportResultsPDF — class-level (unchanged)
  // ==========================================

  async function exportResultsPDF(className,opts={}) {
    if(!className) return showToast('Select a class','error');
    if(!opts||Object.keys(opts).length===0){console.warn('exportResultsPDF called without options - skipping');return;}
    try{
      showLoading('Preparing PDF report...');
      const html=await buildPrintableReportHTML(className,{...opts,includeAnalytics:true});
      const html2pdfLib=await ensureHtml2pdfLib();
      const iframe=document.createElement('iframe');
      iframe.style.cssText='position:absolute;left:-10000px;top:0;width:610px;height:auto;';
      document.body.appendChild(iframe); iframe.srcdoc=html;
      await new Promise(resolve=>{iframe.onload=async()=>{try{const doc=iframe.contentDocument;doc.body.getBoundingClientRect();iframe.style.height=doc.body.scrollHeight+'px';}catch(e){console.warn('Layout wait warning',e);}resolve();};});
      setTimeout(async()=>{
        try{
          await html2pdfLib().set({margin:1,filename:'results_report.pdf',image:{type:'jpeg',quality:0.98},html2canvas:{scale:2,logging:true,useCORS:true,windowWidth:iframe.contentDocument.documentElement.scrollWidth},jsPDF:{unit:'in',format:'a4',orientation:'portrait'}}).from(iframe.contentDocument.body).save();
        }catch(e){console.error('PDF generation error',e);}
        finally{document.body.removeChild(iframe);hideLoading();showToast('✅ PDF downloaded','success');}
      },1000);
    }catch(err){console.error('exportResultsPDF error',err);hideLoading();showToast('⚠️ Failed to prepare PDF: '+(err.message||'Unknown error'),'error');}
  }

  (function wireUI() {

    // Nothing here depends on AutoMark.html existing — if it's ever moved or

    safeOn('exportResultsPdfBtn','click',async()=>{
      try{
