        const level=document.getElementById('results-level-filter')?.value||document.getElementById('results-level')?.value||'';
        const category=document.getElementById('results-category-filter')?.value||'';
        const testName=prompt('please leave the entry field down here blank to continue ☺️:','')||'';
        const comments=prompt('Enter teacher comments for the report📝:','')||'';
        await exportResultsPDF(className,{title:`Results Report - ${className}`,level,category,comments,testName,includeAnalytics:true});
      }catch(e){console.error(e);showToast('Export failed','error');}
    });

    safeOn('exportStudentPdfBtn','click',async()=>{ try{await openIndividualStudentPDFForm();}catch(e){console.error(e);showToast('Failed to open PDF form','error');} });
    safeOn('bulkGenerateReportCardsBtn','click',async()=>{ try{await openBulkReportCardForm();}catch(e){console.error(e);showToast('Failed to open bulk generation form','error');} });

    safeOn('generateAnalyticsBtn','click',async()=>{
      try{const c=document.getElementById('results-analytics');if(c)c.style.display='block';await generateClassAnalytics();}catch(e){console.error(e);showToast('Analytics failed','error');}
    });

    safeOn('deleteAllResultsBtn','click',async()=>{ try{await deleteAllResults();}catch(e){console.error(e);showToast('Delete failed','error');} });

    safeOn('clearResultsInputsBtn','click',()=>{ document.querySelectorAll('#results-table .result-input').forEach(i=>i.value='');showToast('Cleared inputs','info'); });

    const hideHistoryBtn=document.getElementById('hideHistoryBtn');
    if(hideHistoryBtn){hideHistoryBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{const d=document.getElementById('results-history');if(!d)return;d.style.display=d.style.display==='none'?'block':'none';hideHistoryBtn.textContent=d.style.display==='none'?'Show History':'Hide History';});}

    const hideAnalyticsBtn=document.getElementById('hideAnalyticsBtn');
    if(hideAnalyticsBtn){hideAnalyticsBtn.addEventListener('click',()=>{const d=document.getElementById('results-analytics');if(!d)return;d.style.display=d.style.display==='none'?'block':'none';hideAnalyticsBtn.textContent=d.style.display==='none'?'Show Analytics':'Hide Analytics';});}

    (async function populateClasses(){
      showLoading('Loading classes...');
      const sel=document.getElementById('results-class');if(!sel){console.warn('No results-class select found');hideLoading();return;}
      let classes=[]; const maxRetries=10; let retryCount=0; let lastError=null;
      while(retryCount<maxRetries){
        try{classes=await getIndexedDB('classes')||[];if(!Array.isArray(classes)){classes=[];console.warn('Classes data not array—defaulting to empty');}break;}
        catch(e){lastError=e;retryCount++;if(retryCount<maxRetries){console.log(`DB retry ${retryCount}/${maxRetries} in 1000ms...`);await new Promise(r=>setTimeout(r,1000));}else{console.error('populateClasses failed after retries',lastError.name||'Unknown',lastError.message||'No details');showToast(`Failed to load classes (${lastError.message||'DB not ready'})—using empty list`,'warn');}}
      }
      sel.innerHTML='<option value="">Select class</option>';
      classes.forEach(c=>{const o=document.createElement('option');o.value=c.id||c.name||'';o.textContent=c.name||c.id||c;sel.appendChild(o);});
      if(classes.length>0) showToast('Classes loaded','success');
      hideLoading();
    })();
  })();

  window._resultsModuleHelpers = { computeClassStats, buildPrintableReportHTML, buildPrintableReportHTMLAsync:buildPrintableReportHTML };

  window.testGrading = function(){
    const tS=[{score:100,expected:{grade:'1',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:75,expected:{grade:'1',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:74,expected:{grade:'2',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:70,expected:{grade:'2',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:69,expected:{grade:'3',remark:'Merit'}},{score:65,expected:{grade:'3',remark:'Merit'}},{score:64,expected:{grade:'4',remark:'Merit'}},{score:60,expected:{grade:'4',remark:'Merit'}},{score:59,expected:{grade:'5',remark:'Credit'}},{score:55,expected:{grade:'5',remark:'Credit'}},{score:54,expected:{grade:'6',remark:'Credit'}},{score:50,expected:{grade:'6',remark:'Credit'}},{score:49,expected:{grade:'7',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:45,expected:{grade:'7',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:44,expected:{grade:'8',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:40,expected:{grade:'8',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:39,expected:{grade:'9',remark:'Unsatisfactory'}},{score:0,expected:{grade:'9',remark:'Unsatisfactory'}}];
    const tJ=[{score:100,expected:{grade:'1',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:75,expected:{grade:'1',remark:'Distinction'}},{score:74,expected:{grade:'2',remark:'Merit'}},{score:60,expected:{grade:'2',remark:'Merit'}},{score:59,expected:{grade:'3',remark:'Credit'}},{score:50,expected:{grade:'3',remark:'Credit'}},{score:49,expected:{grade:'4',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:40,expected:{grade:'4',remark:'Satisfactory'}},{score:39,expected:{grade:'U',remark:'Unsatisfactory'}},{score:0,expected:{grade:'U',remark:'Unsatisfactory'}}];
    console.log('Testing Senior Secondary:');
    tS.forEach(t=>{const r=zambianGrade(t.score,'Senior Secondary');const p=r.grade===t.expected.grade&&r.remark===t.expected.remark;console.log(`Score:${t.score} grade:${r.grade} ${r.remark} | Expected:${t.expected.grade} ${t.expected.remark} | Pass:${p}`);if(!p)console.error('Test failed for score '+t.score);});
    console.log('Testing Junior/Primary:');
    tJ.forEach(t=>{const r=zambianGrade(t.score,'Junior Secondary');const p=r.grade===t.expected.grade&&r.remark===t.expected.remark;console.log(`Score:${t.score} grade:${r.grade} ${r.remark} | Expected:${t.expected.grade} ${t.expected.remark} | Pass:${p}`);if(!p)console.error('Test failed for score '+t.score);});
    console.log('Run testGrading() in console to validate the grading scale.');
  };
  
  
  // ============================================================
// TeachMate — SMS Results Gateway (ADDON — do not modify above)
// Africa's Talking via Render backend — no API key in the browser
// Paste this block BEFORE the closing })(); of Results Module
// ============================================================

// ── SMS CONFIG STORE ─────────────────────────────────────────
const SMS_SETTINGS_KEY  = 'smsSettings';
const SMS_LOG_KEY       = 'smsLog';
const SMS_CREDITS_KEY   = 'smsCredits';

// Default config shape.
// Backend URL + Gateway Token are pre-filled here so teachers never
// have to open Settings or type anything to use the SMS feature.
// The real Africa's Talking API key is NOT here — it lives only in
// Render's environment variables, server-side, never in this file.
const DEFAULT_SMS_SETTINGS = {
  backendUrl:    'https://sms-teachmate.onrender.com',
  gatewayToken:  '00879990a7897ab16cf7fce4933e6d9a',
  senderId:      'TEACHMATE', // for display purposes only; actual sender is set server-side
};

// ── SMS FORMATTER ─────────────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Builds the compact TeachMate SMS body.
 * Fits school name + results in 2-3 SMS segments (~320 chars).
 *
 * Output example:
 * TEACHMATE
 * ST FRANCIS SECONDARY
 * MIDTERM EXAMINATION
 *
 * JOHN PHIRI
 * MTH:78 ENG:82 SCI:75 SST:80
 * AVG:79% POS:5/42
 */
function formatSMSMessage({ schoolName, assessmentName, studentName, scores, avg, position, classSize }) {
  // Abbreviate subject names to 3-4 chars (cost-saving)
  const scoreStr = Object.entries(scores || {})
    .map(([subj, score]) => {
      const abbr = String(subj)
        .toUpperCase()
        .replace(/\s+/g, '')
        .substring(0, 4);
      return `${abbr}:${score}`;
    })
    .join(' ');

  const school     = (schoolName || 'YOUR SCHOOL').toUpperCase().substring(0, 40);
  const assessment = (assessmentName || 'ASSESSMENT').toUpperCase().substring(0, 30);
  const name       = (studentName || 'STUDENT').toUpperCase().substring(0, 30);
  const posText    = (position && classSize) ? `POS:${position}/${classSize}` : '';
  const avgText    = `AVG:${avg}%`;

  return [
    'TEACHMATE',
    school,
    assessment,
    '',
    name,
    scoreStr,
    `${avgText}${posText ? ' ' + posText : ''}`,
  ].filter(line => line !== null).join('\n');
}

// ── LOG WRITER ────────────────────────────────────────────────
async function logSMSMessage(entry) {
  try {
    const logs = (await getIndexedDB(SMS_LOG_KEY)) || [];
    logs.unshift({
      ...entry,
      sentAt: new Date().toISOString(),
    });
    // Keep last 500 entries
    if (logs.length > 500) logs.length = 500;
    await setIndexedDB(SMS_LOG_KEY, SMS_LOG_KEY, logs);
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('SMS log write failed', err);
  }
}

// ── CREDITS MANAGER ──────────────────────────────────────────
// NOTE: This is now a LOCAL counter for your own bookkeeping/UX only.
// It does NOT reflect your real Africa's Talking balance — check that
// in the AT dashboard. This local count just lets you show teachers
// a rough "X messages sent this term" style number if you want one.
async function getSMSCredits() {
  try {
    const c = await getIndexedDB(SMS_CREDITS_KEY, SMS_CREDITS_KEY);
    return (c && typeof c.balance === 'number') ? c.balance : 0;
  } catch { return 0; }
}

async function deductSMSCredits(count) {
  try {
    const current = await getSMSCredits();
    const newBal  = Math.max(0, current - count);
    await setIndexedDB(SMS_CREDITS_KEY, SMS_CREDITS_KEY, { balance: newBal, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
    return newBal;
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Credit deduction failed', err);
  }
}

async function addSMSCredits(count) {
  try {
    const current = await getSMSCredits();
    await setIndexedDB(SMS_CREDITS_KEY, SMS_CREDITS_KEY, { balance: current + count, updatedAt: new Date().toISOString() });
  } catch (err) {
    console.warn('Credit top-up failed', err);
  }
}

// ── RENDER BACKEND CALLER ─────────────────────────────────────
/**
 * Sends one SMS by calling YOUR Render backend, which holds the
 * real Africa's Talking API key server-side. The browser never
 * sees that key — only backendUrl + gatewayToken, which are just
 * routing/auth details, not the AT secret itself.
 *
 * @returns {{ success: boolean, messageId?: string, error?: string }}
 */
async function callBackendSMS(phone, message, settings) {
  const { backendUrl, gatewayToken } = settings;

  if (!backendUrl || backendUrl.trim() === '' || backendUrl.includes('YOUR-RENDER-APP')) {
    console.log(`[SMS SIMULATION — no backend URL set] To: ${phone}\n${message}`);
    await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 200)); // Fake latency
    return { success: true, messageId: `SIM-${Date.now()}`, simulated: true };
  }

  try {
    const res = await fetch(`${backendUrl.replace(/\/+$/, '')}/send-sms`, {
      method: 'POST',
      headers: {
        'Content-Type': 'application/json',
        ...(gatewayToken ? { 'X-Gateway-Token': gatewayToken } : {}),
      },
      body: JSON.stringify({
        phone: phone.replace(/\s+/g, ''),
        message,
      }),
    });

    let data;
    try {
      data = await res.json();
    } catch {
      return { success: false, error: `Backend returned non-JSON response (HTTP ${res.status})` };
    }

    if (!res.ok) {
      return { success: false, error: data.error || `HTTP ${res.status}` };
    }

    return {
      success:   !!data.success,
      messageId: data.messageId || null,
      error:     data.error || (data.success ? null : 'Unknown failure'),
    };
  } catch (err) {
    return { success: false, error: `Could not reach backend: ${err.message}` };
  }
}

// ── CORE: SEND RESULTS TO ONE STUDENT ────────────────────────
async function sendStudentResultsSMS(className, studentId, opts = {}) {
  const {
    schoolName      = '',
    assessmentName  = '',
    category        = '',
    level           = '',
    phone           = '',
    studentName     = '',
    position        = null,
    classSize       = null,
  } = opts;

  if (!phone || phone.trim() === '') {
    return { success: false, studentId, error: 'No phone number' };
  }

  // Load scores for this student across filtered results
  let results = (await getIndexedDB('results')) || [];
  results = results.filter(r => {
    const c = r.classId || r.class || r.className;
    const matchClass = c && String(c).trim().toLowerCase() === String(className).trim().toLowerCase();
    const matchScore = r.scores && r.scores[studentId] !== undefined;
    const matchCat   = !category || r.category === category;
    const matchLevel = !level || r.level === level;
    return matchClass && matchScore && matchCat && matchLevel;
  });

  if (results.length === 0) {
    return { success: false, studentId, error: 'No results found' };
  }

  // Build subject → score map (use latest score per subject)
  const subjectScores = {};
  results.sort((a, b) => (a.date > b.date ? -1 : 1)); // newest first
  results.forEach(r => {
    const subj = (r.subject || 'Unknown').trim();
    if (!(subj in subjectScores)) {
      subjectScores[subj] = Number(r.scores[studentId]);
    }
  });

  const scoreValues = Object.values(subjectScores);
  const avg = scoreValues.length
    ? round2(scoreValues.reduce((a, b) => a + b, 0) / scoreValues.length)
    : 0;

  const message = formatSMSMessage({
    schoolName,
    assessmentName: assessmentName || category || 'ASSESSMENT',
    studentName,
    scores: subjectScores,
    avg,
    position,
    classSize,
  });

  // Load SMS settings (falls back to the hardcoded defaults above
  // if nothing has been saved to IndexedDB yet — so it works out
  // of the box with zero setup)
  const settings = (await getIndexedDB(SMS_SETTINGS_KEY, SMS_SETTINGS_KEY)) || DEFAULT_SMS_SETTINGS;

  const result = await callBackendSMS(phone.trim(), message, settings);

  // Log every attempt (success or failure)
  await logSMSMessage({
    studentId,
    studentName,
    channel:    'SMS',
    phone:      phone.trim(),
    assessment: assessmentName || category,
    className,
    message,
    status:     result.success ? (result.simulated ? 'SIMULATED' : 'DELIVERED') : 'FAILED',
    messageId:  result.messageId || null,
    error:      result.error || null,
  });

  if (result.success) {
    await deductSMSCredits(1);
  }

  return { ...result, studentId, studentName, phone };
}

// ── CORE: SEND RESULTS TO ENTIRE CLASS ───────────────────────
/**
 * One-click: sends results to every student in the class
 * who has a phone number stored in their student record.
 */
window.sendClassResultsViaSMS = async function () {
  const classEl    = document.getElementById('results-class');
  const categoryEl = document.getElementById('results-category-filter') || document.getElementById('test-category');
  const levelEl    = document.getElementById('results-level-filter')    || document.getElementById('results-level');

  if (!classEl || !classEl.value) {
    return showToast('Select a class first', 'error');
  }

  const className  = classEl.value.trim();
  const category   = categoryEl ? categoryEl.value : '';
  const level      = levelEl    ? levelEl.value    : '';

  // Load students
  let students = (await getIndexedDB('students')) || [];
  students = students.filter(s => classMatches(s, className));

  if (!students.length) {
    return showToast('No students found in this class', 'error');
  }

  // Compute rankings for position field
  const rankMap = await _computeRankings(className, category, level);

  // Prompt for school name & assessment label once
  const schoolName     = prompt('School name for SMS header:', '') || '';
  const assessmentName = prompt('Assessment name (e.g. MIDTERM EXAMINATION):', category || '') || category || '';

  if (!assessmentName) {
    return showToast('Assessment name is required', 'error');
  }

  // Recognizes phone numbers stored under any of these field names —
  // TeachMate's student schema uses guardianPhone, but we keep the
  // others too in case some records use a different field.
  const studentsWithPhone = students.filter(s => s.phone || s.phoneNumber || s.contact || s.guardianPhone);

  if (!studentsWithPhone.length) {
    return showToast('No students have phone numbers stored. Update student records first.', 'error');
  }

  const settings    = (await getIndexedDB(SMS_SETTINGS_KEY, SMS_SETTINGS_KEY)) || DEFAULT_SMS_SETTINGS;
  const isSimulated = !settings.backendUrl || settings.backendUrl.trim() === '' || settings.backendUrl.includes('YOUR-RENDER-APP');

  const confirmed = confirm(
    `📤 Send Results via SMS\n\n` +
    `Class: ${className}\n` +
    `Assessment: ${assessmentName}\n` +
    `Recipients: ${studentsWithPhone.length} students with phone numbers\n` +
    (isSimulated
      ? `\n⚠️  NO BACKEND URL SET — running in simulation mode.\nMessages will be logged but not actually sent.\n`
      : `\nThis will send ${studentsWithPhone.length} real SMS messages via Africa's Talking.\n`) +
    `\nProceed?`
  );

  if (!confirmed) return;

  openSMSSendingModal(
    className, category, level, schoolName, assessmentName,
    students, studentsWithPhone, rankMap, settings, isSimulated
  );
};

// ── COMPUTE RANKINGS ─────────────────────────────────────────
async function _computeRankings(className, category, level) {
  try {
    let results = (await getIndexedDB('results')) || [];
    results = results.filter(r => {
      const c = r.classId || r.class || r.className;
      return (
        c && String(c).trim().toLowerCase() === String(className).trim().toLowerCase() &&
        (!category || r.category === category) &&
        (!level    || r.level    === level)
      );
    });

    const totals = {};
    const counts = {};
    results.forEach(r => {
      Object.entries(r.scores || {}).forEach(([sid, score]) => {
        totals[sid] = (totals[sid] || 0) + Number(score);
        counts[sid] = (counts[sid] || 0) + 1;
      });
    });

    const avgs = Object.entries(totals).map(([sid, total]) => ({
      sid,
      avg: counts[sid] ? round2(total / counts[sid]) : 0,
    })).sort((a, b) => b.avg - a.avg);

    const rankMap = {};
    avgs.forEach((s, i) => {
      rankMap[s.sid] = { position: i + 1, classSize: avgs.length };
    });
    return rankMap;
  } catch {
    return {};
  }
}

// ── SENDING PROGRESS MODAL ────────────────────────────────────
function openSMSSendingModal(
  className, category, level, schoolName, assessmentName,
  allStudents, studentsWithPhone, rankMap, settings, isSimulated
) {
  // Remove existing
  const old = document.getElementById('tm-sms-sending-modal');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const modal = document.createElement('div');
  modal.id = 'tm-sms-sending-modal';
  modal.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;
    background:rgba(10,15,30,0.85);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    padding:16px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
    backdrop-filter:blur(6px);
  `;

  modal.innerHTML = `
    <div style="
      background:#0f172a;border-radius:16px;width:100%;max-width:520px;
      box-shadow:0 30px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);overflow:hidden;
      border:1px solid #1e293b;
    ">
      <!-- Header -->
      <div style="
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#064e3b 0%,#065f46 100%);
        padding:18px 22px;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:12px;
      ">
        <div style="font-size:28px;">📲</div>
        <div>
          <div style="color:#ecfdf5;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;">Sending Results via SMS</div>
          <div style="color:#6ee7b7;font-size:12px;margin-top:2px;">
            ${isSimulated ? '⚠️ Simulation Mode — No Backend URL Set' : "Africa's Talking via Render"}
          </div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Stats bar -->
      <div style="
        display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);
        gap:1px;background:#1e293b;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;
      ">
        <div style="background:#0f172a;padding:12px;text-align:center;">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#34d399;" id="sms-stat-sent">0</div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Sent</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:#0f172a;padding:12px;text-align:center;">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#f87171;" id="sms-stat-failed">0</div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Failed</div>
        </div>
        <div style="background:#0f172a;padding:12px;text-align:center;">
          <div style="font-size:18px;font-weight:800;color:#94a3b8;" id="sms-stat-remain">${studentsWithPhone.length}</div>
          <div style="font-size:10px;color:#64748b;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em;">Remaining</div>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Progress bar -->
      <div style="padding:16px 22px 0;">
        <div style="
          background:#1e293b;border-radius:8px;height:6px;overflow:hidden;
        ">
          <div id="sms-progress-bar" style="
            height:100%;width:0%;background:linear-gradient(90deg,#059669,#34d399);
            border-radius:8px;transition:width 0.3s ease;
          "></div>
        </div>
        <div style="
          display:flex;justify-content:space-between;
          margin-top:6px;font-size:11px;color:#475569;
        ">
          <span id="sms-progress-label">Starting...</span>
          <span id="sms-progress-pct">0%</span>
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Log feed -->
      <div id="sms-log-feed" style="
        margin:14px 22px;height:180px;overflow-y:auto;
        background:#020617;border-radius:10px;padding:10px 12px;
        border:1px solid #1e293b;font-size:11px;font-family:monospace;
        scroll-behavior:smooth;
      "></div>

      <!-- Action row -->
      <div style="padding:0 22px 18px;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:10px;">
        <button id="sms-view-log-btn" style="
          padding:9px 16px;border-radius:8px;
          background:#1e293b;color:#cbd5e1;border:1px solid #334155;
          font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;display:none;
        ">📋 View Full Log</button>
        <button id="sms-close-btn" style="
          padding:9px 20px;border-radius:8px;
          background:#059669;color:#fff;border:none;
          font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;display:none;
        ">Done</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.body.appendChild(modal);

  const logFeed       = modal.querySelector('#sms-log-feed');
  const progressBar   = modal.querySelector('#sms-progress-bar');
  const progressLabel = modal.querySelector('#sms-progress-label');
  const progressPct   = modal.querySelector('#sms-progress-pct');
  const statSent      = modal.querySelector('#sms-stat-sent');
  const statFailed    = modal.querySelector('#sms-stat-failed');
  const statRemain    = modal.querySelector('#sms-stat-remain');
  const closeBtn      = modal.querySelector('#sms-close-btn');
  const viewLogBtn    = modal.querySelector('#sms-view-log-btn');

  function appendLog(text, color = '#94a3b8') {
    const line = document.createElement('div');
    line.style.cssText = `color:${color};margin-bottom:4px;line-height:1.5;`;
    line.textContent = text;
    logFeed.appendChild(line);
    logFeed.scrollTop = logFeed.scrollHeight;
  }

  closeBtn.addEventListener('click',   () => modal.remove());
  viewLogBtn.addEventListener('click', () => { modal.remove(); openSMSLogModal(); });

  // ── Fire off the batch ───────────────────────────────────
  (async () => {
    let sent   = 0;
    let failed = 0;
    const total = studentsWithPhone.length;

    appendLog(`▶ Dispatching to ${total} students...`);

    for (let i = 0; i < studentsWithPhone.length; i++) {
      const student = studentsWithPhone[i];
      const sid     = student.id || student.studentId;
      const name    = student.name || student.studentName || sid;
      // FIX: this line was missing the guardianPhone fallback — it
      // was the actual reason "no phone number" kept firing even
      // after the class-level filter above was already finding
      // students correctly. Now it matches the same field priority
      // used everywhere else.
      const phone   = student.phone || student.phoneNumber || student.contact || student.guardianPhone || '';
      const rank    = rankMap[sid] || {};

      appendLog(`⏳ [${i+1}/${total}] ${name} → ${phone}`);

      const res = await sendStudentResultsSMS(className, sid, {
        schoolName,
        assessmentName,
        category,
        level,
        phone,
        studentName: name,
        position:    rank.position  || null,
        classSize:   rank.classSize || null,
      });

      if (res.success) {
        sent++;
        const tag = res.simulated ? '[SIMULATED]' : '[DELIVERED]';
        appendLog(`  ✅ ${tag} ${name}`, '#34d399');
      } else {
        failed++;
        appendLog(`  ❌ FAILED — ${name}: ${res.error || 'Unknown'}`, '#f87171');
      }

      // Update stats
      const remain = total - (i + 1);
      statSent.textContent   = sent;
      statFailed.textContent = failed;
      statRemain.textContent = remain;
      const pct = Math.round(((i + 1) / total) * 100);
      progressBar.style.width   = pct + '%';
      progressPct.textContent   = pct + '%';
      progressLabel.textContent = remain > 0 ? `Sending to ${remain} more...` : 'Complete';

      // Small delay between sends (avoid hammering API)
      if (i < studentsWithPhone.length - 1) {
        await new Promise(r => setTimeout(r, 300));
      }
    }

    // Done
    const summary = `✅ Done: ${sent} sent, ${failed} failed${isSimulated ? ' (simulation)' : ''}.`;
    appendLog(summary, sent > 0 ? '#34d399' : '#f87171');
    showToast(summary, sent > 0 ? 'success' : 'error');
    closeBtn.style.display   = 'block';
    viewLogBtn.style.display = 'block';
    progressLabel.textContent = 'All done';
  })();
}

  modal.innerHTML = `

        ">Cancel</button>
        <button id="sms-settings-save" style="
          padding:9px 20px;border-radius:8px;border:none;
          background:#4f46e5;color:#fff;
          font-size:12px;font-weight:700;cursor:pointer;
        ">Save Settings</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.body.appendChild(modal);

  modal.querySelector('#sms-settings-close-x').addEventListener('click', () => modal.remove());
  modal.querySelector('#sms-settings-cancel').addEventListener('click',  () => modal.remove());

  modal.querySelector('#sms-settings-save').addEventListener('click', async () => {
    const newSettings = {
      backendUrl:   modal.querySelector('#sms-backend-url').value.trim(),
      gatewayToken: modal.querySelector('#sms-gateway-token').value.trim(),
      senderId:     modal.querySelector('#sms-sender-id').value.trim().substring(0, 11) || 'TEACHMATE',
    };
    await setIndexedDB(SMS_SETTINGS_KEY, SMS_SETTINGS_KEY, newSettings);
    showToast('✅ SMS settings saved', 'success');
    modal.remove();
  });
};

// ── SMS LOG VIEWER ────────────────────────────────────────────
window.openSMSLogModal = async function () {
  const old = document.getElementById('tm-sms-log-modal');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const logs = (await getIndexedDB(SMS_LOG_KEY)) || [];

  const modal = document.createElement('div');
  modal.id    = 'tm-sms-log-modal';
  modal.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;
    background:rgba(10,15,30,0.85);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    padding:16px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
    backdrop-filter:blur(6px);
  `;

  const rows = logs.slice(0, 100).map(l => `
    <tr style="border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">
      <td style="padding:8px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:11px;white-space:nowrap;">${l.sentAt ? new Date(l.sentAt).toLocaleString() : '-'}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;color:#e2e8f0;font-size:11px;">${escapeHTML(l.studentName || l.studentId || '-')}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:11px;">${escapeHTML(l.phone || '-')}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:10px;">${escapeHTML(l.assessment || '-')}</td>
      <td style="padding:8px;">
        <span style="
          padding:2px 8px;border-radius:12px;font-size:10px;font-weight:700;
          background:${l.status === 'DELIVERED' ? '#064e3b' : l.status === 'SIMULATED' ? '#1c1917' : '#450a0a'};
          color:${l.status === 'DELIVERED' ? '#34d399' : l.status === 'SIMULATED' ? '#a8a29e' : '#f87171'};
        ">${l.status || 'UNKNOWN'}</span>
      </td>
    </tr>
  `).join('');

  modal.innerHTML = `
    <div style="
      background:#0f172a;border-radius:16px;width:100%;max-width:700px;max-height:85vh;
      box-shadow:0 30px 80px rgba(0,0,0,0.6);overflow:hidden;
      border:1px solid #1e293b;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
    ">
      <div style="
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1e3a5f 0%,#1e40af 100%);
        padding:16px 22px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;
        flex-shrink:0;
      ">
        <div style="color:#bfdbfe;font-size:15px;font-weight:700;">📋 SMS Message Log</div>
        <button id="sms-log-close" style="background:none;border:none;color:#93c5fd;font-size:22px;cursor:pointer;">&times;</button>
      </div>

      <div style="overflow-y:auto;flex:1;">
        ${logs.length === 0
          ? '<div style="padding:40px;text-align:center;color:#475569;">No messages sent yet.</div>'
          : `<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;">
              <thead style="position:sticky;top:0;background:#0f172a;">
                <tr>
                  <th style="padding:10px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Time</th>
                  <th style="padding:10px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Student</th>
                  <th style="padding:10px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Phone</th>
                  <th style="padding:10px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Assessment</th>
                  <th style="padding:10px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Status</th>
                </tr>
              </thead>
              <tbody>${rows}</tbody>
            </table>`
        }
      </div>

      <div style="padding:14px 22px;border-top:1px solid #1e293b;flex-shrink:0;display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;">
        <button id="sms-log-close-btn" style="
          padding:9px 20px;border-radius:8px;border:none;
          background:#1e293b;color:#cbd5e1;
          font-size:12px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;
        ">Close</button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.body.appendChild(modal);
  modal.querySelector('#sms-log-close').addEventListener('click',     () => modal.remove());
  modal.querySelector('#sms-log-close-btn').addEventListener('click', () => modal.remove());
};

// ── WIRE NEW SMS BUTTONS INTO wireUI ─────────────────────────
// Call this ONCE after wireUI() runs.
// Looks for buttons with specific IDs in the HTML.
// Add these 3 buttons to your results HTML alongside other action buttons:
//
//   <button id="sendClassSMSBtn">📲 Send Results via SMS</button>
//   <button id="smsSettingsBtn">⚙️ SMS Settings</button>
//   <button id="smsLogBtn">📋 SMS Log</button>

(function wireSMSButtons() {
  function safeOn(id, event, handler) {
    const el = document.getElementById(id);
    if (!el) return;
    el.removeEventListener(event, handler);
    el.addEventListener(event, handler);
  }

  safeOn('sendClassSMSBtn', 'click', async () => {
    try { await sendClassResultsViaSMS(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast('SMS send failed', 'error'); }
  });

  safeOn('smsSettingsBtn', 'click', async () => {
    try { await openSMSSettingsModal(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast('Could not open SMS settings', 'error'); }
  });

  safeOn('smsLogBtn', 'click', async () => {
    try { await openSMSLogModal(); } catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast('Could not open SMS log', 'error'); }
  });
})();

// ============================================================
// ADDON EXTENSION — Student Picker for SMS (choose specific students)
// Append this block after the existing "wireSMSButtons" IIFE,
// before the final "End SMS Gateway Addon" comment.
// Reuses sendStudentResultsSMS / _computeRankings / classMatches —
// no duplication of existing send logic.
// ============================================================

/**
 * Opens a polished, scrollable student-selector modal for the
 * currently selected class, letting the teacher pick exactly which
 * students receive the SMS results report.
 */
window.openSMSStudentPickerModal = async function () {
  const classEl    = document.getElementById('results-class');
  const categoryEl = document.getElementById('results-category-filter') || document.getElementById('test-category');
  const levelEl    = document.getElementById('results-level-filter')    || document.getElementById('results-level');

  if (!classEl || !classEl.value) {
    return showToast('Select a class first', 'error');
  }

  const className = classEl.value.trim();
  const category  = categoryEl ? categoryEl.value : '';
  const level      = levelEl    ? levelEl.value    : '';

  let students = (await getIndexedDB('students')) || [];
  students = students.filter(s => classMatches(s, className));

  if (!students.length) {
    return showToast('No students found in this class', 'error');
  }

  const rankMap = await _computeRankings(className, category, level);

  // Sort: students with a phone number first, then alphabetically
  const getPhone = (s) => s.phone || s.phoneNumber || s.contact || s.guardianPhone || '';
  students.sort((a, b) => {
    const aHas = !!getPhone(a), bHas = !!getPhone(b);
    if (aHas !== bHas) return aHas ? -1 : 1;
    return (a.name || '').localeCompare(b.name || '');
  });

  _renderSMSStudentPickerModal(students, { className, category, level, rankMap, getPhone });
};

function _renderSMSStudentPickerModal(students, ctx) {
  const old = document.getElementById('tm-sms-picker-modal');
  if (old) old.remove();

  const modal = document.createElement('div');
  modal.id = 'tm-sms-picker-modal';
  modal.style.cssText = `
    position:fixed;inset:0;z-index:99999;
    background:rgba(8,12,24,0.88);
    display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
    padding:16px;font-family:-apple-system,BlinkMacSystemFont,'Segoe UI',sans-serif;
    backdrop-filter:blur(8px);
  `;

  const rows = students.map((s, idx) => {
    const sid    = s.id || s.studentId;
    const phone  = ctx.getPhone(s);
    const hasPhone = !!phone;
    const rank   = ctx.rankMap[sid] || {};
    const initials = (s.name || sid || '?')
      .split(' ').filter(Boolean).slice(0, 2)
      .map(w => w[0]?.toUpperCase() || '').join('') || '?';

    return `
      <tr class="tm-sp-row" data-sid="${_escSP(sid)}" data-has-phone="${hasPhone}"
        style="
          border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;
          ${hasPhone ? '' : 'opacity:0.45;'}
          transition:background 0.15s;
        ">
        <td style="padding:10px 12px;width:40px;">
          <input type="checkbox" class="tm-sp-checkbox" data-sid="${_escSP(sid)}"
            ${hasPhone ? '' : 'disabled'}
            style="
              width:18px;height:18px;cursor:${hasPhone ? 'pointer' : 'not-allowed'};
              accent-color:#10b981;
            ">
        </td>
        <td style="padding:10px 8px;">
          <div style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;">
            <div style="
              width:32px;height:32px;border-radius:50%;flex-shrink:0;
              background:linear-gradient(135deg,#4f46e5,#7c3aed);
              display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:center;
              font-size:12px;font-weight:700;color:#fff;
            ">${_escSP(initials)}</div>
            <div style="min-width:0;">
              <div style="color:#e2e8f0;font-size:13px;font-weight:600;white-space:nowrap;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;">
                ${_escSP(s.name || s.studentName || sid)}
              </div>
              <div style="color:#64748b;font-size:10.5px;">${_escSP(sid)}</div>
            </div>
          </div>
        </td>
        <td style="padding:10px 8px;color:#94a3b8;font-size:12px;white-space:nowrap;">
          ${rank.position ? `#${rank.position}/${rank.classSize}` : '—'}
        </td>
        <td style="padding:10px 12px;text-align:right;">
          ${hasPhone
            ? `<span style="
                padding:3px 9px;border-radius:999px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;
                background:#064e3b;color:#34d399;white-space:nowrap;
              ">📞 ${_escSP(phone)}</span>`
            : `<span style="
                padding:3px 9px;border-radius:999px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;
                background:#450a0a;color:#f87171;white-space:nowrap;
              ">No phone</span>`
          }
        </td>
      </tr>
    `;
  }).join('');

  const totalWithPhone = students.filter(s => ctx.getPhone(s)).length;

  modal.innerHTML = `
    <div style="
      background:#0f172a;border-radius:18px;width:100%;max-width:640px;max-height:88vh;
      box-shadow:0 30px 90px rgba(0,0,0,0.65);overflow:hidden;
      border:1px solid #1e293b;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
    ">
      <!-- Header -->
      <div style="
        background:linear-gradient(135deg,#1e1b4b 0%,#3730a3 100%);
        padding:18px 22px;flex-shrink:0;
      ">
        <div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;gap:12px;">
          <div>
            <div style="color:#e0e7ff;font-size:16px;font-weight:800;display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;">
              📲 Choose Students for SMS
            </div>
            <div style="color:#a5b4fc;font-size:12px;margin-top:3px;">
              ${_escSP(ctx.className)} · ${students.length} student${students.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} · ${totalWithPhone} with phone
            </div>
          </div>
          <button id="tm-sp-close-x" style="
            background:rgba(255,255,255,0.1);border:none;border-radius:50%;
            width:30px;height:30px;color:#c7d2fe;font-size:18px;cursor:pointer;
            flex-shrink:0;
          ">&times;</button>
        </div>

        <!-- Search -->
        <input type="text" id="tm-sp-search" placeholder="🔍 Search by name or ID..."
          style="
            width:100%;margin-top:14px;padding:10px 14px;border-radius:10px;
            background:rgba(0,0,0,0.25);border:1px solid rgba(255,255,255,0.12);
            color:#e2e8f0;font-size:13px;outline:none;box-sizing:border-box;
          ">
      </div>

      <!-- Toolbar -->
      <div style="
        padding:10px 22px;display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:center;
        background:#0b1120;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;flex-shrink:0;
      ">
        <label style="display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;color:#cbd5e1;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;">
          <input type="checkbox" id="tm-sp-select-all" style="width:16px;height:16px;accent-color:#10b981;cursor:pointer;">
          Select all with phone (${totalWithPhone})
        </label>
        <span id="tm-sp-selected-count" style="color:#34d399;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:700;">0 selected</span>
      </div>

      <!-- Table -->
      <div style="overflow-y:auto;flex:1;min-height:0;">
        <table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;">
          <thead style="position:sticky;top:0;background:#0f172a;z-index:1;">
            <tr>
              <th style="padding:8px 12px;"></th>
              <th style="padding:8px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Student</th>
              <th style="padding:8px 8px;text-align:left;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Rank</th>
              <th style="padding:8px 12px;text-align:right;font-size:10px;color:#475569;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.06em;border-bottom:1px solid #1e293b;">Phone</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody id="tm-sp-tbody">${rows}</tbody>
        </table>
        <div id="tm-sp-empty" style="display:none;padding:40px;text-align:center;color:#475569;font-size:13px;">
          No students match your search.
        </div>
      </div>

      <!-- Footer -->
      <div style="
        padding:16px 22px;border-top:1px solid #1e293b;flex-shrink:0;
        display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:10px;background:#0b1120;
      ">
        <button id="tm-sp-cancel" style="
          padding:10px 18px;border-radius:10px;
          background:#1e293b;color:#cbd5e1;border:1px solid #334155;
          font-size:13px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;
        ">Cancel</button>
        <button id="tm-sp-send" disabled style="
          padding:10px 22px;border-radius:10px;border:none;
          background:#334155;color:#64748b;
          font-size:13px;font-weight:700;cursor:not-allowed;
          display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;transition:background 0.15s,color 0.15s;
        ">📤 Send to <span id="tm-sp-send-count">0</span> Student<span id="tm-sp-send-plural">s</span></button>
      </div>
    </div>
  `;

  document.body.appendChild(modal);

  const tbody         = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-tbody');
  const searchInput    = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-search');
  const selectAllBox   = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-select-all');
  const selectedCountEl = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-selected-count');
  const sendBtn        = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-send');
  const sendCountEl    = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-send-count');
  const sendPluralEl   = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-send-plural');
  const emptyEl        = modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-empty');

  const selected = new Set();

  function updateFooter() {
    const n = selected.size;
    selectedCountEl.textContent = `${n} selected`;
    sendCountEl.textContent     = n;
    sendPluralEl.textContent    = n === 1 ? '' : 's';
    sendBtn.disabled = n === 0;
    sendBtn.style.background = n === 0 ? '#334155' : '#059669';
    sendBtn.style.color      = n === 0 ? '#64748b' : '#fff';
    sendBtn.style.cursor     = n === 0 ? 'not-allowed' : 'pointer';
  }

  function updateSelectAllState() {
    const checkable = [...tbody.querySelectorAll('.tm-sp-checkbox:not(:disabled)')];
    const checked   = checkable.filter(c => c.checked);
    selectAllBox.checked   = checkable.length > 0 && checked.length === checkable.length;
    selectAllBox.indeterminate = checked.length > 0 && checked.length < checkable.length;
  }

  tbody.addEventListener('change', (e) => {
    const cb = e.target.closest('.tm-sp-checkbox');
    if (!cb) return;
    const sid = cb.dataset.sid;
    if (cb.checked) selected.add(sid); else selected.delete(sid);
    updateFooter();
    updateSelectAllState();
  });

  // Row click toggles checkbox too (better mobile tap target)
  tbody.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
    const row = e.target.closest('.tm-sp-row');
    if (!row || e.target.matches('input[type="checkbox"]')) return;
    if (row.dataset.hasPhone !== 'true') return;
    const cb = row.querySelector('.tm-sp-checkbox');
    cb.checked = !cb.checked;
    cb.dispatchEvent(new Event('change', { bubbles: true }));
  });

  selectAllBox.addEventListener('change', () => {
    const checkable = [...tbody.querySelectorAll('.tm-sp-checkbox:not(:disabled)')];
    checkable.forEach(cb => {
      // Only toggle rows currently visible (respects active search filter)
      const row = cb.closest('.tm-sp-row');
      if (row.style.display === 'none') return;
      cb.checked = selectAllBox.checked;
      const sid = cb.dataset.sid;
      if (cb.checked) selected.add(sid); else selected.delete(sid);
    });
    updateFooter();
  });

  searchInput.addEventListener('input', () => {
    const q = searchInput.value.trim().toLowerCase();
    let visibleCount = 0;
    tbody.querySelectorAll('.tm-sp-row').forEach(row => {
      const text = row.textContent.toLowerCase();
      const match = !q || text.includes(q);
      row.style.display = match ? '' : 'none';
      if (match) visibleCount++;
    });
    emptyEl.style.display = visibleCount === 0 ? 'block' : 'none';
    updateSelectAllState();
  });

  modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-close-x').addEventListener('click', () => modal.remove());
  modal.querySelector('#tm-sp-cancel').addEventListener('click',  () => modal.remove());

  sendBtn.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    if (selected.size === 0) return;

    const chosenStudents = students.filter(s => selected.has(s.id || s.studentId));

    const schoolName     = prompt('School name for SMS header:', '') || '';
    const assessmentName = prompt('Assessment name (e.g. MIDTERM EXAMINATION):', ctx.category || '') || ctx.category || '';
    if (!assessmentName) {
      return showToast('Assessment name is required', 'error');
    }

    const settings    = (await getIndexedDB(SMS_SETTINGS_KEY, SMS_SETTINGS_KEY)) || DEFAULT_SMS_SETTINGS;
    const isSimulated = !settings.backendUrl || settings.backendUrl.trim() === '' || settings.backendUrl.includes('YOUR-RENDER-APP');

    const confirmed = confirm(
      `📤 Send Results via SMS\n\n` +
      `Class: ${ctx.className}\n` +
      `Assessment: ${assessmentName}\n` +
      `Recipients: ${chosenStudents.length} selected student${chosenStudents.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''}\n` +
      (isSimulated
        ? `\n⚠️  NO BACKEND URL SET — running in simulation mode.\n`
        : `\nThis will send ${chosenStudents.length} real SMS message${chosenStudents.length !== 1 ? 's' : ''} via Africa's Talking.\n`) +
      `\nProceed?`
    );
    if (!confirmed) return;

    modal.remove();

    // Reuses the existing sending modal/progress UI — same dispatch
    // path as "send to whole class", just with a filtered list.
    openSMSSendingModal(
      ctx.className, ctx.category, ctx.level, schoolName, assessmentName,
      students, chosenStudents, ctx.rankMap, settings, isSimulated
    );
  });

  updateFooter();
}

function _escSP(s) {
  return String(s || '').replace(/&/g,'&amp;').replace(/</g,'&lt;').replace(/>/g,'&gt;').replace(/"/g,'&quot;');
}

// ── WIRE: "Choose Students" button ────────────────────────────
// Add this button alongside your other SMS buttons in the HTML:
//   <button id="smsPickStudentsBtn">🎯 Choose Students for SMS</button>
(function wireSMSPickerButton() {
  const el = document.getElementById('smsPickStudentsBtn');
  if (!el) return;
  el.addEventListener('click', async () => {
    try { await window.openSMSStudentPickerModal(); }
    catch (e) { console.error(e); showToast('Could not open student picker', 'error'); }
  });
})();

// ── End student picker extension ─────────────────────────────


// ── End SMS Gateway Addon ────────────────────────────────


})(); // end Results Module


</script>





<!-- ══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
     TEACHMATE PREMIUM UI ENHANCEMENT LAYER — behaviour (additive v1)
     Pure DOM/JS additions. Does not touch any existing function,
     listener, or variable. ══════════════════════════════════════ -->
<div id="tm-bg-photo"></div>
<div id="tm-bg-photo-fg"></div>
<div id="tm-sprinkles"></div>
<button id="tm-theme-toggle" type="button" title="Toggle light / dark mode" aria-label="Toggle light and dark mode">🌙</button>

<script>
(function () {
  'use strict';

  /* ---------- 1. Dark / light toggle — draggable, position persists ---------- */
  var toggleBtn = document.getElementById('tm-theme-toggle');
  var savedTheme = null;
  try { savedTheme = localStorage.getItem('tm-theme'); } catch (e) {}
  function applyTheme(mode) {
    if (mode === 'dark') {
      document.body.classList.add('tm-dark');
      toggleBtn.textContent = '☀️';
    } else {
      document.body.classList.remove('tm-dark');
      toggleBtn.textContent = '🌙';
    }
  }
  applyTheme(savedTheme === 'dark' ? 'dark' : 'light');

  // Restore a saved drag position, if any
  try {
    var savedPos = JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('tm-toggle-pos') || 'null');
    if (savedPos && typeof savedPos.x === 'number' && typeof savedPos.y === 'number') {
      var w = toggleBtn.offsetWidth || 52, h = toggleBtn.offsetHeight || 52;
      var x = Math.max(4, Math.min(savedPos.x, window.innerWidth  - w - 4));
      var y = Math.max(4, Math.min(savedPos.y, window.innerHeight - h - 4));
      toggleBtn.style.left      = x + 'px';
      toggleBtn.style.top       = y + 'px';
      toggleBtn.style.right     = 'auto';
      toggleBtn.style.bottom    = 'auto';
      toggleBtn.style.transform = 'none';
    }
  } catch (e) {}

  // Drag handling — a small movement threshold distinguishes a drag from a tap,
  // so the button still toggles the theme on a normal click.
  var dragState = null;
  var DRAG_THRESHOLD = 6;

  function pointFromEvent(e) {
    var p = (e.touches && e.touches[0]) || (e.changedTouches && e.changedTouches[0]) || e;
    return { x: p.clientX, y: p.clientY };
  }

  function startDrag(e) {
    var p = pointFromEvent(e);
    var rect = toggleBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    dragState = {
      startX: p.x, startY: p.y,
      origX: rect.left, origY: rect.top,
      moved: false
    };
    toggleBtn.style.transition = 'none';
    document.addEventListener('mousemove', onDragMove);
    document.addEventListener('mouseup', endDrag);
    document.addEventListener('touchmove', onDragMove, { passive: false });
    document.addEventListener('touchend', endDrag);
  }

  function onDragMove(e) {
    if (!dragState) return;
    var p = pointFromEvent(e);
    var dx = p.x - dragState.startX;
    var dy = p.y - dragState.startY;

    if (!dragState.moved && (Math.abs(dx) > DRAG_THRESHOLD || Math.abs(dy) > DRAG_THRESHOLD)) {
      dragState.moved = true;
      toggleBtn.classList.add('tm-dragging');
    }
    if (!dragState.moved) return;

    if (e.cancelable) e.preventDefault(); // stop page scroll while actually dragging

    var w = toggleBtn.offsetWidth, h = toggleBtn.offsetHeight;
    var x = Math.max(4, Math.min(dragState.origX + dx, window.innerWidth  - w - 4));
    var y = Math.max(4, Math.min(dragState.origY + dy, window.innerHeight - h - 4));

    toggleBtn.style.left      = x + 'px';
    toggleBtn.style.top       = y + 'px';
    toggleBtn.style.right     = 'auto';
    toggleBtn.style.bottom    = 'auto';
    toggleBtn.style.transform = 'none';
  }

  function endDrag() {
    if (!dragState) return;
    var wasMoved = dragState.moved;
    toggleBtn.style.transition = '';
    toggleBtn.classList.remove('tm-dragging');

    document.removeEventListener('mousemove', onDragMove);
    document.removeEventListener('mouseup', endDrag);
    document.removeEventListener('touchmove', onDragMove);
    document.removeEventListener('touchend', endDrag);

    if (wasMoved) {
      var rect = toggleBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
      try { localStorage.setItem('tm-toggle-pos', JSON.stringify({ x: rect.left, y: rect.top })); } catch (e) {}
    }
    dragState = null;
  }

  toggleBtn.addEventListener('mousedown', startDrag);
  toggleBtn.addEventListener('touchstart', startDrag, { passive: true });

  // Keep the button on-screen if the window is resized/rotated
  window.addEventListener('resize', function () {
    var rect = toggleBtn.getBoundingClientRect();
    if (rect.left === 0 && rect.top === 0 && !toggleBtn.style.left) return;
    var x = Math.max(4, Math.min(rect.left, window.innerWidth  - rect.width  - 4));
    var y = Math.max(4, Math.min(rect.top,  window.innerHeight - rect.height - 4));
    toggleBtn.style.left = x + 'px';
    toggleBtn.style.top  = y + 'px';
  });

  toggleBtn.addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    // A drag that actually moved the button shouldn't also fire a toggle
    if (dragState && dragState.moved) { e.preventDefault(); return; }
    var next = document.body.classList.contains('tm-dark') ? 'light' : 'dark';
    applyTheme(next);
    try { localStorage.setItem('tm-theme', next); } catch (e2) {}
  });

  /* ---------- 2. Auth background photo — sync with #auth-section visibility ---------- */
  var authSection = document.getElementById('auth-section');
  function syncAuthBg() {
    if (!authSection) return;
    var visible = window.getComputedStyle(authSection).display !== 'none';
    document.body.classList.toggle('tm-auth-active', visible);
  }
  if (authSection) {
    syncAuthBg();
    var authObserver = new MutationObserver(syncAuthBg);
    authObserver.observe(authSection, { attributes: true, attributeFilter: ['style', 'class'] });
    // also catch cases where display is toggled on a parent instead
    window.addEventListener('load', syncAuthBg);
    setInterval(syncAuthBg, 3000); // safety-net only (MutationObserver does the real work)
  }

  /* ---------- 3. Floating sprinkles — layered for depth, richer motion ---------- */
  var sprinkleField = document.getElementById('tm-sprinkles');
  var sprinkleEmojis = ['✨', '⭐', '🌟', '💎', '🎓', '📘', '👑', '💫'];
  var LAYERS = [
    { count: 6, sizeMin: 0.6, sizeMax: 1.0, speedMin: 16, speedMax: 26, opacity: 0.30, cls: 'tm-sprinkle-far'  },
    { count: 5, sizeMin: 1.0, sizeMax: 1.5, speedMin: 10, speedMax: 18, opacity: 0.50, cls: 'tm-sprinkle-mid'  },
    { count: 3, sizeMin: 1.5, sizeMax: 2.1, speedMin: 6,  speedMax: 11, opacity: 0.75, cls: 'tm-sprinkle-near' }
  ];
  if (window.__tmLite) { LAYERS = []; }
  var emojiIdx = 0;
  LAYERS.forEach(function (layer) {
    for (var i = 0; i < layer.count; i++) {
      var s = document.createElement('span');
      s.className = 'tm-sprinkle ' + layer.cls + (Math.random() < 0.5 ? ' tm-sprinkle-alt' : '');
      s.textContent = sprinkleEmojis[emojiIdx % sprinkleEmojis.length];
      emojiIdx++;
      s.style.left = (Math.random() * 100) + 'vw';
      s.style.fontSize = (layer.sizeMin + Math.random() * (layer.sizeMax - layer.sizeMin)) + 'rem';
      s.style.setProperty('--tm-sprinkle-o', layer.opacity);
      var duration = layer.speedMin + Math.random() * (layer.speedMax - layer.speedMin);
      s.style.animationDuration = duration + 's';
      s.style.animationDelay = (-Math.random() * duration) + 's';
      sprinkleField.appendChild(s);
    }
  });
})();
</script>

</body> 
</html>
